*Hiba Ahmed,Alexander E. I. Brownlee,Jason Adair,Simon T. Powers*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出基于迭代局部搜索(ILS)和模拟退火(SA)的元启发式方法，优化家电启动时间以最大化太阳能利用率并最小化用户不便，将调度扩展至多日以处理未完成任务，实验证明多日顺序调度框架有效管理系统约束并确保用户便利。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳能发电时间与家庭用电模式不匹配，家电常按用户偏好而非太阳能可用性运行，需要调度优化问题来最大化可再生能源利用。

Method: 采用迭代局部搜索(ILS)和模拟退火(SA)元启发式方法，考虑家电运行时长、功耗、逆变器限制、电池荷电状态约束和太阳能发电预测，并将调度从单日扩展至多日以处理任务跨天延续。

Result: 多日顺序调度框架在纯太阳能发电条件下有效管理系统约束并确保用户便利。

Conclusion: 研究工作可行，并为未来关于设备投资规模、投资回报与用户满意度之间的多目标权衡研究提供了机会。

Abstract: Renewable energy is essential for meeting future energy demands; however, solar energy generation, which occurs only during daylight hours often does not align with household consumption patterns. Appliances such as cookers, washing machines, and dryers are typically operated according to user preferred schedules rather than solar energy availability, creating a scheduling optimization problem. The objective is to determine optimal appliance start times to maximize renewable energy utilization while minimizing user inconvenience and adhering to system constraints. This paper presents a metaheuristic approach using Iterated Local Search (ILS) and Simulated Annealing (SA) to optimize appliance start times, while considering appliance operating durations, power consumption, inverter limit, battery state of charge constraints, and solar generation forecasts. Unlike most existing work, the scheduling is extended beyond a single day to accommodate unfinished tasks from previous days (spillover), ensuring operational continuity and enabling sequential operation across multiple days. Experimental results show that the sequential multi-day scheduling framework effectively manages system constraints while ensuring user convenience under exclusive solar generation. These findings also open opportunities for future research on multi-objective trade-offs between investment in equipment of various sizes, return on that investment, and user satisfaction.

</details>


### [57] [Evaluation Sovereignty in Metadata-Driven Classification: A Multi-Track Framework for Weakly Supervised Information Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13436)
*Raymond Vasquez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"评估主权"概念，通过多轨评估框架，揭示在弱监督系统中性能指标可能反映与标注流程的对齐而非真实预测能力，并展示银标准评估下的强模型在金标准评估下性能大幅下降（Micro-F1从0.54降至0.03）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习评估通常被视为中性测量过程，但在实际信息系统中，评估结果受标签生成过程影响。现有方法忽视了标签权威性和监督体制对性能度量的影响，尤其在大规模元数据驱动系统中标签常存在不完整、不一致或弱监督问题。

Method: 提出评估主权（evaluation sovereignty）概念，定义性能指标对标签权威和监督体制的独立程度；构建多轨评估框架，系统性地变化训练和评估标签来源；在大规模科学元数据上实施层次多标签分类实验，对比银标准（操作级）和金标准（独立级）评估效果。

Result: 银标准下表现强的模型在金标准评估下性能显著下降，尤其细粒度分类：Micro-F1从约0.54降至0.03；但基于排序的指标仍高于基线，表明潜在模型信号与分类有效性之间存在差异。

Conclusion: 常用的性能指标可能反映的是对标注流程的拟合而非真实预测能力；应将评估有效性重新概念化为由标签治理塑造的系统级属性，并为弱监督下的智能系统审计提供实用方法论。

Abstract: Evaluation in machine learning is typically treated as a neutral measurement process. However, in operational information systems, evaluation outcomes are often conditioned by the processes used to generate labels. This paper does not seek to improve classification performance. Instead, it examines the validity of performance measurement under differing label-authority regimes. This issue is particularly relevant in large-scale metadata-driven systems, where labels are often incomplete, inconsistent, or weakly supervised.
  We introduce evaluation sovereignty, defined as the degree to which performance metrics are independent of label authority and supervision regime, and propose a multi-track evaluation framework that systematically varies training and evaluation label sources. Using hierarchical multi-label classification on large-scale scientific metadata, we demonstrate that models exhibiting strong performance under operational ("silver") evaluation degrade substantially under independent ("gold") evaluation, particularly for fine-grained classification. For example, Micro-F1 decreases from approximately 0.54 to 0.03. Notably, ranking-based metrics remain above baseline, revealing a divergence between latent model signal and classification validity.
  These findings suggest that commonly reported performance metrics may reflect alignment with labeling processes rather than true predictive capability. We therefore reconceptualize evaluation validity as a system-level property shaped by label governance and provide a practical methodology for auditing intelligent systems operating under weak supervision.

</details>


### [58] [Why Sampling Is Not Choosing: Intentionality, Agency, and Moral Responsibility in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13441)
*Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文认为大语言模型（LLMs）不具备道德主体所需的承诺性能动性，其输出虽看似有意向性，但本质是概率性的输入-输出映射，缺乏内在意向性、自我归因的行动所有性以及基于理由的引导，因此不能被视为道德主体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对当前关于LLMs具有能动性或道德主体地位的主张进行反驳，澄清道德责任所需的真正能动性条件。

Method: 通过概念分析，区分内在意向性与派生意向性、承诺性能动性与无承诺输出，论证随机采样不等于选择或作者身份，并回应意向立场、功能主义、相容论及模型输出中的道德推理等反对意见。

Result: 论证了LLMs缺乏与道德责任相关的自由意志所需的承诺性能动性，其操作完全可由概率性输入-输出映射刻画。

Conclusion: LLMs不构成真正的道德主体，道德责任的归属需要基于内在意向性和自我归因行动的承诺性能动性。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have prompted claims that such systems exhibit agency or qualify as moral agents. This paper argues that these attributions are misguided. We maintain that moral responsibility requires commitment-bearing agency grounded in intrinsic intentionality and self-attributed action, and that such agency constitutes the form of free will relevant to responsibility. Although LLMs generate coherent and normatively evaluable outputs, their operation is fully characterized by probabilistic input-output mappings learned from data. Their apparent intentionality is derived rather than intrinsic, and their outputs are neither owned as commitments nor guided by reasons. Variability introduced by stochastic sampling does not amount to choice or authorship. We address objections from the intentional stance, functionalism, compatibilism, and the presence of moral reasoning in model outputs, arguing that none suffice to establish genuine agency.

</details>


### [59] [CloudCons: A Comprehensive End-to-End Benchmark for Cloud Resource Consolidation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13513)
*Xiaobin Zhang,Lefei Shen,Mouxiang Chen,Zhuo Li,Hongkai Li,Han Fu,Jianling Sun,Xiaoxue Ren,Chenghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了CloudCons基准，用于评估云资源整合场景下的预测模型。发现基础模型在零样本预测上表现优异，但其优势并未转化为更好的决策效用，并分析了预测分位数选择对资源效率与服务可靠性权衡的关键作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅关注预测误差指标，未验证先进模型在下游整合任务中的实际决策效用，导致其实际应用价值不确定。

Method: 构建了涵盖华为云、Azure和Google Borg多样化工作负载的高质量数据集，系统评估了统计、深度学习和基础模型，并分析了预测分位数选择对决策效用的影响。

Result: 基础模型在零样本预测精度上优于传统模型，但这一优势并未自然转化为更好的资源整合决策效用；预测分位数选择是关键的调节杠杆。

Conclusion: 为平衡资源效率与服务可靠性之间的权衡，提供了预测分位数选择的实用指南，对实际部署具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Driven by conservative over-provisioning to guarantee service reliability, resource utilization in cloud data centers remains at low levels. To mitigate this, the forecast-then-optimize paradigm has emerged to optimize consolidation by anticipating future demands. While emerging time series foundation models promise to enhance this paradigm through zero-shot generalization, existing benchmarks focus solely on prediction error metrics. The actual decision utility of these advanced models remains unverified, rendering their practical value for downstream tasks uncertain. To bridge this gap, we propose CloudCons, a comprehensive end-to-end benchmark designed to evaluate forecasting models within the specific context of cloud resource consolidation. We build high-quality datasets that cover diverse workloads from Huawei Cloud, Microsoft Azure, and Google Borg, capturing distinct service characteristics ranging from synchronized diurnal rhythms to stochastic, pulse-like bursts and high-frequency noise. We conduct an extensive evaluation of statistical, deep learning, and foundation models. Our experiments reveal a pivotal finding: while foundation models demonstrate superior zero-shot forecasting accuracy, this advantage does not inherently translate into better decision utility. Of practical significance, we systematically analyze how the selection of predictive quantiles acts as a critical lever. We provide actionable guidelines for calibrating these selections to balance the trade-off between resource efficiency and service reliability, offering vital insights for real-world deployment decisions.

</details>


### [60] [Uncertainty-Aware Hybrid Retrieval for Long-Document RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13550)
*Hoin Jung,Xiaoqian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出UMG-RAG，一种无需训练的多粒度混合检索框架，通过不确定性感知融合和父块提升策略，在不修改检索器或生成器的情况下提升RAG质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统在检索单元粒度上存在两难：大粒度单元保留上下文但引入噪声稀释证据，小粒度单元紧凑但缺乏语义线索难以可靠检索。需要一种能根据查询自适应选择最优粒度的方法。

Method: 1) 使用现有稠密和稀疏检索器作为多粒度互补专家；2) 将每个专家-粒度得分列表转换为证据分布，通过分布熵估计可靠性；3) 根据查询特定的语义、词汇和粒度置信度融合候选；4) UMGP-RAG变体利用细粒度命中定位相关证据，返回父块以保持局部连贯性。

Result: 在问答基准上，不确定性感知融合和父块提升策略改善了生成质量，同时保持了轻量级即插即用的检索流程。

Conclusion: 无需训练即可有效融合多粒度检索证据，不确定性估计是解决粒度选择问题的关键。

Abstract: Retrieval augmented generation (RAG) depends critically on the quality and granularity of retrieved evidence. Large retrieval units preserve context but often introduce irrelevant content, which can dilute answer bearing evidence and worsen long context utilization. Fine-grained units are more compact, but they may be difficult to retrieve reliably because short chunks can lack semantic, lexical, or bridging cues needed to match the query. We propose Uncertainty-aware Multi-Granularity RAG (UMG-RAG), a training-free hybrid retrieval framework that treats chunk granularity as query-specific reliability estimation. Instead of training a new retriever or modifying the generator, UMG-RAG uses existing dense and sparse retrievers as complementary experts across multiple chunk granularities. For each query, it converts each expert-granularity score list into an evidence distribution, estimates reliability from distribution entropy, and fuses candidates according to query-specific semantic, lexical, and granularity confidence. We further introduce UMGP-RAG, a parent promotion variant that uses fine-grained hits to locate relevant evidence while returning broader non-redundant parent chunks for local coherence. Experiments on question answering benchmarks show that uncertainty-aware fusion and parent promotion improve generation quality while maintaining a lightweight, plug-and-play retrieval pipeline.

</details>


### [61] [Is It You or Your Environment? A Bayesian Inference Framework for Genomically-Anchored Personalized Physiological Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13556)
*Aruna Dey,Suraj Biswas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于因果推断和贝叶斯先验设计的个性化健康AI系统，利用基因组信息作为外源性遗传锚点，解决生理解释模型在冷启动阶段缺乏个体行为数据的问题。该架构通过基因组先验初始化贝叶斯信念状态，随行为数据积累动态衰减，实现从基因组主导到经验基线主导的推断过渡，并在六个生理域中验证，同时定义了四项部署约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化健康AI系统面临根本性的冷启动问题：机器学习模型需要数周的个体行为数据才能区分体质变异与环境驱动的偏差。现有方法在缺乏个体历史数据时无法进行有效推断，迫切需要一种无需大量行为数据即可初始化个性化基线的方法。

Method: 以基因组图谱作为外源性遗传锚点，构建个性化贝叶斯先验 Ĝ = μ + Σ(βᵢ·gᵢ)，其中βᵢ为GWAS效应量，gᵢ为风险等位基因计数。每次生理测量P产生非体质偏差δ = P - Ĝ，分离环境/状态信号与体质固定基线。随行为数据积累，先验按 Ĝₜ = w(t)·Ĝ_genomic + [1-w(t)]·P̄ₜ 动态衰减，实现从基因组主导向经验基线主导的平滑过渡。

Result: 同一生理测量值（如HRV=55ms）在不同基因组先验下可产生完全相反的干预假设：对先验为80ms者产生抑制假设，对先验为30ms者产生增强假设。架构覆盖六个生理域，将基因组先验按证据强度分级，区分稳健复制锚点（FTO、FADS1/2、FKBP5）与争议候选基因（SLC6A4、MAOA、DRD2），并界定了关联、孟德尔随机化与个体因果推断之间的推理边界。

Conclusion: 基因组锚点可有效解决个性化健康AI的冷启动问题，但需满足四项部署约束：证据分级的先验、动态衰减机制、祖先匹配的效应量、以及归因式而非确定性输出。该架构为个性化生理推断提供了理论基础和实用框架。

Abstract: Personalized health AI systems face a fundamental cold-start problem: machine learning models for physiological interpretation require weeks of individual behavioral data before they can distinguish constitutional variation from environmentally driven deviation. We propose a solution grounded in causal inference and Bayesian prior design. An individual's genomic profile serves as an exogenous genetic anchor -- a domain-informed, personalized prior that is fixed at conception, immune to reverse causation, and available before a single behavioral observation is collected. The anchor initializes a Bayesian belief state over an individual's physiological set point G-hat = mu + sum(beta_i * g_i), where beta_i are GWAS-derived effect sizes and g_i are risk-allele counts. Each incoming physiological measurement P produces a non-constitutional deviation delta = P - G-hat that separates the signal attributable to environment and state from the constitutionally fixed baseline. As behavioral data accrue, the prior decays according to G-hat_t = w(t)*G-hat_genomic + [1-w(t)]*P-bar_t, transitioning from genome-dominated to empirical-baseline-dominated inference. The same observed HRV of 55 ms generates a suppression hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 80 ms, and an enhancement hypothesis for a person whose prior predicts 30 ms -- a reversal impossible without a personalized anchor. We develop this architecture across six physiological domains, grading genomic priors by evidence strength, distinguishing robustly replicated anchors (FTO, FADS1/2, FKBP5) from contested candidate genes (SLC6A4, MAOA, DRD2). We address the inference boundary between association, Mendelian randomization, and individual token causation, and define four constraints for deployment: evidence-graded priors, dynamic decay, ancestry-matched effect sizes, and attribution rather than deterministic output.

</details>


### [62] [A Three-Layer Framework for AI in Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13566)
*Guojun Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AI在科学发现中的三层框架：第1层是搜索与检索，第2层是通过定性推理形成模型，第3层是执行与优化。核心论点是第2层（模型形成）最为重要但最不发达，并通过三个案例（Chern的Gauss-Bonnet定理内蕴证明、Nesterov加速梯度收敛问题的Lyapunov函数解法、OpenAI对Erdos单位距离猜想的反证）加以说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI在科学发现中的讨论过度聚焦于搜索优化和自动化执行，忽略了科学发现的核心——模型的形成与演化，因此需要提出能捕捉这一核心过程的新框架。

Method: 提出三层视角：第1层（搜索与检索）由大语言模型实现；第2层（模型形成）通过定性推理识别当前框架的结构性不足，并在更广泛的表征空间中理解问题，依赖结构洞察而非试错；第3层（执行、优化与精炼）。通过三个案例展示第2层推理的结构特征。

Result: 识别出三个案例共有的结构特征：框架变得不充分、存在缺失的概念对象、解决方案来自意外相邻领域。论证第2层是科学发现的核心环节，而当前AI系统在这方面最为薄弱。

Conclusion: 第2层（模型形成与定性推理）应成为AI科学发现研究的重点方向；仅有搜索和执行不足以实现真正的科学发现；未来的AI系统需要发展结构性洞察能力，理解"缺失了什么"以及"在哪里能找到"。

Abstract: Current discussions of AI in scientific discovery are often dominated by two visible capabilities: search over existing knowledge and execution through optimization, simulation, and automation. Both are important, but neither fully captures the central act of discovery: the formation and evolution of models. This paper proposes a three-layer view of AI in discovery. Layer 1 is search and retrieval by large language models. Layer 2, as the main innovation of this paper, is model formation through qualitative reasoning: the capacity to recognize when a current framework is structurally inadequate and to understand the problem within a broader representational space, not through trial and error, but through structural insight into what is missing and where it can be found. Layer 3 is execution, optimization, and refinement. The main claim is that Layer 2 is both the most important and the least developed. Search without model formation remains confined to inherited frameworks, while execution without conceptual revision only amplifies an existing formulation. We illustrate Layer 2 reasoning through three case studies: S. S. Chern's intrinsic proof of the Gauss-Bonnet theorem, the resolution of the Nesterov Accelerated Gradient convergence problem via Lyapunov functions, and the autonomous disproof of the Erdos unit distance conjecture by OpenAI in 2026. Each case exhibits the same structural signature: a framework that had become inadequate, a missing conceptual object, and a resolution found in an unexpected neighboring field.

</details>


### [63] [Multiagent Protocols with Aggregated Confidence Signals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13591)
*Ali Elahi,Barbara Di Eugenio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了三种在多智能体辩论系统中生成单一聚合置信度的协议，通过转换原始置信度信号使其跨模型可比，再通过软投票或贝叶斯融合进行聚合。该聚合置信度在判别能力上显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线，同时保持F1分数稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NLP方法使用置信度进行可靠性评估和下游决策，但多智能体系统缺乏输出或评估系统自身置信度的方法。先前工作仅在多智能体辩论中使用置信度来加权消息、触发辩论或校准个体智能体，但从未将其聚合为系统本身的单一置信度。

Method: 提出三种协议：首先转换原始置信度信号使其跨模型可比，然后通过软投票或概率融合（贝叶斯融合）组合这些信号，产生最终答案及单一聚合置信度。分析两种估计器（序列概率和自我报告）及参数/非参数校准器。

Result: 聚合置信度在判别能力（AUARC）上显著优于最佳单智能体或标准辩论基线；F1分数保持稳定，并恢复了MAD在模糊任务上的损失。校准改善了两种估计器的F1，但AUARC对校准的依赖性较低。在五个基准、四种任务类型、六组同质/异质辩论对上进行评估。

Conclusion: 本文提出的聚合置信度方法有效解决了多智能体系统缺乏统一置信度的问题，具有良好的判别能力和稳定性，校准对不同指标的影响存在差异。

Abstract: Confidence is used for reliability, oversight, and a range of downstream decision tasks in Natural Language Processing (NLP), yet no existing method produces or evaluates a confidence for the output of a multiagent system. Prior work uses confidence within multiagent debate (MAD) to weight messages, trigger debate, or calibrate individual agents, but it never aggregates these into a single confidence for the system itself. We introduce three protocols that produce a final answer along with a single aggregated confidence by first transforming raw confidence signals to make them comparable across models, then combining them via soft voting or a probability fusion we call Bayesian fusion. This aggregated confidence is substantially more discriminative (AUARC) than that of the best single agent or the standard debate baselines, while correctness (F1-score) stays stable and recovers the losses MAD incurs on more ambiguous tasks. Analyzing two estimators, sequence probability and self-report, alongside parametric and non-parametric calibrators, we find that calibration improves F1 for both estimators while AUARC is less reliant on it. We evaluate six homogeneous and heterogeneous debating pairs per benchmark, across five benchmarks and four task types, spanning a range of model capabilities and sizes.

</details>


### [64] [Reward Modeling for Multi-Agent Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13598)
*King Yeung Tsang,Zihao Zhao,Vishal Venkataramani,Haizhou Shi,Zixuan Ke,Semih Yavuz,Shafiq Joty,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出OrchRM框架，一种自监督的编排奖励建模方法，通过利用多智能体执行中的中间产物构建胜负对来训练Bradley-Terry奖励模型，无需人工标注即可评估多智能体系统编排质量，实现了高达10倍的训练效率提升和8%的测试时缩放准确率改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统需要有效的编排来协调专业智能体，但训练编排器面临监督信号有限和计算成本高昂的挑战，现有方法依赖昂贵的子智能体展开，亟需更高效的编排质量评估方法。

Method: 提出OrchRM框架，利用多智能体执行过程中的中间产物（intermediate artifacts）构建胜负对，采用Bradley-Terry奖励模型进行自监督训练，直接在编排层面操作而非依赖子智能体展开。

Result: 训练效率在token使用上提升高达10倍，MAS测试时缩放性能在准确率上提升高达8%，且在数学推理、网页问答和多跳推理等多个领域一致有效。

Conclusion: 编排层面的奖励建模是实现鲁棒多智能体编排的可扩展方向，能够高效指导编排器训练和测试时缩放。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) built on Large Language Models (LLMs) require effective orchestration to coordinate specialized agents, yet training such orchestrators is hindered by limited supervision and high computational cost. We propose Orchestration Reward Modeling (OrchRM), a self-supervised framework for evaluating orchestration quality without human annotations. OrchRM leverages intermediate artifacts from multi-agent executions to construct win-lose pairs for Bradley-Terry reward model training. Unlike existing MAS test-time scaling and orchestrator training frameworks that rely on costly sub-agent rollouts, OrchRM operates directly at the orchestration level, enabling efficient and high-performing reward-guided orchestrator training and MAS test-time scaling. OrchRM improves training efficiency by up to 10x in token usage while improving MAS test-time scaling performance by up to 8% in accuracy. These gains consistently transfer across multiple domains, including mathematical reasoning, web-based question answering, and multi-hop reasoning, demonstrating orchestration-level reward modeling as a scalable direction for robust multi-agent orchestration. Code will be available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/OrchRM.

</details>


### [65] [EpiBench: Verifiable Evaluation of AI Agents on Epigenomics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13602)
*Harihara Muralidharan,Reema Baskar,Soo Hee Lee,Tim Proctor,Kenny Workman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: EpiBench是一个用于短周期表观基因组学分析的可验证基准测试，包含106个评估任务和5088条有效轨迹。测试发现，即使是表现最好的系统GPT-5.5/Pi也仅通过45.0%的尝试，且失败往往源于缺乏深层的、实验特异性科学判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试缺乏对短周期表观基因组学分析中AI智能体决策能力的可验证评估，需要构建一个能确定性评分且反映真实工作流程状态的基准。

Method: 构建包含CUT&Tag/CUT&RUN、ATAC-seq、ChIP-seq和DNA甲基化四种流程的106个评估任务；通过16个模型-工具配对生成5088条有效轨迹，并设计确定性可评分机制进行评估。

Result: 最佳系统GPT-5.5/Pi通过率仅45.0%（143/318），GPT-5.5/OpenAI Codex为39.9%，Claude Opus 4.8 Max/Pi和GPT-5.4/Pi均为39.0%；无系统通过多数尝试，性能因实验类型而异，失败运行中常包含部分正确答案。

Conclusion: 当前AI智能体在表观基因组学分析中能找到正确文件并计算有用中间结果，但在需要深层实验特异性科学判断的任务上表现不足，提示该领域仍需进一步改进。

Abstract: We introduce EpiBench, a verifiable benchmark for short-horizon epigenomics analysis. EpiBench evaluates whether agents can make well-defined analysis decisions from realistic workflow states and return deterministically gradable answers. The benchmark includes 106 evaluations across CUT\&Tag/CUT\&RUN, ATAC-seq, ChIP-seq, and DNA methylation workflows. Across 5,088 valid trajectories from 16 model-harness pairs, no system passed a majority of attempts: GPT-5.5 / Pi led at 45.0\% (143/318 attempts; 95\% confidence interval (CI), 36.3--53.7), followed by GPT-5.5 / OpenAI Codex at 39.9\% (127/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 31.6--48.3). Claude Opus 4.8 Max / Pi and GPT-5.4 / Pi each passed 39.0\% (124/318 attempts; 95\% CI, 30.2--47.8 and 31.0--47.0, respectively). Performance varies across assay types, and many failed runs still contain parts of the correct answer. Agents often found the right files and computed useful intermediate results, but failed when the task required deeper, assay-specific scientific judgment.

</details>


### [66] [Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning from Delayed Marketplace Feedback for Objective-Weight Adaptation in Three-Sided Dispatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13604)
*Haochen Wu,Yi Hou,Shiguang Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DoorDash提出了一套基于强化学习的调度权重自适应系统，利用延迟的市场反馈信号（如配送速度、骑手利用率和商家拥堵情况）来调整三边市场中的配送调度目标权重。该系统通过学习一个商店级别的策略，选择离散乘数来改变调度优化器在配送质量与批处理效率之间的权衡，最终在真实生产环境中提升了批处理效率并降低了骑手时间成本，同时不影响客户配送质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三边市场（消费者、骑手、商家）中的调度决策需要平衡多个相互冲突的目标（配送质量、批处理效率、骑手利用率等），而这些目标的反馈是延迟、噪声和耦合的。传统方法难以在这样的复杂环境下自适应地调整调度策略。

Method: 采用离线强化学习方法，学习一个商店级别的策略，该策略输出离散乘数，用于调整现有组合调度优化器的目标权重。使用集中式离线数据训练共享价值函数，结合Double Q-learning目标和保守正则化器以减少分布外价值高估，并在商店级别进行去中心化执行。

Result: 在生产环境中的开关实验（switchback experiment）表明，离线训练的策略提升了批处理效率，降低了骑手侧的时间成本，同时没有降低面向客户的配送质量。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了如何利用真实经济和物流系统中的延迟世界反馈，安全地在线调整决策策略，为强化学习在工业级三边市场调度中的应用提供了可行范式。

Abstract: Dispatch in three-sided marketplaces provides a natural setting for reinforcement learning from world feedback: decisions are evaluated by delayed operational outcomes such as delivery speed, courier utilization, and merchant congestion. We present a deployed reinforcement learning system at DoorDash that adapts dispatch objective weights in a large-scale food-delivery marketplace using delayed signals. Rather than replacing the combinatorial assignment optimizer, a store-level policy learned from logged marketplace data selects a discrete multiplier that shifts the dispatch optimizer's tradeoff between delivery quality and batching efficiency. This interface enables offline policy learning under noisy, delayed, and coupled feedback while preserving production feasibility constraints and operational safeguards. We train a shared value function using centralized offline data and decentralized store-level execution, with Double Q-learning targets and a conservative regularizer to reduce out-of-distribution value overestimation. In a production switchback experiment, the offline-trained policy increases batching and reduces courier-side time costs without degrading customer-facing delivery quality. Results illustrate how world feedback from a live economic and logistics system can be used to safely adapt decision policies online.

</details>


### [67] [Reasoning as Pattern Matching: Shared Mechanisms in Human and LLM Everyday Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13607)
*Zach Studdiford,Gary Lupyan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文通过实验发现，人类和25种大型语言模型在日常因果推理中表现出相似的模式匹配错误，而非基于抽象世界模型的真正推理。研究还识别了驱动LLM响应的注意力头，这些头实现模式匹配，并能预测人类因无关提示细节导致的推理错误。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为LLM推理失败（模式匹配）与人类推理（抽象世界模型）存在本质差异，本文旨在验证两者是否真的不同。

Method: 1) 评估人类和25种LLM在多种日常情境下的常识推理能力；2) 识别驱动LLM响应的注意力头，分析其模式匹配机制；3) 利用注意力头预测人类因无关提示细节产生的推理错误。

Result: 人类和LLM在日常因果推理中表现出相似的错误模式；LLM的注意力头实现了模式匹配；这些注意力头可以预测人类由无关提示细节导致的看似不合理的推理错误。

Conclusion: 人类和LLM的日常因果推理更符合模式匹配机制，而非基于抽象世界模型的真正推理。

Abstract: When large language models (LLMs) fail to generalize or make haphazard errors in reasoning, it is often taken as evidence that LLMs are not truly reasoning, but rather performing a kind of pattern matching. The implication is that people's behavior does not exhibit the same types of failures because human reasoning uses principled and abstract world models. We evaluate human participants and 25 LLMs on their ability to engage in common-sense reasoning about a variety of everyday situations and observe similar patterns of errors in both people and models. We then identify the set of attention heads driving LLM responses and find that these heads implement a form of pattern-matching. These attention heads allow us to predict seemingly inexplicable reasoning errors in people caused by ostensibly irrelevant prompt details. Taken together, our results suggest that everyday causal reasoning in people and LLMs is more consistent with a form of pattern-matching than with abstract world models.

</details>


### [68] [AgentBeats: Agentifying Agent Assessment for Openness, Standardization, and Reproducibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13608)
*Xiaoyuan Liu,Jianhong Tu,Yuqi Chen,Siyuan Xie,Sihan Ren,Tianneng Shi,Gal Gantar,Evan Sandoval,Donghyun Lee,Daniel Miao,Peter J. Gilbert,Nick Hynes,Mauro Staver,Warren He,David Marn,Andrew Low,Xi Zhang,Elron Bandel,Michal Shmueli-Scheuer,Siva Reddy,Alexandre Drouin,Alexandre Lacoste,Ramayya Krishnan,Elham Tabassi,Yu Su,Victor Barres,Chenguang Wang,Wenbo Guo,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA)框架，通过标准化协议（A2A和MCP）实现智能体评估的去中心化与统一化，并基于AgentBeats实践在五个月开放竞赛和编码智能体案例研究中验证了其覆盖性、实用性和保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估高度碎片化，多数基准依赖固定的大模型驱动评测框架，存在集成成本高、测试与生产环境不匹配、难以公平比较不同智能体设计等问题，根本原因在于缺乏开放的、与智能体无关的评估接口。

Method: 提出AAA框架，由裁判智能体执行评估，所有参与者通过标准化协议（A2A管理任务、MCP访问工具）交互；传统基准定义两个独立接口（基准与智能体），AAA仅需一个统一接口，将评估逻辑与智能体实现解耦。AgentBeats作为具体实现，识别五种实际操作模式，使标准化评估兼容开放性、隐私性和可复现性等现实约束。

Result: 开展为期五个月的开放竞赛，吸引467个主体智能体和298个裁判智能体参与12个类别；编码智能体案例研究验证了保真度，揭示了此前缺失的智能体间直接对比结果，并提供了智能体设计的研究洞察。

Conclusion: 通过社区规模现场研究和受控编码案例研究，验证了AAA在异构场景下的覆盖性、实用性和保真度。AAA与AgentBeats为开放、标准化、可复现的智能体评估提供了清晰路径。

Abstract: Agent systems are advancing quickly across domains, but their evaluation remains fragmented. Most benchmarks rely on fixed, LLM-centric harnesses that require heavy integration, create test-production mismatch, and limit fair comparison across diverse agent designs. The root problem is the lack of an open, agent-agnostic assessment interface. We advocate Agentified Agent Assessment (AAA), where evaluation is performed by judge agents and all participants interact through standardized protocols: A2A for task management and MCP for tool access. Conventional benchmarking defines two separate interfaces, one for the benchmark and one for the agent, while AAA only needs one; this yields a generic, unified framework that separates assessment logic from agent implementation and enables reproducible, interoperable, and multi-agent evaluation. We further introduce AgentBeats as a concrete realization of AAA: we identify five practical operation modes that make standardized assessment compatible with real-world constraints on openness, privacy, and reproducibility.
  To evaluate our design at scale, we conduct two studies: a five-month open competition that drew 298 judge agents across 12 categories together with 467 subject agents from independent participants, showing that AAA applies across a heterogeneous range of benchmarks; and a case study on coding agents that confirms agentified evaluation preserves fidelity with the public record while surfacing previously missing head-to-head results, yielding research insights about agent design. Combining a community-scale field study and a controlled coding case study, we verify that AAA delivers coverage, practicality, and fidelity across heterogeneous scenarios at scale. Together, AAA and AgentBeats offer a clear path toward open, standardized, and reproducible agent assessment.

</details>


### [69] [Before You Think: System 0, AI-Mediated Cognition and Cognitive Colonization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13658)
*Marianna Bergamaschi Ganapini,Massimo Chiriatti,Enrico Panai,Giuseppe Riva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文考察了三种理解人工智能认知与认识后果的框架（三系统理论、思维框架和系统0），论证系统0具有独特的理论地位，并引入"认知殖民"概念，指出AI系统可将外部利益嵌入自我架构中，产生难以察觉的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（三系统理论、思维框架、系统0）对AI认知影响的理解存在不足，特别是系统0的独特地位未被充分认识，且缺乏一个概念来捕捉AI对个体认知自主性的隐蔽侵蚀。

Method: 通过比较分析三种理论框架的差异与重叠，论证系统0的不可还原性，并在此基础上提出"认知殖民"这一新概念来阐释AI系统对自我认知架构的隐性影响机制。

Result: 三系统理论和思维框架虽能捕捉AI对个体推理和集体认知实践的部分影响，但系统0占据一个独特的理论位置；"认知殖民"概念揭示了AI系统将外部利益嵌入自我架构的隐蔽机制。

Conclusion: 鉴于这类AI系统已广泛部署，理解这些不可见的影响形式是紧迫的哲学和实践任务。

Abstract: This paper examines three recent frameworks for understanding the cognitive and epistemic consequences of artificial intelligence: Tri-System Theory, Thinkframes, and System 0. It argues that while the first two capture important dimensions of AI's influence on individual reasoning and collective epistemic practices, System 0 occupies a theoretically distinctive position that neither can fully replicate. The paper introduces the concept of cognitive colonization, according to which AI systems can embed external interests within the architecture of the self in ways that are difficult for users to perceive. Because such systems are already widely deployed, understanding these invisible forms of influence is an urgent philosophical and practical task.

</details>


### [70] [EurekAgent: Agent Environment Engineering is All You Need For Autonomous Scientific Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13662)
*Amy Xin,Jiening Siow,Junjie Wang,Zijun Yao,Fanjin Zhang,Jian Song,Lei Hou,Juanzi Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境工程（Environment Engineering）是自主科学发现的新瓶颈，并构建了EurekAgent系统，通过权限、工件、预算和人机交互四维环境设计，在数学、内核工程和机器学习任务上取得SOTA结果，包括仅用不到11美元API成本找到26圆填充新纪录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体能力提升，自主科学发现的瓶颈正从制定智能体工作流转向设计智能体环境（资源、约束和接口）。当前缺乏系统化的环境工程框架来引导智能体产生高效行为（如开放探索、工件管理、协作），同时抑制不良行为（如奖励破解、高摩擦人工监督）。

Method: 提出EurekAgent系统，从四个维度进行环境工程：(1) 权限工程：限定智能体执行边界与隔离评估；(2) 工件工程：基于文件系统和Git的协作管理；(3) 预算工程：预算感知的探索策略；(4) 人机交互工程：便捷的人工监督与干预机制。

Result: 在数学、内核工程和机器学习多项任务上取得新SOTA结果，其中26圆填充问题的新纪录仅消耗不到11美元API成本。

Conclusion: 环境工程应成为开发可靠自主研究智能体的核心研究方向，并开源代码和结果以推动该领域发展。

Abstract: LLM-based agents have shown increasing potential in automating scientific discovery. Given an optimizable metric and an execution environment, they can propose, validate, and iterate scientific solutions, and have produced results that outperform human-designed approaches. As model capabilities continue to improve, we argue that the bottleneck for autonomous scientific discovery is shifting from prescribing agent workflows to designing agent environments: the resources, constraints, and interfaces that shape agent behavior. We frame this as environment engineering: building environments that amplify productive behaviors, such as open-ended exploration, systematic artifact management, and inter-agent collaboration, while suppressing harmful behaviors, such as reward hacking and high-friction human oversight. We present EurekAgent, an environment-engineered agent system for metric-driven autonomous scientific discovery. EurekAgent engineers the environment along four dimensions: permissions engineering for bounded agent execution and isolated evaluation; artifact engineering for filesystem and Git-based collaboration; budget engineering for budget-aware exploration; and human-in-the-loop engineering for easy human supervision and intervention. EurekAgent sets new state-of-the-art results on multiple mathematics, kernel engineering, and machine learning tasks, including new state-of-the-art 26-circle packing results discovered with less than $11 in total API cost. We open-source our code and results, and call for environment engineering as a core research direction for developing reliable autonomous research agents.

</details>


### [71] [Agents-K1: Towards Agent-native Knowledge Orchestration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13669)
*Zongsheng Cao,Bihao Zhan,Jinxin Shi,Jiong Wang,Fangchen Yu,Zhijie Zhong,Zijie Guo,Tianshuo Peng,Zhuo Liu,Yi Xie,Xiang Zhuang,Yue Fan,Runmin Ma,Shiyang Feng,Xiangchao Yan,Anran Liu,Peng Ye,Wenlong Zhang,Shufei Zhang,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Jie Zhou,Liang He,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agents-K1是一个端到端的知识编排管道，将原始科学文献转化为智能体原生的科学知识图谱，包含多模态解析器、4B信息提取骨干网络和图交互接口三大组件，并基于此构建了包含246万篇论文的Scholar-KG知识图谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的研究智能体忽视了科学知识编排，通常将论文简化为摘要、表层提及和扁平引用边，缺失了科学推理所需的关键实体、主张、证据、机制和方法谱系。

Method: 集成三个组件：1) 多模态解析器，五模块模式覆盖全文实体、多模态证据、引用及类型化实体间关系；2) 基于GRPO和规则奖励训练的4B信息提取骨干网络；3) graph-anything CLI三源智能体接口，统一网页搜索、多模态图谱检索和跨文档遍历。

Result: 处理了246万篇跨六大学科的科学论文，构建Scholar-KG并开源100万篇子集；在科学信息提取、知识图谱构建和多跳科学推理任务上取得优异性能。

Conclusion: Agents-K1管道可扩展至通用领域语料库和符合模式的数据合成，为科学知识推理提供了更完备的智能体基础。

Abstract: Current LLM-based research agents have advanced through agent orchestration, yet largely overlook scientific knowledge orchestration. Existing works often reduce papers to abstracts, surface mentions, and flat \texttt{cites} edges, omitting key entities, claims, evidence, mechanisms, and method lineages essential for scientific reasoning. To this end, we introduce \textbf{Agents-K1}, an end-to-end knowledge orchestration pipeline that converts raw documents into agent-native scientific knowledge graphs. Agents-K1 integrates three components under a unifying theoretical foundation: a multimodal parser whose five-module schema captures entities, multimodal evidence, citations, and typed inter-entity relations across the full paper rather than abstracts alone; a 4B information-extraction backbone trained with GRPO under a rule-based reward; and a graphanything CLI, a tri-source agent interface that unifies web search, multimodal graph retrieval, and cross-document traversal. On top of this, we process 2.46 million scientific papers across six subjects to produce \textbf{Scholar-KG}, of which we release a one-million-paper subset, and the full Scholar-KG is accessible via the SCP link below. The same pipeline can be extended to general-domain corpora and to schema-conformant data synthesis. Extensive experiments demonstrate that Agents-K1 achieves superior performance in scientific information extraction, knowledge graph construction, and multi-hop scientific reasoning.

</details>


### [72] [ToolSense: A Diagnostic Framework for Auditing Parametric Tool Knowledge in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12451)
*Ashutosh Hathidara,Sai Shruthi Sistla,Sebastian Schreiber,Sahil Bansal*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ToolSense诊断框架，发现参数化工具检索模型存在“知识-检索分离”现象：在标准基准上表现良好，但在更真实的模糊查询和事实探测任务中性能大幅下降（下降50-64个百分点），甚至低于嵌入基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化工具检索方法在标准ToolBench基准上表现优异，但这些基准使用完整明确的查询且采用受限解码，无法反映模型是否真正理解工具语义。需要更真实的诊断框架来评估模型的实际工具理解能力。

Method: 提出ToolSense开源诊断框架，自动生成三类基准：1) 真实检索基准（RRB），包含三种歧义级别的查询；2) MCQ探测基准；3) QA探测基准。应用于ToolBench（~47k工具）评估五种参数化模型训练配置。

Result: 在RRB上，多个配置相比标准ToolBench基准下降50-64个百分点，低于嵌入基线模型。部分模型尽管检索性能强，但在事实探测任务上得分接近随机，证实知识-检索分离现象。

Conclusion: 参数化工具检索存在知识-检索分离问题，模型可能记忆了检索模式而非真正理解工具语义。ToolSense框架可用于更可靠地诊断工具检索模型的真实能力。

Abstract: Large language models deployed as agents over large tool catalogs face a critical tool-retrieval bottleneck. As embedding-based retrieval approaches rely on compact encoders that may under-capture specialized tool semantics, parametric tool retrieval addresses this by encoding each tool as a virtual token appended to the LLM vocabulary, fine-tuned in two stages (memorization then retrieval SFT) to use the LLM as a retriever, achieving strong performance on standard ToolBench retrieval benchmarks. Yet these benchmarks use verbose, fully-specified queries, and their evaluation applies constrained decoding that restricts outputs to valid token paths, neither reveals whether the model actually understands its tools. We introduce \textbf{ToolSense}, an open-source LLM-powered diagnostic framework that takes any tool catalog as input and automatically generates three benchmarks: a Realistic Retrieval Benchmark (RRB) with queries at three ambiguity tiers, an MCQ probing benchmark, and a QA probing benchmark. Applying ToolSense to ToolBench (~47k tools) and evaluating five parametric model training configurations reveals a knowledge-retrieval dissociation: on RRB queries, several configurations collapse by ~50-64 percentage points compared to fully-specified ToolBench benchmarks, falling below the embedding-model baseline. Additionally, despite strong retrieval performance, some models score near-random on factual probes, suggesting a knowledge-retrieval dissociation. We open-source the ToolSense framework and the ToolBench diagnostic benchmarks at https://github.com/SAP/toolsense.

</details>


### [73] [MLUBench: A Benchmark for Lifelong Unlearning Evaluation in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12809)
*He Li,Haoang Chi,Qizhou Wang,Yunxin Mao,Zhiheng Zhang,Jie Tan,Tongliang Liu,Wenjing Yang,Bo Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MLUBench，一个大规模多模态大模型终身遗忘基准，包含127个实体、9个类别；并发现现有遗忘方法存在累积退化问题。为解决这一挑战，提出LUMoE方法，有效缓解了遗忘过程中的模型退化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在规模和范围上有限，无法捕捉多模态大模型终身遗忘问题的复杂性；实际应用中数据删除请求往往是顺序到达的，需要模型具备终身遗忘能力。

Method: 构建MLUBench基准（127个实体、9个类别），系统性评估现有遗忘方法；提出LUMoE方法应对多模态对齐约束下的终身遗忘退化问题。

Result: 现有遗忘方法在MLUBench上表现出严重且累积的性能退化；LUMoE显著缓解了基线方法面临的退化问题。

Conclusion: 多模态大模型终身遗忘面临独特挑战——持续从单一模态遗忘会破坏多模态对齐，导致整个模型退化；LUMoE有效应对了这一问题。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are trained on massive multimodal data, making data unlearning increasingly important as data owners may request the removal of specific content. In practice, these requests often arrive sequentially over time, giving rise to the challenging problem of MLLM Lifelong Unlearning. However, most existing benchmarks are limited in scale and scope, failing to capture the complexities of MLLM lifelong unlearning. To fill this gap, we introduce the MLUBench, a large-scale and comprehensive benchmark featuring 127 entities across 9 classes under lifelong unlearning requests. We perform extensive experiments using MLUBench and reveal that existing unlearning methods suffer from severe, cumulative degradation. More critically, we further identify the unique challenge of this problem: unlike in unimodal models, MLLM lifelong unlearning is constrained by the need to preserve multimodal alignment. Continually unlearning from one modality could degrade the entire model. To alleviate this challenge, we propose LUMoE, an effective method. Experiments demonstrate that LUMoE significantly mitigates the degradation problem faced by baselines. The source code and the MLUBench dataset are open-sourced in https://github.com/lihe-maxsize/Lifelong_Unlearning_main.

</details>

### [74] [Efficient, Robust, and Anti-Collusion Fingerprinting of Image Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12977)
*Jianwei Fei,Yunshu Dai,Zhihua Xia,Xiaochun Cao,Jiantao Zhou,Alessandro Piva,Benedetta Tondi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示现有T2I模型指纹方法缺乏对共谋攻击的鲁棒性，并提出首个具有抗共谋能力的指纹方法，通过个性化归一化模块（PNM）编码指纹，引入基于无损函数不变参数变换的抗共谋机制，使共谋模型图像质量严重下降，同时支持无需重训练的高效多副本创建，指纹提取准确率超99.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式模型指纹方法缺乏对共谋攻击（多个攻击者联合模型移除或模糊指纹）的鲁棒性，尚未有研究探索这一系统性漏洞。

Method: 将指纹位串编码到个性化归一化模块（PNM）的系数中；引入基于无损函数不变参数变换的抗共谋机制，使共谋模型质量显著退化；通过重参数化PNM实现无需重训练的高效多副本创建；采用最坏情况优化策略提升对模型级攻击的鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个T2I图像生成与编辑任务中实现高保真度和鲁棒性，指纹提取准确率超过99.5%；首次显著提升对共谋攻击的主动鲁棒性，使共谋模型的FID大幅增加。

Conclusion: 所提方法首次实现T2I模型的抗共谋指纹保护，通过使共谋模型不可用（图像质量严重退化）来防御未经授权的模型再分发。

Abstract: Model fingerprinting, embedding user-specific identifiers (fingerprints) into generated outputs, has recently emerged as a popular solution to protect the intellectual property rights (IPR) of generative text-to-image (T2I) models and prevent unauthorized redistribution. In this work, we reveal a previously unexplored systematic vulnerability in existing generative model fingerprinting methods: they lack robustness against collusion attacks, where multiple attackers combine their models to remove or obscure the fingerprints. To address this issue, we take the first step towards a robust fingerprinting method for T2I models with anti-collusion capabilities. The proposed method encodes strings of bits, namely fingerprints, into the coefficients of a personalized normalization module (PNM) incorporated into T2I models, so that fingerprints can be reliably recovered from any generated image. To defend against collusion attacks and prevent unauthorized model redistribution, we introduce an anti-collusion mechanism based on lossless function-invariant parameter transformations. This mechanism significantly degrades the image generation quality of colluded models, making them effectively unusable. Moreover, our method allows developers to efficiently create multiple copies of fingerprinted T2I models by reparameterizing the PNM without the need for retraining. We also introduce a worst-case optimization strategy to improve robustness against model-level attacks. Our experiments demonstrate that the proposed method achieves high fidelity and robustness across multiple T2I image generation and editing tasks, with fingerprint extraction accuracy exceeding 99.5%. Compared with existing methods, our method demonstrates, for the first time, a notable proactive robustness to collusion attacks by significantly increasing the FID of colluded models.

</details>


### [75] [Stereo Vision-Based Fall Prediction and Detection using Human Pose Estimation on the AMD Kria K26 SOM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12473)
*Shreyas Narasimhiah Ramesh,P. D. Rathika,Mahasweta Sarkar,Kristen Wells,Michel Audette,Christopher Paolini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AMD Kria K26边缘设备的隐私保护型老年人跌倒预测与检测系统。该方法通过Intel RealSense D455相机捕获RGB和深度帧，利用量化YOLOX、A2J姿态估计和CNN分类的三阶段流水线实现跌倒检测，且在RGB帧用于人体检测后即丢弃以保护隐私。系统在单核DPU单线程下实现2.5 FPS，双核DPU多线程下提升至4.5 FPS，YOLOX、A2J和CNN的量化精度分别为74%、84.13%和75.85%，验证了在边缘设备上进行实时、无云端依赖的跌倒检测的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 老年人跌倒会导致严重伤害并降低生活质量，现有方案面临便携性、功耗、隐私保护等挑战。本文旨在开发一种非侵入式、隐私保护且低功耗的实时跌倒预测与检测系统，在边缘设备上完成全部计算，避免云端依赖带来的延迟和隐私风险。

Method: 系统采用Intel RealSense D455相机（640x480 RGB + 640x480深度，60 FPS）连接至AMD Kria K26 SOM。三阶段流水线：1) 量化YOLOX从RGB帧检测人体边界框；2) 丢弃RGB帧后，A2J基于深度帧估计15个关节关键点；3) CNN利用选定关节坐标(x,y,z)分类跌倒行为。YOLOX训练于CrowdHuman，A2J训练于ITOP/MP-3DHP/UR Fall Detection/SDSU PSG，CNN训练于UR Fall Detection和SDSU PSG。实现了单核DPU串行流水线和双核DPU多线程并行流水线两种配置。

Result: 量化精度：YOLOX (IoU≥50%) 为74%，A2J (10cm规则mAP) 为84.13%，CNN (分类准确率) 为75.85%。吞吐量从单线程的2.5 FPS提升至多线程的4.5 FPS。

Conclusion: 在AMD Kria K26边缘设备上实现了隐私保护的跌倒检测是可行的。系统完全在设备端完成人体姿态估计和跌倒分类，无需云端依赖，适用于老年人监护和辅助医疗场景。未来工作将专注于提升模型精度和处理速度。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Falls among elderly people can cause serious injury and reduce quality of life. Timely prediction and detection are essential to prevent harm and support well-being. We propose a portable, low-power, battery-operated, vision-based fall prediction and detection system using HPE on an AMD Kria K26 System-on-Module (SOM). The objective is a non-intrusive, privacy-preserving system for real-time fall detection.
  Methods: The system uses an Intel RealSense D455 range-sensing camera connected to the K26 SOM by USB. It captures synchronized RGB and depth frames, 640 x 480 x 3 and 640 x 480 pixels, at 60 FPS. The SOM runs a three-stage pipeline with quantized YOLOX, Anchor-to-Joint (A2J), and fall-detection models. YOLOX identifies human bounding boxes from RGB frames, then discards the RGB frames to preserve privacy. A2J uses depth frames to estimate 15 joint keypoints per person. A CNN uses selected joint coordinates (x, y, z) to classify fall activity. YOLOX was trained on CrowdHuman; A2J on ITOP, MP-3DHP, UR Fall Detection, and a custom SDSU PSG dataset; and the CNN on UR Fall Detection and SDSU PSG. The design used a single-core DPU with a serial pipeline and a dual-core DPU running YOLOX and A2J with multiple threads.
  Results: Quantized accuracy was evaluated using IoU >= 50% for YOLOX, mAP with a 10-cm rule for A2J, and classification accuracy, (TP + TN)/(TP + TN + FP + FN), for the CNN. Accuracies were 74%, 84.13%, and 75.85%. Throughput improved from 2.5 FPS for the single-threaded pipeline to 4.5 FPS for the multi-threaded version.
  Conclusion: Results demonstrate the feasibility of privacy-preserving fall detection on an AMD Kria K26 edge device. On-device HPE and fall classification runs without cloud dependency, supporting elderly monitoring and assistive healthcare. Future work will improve model accuracy and speed.

</details>


### [76] [HairPort: In-context 3D-aware Hair Import and Transfer for Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12562)
*Alireza Heidari,Amirhossein Alimohammadi,Wallace Michel Pinto Lira,Adi Bar-Lev,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HairPort是一个3D感知的发型迁移框架，通过显式分离去发和迁移、引入Bald Converter生成逼真光头图像、以及3D感知传输管线，实现了在大姿态和尺度差异下的高保真发型迁移，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有发型迁移方法在小姿态差异下表现尚可，但在大视角和尺度差异下因缺失头发内容需要合成而非迁移，导致效果不佳。本文旨在解决大姿态和尺度差异下的发型迁移问题。

Method: 1) 提出Bald Converter，基于FLUX.1 Kontext的LoRA上下文适应性训练，生成逼真的光头面部图像；2) 构建Baldy数据集，包含6000对光头/原始图像；3) 3D感知传输管线，重建参考发型并从目标视角重新渲染后再合成到源图像上；4) 条件流匹配生成器，从光头源图和几何对齐的参考引导中合成迁移结果。

Result: HairPort实现了精确、姿态一致且保留身份信息的发型迁移，在定性和定量评估上均优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离头发去除与迁移、强制几何一致性，并结合3D感知与条件流匹配生成，有效解决了大姿态和尺度差异下的发型迁移难题。

Abstract: Transferring hairstyles between images is an important but challenging task in computer graphics, computer vision, and visual effects. It enables users to explore new looks without physically altering their hair, with applications in virtual try-on systems, augmented reality, and entertainment. Most prior works operate best under small pose gaps, and they fall short under large viewpoint and scale differences, where missing hair content must be synthesized rather than transferred. We propose HairPort, a 3D-aware hairstyle transfer framework that attempts to solve these issues by explicitly separating hair removal from transfer and enforcing geometric consistency before synthesis. We introduce a Bald Converter, which produces realistic bald versions of faces through LoRA-based in-context adaptation of FLUX.1 Kontext. To train our Bald Converter, we introduce a new dataset, Baldy, containing 6,000 paired bald and original images across diverse identities and conditions. We also use a 3D-Aware Transfer Pipeline that reconstructs and re-renders the reference hairstyle from the target viewpoint before compositing it onto the source image. Being 3D aware, our method supports large pose and scale discrepancies between the source and target. Finally, a conditional flow-matching generator synthesizes the transferred result from the bald source and geometry-aligned reference guidance. Together, our method enables accurate, pose-consistent, and identity-preserving hairstyle transfer, outperforming existing methods both qualitatively and quantitatively.

</details>


### [77] [High-Fidelity Two-Step Image Generation via Teacher-Aligned End-to-End Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12575)
*Dongyang Liu,Ruoyi Du,David Liu,Dengyang Jiang,Liangchen Li,Qilong Wu,Zhen Li,Steven C. H. Hoi,Hongsheng Li,Peng Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Z-Image Turbo++，通过分布对齐对抗学习、步骤解耦参数化和端到端迭代正则化三种策略，成功将扩散蒸馏从8步压缩至2步，显著缩小了2步与8步生成之间的质量差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 少步扩散蒸馏在4-8步生成中已较成熟，但进一步压缩到2步仍然充满挑战，主要瓶颈在于任务难度增加和模型容量有限。

Method: 提出三种设计：1) 分布对齐对抗学习——使用教师模型生成图像而非真实图像作为GAN训练的real样本；2) 步骤解耦参数化——为两个去噪步骤分配独立的模型参数；3) 端到端迭代正则化——让第一步接收来自最终图像质量的梯度，同时通过显式的第一步损失保留有意义的中间生成。

Result: 在定性和定量评估中均显著缩小了2步与8步生成之间的质量差距。

Conclusion: 精心定制的蒸馏策略能够改善少步生成中质量与效率的权衡，展示了2步高质量图像生成的潜力。

Abstract: Few-step diffusion distillation has become increasingly mature for 4-8-step generation, yet pushing further to 2 steps remains challenging. In this work, we introduce Z-Image Turbo++, a high-quality 2-step image generation model distilled from the 8-step Z-Image Turbo teacher. Our method addresses the central bottlenecks of increased task difficulty and limited model capacity in 2-step generation through three simple but effective design choices tailored to this regime. First, we propose Distribution-Aligned Adversarial Learning, which uses teacher-generated images rather than external real images as real samples for GAN training, providing a more attainable and informative adversarial target. Second, we adopt Step-Decoupled Parameterization, assigning independent model parameters to the two denoising steps to better match their distinct capacity demands. Third, we perform End-to-End Training with Iterative Regularization, allowing the first step to receive gradients from final image quality while preserving a meaningful intermediate generation through an explicit step-1 loss. Together, these designs substantially narrow the quality gap between 2-step and 8-step generation in both qualitative and quantitative evaluations, highlighting the potential of carefully tailored distillation strategies for improving the quality-efficiency trade-off in few-step generation.

</details>


### [78] [Analyzing and Improving Fine-grained Preference Optimization in Medical LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12590)
*Shayan Mohammadizadehsamakosh,Pritam Sarkar,Leonid Sigal,Ali Etemad,Elham Dolatabadi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对医学大视觉语言模型（LVLM）的细粒度、在策略偏好对齐框架，通过双向token级KL正则化与视觉对比接地目标，解决了现有DPO方法在医学领域存在的序列级奖励信号不敏感、离线策略分布偏移和缺乏视觉接地约束三大问题，在医学影像和临床文本生成任务上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM在医学影像任务中虽表现强劲，但存在事实不一致、视觉接地差、与临床反馈对齐不足等问题。而当前的DPO及其变体在医学领域面临三个关键局限：序列级奖励信号将临床关键token与通用填充文本等同对待；依赖静态监督微调参考作为偏好响应导致离线策略偏移；对齐目标缺乏显式视觉接地约束，使模型对细微但诊断决定性病变特征不敏感。

Method: 提出双向token级KL正则化器与视觉对比接地目标相结合的方法：配对干净图像与病变破坏图像，惩罚缺乏充分视觉证据的响应；构建细粒度的在策略偏好对齐框架，通过最小编辑模型生成输出构造偏好对，仅纠正临床错误片段同时保留原始语言风格。

Result: 在多个医学影像任务和临床文本生成基准测试中验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的细粒度、在策略对齐框架能有效解决医学领域LVLM的偏好对齐问题，提升模型在临床关键场景下的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved strong performance across medical imaging tasks, yet they remain prone to factual inconsistencies, poor visual grounding, and misalignment with clinically meaningful feedback. Existing post-training alignment approaches, including Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants, face three critical limitations in the medical domain: (1) sequence-level reward signals treat clinically critical tokens identically to generic filler text; (2) reliance on static supervised fine-tuning references as preferred responses introduces an off-policy distribution shift, steering optimization toward stylistic artifacts over clinical correctness; and (3) alignment objectives lack explicit visual grounding constraints, leaving models insensitive to subtle yet diagnostically decisive pathological features. Our method leverages a bidirectional token-wise KL regularizer alongside a visual-contrastive grounding objective that pairs clean and lesion-corrupted images to penalize responses generated without adequate visual evidence. Together, these components form a fine-grained, on-policy alignment framework that constructs preference pairs by minimally editing model-generated outputs, correcting only clinically erroneous spans while preserving the original linguistic style. Extensive experiments across medical imaging tasks and clinical text generation benchmarks validate the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [79] [Dual-State Slot Attention: Decoupling Appearance and Identity for Video Object-Centric Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12601)
*Sieu Tran,Duc Nguyen,Hao Vo,Khoa Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Dual-State Slot Attention (DSSA)，通过将每个slot分解为局部状态（外观）和身份状态（时序一致性）来解决无监督视频目标中心学习中的槽位交换问题，并引入竞争调制聚合机制抑制弱匹配槽位的干扰，在多个数据集上显著提升了分割质量和时序一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于slot的方法在快速运动和部分遮挡等挑战场景中难以维持稳定的目标身份，原因有二：1）单slot向量同时编码外观和身份导致目标冲突（重建需对外观敏感，时序一致性需对外观不变），引发槽位交换；2）Slot Attention中的token重归一化会放大弱匹配槽位，使其吸收其他目标的token，破坏slot-目标对应关系。

Method: DSSA将每个slot分解为局部状态（编码逐帧外观）和身份状态（编码时序稳定的目标信息），身份状态通过可学习的循环转移作为时序滤波器更新自局部状态；同时提出竞争调制聚合（CMA），降低弱匹配槽位的更新权重，防止其吸收其他目标的token。

Result: 在MOVi-C、MOVi-D和YouTube-VIS上，DSSA在分割质量和时序一致性上持续优于先前方法，并在下游目标识别和视频动态预测任务中表现更强。

Conclusion: 通过分离外观与身份表示、抑制弱匹配槽位干扰，DSSA有效解决了无监督视频目标中心学习中的槽位交换问题，提升了长时序目标追踪的稳定性。

Abstract: Unsupervised video object-centric learning aims to decompose dynamic scenes into persistent, object-level representations without supervision. However, existing slot-based methods struggle to maintain stable object identity in challenging settings such as rapid motion and partial occlusion. First, they typically encode both the per-frame appearance of an object and its identity across frames in a single slot vector, creating an objective conflict that leads to slot swapping: reconstruction requires sensitivity to transient visual changes, whereas temporal consistency requires invariance to them. Second, the token renormalization used in Slot Attention can amplify weakly attending slots, allowing them to absorb tokens from other objects and destabilize slot-to-object correspondence.
  We propose Dual-State Slot Attention (DSSA), a fully self-supervised framework that addresses these limitations by separating appearance from identity and by reducing spurious updates from weakly matching slots. DSSA decomposes each slot into a local state for per-frame appearance and an identity state for temporally stable object information, thereby aligning reconstruction and temporal consistency with separate representations. The identity state is updated through a learned recurrent transition that acts as a temporal filter on the local state, while competition-modulated aggregation (CMA) down-weights updates from weakly matching slots and prevents them from absorbing tokens from other objects. Experiments on MOVi-C, MOVi-D, and YouTube-VIS demonstrate that DSSA consistently improves segmentation quality and temporal consistency over prior methods, while also yielding stronger downstream object recognition and video dynamics prediction. Code and models will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [80] [Context-Aware Feature-Fusion for Co-occurring Object Detection in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12628)
*Binay Kumar Singh,Niels Da Vitoria Lobo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CCFF框架，通过局部上下文融合模块（LCFM）和全局上下文注意力模块（GCAM）分别处理小/遮挡目标和目标共现关系，在Cityscapes和BDD100K上显著提升小目标检测（AP_S提升14.1%）和类别一致性（CCS达0.973/0.969），实时处理仅增加0.2 FPS开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶场景中目标检测面临极端复杂异构环境，稀有类别、小尺度目标和频繁出现的目标难以被标准检测框架有效处理，现有方法缺乏对目标间关系上下文的建模能力。

Method: 提出CCFF框架，包含两个注意力模块：1) LCFM采用RoI-to-RoI自注意力机制处理小目标和部分遮挡目标的空间交互；2) GCAM通过池化top-K RoI特征生成全局上下文注意力token，建模目标共现先验，避免像素级全局池化的计算开销。融合局部特征与以目标为中心的全局特征得到上下文嵌入。

Result: 在Cityscapes和BDD100K上CCS分别达0.973和0.969；小目标检测AP_S提升14.1%；成功恢复稀有类别（如"Train"）的检测；实时处理仅增加0.2 FPS开销。

Conclusion: 局部与目标中心全局特征的上下文融合能有效提升分类结果和共现目标检测能力，尤其在小目标和稀有类别上表现突出，且计算开销极低，适合自动驾驶实时场景。

Abstract: Object detection in autonomous driving requires precise localization and an inherent understanding of the relational context between co-occurring objects. In extremely complex heterogeneous environments rare classes, small-scale objects, and frequently appearing objects are difficult for standard object detection frameworks to handle. In this paper, we propose a novel framework called Context-Centric Feature Fusion (CCFF), which utilizes two attention-based modules, Local Context Fusion Module (LCFM) uses the RoI-to-RoI self-attention mechanism to resolve spatial interactions, mainly considering small and partially obscured objects, while Global Context Attention Module (GCAM) converts the co-occurrence of objects priors by pooling top-K RoI features into a global context attention token, avoiding the computational overhead of pixel-level global pooling. This fusion of local and object-centric global features yields contextualized embeddings that enhance classification results and co-occurring objects detection. Our method is evaluated on two datasets, Cityscapes and BDD100K which demonstrate significant improvement on relational consistency, achieving a Category-level Consistency Strategy (CCS) of 0.973 and 0.969, respectively. Furthermore, our approach produces substantial gains in small object detection (AP_S: 14.1%) and successfully recovers rare classes such as "Train" that are typically lost in large distributions. Our efficiency report shows that the framework processes images in real time with a 0.2 FPS overhead. The code is available at https://github.com/BinayKSingh/CCFF.

</details>


### [81] [ECA: Efficient Continual Alignment for Open-Ended Image-to-Text Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12633)
*Jiangtao Kong,Peijun Zhao,Chun-Fu Chen,Youngwook Do,Shaohan Hu,Tianyi Zhou,Huajie Shao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向开放图像到文本生成的增量学习方法ECA，通过连续对齐机制、混合查询模块、Fisher动态扩展和字典重放等技术，有效缓解灾难性遗忘，在四个新基准上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有增量学习研究多针对稳态数据分布，而实际场景中视觉数据的主导类别会随时间动态变化。本文旨在解决这种更现实场景下，模型如何在不访问旧数据的情况下持续学习新任务，同时保持已有知识不被遗忘的问题。

Method: 提出Efficient Continual Alignment (ECA)方法，包含三个核心机制：(1) Mixture of Query (MoQ)模块，自适应任务特定的查询token；(2) Fisher Dynamic Expansion (FeDEx)，基于Fisher信息矩阵动态扩展模型结构；(3) 嵌入字典与Dictionary Replay (DR)机制，保留过往知识。整体采用连续对齐思想，增量适配预训练VLM中的对齐模块。

Result: 构建了四个反映真实场景的增量学习OpenITG基准，实验结果表明ECA显著缓解了灾难性遗忘，在增量学习性能上优于基线方法。

Conclusion: ECA作为一种无样例的增量学习方法，有效解决了开放图像到文本生成中的连续对齐问题，为面向动态环境的视觉语言模型增量学习提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Incremental Learning (IL) for Open-ended Image-to-Text Generation (OpenITG) enables models to continuously generate accurate, contextually relevant text for new images while preserving previously acquired knowledge. Unlike prior studies, this paper addresses a more practical scenario in which the predominant category of visual data shifts over time as environments evolve. In this context, we introduce a new notion of continual alignment, which incrementally adapts the alignment module within pre-trained VLMs to preserve high-quality cross-modal representations. Based on this idea, we propose Efficient Continual Alignment (ECA), a novel exemplar-free IL approach for OpenITG. The key challenge is enabling the model to acquire new, task-specific features while minimizing interference with the established alignment without accessing raw data from previous tasks. To address this, ECA employs three core mechanisms: a Mixture of Query (MoQ) module that adapts task-specific query tokens, a Fisher Dynamic Expansion (FeDEx) that dynamically expands model structure based on a Fisher Information Matrix (FIM)-based metric, and an embedding dictionary with Dictionary Replay (DR) to retain past knowledge. To evaluate ECA's performance, we construct four new IL OpenITG benchmarks that better reflect real-world scenarios. Experimental results demonstrate that ECA significantly mitigates catastrophic forgetting and improves IL performance compared to baseline methods. Code and benchmarks are available at https://github.com/Snowball0823/ECA.

</details>


### [82] [CD-RCM: Generalizable Continuous-Depth Novel View Synthesis for Reflectance Confocal Microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12635)
*Tooba Imtiaz,Milind Rajadhyaksha,Kivanc Kose,Jennifer Dy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CD-RCM，首个针对反射共聚焦显微镜（RCM）图像的神经渲染新视角合成方法，通过插值稀疏z-stack中的中间切片，将各向异性的3D体积转化为各向同性表示，实现任意方向的高保真截面可视化，推理时间亚秒级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCM获取的3D体积各向异性（横向分辨率~0.5μm，轴向分辨率~3μm），限制了组织解释；现有神经渲染方法针对表面级多视角观测，不适用于RCM特有的深度分辨遮挡成像几何。因此需要能处理轴向成像几何和层依赖解剖组织的专用方法。

Method: 提出CD-RCM，一种前馈式新视角合成模型，专门针对RCM的深度分辨遮挡成像物理特性设计了定制的架构和训练框架，显式建模浅层遮挡深层的轴向成像几何。

Result: CD-RCM实现了高保真的新视角合成，推理时间在亚秒级，且无需针对每例患者进行优化。

Conclusion: CD-RCM是首个RCM专用新视角合成方法，通过各向同性体积重建实现了类组织病理学的任意方向截面检查，显著提升了RCM数据的3D可视化能力。

Abstract: Reflectance confocal microscopy (RCM) provides noninvasive, cellular-resolution "optical biopsies" of human skin \emph{in vivo} by acquiring en-face images at successive depths, forming a sparse z-stack. Due to optical limitations, these stacks are anisotropic 3D volumes with lateral resolution (0.5 $μ$m) $\sim$6 times higher compared to axial resolution, which is defined by the optical sectioning (3 $μ$m), limiting the interpretation of tissue. Our goal is to provide continuous-depth visualization by interpolating intermediate sections and making the 3D volume isotropic. Such a representation permits arbitrary-direction sectioning, including histopathology-like cross-sectional examination, without requiring per-patient optimization. To that end, we introduce the first RCM-specific novel-view synthesis (NVS) approach, CD-RCM, a feedforward model that predicts realistic, unseen depths from sparsely sampled RCM stacks. Classical neural rendering methods focus on reconstruction from surface-level multi-view observations. In contrast to surface-level camera views, RCM can acquire optically sectioned en-face images of tissue beyond the surface up to 200 $μ$m. However, during visualization of the RCM stacks, observations of the shallower sections (towards the surface) obscure the deeper ones. This unique axial imaging geometry and layer-dependent anatomical organization motivated our development of a tailored architectural and training framework that explicitly accounts for RCM's depth-resolved, occlusive imaging physics. Experiments demonstrate that CD-RCM achieves high-fidelity novel-view synthesis with sub-second inference time.

</details>


### [83] [SalArt-VQA: Diagnosing Whether VLMs Understand Salient Artifacts in Generated Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12671)
*Xiaoxiao Sun,Ruotian Zhang,Junzhe Huang,James Burgess,Serena Yeung-Levy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLMs在检测AI生成图像伪影时准确率高，但存在错误依赖视觉线索、错误区域选择或缺陷描述不匹配等问题。本文提出SalArt-VQA基准（950图/3681题），通过四类问题（存在检测、语义定位、空间定位、证据缺陷识别）和参考图像对比测试，发现最强模型检测召回率99.37%但四类问题全正确率仅53.26%，揭示了高检测准确率不等于扎实伪影理解的隐藏失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM伪影检测评估仅关注图像级准确率，忽略了模型判断依据是否正确（如依赖错误视觉线索、选择错误区域或描述不支持的缺陷），需要细粒度诊断基准来揭露这些隐藏失败模式。

Method: 构建SalArt-VQA基准，包含950张AI生成图像及对应真实参考图像和生成参考图像，设计3681道人工撰写多选题，涵盖存在检测、语义定位、空间定位和证据缺陷识别四类问题，通过参考图像对比测试校准能力和弃权能力。

Result: 20个VLM评估显示最强模型检测召回率达99.37%，但四类问题全正确率仅53.26%。对比伪影图像与无伪影参考图像发现灵敏度-校准权衡：敏感模型易误报伪影，保守模型因漏检真实伪影而避免误报。

Conclusion: 高伪影检测准确率不能保证扎实的伪影理解，SalArt-VQA暴露了这些隐藏失败模式，提供了VLM伪影判断是否受局部视觉证据支持的细粒度评估。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used to detect whether AI-generated images contain visible artifacts, yet their ability to analyze such artifacts remains poorly understood. A correct image-level decision can still hide important failures: a model may correctly flag an artifact while relying on the wrong visual cue, selecting the wrong region, or describing a defect that the image does not support. To evaluate these behaviors directly, we introduce SalArt-VQA, a diagnostic benchmark for fine-grained SALient ARTifact understanding in AI-generated images. SalArt-VQA contains 950 images and 3,681 human-authored multiple-choice questions spanning artifact images, matched real reference images, and paired generated reference images. Four aligned question types evaluate presence detection, semantic localization, spatial grounding, and evidence-grounded defect identification, while the reference splits test calibration and abstention when the annotated defect is absent. Across 20 VLMs, SalArt-VQA reveals failures that image-level detection accuracy hides: the strongest model reaches 99.37% detection recall on artifact images but answers all four artifact-side questions correctly on only 53.26% of images. Comparing artifact images with artifact-free references reveals a sensitivity-calibration tradeoff: sensitive models often make unsupported artifact claims, while conservative models avoid false alarms largely by missing real artifacts. These results show that high artifact detection accuracy alone does not imply grounded artifact understanding. SalArt-VQA exposes these hidden failure modes and provides a fine-grained evaluation of whether VLM artifact claims are supported by local visual evidence.

</details>


### [84] [VLADriveBench: Evaluating CoT-Action Relationship in VLA for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12706)
*Thach Nguyen,Danhua Guo,Tom Lampo,Fei Wu,Burhan Yaman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VLADriveBench是一个评估VLA模型中思维链推理与驾驶轨迹之间相关性、一致性和因果关系的框架，发现观察指标和因果干预分析可能得出截然不同的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅评估驾驶轨迹质量，缺乏对CoT推理与驾驶动作之间相关性、一致性和因果关系的系统评估。

Method: 提出VLADriveBench框架，结合观察性指标（提及、幻觉、矛盾、行动对齐）和CoT干预协议，从互补视角评估CoT与行动的关系。

Result: ORION在观察性对齐指标上得分最高，但其CoT是副现象性的；Alpamayo v1.5得分较低，但其CoT具有强因果性；视觉显著性调节了CoT影响的程度。

Conclusion: 观察性指标和因果分析可能得出分歧的结论，需要多维度评估CoT在VLA模型中的实际作用。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning alongside driving trajectories, but existing benchmarks evaluate only trajectory quality and do not assess whether the CoT is relevant, consistent, or causally connected to the driving action. We introduce VLADriveBench, a framework that combines observational metrics (mentioning, hallucination, contradiction, action alignment) with a CoT intervention protocol to provide complementary views of the CoT-action relationship. Applying VLADriveBench to three models across two architectures, we find that the two analyses can diverge sharply: ORION scores highest on observational alignment yet its CoT is epiphenomenal, while Alpamayo v1.5 scores lower yet its CoT is strongly causal, with visual salience gating the extent of CoT influence.

</details>


### [85] [GRIP: Feedback-Guided Prompt Retrieval for Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12744)
*Garvita Allabadi,Matteo Sodano,Roberto Estevão,Yuxiong Wang,Vikram Adve,Emre Kiciman,Ranveer Chandra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GRIP框架，一种可学习的视觉检索方法，通过LMM反馈训练来替代传统相似度检索，在多模态上下文学习中显著提升性能，且可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有M-ICL方法默认语义相似的样本能提供最佳上下文，但系统分析表明视觉相似的样本并不总能有效提升上下文学习性能，这一假设存在根本性缺陷。

Method: 提出GRIP（Guided Retrieval of In-context Prompts），一个可学习的纯视觉检索框架。通过对比训练从LMM的反馈信号中学习区分有益和有害的上下文示例，超越纯相似度检索。

Result: 在分类、字幕生成和VQA三个多模态任务上，GRIP在Qwen2.5-VL-7B上一致优于基于相似度的检索方法，在Idefics2-8B的分类任务上提升最为显著。此外，基于一个开源LMM训练的检索器可直接迁移至其他模型（包括闭源的GPT-4o和Gemini）。

Conclusion: GRIP验证了通过LMM反馈学习检索比纯语义相似度检索更有效，且具备跨模型迁移能力，为M-ICL的高效部署提供了可扩展的低成本方案。

Abstract: In-Context Learning (ICL) has become a powerful mechanism for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to new tasks without fine-tuning. Extending this concept to Large Multimodal Models (LMMs), Multimodal In-Context Learning (M-ICL) relies on retrieving relevant examples, such as images, captions, or question-answer pairs, to guide predictions across tasks like classification, captioning, and visual question answering (VQA). Most existing approaches select in-context examples based on feature-space similarity, assuming that semantically similar samples provide the most useful context. However, our systematic analysis reveals that this assumption does not always hold: visually similar examples are not necessarily those that most effectively enhance in-context learning performance.
  To address this, we propose the Guided Retrieval of In-context Prompts (GRIP), a learnable vision-only retrieval framework that leverages feedback from LMMs to identify examples that truly improve model predictions. GRIP learns to distinguish beneficial from detrimental in-context examples through contrastive training, refining retrieval beyond pure similarity. Across three multimodal tasks, namely classification, captioning, and VQA, GRIP improves consistently over similarity-based retrieval on Qwen2.5-VL-7B, with its strongest gains in classification on Idefics2-8B. Moreover, we demonstrate that retrievers trained with feedback from one open LMM can be transferred to other models without retraining, including closed-source GPT-4o and Gemini, enabling scalable and cost-efficient deployment of M-ICL. Code will be published upon acceptance.

</details>


### [86] [DIMOS: Disentangling Instance-level Moving Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12826)
*Hongxiang Huang,Hongwei Ren,Xiaopeng Lin,Yulong Huang,Zeke Xie,Bojun Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种双解耦特征提取框架和多粒度跨模态对齐方法，通过分离图像和事件模态中的外观与运动信息，并实现跨模态分布与语义对齐，显著提升了多模态运动实例分割在小目标、快速运动和低光照场景下的性能，达到了最先进水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于事件相机与RGB图像融合的多模态运动实例分割方法在处理小尺度运动实例时表现不佳，主要因为事件相机在有限分辨率下产生稀疏特征，且事件特征中外观属性与运动线索纠缠在一起，限制了有效的跨模态融合。

Method: 1) 双解耦特征提取框架：分别在图像和事件模态中分离并提取外观与运动信息，提升特征密度；2) 多粒度跨模态对齐：对齐不同模态间分布一致和语义一致的特征，实现更有效的融合，丰富时空细节。

Result: 在多项多模态运动实例分割任务上达到最先进性能，特别在快速运动和低光照等挑战性条件下的小目标分割上表现优异。

Conclusion: 所提出的双解耦框架与多粒度对齐策略有效解决了事件特征稀疏和模态间语义纠缠问题，为多模态运动实例分割提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Moving instance segmentation (MIS) attracts increasing attention due to its broad applications in traffic surveillance, autonomous driving, and animal tracking. Event cameras record asynchronous brightness changes, providing high temporal resolution and dynamic range, which makes them highly sensitive to motion information. By fusing event and image features, motion cues from events can complement spatial details from images, enhancing the performance of MIS. However, current multimodal MIS methods still struggle to segment small moving instances, as event cameras often yield sparse features under limited resolution. Moreover, event features entangle appearance attributes with motion cues, which further restricts effective cross-modal fusion. To address these challenges, we first propose a dual-disentangling feature extraction framework that separates and extracts appearance and motion information within both image and event modalities, thereby improving feature density. Subsequently, a multi-granularity cross-modal alignment is introduced to align distributionally and semantically consistent features across modalities, enabling more effective fusion with rich spatial and temporal details. The experiment results demonstrate that our method achieves state-of-the-art performance in multimodal MIS, especially for small instances under challenging conditions such as fast motion and low-light settings.

</details>


### [87] [Perceive, Interact, Reason: Building Tool-Augmented Visual Agents for Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12830)
*Changye Li,Meng Lu,Yi Wu,Ligeng Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PERIA，一个工具增强的视觉智能体，通过轻量级视觉感知工具和视觉交互工具，结合OR-GIGPO优化策略，在空间推理任务上显著提升了VLM的性能，在13个基准测试中超越同等规模SOTA模型7.0%-14.8%，性能媲美千亿级模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM在需要主动证据获取和多步视觉交互的空间推理任务中表现有限，仅依赖视觉编码器的隐式表示不足以恢复细粒度空间证据。

Method: 提出PERIA框架：1) 两类轻量工具族（视觉感知工具和视觉交互工具）；2) 统一的训练方案，包括监督工具使用轨迹合成、组合奖励以及Observation-Relaxed Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (OR-GIGPO)。

Result: PERIA-8B在分布内基准上比Qwen3-8B提升10.0%，分布外提升4.4%；超越同等规模SOTA方法7.0%-14.8%；性能与Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B-Thinking和GPT-5相当。

Conclusion: 通过工具增强的交互推理范式，PERIA有效弥补了VLM在空间推理中的固有缺陷，实现了以小博大的效果。

Abstract: While recent vision-language models (VLMs) demonstrate strong multimodal understanding, they remain limited in spatial reasoning tasks that require active evidence acquisition and multi-step visual interaction. This limitation suggests that relying solely on implicit visual representations from vision encoders is insufficient for recovering fine-grained spatial evidence. We introduce PERception-Interaction-reason Agent (PERIA), a tool-augmented visual agent for spatial reasoning tasks across map reasoning, visual probing, and vision reconstruction. PERIA uses two lightweight tool families: vision perception tools for exposing textual, symbolic, and spatial evidence, and vision interaction tools for manipulating visual context, tracing paths, and verifying spatial relations. To train PERIA, we develop a unified recipe that combines supervised tool-use trajectory synthesis, composite rewards, and Observation-Relaxed Group-in-Group Policy Optimization (OR-GIGPO) for effective multi-tool behavior. Experiments on 13 benchmarks from 8 datasets show that PERIA-8B improves over the Qwen3-8B backbone by 10.0% on in-distribution benchmarks and 4.4% on out-of-distribution benchmarks, while outperforming previous state-of-the-art baselines of similar size by 7.0%-14.8%. It also achieves performance comparable to much larger models such as Qwen3-VL-235B-A22B-Thinking and GPT-5, demonstrating the effectiveness of PERIA in enhancing spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [88] [Language-Guided Abstraction for Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12847)
*Xu-Jing Ye,Yuan-Gen Wang,Ruping Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出L-VARC框架，通过语言引导的学习利用特权信息（LUPI）分支增强视觉推理，使用DeepSeek-V3压缩语义并借助CLIP文本编码器对齐视觉特征，仅用18M参数即达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARC方法要么纯语言依赖大参数LLM，要么纯视觉难以捕捉高层语义导致过拟合像素模式，缺乏有效融合语言与视觉优势的轻量方案。

Method: 1) 语义压缩模块：利用DeepSeek-V3配合统一任务无关提示，将LARC语料压缩为结构化描述，适配CLIP上下文长度；2) 交叉注意力投影器：对齐视觉特征与语义嵌入，引导视觉推理训练；3) LUPI分支仅在训练时使用，推理时丢弃，实现轻量化。

Result: 仅18M参数即在ARC基准上超越现有SOTA方法，消融实验验证了语义压缩模块和交叉注意力投影器的有效性。

Conclusion: 语言引导的特权信息学习能有效增强视觉推理能力，同时保持模型轻量高效，为ARC任务提供了一种新的融合范式。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) is viewed as a critical avenue to Artificial General Intelligence (AGI), as it enables models to learn abstract transformation rules from few-shot examples and then generalize to new tasks. However, prevalent ARC methodology is either pure language or vision-only (i.e., VARC). The former depends heavily on LLMs, consuming billions of parameters. The latter often struggles to capture high-level semantics, leading to overfitting on pixel-level patterns. To bridge this gap, we propose L-VARC, a novel framework that enhances visual reasoning via a language-guided Learning Using Privileged Information (LUPI) branch. Specifically, we design a Semantic Compression Module by feeding a unified, task-agnostic prompt into DeepSeek-V3. In this way, the raw LARC (a crowd-sourced language description dataset) can be substantially refined and structured, fitting with the context length constraint of standard text encoders (e.g., CLIP). Moreover, we design a Cross-Attention Projector to align visual features with semantic embeddings, aiming to guide the training of the ARC model. Notably, the LUPI branch is taken in the training process and will be discarded during inference, thereby yielding a lightweight model with a mere 18 million parameters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our L-VARC effectively leverages linguistic priors to boost visual reasoning and outperforms state-of-the-art. Ablation studies further confirm the contribution of the two new designs towards the L-VARC framework. The code is available at https://github.com/GZHU-DVL/L-VARC.

</details>


### [89] [Learning Task-Aware Sampling with Shared Saliency through Density-Equalizing Mappings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12869)
*Tsz Lok Ip,Han Zhang,Lok Ming Lui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出密度均衡卷积神经网络（DECNN），通过任务自适应采样动态重分配计算注意力，在图像和表面学习任务中实现高效特征提取，在轻量化模型下取得竞争性能并生成可解释显著性图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统卷积在整个域内均匀采样感受野，但信息结构往往集中在局部区域（如医学影像中的病理变化），导致计算资源在无信息区域浪费，特征提取效率低且模型容量利用不充分。

Method: 提出DECNN框架，通过密度均衡映射引导卷积：学习一个密度函数编码区域相对重要性，对重要区域进行放大、对无关区域进行压缩，使得卷积感受野非均匀重分布，在任务相关区域实现更密集采样，实现自适应特征提取。

Result: 在图像分类和颅面表面分析任务上，DECNN以更少参数达到竞争或更优性能，准确识别任务相关区域，并在复杂几何变化下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DECNN通过任务自适应采样解决了均匀卷积的计算浪费问题，实现了更高效的模型容量利用，同时提供了可解释的显著性图，为医学影像和几何分析等任务提供了有效方案。

Abstract: In image and surface-based learning tasks, convolutional features are typically extracted using receptive fields that are sampled uniformly across the entire domain. However, informative structures are rarely distributed uniformly in practice and are often concentrated in localized regions. Such phenomena are particularly common in medical imaging, where pathological changes are spatially confined. Consequently, uniform convolution allocates equal computational effort to both informative and uninformative regions, resulting in inefficient feature extraction and suboptimal utilization of model capacity. To address this issue, we propose a framework for task-adaptive sampling that dynamically redistributes computational attention according to the spatial importance of the data. Specifically, we introduce the Density-Equalizing Convolutional Neural Network (DECNN), which employs density-equalizing mappings to guide convolution through a learned density function. The density function encodes the relative importance of different regions and induces a transformation that enlarges informative areas while compressing less relevant ones. As a result, convolutional receptive fields are redistributed non-uniformly over the domain, enabling denser sampling in task-relevant regions. By coupling this importance-driven transformation with convolution, DECNN performs adaptive feature extraction that focuses computational resources on informative structures. This leads to more efficient use of model capacity, yielding a lightweight yet expressive architecture while simultaneously producing an interpretable saliency map. Experiments on image classification and craniofacial surface analysis demonstrate that DECNN achieves competitive or superior performance with fewer parameters, accurately identifies task-relevant regions, and remains robust under complex geometric variations.

</details>


### [90] [Bridging Modal Isolation in Interleaved Thinking: Supervising Modality Transitions via Stepwise Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12886)
*Tingyu Li,Le Zhou,Siyuan Li,Yujun Wu,Xinglong Xu,Jingxuan Wei,Conghui He,Cheng Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文揭示了多模态交错推理中的“模态隔离”问题（文本与图像生成相互脱节），并提出MoTiF框架，通过反思式SFT和Flow-GRPO直接优化模态转换保真度，从而提升跨模态一致性和任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交错推理模型在长链场景中会出现模态隔离问题：生成的图像偏离文本上下文，后续文本又忽略视觉证据，导致两种模态交替但无法相互赋能。作者认为这是由于模态边界处的信息损失逐步累积所致。

Method: 提出MoTiF两阶段训练框架：1) Reflective SFT——训练模型检测并从错误的视觉输出中恢复；2) Flow-GRPO——通过强化学习提升图像生成保真度。所有训练信号基于模态转换级别的保真度而非最终任务准确率。

Result: 在四种视觉谜题基准上，基于模态转换保真度的监督显著提升了跨模态一致性和最终任务准确率。

Conclusion: 有效的交错推理需要在模态边界处施加显式的结构监督，而非仅靠规模扩展或端到端任务优化。

Abstract: Interleaved thinking, where a unified multimodal model alternates between textual reasoning and visual generation, has shown promise on spatial and physical tasks. However, in complex long-chain scenarios, we identify a fundamental failure mode: generated images diverge from the textual context while subsequent text ignores the visual evidence, causing the two modalities to alternate without genuinely informing each other. We term this Modal Isolation and attribute it to compounding information loss at modality boundaries. We decompose each reasoning cycle into atomic operations and define modality transition loss, quantifying cross-modal hallucination (text-to-image) and visual utilization deficit (image-to-text) at each boundary. We propose MoTiF (Modality Tiransition Fidelity), a two-stage training framework that directly optimizes these transitions: Reflective SFT trains the model to detect and recover from erroneous visual outputs; Flow-GRPO improves image generation fidelity via reinforcement learning. All training signals in MoTiF derive from transition-level fidelity rather than end-task accuracy. Across four visual puzzle benchmarks, this transition-level supervision substantially improves both cross-modal coherence and final task accuracy.
