The results demonstrate that effective interleaved reasoning requires explicit structural supervision at modality boundaries, not merely scaling or end-task optimization.

</details>


### [91] [Magnifying What Matters: Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering for Visual Text Comprehension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12898)
*Shenglai Zeng,Qirui Wang,Kai Guo,Xinnan Dai,Xianxuan Long,Hui Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AGAR方法，利用VLM自身中后层注意力机制自适应地重新渲染文本图像中的关键词跨度，以提升视觉文本理解任务的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VTC流水线将渲染和布局视为固定且与内容无关的预处理步骤，缺乏对VLM内部如何处理可视化文本的机制性理解，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出AGAR（注意力引导自适应渲染）：利用VLM中后层注意力识别top-K重要视觉补丁，映射回词跨度，放大这些跨度后重新渲染页面并重新推理答案。该方法无需训练、与模型无关。

Result: 在9个VTC基准（短文本、长上下文、多页记忆QA）和4个VLM骨干上，AGAR一致提升了现成VLM的性能，可与后训练组合获得进一步增益，且在视觉和文本输入退化下保持鲁棒。

Conclusion: VLMs存在"定位而不利用"的现象：证据定位注意力与答案正确性脱钩，但放大定位跨度可恢复大量失败；AGAR通过自适应重渲染有效缓解这一问题。

Abstract: Visual Text Comprehension (VTC) renders text into images for a vision-language model (VLM) to read, sidestepping LLM context-window limits and powering applications from long-page OCR to multi-page memory QA. Yet existing VTC pipelines treat rendering and layout as a fixed, content-agnostic preprocessing step and offer little mechanistic understanding of how VLMs internally process visualized text. Through a focused empirical study on VTC QA tasks, we reveal that VLMs exhibit a localization-without-utilization regime: evidence-localizing attention emerges sharply in the middle-to-late layers and is largely decoupled from answer correctness, yet simply enlarging the localized spans on the rendered page recovers a large fraction of the failures. Building on these observations, we propose AGAR (Attention-Guided Adaptive Rendering), a training-free, model-agnostic method that leverages a VLM's own middle-to-late layer attention to identify the top-K important visual patches, maps them back to word spans, and re-renders the page with those spans enlarged before re-inferring the answer. Extensive experiments across nine VTC benchmarks (short-form, long-context, and multi-page memory QA) and four VLM backbones show that AGAR (i)consistently improves off-the-shelf VLMs as a plug-and-play enhancement, (ii)composes with VLM post-training to yield further gains, and (iii)remains robust under both visual- and text-side input degradation.

</details>


### [92] [Multi-Label Test-Time Adaptation with Bayesian Conditional Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12925)
*Qiru Li,Ao Zhou,Zhiwei Jiang,Zifeng Cheng,Cong Wang,Yafeng Yin,Qing Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种无需梯度、无需微调骨干网络的测试时自适应方法BCP，通过贝叶斯条件先验估计注入标签依赖关系，仅基于零样本logits和在线统计即可显著提升多标签识别在分布偏移下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结视觉-语言模型（VLM）在分布偏移下的多标签识别中，零样本推理逐标签独立打分，忽略标签共现结构，导致主导概念压制兼容弱标签，输出不连贯的标签集合。

Method: 将零样本logits视为固定图像-文本似然下的边缘后验近似，将分布偏移误差归因于标签先验不匹配。对每个测试图像，先选取高置信度锚点标签，再执行锚点条件下的贝叶斯精炼更新（logit空间的闭式解），等价于点互信息（PMI）形式。通过在线轻量级二阶共现统计估计锚点条件先验，无需目标标注和额外前向传播。

Result: 在多个多标签基准和CLIP骨干上，BCP一致超越强TTA基线：RN50平均mAP从57.31提升至69.22，ViT-B/16从62.61提升至71.79。

Conclusion: BCP是一种高效、无梯度的测试时自适应方法，通过注入标签依赖关系有效缓解VLM在分布偏移下的多标签识别脆弱性问题。

Abstract: Multi-label recognition with frozen Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is brittle under distribution shift: standard zero-shot inference scores labels independently, ignoring co-occurrence structure and producing incoherent label sets where dominant concepts suppress weaker but compatible labels. We introduce Bayesian Conditional Priors (BCP) Estimation, a gradient-free test-time adaptation method that injects label dependency without tuning the backbone. BCP views zero-shot logits as a proxy for marginal posteriors under a fixed image-text likelihood and attributes shift-induced errors mainly to a mismatched label prior. For each test image, it selects a high-confidence anchor label and applies an anchor-conditioned Bayesian refinement. This update is closed-form in logit space and admits a pointwise mutual information (PMI) interpretation, explicitly promoting compatible labels and suppressing incompatible ones. BCP operates without target annotations by estimating anchor-conditioned priors online from the unlabeled test stream via lightweight second-order co-occurrence statistics, adding negligible overhead beyond a single forward pass. Across standard multi-label benchmarks and multiple CLIP backbones, BCP consistently outperforms strong TTA baselines, e.g., improving RN50 average mAP from 57.31 to 69.22 and ViT-B/16 from 62.61 to 71.79.

</details>


### [93] [MAMVI: 3D Test-Time Adaptation via Masked Multi-View Point Clouds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12939)
*Inseok Kong,Geunyoung Jung,Jiyoung Jung*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MAMVI提出了一种掩码多视图测试时适应方法，通过统一单步优化替代传统顺序优化，在保持高精度的同时实现4.9-8.9倍推理加速，适用于实时3D点云模型适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D点云测试时适应方法中，多视图增强虽有效但采用顺序优化（逐视图独立处理），导致推理延迟高，无法满足实时应用需求。

Method: 提出MAMVI，核心设计包括：(1) 混合掩码策略（固定比率+贝塔分布采样）保证稳定性与多样性；(2) 多视图损失聚合，通过单次反向传播完成统一适应；(3) 基于置信度的自适应学习率动态调整适应强度。

Result: 在ModelNet-40C、ShapeNet-C和ScanObjectNN-C上，MAMVI在ShapeNet-C和ScanObjectNN-C上达到SOTA精度；在ModelNet-40C上具有竞争力；推理速度提升4.9-8.9倍。

Conclusion: MAMVI通过单步多视图共识适应有效解决了顺序优化的延迟问题，在精度与效率之间取得良好平衡，适合实时3D点云应用场景。

Abstract: 3D point cloud models suffer significant performance degradation under distribution shifts caused by sensor noise, occlusions, and environmental changes. Test-time adaptation (TTA) has emerged as a practical paradigm for mitigating this issue during inference. Recently, leveraging multi-view augmentation has shown promise in improving 3D TTA performance. However, existing multi-view approaches are often constrained by sequential optimization that treats each view independently. This sequential optimization leads to substantial inference latency due to repetitive optimization steps, making real-time adaptation impractical. To address this, we propose Masked Multi-View Test-Time Adaptation (MAMVI), which replaces sequential optimization with a unified single-step adaptation. Specifically, MAMVI utilizes a hybrid masking strategy that combines fixed ratios for stability with Beta-distributed sampling for diversity. By aggregating losses across multiple views, MAMVI performs adaptation through a single backward pass based on multi-view consensus. Additionally, a confidence-based adaptive learning rate is used to dynamically adjust the adaptation intensity for each sample. Extensive experiments on ModelNet-40C, ShapeNet-C, and ScanObjectNN-C demonstrate that MAMVI achieves state-of-the-art accuracy on ShapeNet-C and ScanObjectNN-C. Moreover, it remains competitive on ModelNet-40C while delivering 4.9-8.9 times faster inference, making it highly suitable for real-time applications. Our code is available at https://github.com/Inseok-kong/MAMVI

</details>


### [94] [YOLO-AMC: An Improved YOLO Architecture with Attention Mechanisms for Building Crack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12958)
*Ching-Yu Tsai,Chia-Min Lin,Chih-Hsiang Yang,Yung-Che Wang,Jen-Shiun Chiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出YOLO-AMC，一种基于YOLOv11改进的裂缝检测架构，通过去除C2PSA模块并在Neck层引入GAM、Res-CBAM和SA三种注意力机制，实现跨尺度特征融合增强。实验表明，GAM方案性能最优（mAP@0.5=0.9917），在保持7.6 GFLOPs计算量的同时达到110.95 FPS（RTX 4090）和约5 FPS（树莓派5），在精度与部署效率间取得良好平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 裂缝通常呈现为细长、低对比度结构，易受背景噪声干扰，现有目标检测模型难以有效检测，亟需提升自动化裂缝检测性能的方法。

Method: 基于YOLOv11，移除原C2PSA模块，在Neck的多尺度特征融合层中引入全局注意力机制（GAM）、残差卷积块注意力模块（Res-CBAM）和Shuffle Attention（SA）三种注意力机制，增强跨尺度特征融合能力。

Result: GAM方案在测试集上达到mAP@0.5=0.9917、mAP@0.5:0.95=0.9506，优于YOLOv11（0.9833/0.9112）和YOLOv8（0.9707/0.8921）；计算量7.6 GFLOPs，RTX 4090上达110.95 FPS，树莓派5上约5 FPS。

Conclusion: YOLO-AMC在裂缝检测任务上显著优于基线模型，GAM注意力机制效果最佳，模型在精度与部署效率间取得良好平衡，具备边缘设备部署潜力。

Abstract: Crack detection plays an important role in infrastructure inspection and Structural Health Monitoring (SHM). However, cracks typically appear as thin, low-contrast structures and are easily affected by background noise, posing challenges for existing object detection models. This study proposes an improved YOLO-based architecture with integrated attention mechanisms, termed YOLO-AMC (YOLO with Attention Mechanisms for Crack Detection), to enhance automated crack detection performance. Based on YOLOv11, the original C2PSA module is removed, and multiple attention mechanisms, including Global Attention Mechanism (GAM), Residual Convolutional Block Attention Module (Res-CBAM), and Shuffle Attention (SA), are introduced into the multi-scale feature fusion layers of the Neck to strengthen cross-scale feature integration. Experimental results demonstrate that YOLO-AMC consistently outperforms baseline models YOLOv11n and YOLOv8n across multiple evaluation metrics. Among the evaluated attention modules, GAM achieves the best detection performance, obtaining mAP@0.5 = 0.9917 and mAP@0.5:0.95 = 0.9506 on the test dataset, which are higher than those of YOLOv11 (0.9833 / 0.9112) and YOLOv8 (0.9707 / 0.8921). Furthermore, while maintaining a computational complexity of 7.6 GFLOPs, the proposed model achieves 110.95 FPS on an NVIDIA RTX 4090 platform and approximately 5 FPS on a Raspberry Pi 5 edge device, demonstrating a favorable trade-off between accuracy and deployment efficiency. The implementation code for this study is available on GitHub at https://github.com/CY-Tsai24/YOLO-AMC.

</details>


### [95] [Camera and LiDAR BEV Fusion for Cooperative 3D Object Detection on TUMTraf V2X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12981)
*Muhammad Shahbaz,Shaurya Agarwal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于TUMTraf V2X协同3D目标检测的相机与激光雷达融合检测器，在Codabench测试集上达到0.85 mAP。作者发现50个测试帧中有44帧与训练/验证集标签重叠，通过微调（0.89 mAP）和用真实标签替换预测（0.99 mAP）两种研究量化了数据泄漏对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发面向车路协同场景的3D目标检测器，并探究测试集与训练集数据重叠（数据泄漏）对评测分数的实际影响。

Method: 融合三个路侧相机与基础设施-车辆联合点云，在共享BEV空间中使用CenterPoint-style头部（含广义IoU回归损失和IoU质量重排序头）进行预测。

Result: 原始模型在测试集上达到0.85 mAP；针对重叠帧微调后升至0.89 mAP；用真实标签替换重叠帧预测后达到0.99 mAP。

Conclusion: 测试集与训练/验证集存在显著数据泄漏（44/50帧），导致评测分数虚高，需谨慎解读排行榜结果。

Abstract: We describe a Camera and LiDAR fusion detector developed for the TUMTraf V2X cooperative 3D object detection track of the DriveX 2026 challenge. The detector fuses three roadside cameras with a fused infrastructure-plus-vehicle point cloud in a shared bird's-eye-view space and predicts boxes through a CenterPoint-style head with a generalized IoU regression loss and an IoU quality re-ranking head. Trained on the provided train and validation splits, the model reaches a 3D mAP of 0.85 on the public Codabench test split. While iterating on the system, we observed that 44 of the 50 test frames are also present in the released train (40) and validation (4) splits with their labels. We therefore conducted two additional studies to quantify how this overlap affects the final score: (1) a finetuning run that oversamples the 44 overlapping frames, reaching 0.89 mAP, and (2) a post-processing run that replaces predictions on those frames with the released ground truth, reaching 0.99 mAP (uploaded to our Codabench account for testing but not published on the leaderboard). All three configurations and their per-class results are reported.

</details>


### [96] [Objects Before Words: Object-First Inductive Biases for Grounding Language in Child-View Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12985)
*Sathira Silva,Abrham Kahsay Gebreselasie,Muhammad Umer Sheikh,Kartik Kuckreja,Daniel Harari,Muhammad Haris Khan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: BabyMind提出了一种面向儿童的对比学习框架，通过物体优先的偏置、离线掩码区域接口、轻量级物体文件追踪以及原型空间的多实例对比目标，在稀疏、弱同步的SAYCam风格数据中更准确地学习语义词汇，在SAYCam-S上相比CVCL提升+2.6个百分点的15选1准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在婴儿视角的自然经验学习中，存在两种歧义：命名指称物出现的时间不确定以及它在杂乱画面中的位置不确定。现有的单帧对比配对方法在稀疏且弱同步的监护者语音与自我中心视频数据中会产生噪声正样本（目标物体缺失或被干扰物混淆），因此需要一个能够应对这种稀疏、噪声监督的对比学习框架。

Method: 1) 使用离线掩码区域接口提取候选物体嵌入；2) 通过追踪将短话语窗口内的候选物体链接成轻量级物体文件；3) 采用原型空间的多实例对比目标将话语与物体文件袋对齐；4) 引入轨迹一致性和全局物体一致性正则化来稳定学习，并将物体文件结构转移到全局帧嵌入中。

Result: 在SAYCam-S数据集上，BabyMind在Labeled-S 15选1强制选择任务中比CVCL提升+2.6个百分点，并在词库内、分布外基准测试上取得一致的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过引入物体优先的偏置和基于物体文件的多实例对比方法，BabyMind能够更有效地从稀疏、噪声的婴儿视角数据中学习词汇语义，验证了先验结构在弱监督多模态学习中的价值。

Abstract: Learning grounded word meaning from natural experience requires resolving two ambiguities in infant-view recordings: when the named referent appears and where it is in a cluttered frame. In SAYCam-style data, caregiver speech is sparse and weakly synchronized with egocentric video, so single-frame contrastive pairing yields noisy positives in which the intended object is absent or entangled with distractors. We propose BabyMind, an object-first bias for child-view contrastive learning under sparse, noisy supervision. BabyMind extracts candidate object embeddings using an offline mask-based region interface, links candidates across a short utterance-centered window into lightweight object files via tracking, and aligns utterances to bags of object files with a prototype-space multiple-instance contrastive objective. Track-coherence and global-object agreement regularizers stabilize learning and transfer object-file structure into the global frame embedding used at evaluation. On SAYCam-S, BabyMind improves Labeled-S 15 forced-choice accuracy by +2.6 points over CVCL and yields consistent gains on in-vocabulary out-of-distribution benchmarks. Code is available at https://github.com/sathiiii/BabyMind.

</details>


### [97] [Diffusion Transformer World-Action Model for AV Scene Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12987)
*Ruslan Sharifullin,Benjamin Jiang,Kai Xi Chew*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种紧凑的潜空间世界模型，用于自动驾驶的未来场景预测。通过对比多种表征编码器，发现V-JEPA2结合时序上下文最优；训练潜空间扩散Transformer（DiT）时，识别出四个关键组件：空间token、x₀目标、残差锚定和匹配不确定性的采样。在Stable-Diffusion-VAE编-预-解码流程中，揭示了感知-失真权衡：扩散模型在FID/KID指标上远优于回归（KID 4.8倍提升），且可训练校准使其无需测试真值。模型具有真实动作可控性（转向与场景位移ρ=0.81），并设计1.7M参数跳跃模型恢复完整运动幅度（1.02倍GT）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶世界模型在紧凑可训练规模下，未来预测具有模糊性；且标准失真度量（如余弦相似度、SSIM）误导性地奖励模糊回归均值而非真实预测，无法反映生成质量。

Method: 1. 在六个冻结编码器（四类表征族）上比较预测位置，确定V-JEPA2最优；2. 训练潜空间DiT，诊断出四个必要成分；3. 采用Stable-Diffusion-VAE pipeline编-预-解码；4. 使用感知指标（FID/KID）揭示感知-失真边界；5. 设计1.7M参数跳跃模型解决单次运动受限问题。

Result: V-JEPA2降低转向RMSE 40%；扩散模型KID 0.078 vs 回归0.375（4.8倍提升）；动作可控性ρ=0.81；跳跃模型恢复1.02倍GT运动幅度。

Conclusion: 扩散世界模型在感知质量上显著优于回归，但需合适的度量标准来揭示；紧凑跳跃模型可恢复完整运动信息，为自动驾驶预测与规划提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Action-conditioned world models let an autonomous vehicle predict future camera scenes from its own planned controls, enabling planning and simulation without real-world rollouts, but at compact, trainable scale the futures are ambiguous and the field's standard distortion metrics actively mislead: they reward a blurry regression mean over a realistic prediction. We confront this with a compact latent world model that, given the present front-camera latent and a sequence of ego-actions, predicts future scene latents a frozen decoder renders to $256 \times 256$ frames up to 8 seconds ahead, evaluated on 150 held-out nuScenes scenes. We first benchmark where to predict: across six frozen encoders spanning four representation families, V-JEPA2 with temporal context reduces steering RMSE by 40% over the best single-frame encoder. We then train a latent Diffusion Transformer (DiT) and, through a controlled diagnosis, identify the four ingredients it needs: spatial tokens, the $x_0$ objective, residual anchoring, and sampling matched to target uncertainty. In a Stable-Diffusion-VAE encode-predict-decode pipeline we expose the central tension: distortion metrics (cosine similarity, SSIM) favor the blurry mean, masking that the diffusion model is far closer to the real frame distribution. Inception-based FID and KID reveal a clean perception-distortion frontier: diffusion attains KID 0.078 versus 0.375 for regression ($4.8\times$ better), and a deployable train-derived calibration makes this practical without test-time ground truth. The model is genuinely action-controllable (steering drives scene displacement, Spearman $ρ= 0.81$, vs $-0.18$ for regression). We trace limited single-pass motion to a shared-present anchor and engineer a compact 1.7M-parameter "jump" model that recovers full ground-truth motion magnitude ($1.02\times$ GT), where single-pass models capture less than half.

</details>


### [98] [A Machine Learning Framework for Real-Time Personalized Ergonomic Pose Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12988)
*Manex Atxa,Bruno Simoes,Julen Balzategui*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于三维体积视频数据进行实时人体姿态（人体工效学/非工效学）预测的新方法，通过多视角3D点云分析克服传统摄像头的固定视角与遮挡问题，结合用户手动标注的骨骼数据训练个性化深度学习分类器，在负载举升任务案例中实现了实时推理，为工作场所安全监测提供了可扩展的实用方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统摄像头在人体工效学评估中存在固定视角和遮挡限制，导致姿态评估数据不完整，亟需一种能进行多角度实时分析的方法来提升工作场所安全健康监测的准确性和实用性。

Method: 使用RGB-D相机捕获3D体积视频数据，生成三维点云，系统自动从选定视角进行实时姿态推断；用户手动选择和标注的姿态用于训练个性化深度学习分类器，训练完成后对新流式数据进行实时推理。

Result: 通过负载举升任务的案例研究验证了方法有效性，RGB-D相机实现了实时骨骼标注，训练后的模型能够对新的流式数据执行实时姿态推理。

Conclusion: 该方法将先进3D数据技术与传统2D姿态估计算法相结合，为实时工效学评估提供了可扩展且实用的方案，有效应对了工作场所安全健康监测日益增长的需求，是该领域的重要贡献。

Abstract: This paper introduces a new methodology for real-time prediction of ergonomic and non-ergonomic human poses using volumetric video data in three dimensions. Although the methodology was designed for ergonomic assessments, it can be adapted to other applications requiring real-time analysis of human posture. One aspect that makes this system stand out is its ability to analyze 3D point clouds during the assessment, enabling computation from multiple angles. This overcomes a critical limitation of cameras which provide often a fixed viewpoint, thereby restricting the data available for a thorough postural evaluation, especially when occlusions occur. The system continuously and automatically performs pose inference using the chosen perspective on the real-time streaming data; however, only the poses manually selected and labeled by the user are used to train the personalized deep learning classifier. The methodology has been refined through a case study in which RGB-D cameras captured subjects performing load-lifting tasks, enabling real-time skeletal labeling. The model was trained on this data and, following the training phase, performs inference on new streaming data in real time. This research offers a scalable and pragmatic approach for real-time ergonomic evaluation by combining state-of-the-art 3D data technologies and traditional 2D pose estimation algorithms. It addresses the increasing need for safety and health monitoring in workplace environments, marking a notable contribution to the domain.

</details>


### [99] [Quality-Preserving Imperceptible Adversarial Attack on Skeleton-based Human Action Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13022)
*Ziyi Chang,Kanglei Zhou,Xiaohui Liang,Hubert P. H. Shum*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于分布的对抗攻击方法，在保持动作质量的同时实现对骨骼人体动作识别模型的高效攻击，并引入了一个与人类感知对齐的新评估指标。实验表明该方法在攻击成功率和动作质量保持方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗攻击方法引入类似噪声的扰动，导致攻击后动作质量下降，容易被先进的S-HAR系统察觉。作者发现这一问题源于攻击优化过程中经验风险与真实风险之间的差距。

Method: 提出一种基于分布的对抗攻击方法，通过最小化风险差距来生成对抗动作，避免引入噪声类扰动；同时提出与人类感知对齐的新评估指标来忠实评价动作质量。

Result: 在两个数据集上针对最先进的S-HAR方法进行实验，定性和定量分析均表明该方法在攻击成功率和攻击后动作质量方面具有优越性。

Conclusion: 该质量保持攻击方法揭示了动作识别器的鲁棒性存在严重问题，突显了该领域需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on skeletal human action recognition have received significant attention. However, existing methods typically introduce noise-like perturbations that degrade motion quality post-attack, and thereby are inherently perceptible with recent advancements in S-HAR systems. We discover that this degradation stems from the gap between empirical and true risks during the optimization process of previous adversarial attacks. To address this issue, we propose an attack where adversarial motions are obtained without compromising their motion quality. To minimize the risk gap and preserve motion quality, we propose a distribution-based adversarial attack method without introducing noise-like perturbations. To faithfully evaluate the motion quality, we propose a new metric that aligns with human perception on real-world naturalness. Experiments have been conducted on the state-of-the-art S-HAR methods across two datasets, demonstrating the superiority of our method in both the attack success rate and the post-attack motion quality through qualitative and quantitative analyses. The success of our quality-preserving attack application and distribution-based method raises serious concerns about the robustness of action recognizers, highlighting the need for further enhancements in this domain.

</details>


### [100] [A Multi-Modal Framework with Cross-Subject Pseudo-Labeling and Semantic Alignment for Micro-Gesture Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13030)
*Haoran Zhang,Haokun Zhang,Pengyu Liu,Yujia Zhang,Weibao Xue,Yanbin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对微手势识别任务（Track 1 of 4th MiGA-IJCAI Challenge），提出了一种多模态融合框架，包含显著性引导的多模态提取、温和平方根平滑加权与正交语义嵌入损失、跨模态伪标签领域自适应策略以及温度缩放软投票融合，最终取得68.13% F1-score，排名第4。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微手势作为自发且细微的身体动作，常隐含人类情绪信息，但在未裁剪视频中识别面临三大挑战：极低的信噪比、严重的长尾类别分布以及跨被试评估场景下的领域偏移问题。

Method: 1）显著性引导的多模态提取：融合68关键点骨架坐标、3D热图体素和高分辨率RGB视觉特征；2）温和平方根平滑加权机制 + 正交语义嵌入损失，保护尾部类别；3）跨模态伪标签（CMPL）策略用于无监督领域自适应；4）温度缩放软投票机制用于缓解晚期融合中的过度自信问题。

Result: 在比赛Track 1上取得了68.13%的F1-score，排名第4。

Conclusion: 提出的多模态框架有效应对了微手势识别中的低信噪比、长尾分布和跨被试领域偏移问题，验证了各模块（尤其是CMPL策略）对提升单模态鲁棒性和整体识别性能的有效性。

Abstract: Micro-gestures (MGs) are spontaneous and subtle body movements that frequently convey hidden human emotions. Recognizing MGs in untrimmed videos remains highly challenging due to their extremely low signal-to-noise ratio, severe long-tailed class distribution, and the inherent domain shift encountered in cross-subject evaluation scenarios. In this paper, we propose a comprehensive multi-modal framework for Track 1 of the 4th MiGA-IJCAI Challenge. To capture fine-grained representations, we design a saliency-guided multi-modal extraction pipeline integrating 68-keypoint skeleton joint coordinates, 3D heatmap volumes, and high-resolution RGB visual features. We introduce a gentle square-root smoothed weighting mechanism paired with an Orthogonal Semantic Embedding Loss to protect tail classes without compromising overall recognition capabilities. More importantly, to bridge the cross-subject generalization gap, we propose a Cross-Modal Pseudo-Labeling (CMPL) strategy for unsupervised domain adaptation, which significantly boosts single-modal robustness. A temperature-scaled soft-voting mechanism is finally utilized to alleviate overconfidence during late fusion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our framework achieves a competitive F1-score of 68.13\%, securing the 4th place.

</details>


### [101] [GeoCFNet: Geometry-Aware Confidence Field Network for Robot-Assisted Endoscopic Submucosal Dissection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13032)
*Rui Tang,Guankun Wang,Long Bai,Haochen Yin,Huxin Gao,Jiewen Lai,Jiazheng Wang,Hongliang Ren*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GeoCFNet，一种基于DINOv3的几何感知置信场网络，用于机器人辅助内镜黏膜下剥离术（ESD）的视觉引导，通过Token-Differentiated Fusion模块和几何感知空间正则化，解决动态内镜场景下的烟雾、镜面高光、组织变形等挑战，实现精确且几何稳定的置信场估计（RMSE 0.0480, PSNR 27.1995, SSIM 0.3397, CC 0.2466）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人辅助ESD手术需要稳定精确的视觉引导以维持准确的剥离通道和安全组织边界，但动态内镜场景中存在烟雾、镜面高光、组织变形、弱纹理及目标区域薄几何结构等挑战，使得可靠的置信场估计变得困难。

Method: GeoCFNet基于预训练DINOv3骨干网络，包含三个核心组件：Token-Differentiated Fusion模块聚合类令牌上下文与密集块表示；SegFormer解码器进行置信回归；Geometry-Aware Spatial Regularization（GASR）保持空间一致性和局部几何过渡。

Result: GeoCFNet在置信场估计任务上达到RMSE 0.0480、PSNR 27.1995、SSIM 0.3397、CC 0.2466，实现了精确且几何稳定的估计。

Conclusion: GeoCFNet通过几何感知的置信场估计方法，有效解决了动态内镜场景中的多重挑战，为机器人辅助ESD提供可靠的视觉引导，具有临床应用潜力。

Abstract: Advanced surgical robotics has made robot-assisted endoscopic submucosal dissection (ESD) a promising approach for the en-bloc resection of large lesions, with the potential to reduce recurrence and improve long-term outcomes. However, the technical complexity and risk of complications in ESD demand stable and precise visual guidance to maintain an accurate dissection corridor and a safe tissue margin. Dense confidence fields provide an effective representation for this purpose by describing both the preferred dissection region and its spatial transition to surrounding tissue. However, reliable confidence field estimation remains challenging in dynamic endoscopic scenes due to smoke, specular highlights, tissue deformation, weak texture, and the thin geometric structure of the target region. To address these challenges, we formulate dissection guidance as a geometry-aware confidence field estimation problem and propose GeoCFNet, a geometry-aware confidence field network built on a pretrained DINOv3 backbone. GeoCFNet integrates a Token-Differentiated Fusion module to aggregate class-token context with dense patch representations, a SegFormer decoder for confidence regression, and Geometry-Aware Spatial Regularization (GASR) to preserve spatial coherence and local geometric transitions. Experimental results show that GeoCFNet achieves RMSE 0.0480, PSNR 27.1995, SSIM 0.3397, and CC 0.2466, indicating accurate and geometrically stable confidence field estimation for robot-assisted ESD guidance.

</details>


### [102] [SAM-Deep-EIoU: Selective Mask Propagation for Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13033)
*Alexander Holmberg*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出选择性掩码传播算法，通过不确定性信号在基础跟踪器和VOS模型之间动态调度，仅在困难帧上使用昂贵的VOS模型，在DanceTrack和SportsMOT上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标跟踪中帧的难度分布呈重尾分布，大部分帧容易处理但少量困难帧导致基础跟踪器失败；VOS模型能在困难帧中保持身份但计算成本过高，需要在效率和准确性之间取得平衡。

Method: 基于分配不确定性信号触发机制，仅在触发窗口从基础跟踪器切换到VOS模型；只有当VOS模型做出与基础跟踪器矛盾且置信度高的预测时才修改输出，弱/不确定预测保留基础结果；无需训练，将各组件视为黑箱。

Result: 在DanceTrack上改进三种不同基础跟踪器；在SportsMOT上SAM3-Deep-EIoU结合全局轨迹关联达到86.8 HOTA的SOTA性能。

Conclusion: 选择性掩码传播方法能有效利用VOS模型处理困难帧的同时控制计算开销，且该方法具有通用性，可替换更强大的VOS模型进一步提升性能。

Abstract: Multi-object tracking has a heavy-tailed difficulty distribution: most frames are easy for a lightweight base tracker, while a small fraction are intrinsically hard. Video object segmentation (VOS) models can often preserve identity through the hard frames where the base tracker fails, but they are much more expensive in compute and memory. We propose selective mask propagation, a tracking algorithm that dispatches from a base tracker to a VOS model only on windows where an assignment-uncertainty signal fires. The base tracker's output is modified only when the VOS model makes a confident prediction that contradicts the base tracker's identity assignment; weak or inconclusive predictions preserve the base output. The method is training-free, treats both the base tracker and the VOS model as black boxes, and can benefit from replacing the VOS component with a more capable model. On DanceTrack, selective mask propagation improves three different base trackers. On SportsMOT, where identity preservation is central to sports analytics, SAM3-Deep-EIoU with global track association achieves state-of-the-art performance on the benchmark with 86.8 HOTA.

</details>


### [103] [TetherCache: Stabilizing Autoregressive Long-Form Video Generation with Gated Recall and Trusted Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13035)
*Yu Meng,Xiangyang Luo,Letian Li,Wenyuan Jiang,Chen Gao,Xinlei Chen,Yong Li,Xiao-Ping Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TetherCache是一种无需训练、即插即用的缓存管理策略，通过GRAB（基于门控注意力与多样性平衡的长程记忆选择）和TAME（基于统计对齐的可信上下文编辑）两种机制，解决自回归视频扩散模型在长视频生成中的KV缓存限制和上下文分布漂移问题，在30s、60s、240s设置下显著提升生成质量，将240s生成的质量漂移从7.84降至1.33。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型虽适合流式变长视频生成，但难以扩展到分钟级生成：KV缓存预算有限，模型无法保留完整历史；反复基于自生成帧进行条件化会累积上下文分布漂移，导致视觉伪影、质量退化和时序漂移。

Method: TetherCache将缓存组织为sink、memory和recent三个区域，并引入两种互补机制：1）GRAB：使用结合注意力相关性和时间多样性的门控分数选择长程记忆帧，在固定缓存预算下保持信息丰富且多样化的历史上下文；2）TAME：通过将新召回的记忆token的统计量对齐到可信上下文分布，减少漂移历史特征造成的污染。

Result: 在Self-Forcing框架上，TetherCache在VBench-Long的30s、60s、240s设置下持续提升长视频生成质量。240s生成中，整体和语义分数显著提升，质量漂移从7.84降至1.33。

Conclusion: TetherCache通过即插即用的缓存管理策略有效缓解了自回归视频扩散模型在长时域生成中的漂移问题，验证了其在稳定长视频生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion models provide a natural formulation for streaming and variable-length video generation by conditioning newly generated frames on previously generated content. However, extending these models to minute-level generation remains challenging: the limited KV-cache budget prevents the model from retaining the full history, while repeatedly conditioning on self-generated frames induces a context distribution shift that accumulates over time, leading to visual artifacts, quality degradation, and temporal drift. In this paper, we propose TetherCache, a training-free and plug-and-play cache management strategy for drift-resistant long video generation. TetherCache organizes the cache into sink, memory, and recent regions, and introduces two complementary mechanisms. First, GRAB (Gated Recall with Attention-Diversity Balancing) selects long-range memory frames using a gated score that combines attention-based relevance with temporal diversity, preserving informative yet diverse historical context under a fixed cache budget. Second, TAME (Trusted Alignment via Memory Editing) lightly edits newly recalled memory tokens by aligning their statistics to a trusted context distribution, reducing the pollution caused by drifted historical features. Built on Self-Forcing, TetherCache consistently improves long-video generation quality on VBench-Long across 30s, 60s, and 240s settings. In particular, for 240s generation, it substantially improves overall and semantic scores while reducing quality drift from 7.84 to 1.33, demonstrating its effectiveness for stable long-horizon autoregressive video diffusion.

</details>


### [104] [SeamEdit: A Black-Box VLM-Agnostic Pipeline for Large-Image Semantic Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13041)
*Xiangyu Lyu,Dan Lei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SeamEdit是一个无需训练、模型无关的流水线，通过五阶段后处理方法解决大图像语义区域编辑中闭源模型直接应用于分块编辑时出现的语义变形、画面对齐漂移和接缝伪影问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大图像的语义区域编辑需要同时满足高生成质量和与周围内容的自然融合，但现有方法要么依赖白盒模型而未能充分利用闭源模型的强大生成能力，要么直接将闭源模型应用于分块编辑导致语义变形、对齐漂移和接缝伪影等问题。

Method: SeamEdit是一个五阶段后处理流水线：基于覆盖的图块分解、黑盒VLM修复、几何与颜色一致性校正、基于接缝风险的多候选排序、动态规划曲线接缝融合。

Result: 该方法能够减少接缝可见性，支持任意图块区域的语义修改。

Conclusion: SeamEdit以无需训练、模型无关的方式解决了闭源模型直接用于分块编辑时的关键缺陷，实现了高质量的大图像语义区域编辑。

Abstract: Semantic region editing for large images must satisfy two requirements at the same time: high generative quality and natural integration with surrounding content. Some related methods rely on white-box models and leave the strong generation capability of closed-source models underexplored. Directly applying closed-source models to tiled editing, however, introduces several failure modes: semantic deformation, canvas-level alignment drift, and visible seam artifacts. This paper presents SeamEdit, a training-free and model-agnostic pipeline that treats any VLM with inpainting capability as a black-box oracle. SeamEdit mitigates these issues through a five-stage post-hoc pipeline: overlay-based tile decomposition, black-box VLM inpainting, geometric and color-consistency correction, seam-risk-based multi-candidate ranking, and dynamic-programming curved seam fusion. The pipeline reduces seam visibility and supports semantic modification of arbitrary tile regions.

</details>


### [105] [LaME: Learning to Think in Latent Space for Multimodal Embedding via Information Bottleneck](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13061)
*Peixi Wu,Biao Yang,Feipeng Ma,Bosong Chai,Bo Lin,Wei Yuan,Fan Yang,Tingting Gao,Hebei Li,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出LaME（潜在推理多模态嵌入），通过可学习推理令牌在潜在空间完成嵌入推理，避免了显式CoT推理的高计算成本和注释依赖，实现60倍推理加速且性能竞争力强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CoT推理的多模态嵌入方法存在两大瓶颈：(i)自回归CoT推理计算成本高，不适合低延迟检索；(ii)嵌入性能严重依赖CoT注释质量，大规模训练不可靠。这引发根本性问题：文本CoT是否是嵌入推理的最优形式？能否在潜在空间完成有效嵌入推理？

Method: 提出LaME，将面向嵌入的潜在推理建模为弱监督信息瓶颈。使用K个可学习推理令牌作为固定容量瓶颈，单次前向传播完成所有推理。两个弱监督信号在结构上解耦对比目标和自回归目标，消除对CoT注释的依赖；两阶段训练流程确保稳定收敛。

Result: 在MMEB-v2和MRMR基准上，LaME取得竞争性性能，超越部分显式CoT模型；推理速度比显式CoT方法快60倍，比潜在基线快2倍，吞吐量与判别式嵌入模型相当。

Conclusion: 潜在空间推理可替代文本CoT实现高效嵌入推理，LaME在性能和效率之间取得了良好平衡，验证了弱监督信息瓶颈范式在多模态嵌入中的有效性。

Abstract: Reasoning-driven universal multimodal embedding has advanced rapidly by introducing Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning into the embedding pipeline. Despite the strong performance across both general and complex tasks, this paradigm suffers from two core limitations: (i) autoregressive CoT reasoning incurs high computational cost, making it impractical for low-latency retrieval; and (ii) embedding performance is heavily coupled with CoT annotation quality, making large-scale training unreliable. These raise fundamental questions: Is textual CoT the optimal form of reasoning for embedding, and can effective embedding reasoning be accomplished in latent space? To this end, we propose LaME (Latent Reasoning Multimodal Embedding), which formulates embedding-oriented latent reasoning as a weakly supervised information bottleneck. LaME employs K learnable reason tokens as a fixed-capacity bottleneck, completing all reasoning within a single forward pass. The two weak supervision signals structurally decouple contrastive from autoregressive objectives and eliminate dependence on CoT annotations, while a two-stage training pipeline ensures stable convergence. Experiments on MMEB-v2 and MRMR show that LaME achieves competitive performance, surpassing some explicit CoT-based models, while delivering 60x faster inference than explicit CoT methods and 2x faster than latent baselines with throughput comparable to discriminative embedding models. Code will be released.

</details>


### [106] [Unified MRI Brain Image Translation via Hierarchical Tumor Structure Comparison](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13096)
*Yupeng Cai,Jia Wei,Jianlong Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HTSCGAN，一种通过引入肿瘤区域层次结构信息（三个不同尺度的Patch对比模块+预训练的分类器和结构感知编码器）来提升多模态脑图像翻译质量及肿瘤区域保真度的生成对抗网络，在BraTS2020和BraTS2021上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有脑图像翻译方法忽略了不同肿瘤区域的结构信息，导致翻译后肿瘤区域保真度不足，影响临床可用性。

Method: 提出HTSCGAN，生成器使用三个不同patch大小的Patch对比模块（PCM）捕获肿瘤区域的层次结构信息；同时采用预训练的Patch分类器（PC）和结构感知编码器（SAE），通过patch分类损失和肿瘤感知损失确保生成图像与真实图像具有相同的肿瘤区域结构。

Result: 在BraTS2020和BraTS2021数据集上，模型在翻译任务和下游分割任务中均表现出强性能，证明了其提升翻译图像质量和临床相关性的有效性。

Conclusion: HTSCGAN通过显式建模肿瘤区域的层次结构信息，有效提升了多模态脑图像翻译的质量和临床适用性，开源代码可供复现。

Abstract: Multi-modal MRI brain image translation via available modalities holds significant practical importance in modern medicine, providing robust support for early diagnosis, treatment planning, and outcome assessment of diseases. For this purpose, it is important to ensure the fidelity of the tumor regions after translation. However, existing brain image translation methods ignore the structure information of different tumor regions, which could assist translation models in enhancing the quality and clinical applicability of the translated images.
  In this work, we propose a novel translation model called HTSCGAN, which is a unified multi-modal brain image translation generative adversarial model integrating the structural information within tumor regions with the aim of improving the quality of brain image translation. Specifically, the generator employs three Patch Contrast Module (PCM) with different patch sizes to capture the hierarchical structural information of the tumor regions. In addition, a pretrained Patch Classifier (PC) and a pretrained Structure-Aware Encoder (SAE) are employed to derive the generated image containing the same tumor region structure as the ground truth image via patch classification loss and tumor perceptual loss, respectively. The experiments on BraTS2020 and BraTS2021 demonstrate strong performance of our model in both translation tasks and down stream segmentation tasks, highlighting its effectiveness in enhancing the quality and clinical relevance of the translated brain images. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/HTSCGAN.

</details>


### [107] [PP-OCRv6: From 1.5M to 34.5M Parameters, Surpassing Billion-Scale VLMs on OCR Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13108)
*Yubo Zhang,Xueqing Wang,Manhui Lin,Yue Zhang,Penglongyi Deng,Ting Sun,Tingquan Gao,Zelun Zhang,Jiaxuan Liu,Changda Zhou,Hongen Liu,Suyin Liang,Cheng Cui,Yi Liu,Dianhai Yu,Yanjun Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PP-OCRv6 提出了一种轻量级 OCR 系统，通过统一的 MetaFormer 风格构建块重构骨干网络、检测颈和识别颈，结合结构重参数化与数据驱动优化，在显著降低参数量的同时，在识别精度和检测性能上全面超越 PP-OCRv5_server 及大规模 VLM 模型（如 Qwen3-VL-235B、GPT-5.5、Gemini-3.1-Pro），并实现 CPU 上 3.9 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLM 在通用视觉-语言任务上表现优异，但在专用 OCR 场景中存在幻觉、定位不精确和计算开销过大等问题，亟需一种轻量级、高精度且高效的 OCR 系统。

Method: 采用统一的 MetaFormer 风格构建块（含结构重参数化），将空间 token 混合与通道混合解耦；针对检测和识别任务分别设置不同的步长配置；设计三个模型规模（medium/small/tiny）共享相同块原语，覆盖从服务器到边缘的部署场景。

Result: PP-OCRv6_medium 在内部基准上识别精度达 83.2%，检测 Hmean 达 86.2%，分别比 PP-OCRv5_server 高出 +5.1% 和 +4.6%；以数量级更少的参数超越 Qwen3-VL-235B、GPT-5.5、Gemini-3.1-Pro；tiny 版本在 Intel Xeon CPU 上推理速度比 PP-OCRv5_mobile 快 3.9 倍，精度相当。

Conclusion: PP-OCRv6 通过架构创新（MetaFormer 统一块）与数据驱动优化的结合，在轻量级 OCR 系统中实现了精度与效率的双重突破，为专用 OCR 部署提供了高性能解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have achieved impressive results on general vision-language tasks, yet they suffer from hallucination, imprecise localization, and prohibitive computational cost when applied to dedicated OCR scenarios. This paper presents PP-OCRv6, a lightweight OCR system that combines architectural innovation with data-centric optimization. PP-OCRv6 redesigns the backbone, detection neck, and recognition neck around a unified MetaFormer-style building block with structural reparameterization, decoupling spatial token mixing from channel mixing and supporting both tasks through task-specific stride configurations. Three model tiers (medium, small, tiny) share the same block primitives, covering deployment scenarios from server to edge. On our in-house benchmarks, PP-OCRv6_medium achieves 83.2% recognition accuracy and 86.2% detection Hmean, outperforming PP-OCRv5_server by +5.1% and +4.6% respectively while surpassing Qwen3-VL-235B, GPT-5.5, and Gemini-3.1-Pro with orders of magnitude fewer parameters. The tiny tier achieves 3.9$\times$ faster inference than PP-OCRv5_mobile on Intel Xeon CPU while maintaining comparable accuracy.

</details>


### [108] [Fully Distributed Multi-View 3D Tracking in Real-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13127)
*Byron Hernandez,Fangyu Li,Aotian Wu,Paul J. Shin,Kaustubh Purandare,Henry Medeiros*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MV3DT是一个完全分布式的实时多视图3D跟踪框架，通过点对点协作在各摄像头节点间实现身份传播和遮挡恢复，无需中心化聚合。在WILDTRACK数据集上达到94.3% IDF1和93.3% MOTA，支持100个摄像头以30 FPS运行，延迟低于10毫秒，通信开销仅2.2%，且无需场景特定训练即可零样本部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于重叠视野的多摄像头跟踪方法通常依赖中心化融合，导致计算瓶颈，难以在大规模摄像头网络中部署。

Method: 提出MV3DT，一个完全分布式的框架，每个摄像头节点执行轻量级模块化流水线，包括单目3D感知、分布式多视图关联和通过轻量级消息传递实现的协作融合，通过点对点协调实现身份传播和遮挡恢复。

Result: 在WILDTRACK上达到94.3% IDF1和93.3% MOTA，与最先进的中心化方法竞争力相当；在100个摄像头上以30 FPS运行，摄像头间延迟低于10毫秒，通信开销仅2.2%。

Conclusion: MV3DT是面向大规模重叠摄像头网络中实时多视图跟踪的实用解决方案，在零样本设置下可直接部署于新环境。

Abstract: Multi-camera tracking with overlapping fields of view typically relies on centralized fusion, which creates computational bottlenecks that prevent deployment at scale. We present MV3DT, a fully distributed framework for real-time multi-view 3D tracking that achieves accurate identity propagation and occlusion recovery through peer-to-peer coordination, eliminating the need for central aggregation. Each camera node executes a lightweight modular pipeline comprising monocular 3D perception, distributed multi-view association, and collaborative fusion via lightweight messaging. MV3DT achieves 94.3% IDF1 and 93.3% MOTA on WILDTRACK, competitive with state-of-the-art centralized methods, while demonstrating superior scalability by sustaining 30 FPS on 100 cameras with less than 10 ms inter-camera latency and only 2.2% communication overhead. MV3DT operates in a zero-shot regime given camera calibrations, requiring no scene-specific learning and making it directly deployable in new environments. These results establish MV3DT as a practical solution for real-time multi-view tracking in large-scale overlapping camera networks.

</details>


### [109] [Cascade Classification of Dermoscopic Images of Skin Neoplasms with Controllable Sensitivity and External Clinical Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13135)
*Elena S. Kozachok,Sergey S. Seregin,Aleksandr V. Kozachok,Ilya P. Latyshev,Oleg I. Samovarov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究比较了四种深度学习架构（ViT-B/16、Swin-S、ConvNeXt-S、EfficientNetV2-S）在皮肤镜图像分类中的三种方案（二分类、单阶段四分类、两阶段级联），发现从国际开放数据集迁移到俄罗斯临床数据集时存在明显的泛化差距（ROC-AUC从0.95-0.97降至0.80-0.89），两阶段级联方案可恢复部分被误分类为良性的恶性病变，但所有模型在临床部署前均需外部验证和重校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估不同深度学习架构和分类方案在皮肤肿瘤皮肤镜图像上的表现，并考察从国际开放数据集向俄罗斯临床独立数据集迁移时的泛化能力。

Method: 比较四种架构（ViT-B/16、Swin-S、ConvNeXt-S、EfficientNetV2-S），在三种分类方案（二分类恶性/良性、单阶段四分类良性/MEL/SCC/BCC、两阶段级联即二分类分诊后三分类鉴别）下进行实验。所有模型使用ImageNet预训练权重和统一的数据增强，在ISIC Archive聚合数据上训练，并在内部留存样本和两个临床数据集（Melanoscope AI移动系统、Sechenov大学）上评估。

Result: 内部二分类ROC-AUC为0.952-0.966；在Sechenov大学数据集下降至0.797-0.893，灵敏度降至0.53-0.67，ECE从0.02升至0.27-0.39，存在明显的排名和校准泛化差距。配对检验确认ViT-B/16在二分类阶段存在缺陷（p<0.05），鉴别阶段无架构有显著优势。级联方案在大多数架构上提高了宏F1，但仅对ViT-B/16显著，通过恢复被误分类为良性类别的恶性病变实现。

Conclusion: 可调的分诊阈值提供了标准单阶段（argmax）分类无法实现的灵敏度控制，并能更好地复现临床鉴别诊断逻辑。持续的泛化差距要求在部署前必须进行外部临床验证和重校准。

Abstract: Purpose. To compare deep learning architectures and classification schemes for dermoscopic images of skin neoplasms and assess their generalization on transfer from open international datasets to independent clinical datasets of Russian practice.
  Methods. Four architectures (ViT-B/16, Swin-S, ConvNeXt-S, EfficientNetV2-S) were compared in three schemes: binary (malignant/benign), single-stage four-class (benign, MEL, SCC, BCC), and a two-stage cascade (binary triage, then three-class differentiation MEL/SCC/BCC). All models used ImageNet-pretrained weights and a single augmentation protocol on aggregated open ISIC Archive data, and were evaluated on an internal held-out sample and two clinical datasets (Melanoscope AI mobile system; Sechenov University).
  Results. Internally the binary stage attains ROC-AUC 0.952-0.966; on Sechenov University it drops to 0.797-0.893, sensitivity to 0.53-0.67, and ECE rises from 0.02 to 0.27-0.39 with underestimation of malignancy, quantifying a generalization gap in ranking and calibration. Paired tests confirm one inter-architecture result on clinical data: the deficit of ViT-B/16 at the binary stage (p<0.05); at the differentiation stage no architecture has a proven advantage. The cascade raises macro F1 over single-stage four-class classification for most architectures, but significantly only for ViT-B/16, by recovering malignant lesions assigned to the dominant benign class. On ISIC MILK10k, direct 11-class classification yields mean-class sensitivity 0.525.
  Conclusion. A tunable triage threshold gives sensitivity control not attainable in standard single-stage (argmax) classification and better reproduces clinical differential-diagnosis logic. The persistent generalization gap mandates external clinical validation and recalibration before deployment.

</details>


### [110] [An Extensible and Lightweight Unified Architecture for Demosaicing Pixel-bin Image Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13136)
*Saurabh Kumar,Nutan Sairam Yenneti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种模块化统一架构，用于处理多种像素合并传感器的去马赛克问题，并引入无需学习的CFA识别模块实现即插即用，在提高图像质量的同时保持轻量和可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 像素合并传感器虽已成为智能手机相机的主流选择，但其较大的颜色间分离度使得去马赛克过程具有挑战性；同时，现有深度学习去马赛克方法仅针对特定CFA设计，需维护多个独立模型，占用宝贵的片上资源并增加开发维护成本。

Method: 提出模块化统一架构，可处理多种像素合并传感器的去马赛克；引入无需学习的CFA识别模块，通过检测原始数据的CFA类型实现即插即用操作。

Result: 该架构在提供更高质量图像的同时，具备轻量化和可扩展性；CFA识别模块能准确识别CFA类型。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一架构可有效应对像素合并传感器去马赛克的挑战，减少模型数量与开发维护成本，适合资源受限的移动设备场景。

Abstract: Pixel-bin image sensors are becoming the default choice for smartphone cameras due to their resolution vs light-gathering trade-off. However, their larger inter-color separation compared to the Bayer color filter array (CFA) makes them challenging to demosaic. Furthermore, existing deep learning-based demosaicing methods are CFA-specific, requiring multiple individual models that take up precious onboard resources and demand larger development and maintenance efforts. In this work, we propose a modular unified architecture for demosaicing various pixel-bin sensors that provides higher image quality while being extensible and lightweight. Additionally, to enable plug-and-play operation, we introduce a learning-free CFA-identification module to detect the CFA type of raw data accurately.

</details>


### [111] [Iterative Visual Thinking: Teaching Vision-Language Models Spatial Self-Correction through Visual Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13156)
*Animesh Tripathy,Aswanth Krishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出迭代视觉思维（IVT）框架，通过两阶段训练（SFT+GRPO）使视觉语言模型具备空间自校正能力，仅用2400样本在单GPU上将定位精度从79.6%提升至82.0%，并将多步迭代中的IoU退化降低5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型虽能一次性完成空间定位，但缺乏观察和修正自身预测的机制，直接让模型迭代预测会导致性能严重下降（Acc@0.5从79.6%暴跌至48.7%），存在基础定位能力与自校正能力之间的根本差距。

Method: 提出IVT闭环框架：1）模型预测边界框→将预测渲染到图像上→通过视觉反馈迭代优化；2）两阶段训练：第一阶段利用基础模型自身预测作为真实错误，引导教师VLM生成校正推理轨迹，无需人工标注；第二阶段采用GRPO结合IoU奖励稳定多步细化过程。

Result: 在RefCOCOg、Ref-Adv和Ref-L4混合基准（505样本）上，SFT预热的IVT在所有指标上超越单次基础模型：Acc@0.5达82.0%（+2.4pp），Acc@0.7达74.1%（+3.2pp），Acc@0.9达48.3%（+2.8pp）。GRPO将每步IoU退化降低5倍，所有训练仅用2400样本和单GPU。

Conclusion: 空间自校正是一种可通过适度规模训练习得的能力，IVT有效弥合了VLM的定位能力与自校正能力之间的差距。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) achieve strong singleshot spatial grounding, yet lack any mechanism to observe and correct their own predictions. We find that naively prompting a VLM to iterate over rendered visualizations of its predictions causes catastrophic failure: Acc@0.5 on referring expression comprehension collapses from 79.6% to 48.7% (a 31 percentage point drop), revealing a fundamental gap between grounding capability and self-correction ability. We propose Iterative Visual Thinking (IVT), a closed-loop framework in which the model predicts a bounding box, observes the prediction rendered on the image, and iteratively refines through visual feedback. A two-phase training recipe closes the self-correction gap: first, we exploit the base model's own predictions as realistic errors and prompt a teacher VLM to generate corrective reasoning traces, yielding supervised data without human annotation; second, we apply Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) with a simple IoU reward to stabilize multi-step refinement. On a mixed benchmark spanning RefCOCOg, Ref-Adv, and Ref-L4 (505 test samples), SFT warm-up with IVT surpasses the single-shot base model on every metric: Acc@0.5 rises to 82.0% (+2.4pp), Acc@0.7 to 74.1% (+3.2pp), and Acc@0.9 to 48.3% (+2.8pp). GRPO further reduces per-step IoU degradation by 5x, stabilizing the refinement trajectory. All training uses only 2,400 samples on a single GPU, demonstrating that spatial self-correction is a learnable capability that can be instilled at modest scale.

</details>


### [112] [DuET: Dual Expert Trajectories for Diffusion Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13303)
*Lidia Troeshestova,Alexander Ustyuzhanin,Sergey Kastryulin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DuET是一种无需训练的新型推理方法，通过在编辑过程中临时放松源图像条件，过渡到文本到图像阶段再返回编辑模式，从而在保持结构优势的同时提升编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散编辑模型在每一步都依赖源图像条件，导致当目标场景与输入差异较大时，编辑不彻底且结果不自然。作者希望在不修改模型权重或增加采样成本的前提下，解决源图像条件限制编辑效果的问题。

Method: 提出DuET（Dual Expert Trajectories），一种无需训练的推理方法。核心思想是在去噪轨迹中临时放松源图像条件，通过过渡到文本到图像（T2I）阶段，让轨迹向目标分布移动，之后再返回编辑模式，从而结合图像条件编辑的结构优势与目标分布的生成能力。

Result: 在多种模型和基准上，DuET一致提升了指令相关性、语义保真度和感知质量。同时发现存在源图像保留与编辑保真度之间的可预测权衡，部分提升以轻微降低源图像保留为代价。

Conclusion: DuET无需微调或增加采样成本即可显著提升基于指令的图像编辑效果，特别是在目标场景与源图像差异较大的情况下。该方法揭示了源保留与编辑保真度之间的权衡关系，为后续研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: Recent diffusion editors perform diverse instruction-based edits while conditioning on the source image at every denoising step. Yet persistent source-image conditioning can limit how fully an edit is executed and how natural the result appears, especially when the target scene diverges substantially from the input. We introduce DuET (Dual Expert Trajectories), a training-free inference method that temporarily relaxes source-image conditioning by transitioning through a text-to-image phase before returning to edit mode, allowing the denoising trajectory to move toward the target distribution while retaining the structural benefits of image-conditioned editing. Without modifying model weights or increasing sampling cost, DuET consistently improves instruction relevance, semantic fidelity, and perceptual quality across diverse models and benchmarks. In some cases, these gains come with a modest reduction in source-image preservation, revealing a predictable trade-off between source preservation and edit fidelity.

</details>


### [113] [ReFree: Towards Realistic Co-Speech Video Generation via Reward-Free RL and Multilevel Speech Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13304)
*Salaheldin Mohamed,M. Hamza Mughal,Rishabh Dabral,Christian Theobalt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ReFree-S2V是一个基于流匹配的语音驱动肖像动画框架，通过多级语音表示和免奖励强化学习，在保持精确唇同步的同时实现自然表情和头部运动，性能达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语音驱动动画方法难以同时实现精确的唇部同步和富有表现力的面部表情与头部运动，通常需要在这两者之间进行权衡。

Method: 提出ReFree-S2V框架：(1) 基于预训练视频生成模型构建流匹配架构；(2) 设计多级语音表示捕获局部和全局的音素与韵律信息；(3) 通过可学习层级选择器选择性注入Transformer块；(4) 引入免奖励强化学习方案，无需手工同步指标、奖励模型或人工偏好标注，抑制感知上不合理的运动。

Result: 在定量唇同步精度和定性自然度、表现力的人类评估中均显著优于现有方法，达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: ReFree-S2V有效解决了唇同步与表情表达之间的权衡问题，在无需复杂奖励设计的条件下实现了自然且同步的语音驱动肖像动画。

Abstract: Speech-driven talking character animation seeks to generate life-like portrait videos that convey natural conversation behavior, aligning facial motion with spoken audio. Although recent advances in video generation have substantially improved realism in video-based animation, achieving both accurate lip articulation and expressive behavior remains challenging. Existing approaches typically trade off precise phoneme-to-lip synchronization against dynamic facial expressions and head motion, yielding animations that are either accurate yet rigid, or expressive but poorly synchronized. We address this challenge by proposing ReFree-S2V, a flow-matching speech-to-portrait animation framework that builds upon a pretrained video generation model to achieve fine-grained speech articulation and high-level expressive cues in speech-driven portrait animation. This model introduces a multi-level speech representation capturing phonetic and prosodic information at both local and global granularities. These representations are selectively injected into transformer blocks via learnable level selectors, enabling both accurate lip synchronization and natural expressive motion. To achieve natural head movements, we further introduce a novel reward-free reinforcement learning scheme into flow-matching training to discourage perceptually implausible motion without relying on handcrafted synchronization metrics or reward models, or the high cost of human preference annotation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ReFree-S2V achieves state-of-the-art performance, significantly outperforming existing methods in both quantitative lip-sync accuracy and qualitative human evaluations of naturalness and expressivity.

</details>


### [114] [MagPlus: Bridging Micro-to-Regular Facial Expressions through Learnable Magnification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13312)
*Sliman Jammal,Andrei Sharf*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出MagPlus管道，通过将微表情放大为常规表情强度，利用现成的面部动画模型进行处理，再通过DeMagPlus还原回微表情强度，无需重新训练即可生成逼真的微表情。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微表情数据标注有限且面部运动极其微弱，现有生成方法质量差、鲁棒性弱、泛化能力不足。

Method: MagPlus将微弱的面部运动放大到常规表情强度范围，使微表情与现有面部表情处理模型兼容；DeMagPlus将生成的运动恢复回微表情强度，同时保留合成动态。

Result: 在FOMM、FSRT、MetaPortrait、EmoPortraits四个未经微表情数据训练的面部动画模型上验证，MagPlus-DeMagPlus使预训练的宏表情模型能够生成更逼真的微表情运动。

Conclusion: 该管道实现了微表情分析与标准面部动画模型的有效连接，无需重新训练即可提升微表情生成质量。

Abstract: Facial micro-expressions are subtle and short-lived facial movements that provide important cues about genuine human emotions. However, modeling and generating them remains difficult because annotated micro-expression data is limited and the underlying facial motions are extremely weak. Existing micro-expression generation methods therefore often suffer from limited quality, weak robustness, and poor generalization. We propose MagPlus, a transferable micro-expression processing pipeline that connects micro-expression analysis with standard facial animation models. Instead of training a dedicated generator from scratch, MagPlus learns to magnify subtle facial motions into the range of regular facial expressions, transforming micro-expressions into signals that are compatible with existing facial expression processing models. The magnified sequence is then used by a standard facial expression model for tasks such as transfer and synthesis. A complementary DeMagPlus module then restores the generated motion back to realistic micro-expression intensity levels while preserving the synthesized dynamics. We evaluate the framework using four facial animation models: FOMM, FSRT, MetaPortrait, and EmoPortraits. None of these models are trained on micro-expression data. Experiments show that MagPlus-DeMagPlus enables pretrained macro-expression models to generate more realistic micro-expression motion without retraining the backbones.

</details>


### [115] [Masked and Predictive Self-Supervised Foundation Models for 3D Brain MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13315)
*Esra Ergün,Hersh Chandarana,Dan Sodickson,Gözde Ünal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了两种自监督预训练范式（MAE和JEPA）用于MRI疾病检测，引入频谱域重建损失和方差-协方差正则化，发现自监督目标的收益取决于下游任务结构：频谱正则化利于高频解剖结构任务，协方差正则化利于多维度解耦特征任务，MAE+频谱监督在MRI疾病检测中表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI基础模型研究主要集中于分割和密集预测任务，而对基于MRI疾病检测的自监督基础模型的系统研究仍然有限。

Method: 研究了两种自监督预训练范式：基于掩码自编码器（MAE）的重建学习和基于联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）的预测性表示学习。引入频谱域重建损失增强MAE对精细解剖结构的敏感性，在JEPA框架中集成方差-协方差正则化（VCR）鼓励解耦潜在表示。模型在异质性单对比MRI体积上以对比无关方式进行预训练。

Result: 在五个下游疾病检测任务中，频谱正则化在下游判别信号以强高频解剖结构为特征时提升最大；协方差正则化在判别信息跨越多个解耦特征维度时最有益。MAE+频谱域监督在MRI疾病检测中持续取得最优下游性能。

Conclusion: 自监督目标在医学影像中编码了特定的归纳偏差，其下游收益根本上取决于任务结构。

Abstract: Self-supervised foundation models have shown strong promise in medical imaging. However, existing MRI foundation-model studies have primarily emphasized segmentation and dense prediction tasks, while systematic investigation of self-supervised foundation models for MRI-based disease detection remains limited. In this work, we investigate two major self-supervised pretraining paradigms for MRI-based disease detection: reconstruction-based learning via Masked Autoencoders (MAE) and predictive representation learning via Joint Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPA). We study the role of auxiliary objectives by introducing a novel spectral-domain reconstruction loss for MAE to enhance sensitivity to fine-grained anatomical structure, and by integrating variance--covariance regularization (VCR) within our JEPA framework to encourage decorrelated latent representations. Our models are pretrained on heterogeneous single-contrast MRI volumes in a contrast-agnostic setting, without modality concatenation. Across five downstream disease detection tasks, our results highlight the importance of self-supervised objective design for medical foundation model pretraining, demonstrating that the downstream benefit of each objective is determined by its relevance to the task's structure. Specifically, spectral regularization yields the largest improvements when the downstream discriminative signal is characterized by strong high-frequency anatomical structures, while covariance regularization is most beneficial when discriminative information spans multiple decorrelated feature dimensions. MAE with spectral-domain supervision consistently achieves superior downstream performance for MRI-based disease detection. These findings suggest that self-supervised objectives in medical imaging encode specific biases, and their downstream benefit is fundamentally conditioned on the task's structure.

</details>


### [116] [OR-Action: Multi-Role Video Understanding with Fine-Grained Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13332)
*Felix Tristram,Ege Özsoy,Christian Benz,Marcel Walch,Ghazal Ghazaei,Nassir Navab*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个基于公开OR数据集的细粒度动作中心基准，通过场景图状态变化蒸馏生成密集动作片段。实验表明当前场景图预测方法缺乏时间建模能力，因此引入纯视觉时序模型，该模型在全视角输入下显著优于图方法，并进一步提出多视角到单视角特征对齐策略，提升单视角多角色动作识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术室活动细粒度理解面临杂乱、遮挡和传感受限的挑战。现有方法多采用场景图作为OR交互的可解释表示，但将逐帧关系预测转化为时间上连续的细粒度动作缺乏显式时序建模，且缺乏原则性的时序评估基准。

Method: 1) 构建首个动作中心基准，基于公开的ego-exocentric OR数据集，定义细粒度多角色动作分类法，通过场景图状态变化蒸馏生成密集动作片段；2) 提出纯视觉时序模型，在全视角ego视频输入下显著优于基于图的方法；3) 提出多视角到单视角特征对齐策略，提升单视角多角色动作识别性能。

Result: 当前场景图预测方法（即使加入GNN显式建模）在时序结构建模上表现不佳；所提纯视觉时序模型在全视角ego视频输入下显著优于图方法；多到单视角特征对齐策略有效提升了单视角多角色动作识别性能。

Conclusion: 场景图方法在OR动作识别中时序建模能力有限，纯视觉时序模型结合多到单视角特征对齐是一种更有效的方案，可减少对广泛ego视频采集的依赖。

Abstract: Fine-grained understanding of operating room (OR) activity could enable workflow-aware assistance, yet remains difficult due to clutter, occlusions, and limited sensing. The prevailing approach to model this environment is scene graphs as an interpretable representation of OR interactions. Converting their frame-wise relational predictions into temporally extended, fine-grained actions however, is challenging without explicit temporal modeling. To enable a principled temporal evaluation of current OR understanding methods, we introduce the first action-centric benchmark built on a publicly available ego-exocentric OR dataset by defining a fine-grained, multi-role action taxonomy and generating dense action segments via distillation from ground-truth scene graph state changes. Experiments on this benchmark show that current scene graph prediction methods struggle to model temporal structure, even when adding explicit modeling through Graph Neural Networks. We therefore introduce a vision-only temporal model that outperforms graph-based methods significantly when using all available egocentric video as input. Building on this model we also introduce a novel multi- to single-view feature alignment strategy that improves single-view performance on multi-role action recognition, mitigating the need for extensive egocentric video capture. Benchmark and code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [117] [Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network for Multimodal CT-PET Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13341)
*Gabriel Steele,Alzahra Altalib,Alessandro Perelli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DDE-GAN通过联合空间域与频率域学习，并引入旋转等变性损失，实现了多模态CT-PET图像的合成，在HECKTOR 2022数据集上表现优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于GAN的图像合成方法仅在空间域操作且忽略几何一致性，导致结构保真度有限。

Method: 1) 双域学习：联合空间域和频率（傅里叶）域；2) 在生成器和判别器的损失中嵌入旋转等变性；3) 层次化双域训练策略，通过多阶段损失函数强化域内和域间一致性。

Result: 在HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET数据集上，DDE-GAN的合成质量优于多种基线模型，表明双域学习结合几何等变性显著提升了多模态图像合成的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 双域学习与几何等变性的结合能有效增强多模态图像合成性能，可应用于PET补全和数据增强等实际场景。

Abstract: We present a Dual-Domain Equivariant Generative Adversarial Network (DDE-GAN) for multimodal CT-PET image synthesis. Traditional GAN-based approaches often operate solely in the spatial domain and ignore geometric consistency, resulting in limited structural fidelity. DDE-GAN addresses these challenges by jointly learning from both spatial and frequency (Fourier) domains, capturing complementary anatomical and spectral information. Furthermore, rotational equivariance embedded in the physics of the CT and PET measurements are integrated into the loss of both the generator and discriminator to ensure consistent responses under rotations, improving anatomical accuracy. A hierarchical dual-domain training strategy enforces intra- and inter-domain consistency through multi-stage loss functions. Evaluated on the HECKTOR 2022 CT-PET dataset, DDE-GAN achieves superior synthesis quality over baseline models for CT-PET image synthesis. The results demonstrate that combining dual-domain learning with geometric equivariance substantially enhances multimodal image synthesis accuracy and robustness, enabling practical applications in PET completion and data augmentation.

</details>


### [118] [JointEdit3D: Feed-Forward 3D Scene Editing in a Unified Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13345)
*Xinnan Zhu,Ruijie Xu,Jiayu Ying,Daoguo Dong,Jiachen Xu,Yuan Xie,Xin Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: JointEdit3D是一个基于统一RGB-几何重建生成潜在空间的3D场景编辑框架，通过非对称潜在修复实现仅从单个编辑参考RGB视图生成其余RGB视图和编辑后的几何潜在，并引入SceneAnchor Branch和编辑/背景感知损失来平衡编辑区域保真度与未编辑内容保持。同时提出SceneEdit3D-15K数据集和SceneEdit3D-Bench基准用于标准化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景编辑方法依赖逐场景优化或级联编辑-重建流程，导致测试成本高、3D感知有限和结构不一致性问题。

Method: 基于统一RGB-几何重建生成潜在空间，采用非对称潜在修复（仅观察单个编辑后的RGB参考潜在，生成剩余RGB视图和编辑后的几何潜在），引入SceneAnchor Branch注入源场景结构，并采用编辑/背景感知损失平衡编辑区域保真度与未编辑内容保持。

Result: JointEdit3D在编辑区域质量和3D结构完整性上优于先前基线，同时保持有竞争力的背景保持性能。构建的SceneEdit3D-15K数据集包含15K配对编辑样本和3D标注，以及包含100个样本的SceneEdit3D-Bench基准。

Conclusion: JointEdit3D通过前馈式统一RGB-几何潜在空间实现了高效且结构一致的3D场景编辑，并提供了标准化评估数据集和基准。

Abstract: Existing 3D scene editing methods typically rely on per-scene optimization over explicit 3D representations or cascaded edit-and-reconstruct pipelines, resulting in high test-time cost, limited 3D awareness, and structural inconsistencies. To couple appearance synthesis and geometry prediction during editing, we build on a unified RGB-geometry reconstruction-generation latent space and adapt it to feed-forward 3D scene editing. The resulting framework, \textbf{JointEdit3D}, performs asymmetric latent inpainting by observing only a single edited RGB reference latent and generating the remaining RGB views and edited geometry latent under source-scene anchoring. JointEdit3D introduces a dedicated SceneAnchor Branch to inject source-scene structure without forcing direct copying, and adopts edit/background-aware losses to balance edited-region fidelity with unedited-content preservation. To address the lack of paired resources for standardized 3D scene editing evaluation, we introduce SceneEdit3D-15K, a dataset with 15K paired editing samples and renderer-provided 3D annotations, together with SceneEdit3D-Bench, a curated 100-sample benchmark. Experiments show that JointEdit3D improves edited-region quality and 3D structural completeness over prior baselines while maintaining competitive background preservation.

</details>


### [119] [Dual-Constrained Diffusion Image Compression for Operational Rate-Distortion-Perception Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13366)
*Sanxin Jiang,Jiro Katto,Heming Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DCIC（双约束扩散图像压缩）框架，通过联合失真约束和幂等约束引导扩散解码器，在无需额外码率开销的情况下实现率-失真-感知（RDP）曲面的完整导航，并通过双衰减因子(K_D, K_P)实现失真-感知平面帕累托前沿的连续调节，实验证明其在BD-PSNR和BD-FID指标上均达到或超越现有感知编码方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作虽能实现接近最优的率-感知权衡，但实际框架难以完整实现RDP曲面，主要困难在于解码器端引入公共随机性。本文旨在解决这一难题，提出能同时控制保真度和感知真实感的统一框架。

Method: 将学习型编解码器与扩散解码器结合，引入双重约束：失真约束限制重建相对基础编解码器输出的保真度；幂等约束要求对恢复图像重新编码能重建基础编解码器输出，作为感知分布约束的可处理替代。通过迭代优化和一致噪声注入引导逆向去噪过程，实现无额外码率开销的公共随机性。双衰减因子(K_D, K_P)联合导航失真-感知平面的帕累托前沿。

Result: 在CelebA-HQ、CLIC2020和ImageNet-1K数据集上，跨CNN、Transformer和混合架构实验表明：DCIC_{RDP}在BD-PSNR上优于所有感知编解码器；DCIC_{RP}在BD-FID上与专用感知导向方法相匹配；DCIC_{RD}和DCIC_{RP}作为边界曲线，DCIC_{RDP}实现最优内部操作点。

Conclusion: DCIC成功实现了率-失真-感知曲面的完整导航，验证了RDP曲面在实际图像压缩中的实用价值，为神经图像压缩中保真度与感知真实性的连续可调权衡提供了有效方案。

Abstract: The rate-distortion-perception (RDP) trade-off extends classical rate--distortion theory by imposing a distributional constraint on reconstructions, providing a unified framework for neural image compression that jointly governs fidelity and perceptual realism. While prior work achieves near-optimal rate--perception trade-offs, practical frameworks explicitly realizing the full RDP surface remain scarce, primarily due to the difficulty of introducing common randomness at the decoder. We propose DCIC (Dual-Constrained Diffusion Image Compression), which integrates a learned codec with a diffusion-based decoder governed by joint distortion and idempotence constraints. The distortion constraint bounds reconstruction fidelity relative to the base codec output; the idempotence constraint -- requiring that re-encoding the restored image recovers the base codec reconstruction -- serves as a tractable surrogate for the distributional perception requirement. Together, they steer the reverse denoising process via iterative optimization with consistent noise injection, realizing common randomness without additional rate overhead. At fixed rate, dual attenuation factors $(K_D, K_P)$ jointly navigate the Pareto frontier of the distortion-perception plane, enabling continuously adjustable fidelity-realism trade-offs from a single bitstream. DCIC$_{RD}$ ($K_P{=}0$) and DCIC$_{RP}$ ($K_D{=}0$) arise as boundary curves, with DCIC$_{RDP}$ ($K_D = K_P=1$) realizing the optimal interior operating point. Experiments on CelebA-HQ, CLIC2020, and ImageNet-1K across CNN, Transformer, and hybrid architectures confirm that DCIC$_{RDP}$ achieves superior BD-PSNR over all perceptual codecs, while DCIC$_{RP}$ matches dedicated perception-oriented methods in BD-FID, validating the practical value of full RDP surface navigation.

</details>


### [120] [MoVerse: Real-Time Video World Modeling with Panoramic Gaussian Scaffold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13376)
*Yang Zhou,Ziheng Wang,Yuqin Lu,Haofeng Liu,Jun Liang,Shengfeng He,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoVerse是一种从单张窄视场图像实时创建可交互漫游360°场景的视频世界模型，通过将世界构建与观测渲染分离，结合拓扑感知扩散、全景3D高斯支架和高斯条件视频渲染器，在单张RTX 4090上实现8 FPS的实时场景漫游。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决从单张窄视场图像生成可交互漫游的完整3D场景这一极具挑战性的问题：输入仅观察到环境的一小部分，而交互式漫游需要完整的周围世界、持久几何、可控相机运动和时序一致的高保真观测。

Method: (1) 拓扑感知扩散：将输入扩展为重力对齐的360°全景图，填补缺失视场；(2) 全景几何感知残差预测：将全景图提升为持久的3D高斯支架，形成可直接渲染的空间记忆；(3) 高斯条件视频渲染器：沿用户指定相机轨迹将支架渲染转为逼真视频，训练双向扩散教师模型用于高质量条件渲染，再蒸馏为因果自回归学生模型实现有界延迟流式输出。

Result: 在单张NVIDIA RTX 4090 GPU上实现8 FPS的实时场景漫游，验证了该方法的实际可行性。

Conclusion: MoVerse将显式3D表示的可控性与长程一致性同生成式视频模型的感知质量相结合，为单图像世界创建与交互式视频输出提供了一条实用路径。

Abstract: We present MoVerse, a real-time video world model that creates an interactively navigable scene from a single narrow-field-of-view image. This setting is challenging because the input observes only a small fraction of the environment, while interactive roaming requires a complete surrounding world, persistent geometry, controllable camera motion, and temporally coherent high-fidelity observations. MoVerse addresses this problem by separating world construction from observation rendering. It first expands the input into a gravity-aligned 360$^\circ$ panorama with topology-aware diffusion, closing the missing field of view before 3D reasoning. It then lifts the panorama into a persistent 3D Gaussian scaffold using panoramic geometry-aware residual prediction, yielding a dense and directly renderable spatial memory. Finally, a Gaussian-conditioned video renderer translates scaffold renderings along user-specified camera trajectories into photorealistic video. To make this renderer practical for interaction, we train a bidirectional diffusion teacher for high-quality conditional rendering and distill it into a causal autoregressive student for bounded-latency streaming. This design combines the controllability and long-range consistency of explicit 3D representations with the perceptual quality of generative video models. MoVerse supports real-time scene roaming at 8~FPS on a single NVIDIA RTX~4090 GPU, demonstrating a practical path toward single-image world creation with interactive video output.

</details>


### [121] [SmartFont: Dynamic Condition Allocation for Few-Shot Font Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13382)
*Zian Yang,Zixin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SmartFont是一个基于扩散模型的少样本字体生成框架，通过多层级条件分配机制结合全局内容-风格生成与弱监督局部校正专家，实现了全局结构完整性与细粒度局部风格保真度的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少样本字体生成方法要么依赖全局内容-风格建模（鲁棒但解耦不完美），要么强调组件/局部建模（捕捉细节但严重依赖局部先验和参考覆盖）。关键挑战不在于学习更纯的条件，而在于通过生成过程中的多层级分配来组织互补但有偏的全局和局部条件。

Method: 提出SmartFont框架：1）局部分支通过弱组件监督学习专家级局部概念和语义空间映射，实现无需显式组件条件推理的细粒度校正；2）去噪状态条件分配模块自适应地在不同时间步和注入块中对全局内容、全局风格和局部校正特征进行加权。

Result: 大量实验表明SmartFont实现了更好的全局-局部平衡，提升了字形质量和局部细节保真度。

Conclusion: SmartFont通过多层级条件分配机制有效解决了少样本字体生成中全局结构与局部细节的权衡问题，为字体生成任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Few-shot font generation simultaneously requires global structural completeness and fine-grained local style fidelity. Existing methods usually either rely on global content-style modeling, which is robust but imperfectly disentangled, or emphasize component/local modeling, which captures fine details but relies heavily on local priors and reference coverage. We argue that the key challenge is not merely to learn purer conditions, but to organize complementary yet biased global and local conditions through multi-level allocation during generation. To this end, we propose SmartFont, a diffusion-based few-shot font generation framework that combines global content-style generation with weakly supervised local corrective experts. The local branch performs semantic-spatial allocation by learning expert-wise local concepts and semantically meaningful spatial maps under weak component supervision, enabling fine-grained correction without requiring explicit component-conditioned inference. On top of this, a denoising-state condition allocation module adaptively weights global content, global style, and local corrective feature across timesteps and injection blocks. Extensive experiments show that SmartFont achieves better global-local balance, improves glyph quality and local detail fidelity.

</details>


### [122] [Person Identification from Contextual Motion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13410)
*Igor Kviatkovsky,Ehud Rivlin,Ilan Shimshoni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于运动风格的人物身份识别方法，并首次引入了交互式身份识别场景。系统通过序列化消息交换过程，向受试者呈现视觉刺激并记录其运动响应，利用互信息最大化原则选择最优线索，逐步更新身份后验概率，直到达到足够置信度。在五个公开数据集和一个包含4476条记录的自主数据集上取得了高识别率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的人体运动身份识别研究多局限于被动观测场景（如监控）和主动认证场景，缺乏一种能够主动与被识别对象交互、通过动态调整刺激来高效获取判别性运动信息的身份识别框架。

Method: 提出了一种基于人类信息处理（HIP）范式的概率生成模型来描述动作实例的创建过程。在交互式场景中，系统与受试者进行序列化消息交换：系统选择能最大化期望响应与身份之间互信息的视觉线索作为刺激，受试者产生运动响应，系统据此更新身份后验概率，迭代直至分类置信度达到阈值。

Result: 在五个公开数据集以及自主采集的包含22名受试者对15种线索的4476条记录的数据集上均取得了高识别率。

Conclusion: 这是首次在交互式设定下解决人物身份识别问题，实验结果表明该主动交互式方法能够有效利用运动风格进行身份鉴别。

Abstract: We consider the problem of identifying people based on their motion styles. We present a generative model describing the action instance creation process and derive a probabilistic identity inference scheme for two common person identification scenarios motivated by the surveillance and authentication applications. We introduce a novel, \emph{interactive}, scenario for person identification from motion patterns. To this end, we formalize the identification process in the context of a sequential message exchange session between the subject and the system. The subject's behavior is modeled using a probabilistic generative model inspired by the Human Information Processing (HIP) paradigm. At each stage, the system presents a visual stimulus (a cue) to the subject and records their motion response. The cue is selected so as to maximize the mutual information of the expected response and the subject's identity. Once recorded, the response is used to update the a posteriori probability over possible subjects' identities. The process terminates once a sufficient classification confidence level is reached. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first time person identification is addressed in such interactive setting. We report high recognition rates on five publicly available datasets and our own novel dataset consisting of 4,476 recordings of 22 test subjects responding to 15 cues.

</details>


### [123] [VietFashion: Benchmarking Sketch-Text Composed Image Retrieval for Cultural Outfits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13427)
*Hoang-Nguyen Cao,Le-Hoang Bui,Dinh-Khoi Vo,Minh-Triet Tran,Trung-Nghia Le*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VietFashion 是一个面向越南传统服饰“奥黛”的草图-文本组合图像检索基准，包含650张手绘草图和通过生成模型扩展的21000+张逼真图像，采用多目标检索设置，实验表明现有模型在细粒度文化语义建模与多模态组合方面存在显著性能差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文化服装的视觉检索面临独特挑战，因为其身份识别往往依赖于微妙的结构和符号细节，而标准AI模型难以捕捉这些细节。现有检索系统缺乏针对文化服饰的细粒度基准，因此需要构建一个专注于传统服饰的草图-文本组合图像检索数据集。

Method: 构建VietFashion数据集：初始化650张手绘草图，利用生成模型扩展至21000+张照片级逼真图像并配有对齐描述；从时尚杂志提取文本提示以确保真实性和多样性；采用多目标检索设置（单查询对应多个有效结果）；建立标准化评估协议并基准测试了最先进的组合图像检索方法。

Result: 实验结果表明，现有最先进方法在建模细粒度文化语义和多模态组合方面存在显著性能差距，证实VietFashion是一个具有挑战性的细粒度时尚检索基准。

Conclusion: VietFashion填补了文化服饰细粒度检索基准的空白，揭示了现有模型在处理文化语义和多模态组合方面的不足，为未来研究提供了标准化评估平台。

Abstract: Cultural garments pose a unique challenge for visual retrieval systems, as their identity often depends on subtle structural and symbolic details that are poorly captured by standard AI models. We introduce VietFashion, a new benchmark for sketch-text composed image retrieval centered on the Ao Dai, a traditional Vietnamese garment. VietFashion enables designers and researchers to retrieve culturally meaningful outfits using a combination of hand-drawn sketches, which convey garment structure, and textual descriptions, which encode cultural semantics. The dataset is initialized with 650 sketches and expanded using generative models to produce over 21,000 photorealistic images with aligned captions. Textual prompts that describe detailed outfit attributes, which are extracted from fashion magazines to ensure authenticity and diversity. To better reflect the inherent ambiguity of design intent, VietFashion adopts a multi-target retrieval setting, where a single query may correspond to multiple valid results. We establish standardized evaluation protocols and benchmark state-of-the-art composed image retrieval methods. Experimental results reveal significant performance gaps in modeling fine-grained cultural semantics and multi-modal composition, positioning VietFashion as a challenging benchmark for fine-grained fashion retrieval. The dataset is publicly available at: https://hng0303.github.io/VietFashion.

</details>


### [124] [OmniDirector: General Multi-Shot Camera Cloning without Cross-Paired Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13432)
*Jiwen Liu,Shujuan Li,Zhixue Fang,Xiaohan Li,Yan Zhou,Zijie Meng,Zhimin Zhang,Yawen Luo,Guoxin Zhang,Yu-Shen Liu,Pengfei Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniDirector框架，通过将相机运动表示为网格运动视频（camera grid），在百万级数据集上训练，实现了对多模态扩散变换器的导演级控制，能够精准克隆参考视频中的相机运动并协调角色、动作与镜头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么使用参数化表示无法处理多镜头生成，要么合成跨配对数据面临数据稀缺问题，导致复杂相机运动克隆效果差。

Method: 提出通用相机运动表示——相机网格（camera grid），将相机参数可视化为网格运动视频；基于此构建OmniDirector统一框架，在百万级相机网格-视频对上训练；设计层次化提示扩展代理（hierarchical prompt expansion agent），系统描述相机运动与视觉内容以协调多控制信号。

Result: 大量实验表明OmniDirector框架具有优越性能和出色的可控性。

Conclusion: OmniDirector通过新颖的相机网格表示和层次化提示机制，有效解决了复杂相机运动克隆中的多镜头生成与数据稀缺问题，实现了导演级的视频生成控制。

Abstract: Cloning camera motion from reference videos is an important task in video generation, as videos provide intuitive and precise control. Existing methods either directly use parametric representations that fail to handle multi-shot generation or synthesize cross-paired data, which suffer from data scarcity, resulting in poor performance in complicated camera motion cloning. To address these issues, we introduce a general camera motion representation that encodes cameras as grid motion videos. This camera grid represents the camera parameters visually and supports the integration of diverse trajectories for multi-shot video generation. Building upon this, we propose OmniDirector, a unified framework trained on a million-scale camera grid-video pairs that coordinates characters, actions, and cameras to provide director-level control for multimodal diffusion transformers. Furthermore, we design a novel hierarchical prompt expansion agent that harmoniously integrates different control signals by systematically describing camera motion and visual content through understanding signal relationships. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superior performance and outstanding controllability of our framework. Project page: https://ymlinfeng.github.io/OmniDirector.github.io/

</details>


### [125] [MaskWAM: Unifying Mask Prompting and Prediction for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13515)
*Hanyang Yu,Haitao Lin,Jingbo Zhang,Wenyao Zhang,Chenghao Gu,Heng Li,Ping Tan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MaskWAM通过统一掩码预测的混合Transformer架构，解决了现有世界动作模型在空间歧义和语义偏差上的根本瓶颈，在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实任务中显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界动作模型存在两个关键问题：(1)标准文本输入在杂乱场景中引入指代歧义；(2)非结构化的RGB预测缺乏语义锚定，且受任务无关背景的干扰。因此需要一种对象中心化的方法来解决这些空间瓶颈。

Method: 提出MaskWAM，核心创新在于通过统一的混合Transformer（MoT）架构，将掩码同时作为显式输入和预测目标。具体地：(1)预测未来掩码提供对象中心化的语义监督，抑制视觉噪声；(2)结合首帧视觉提示（如目标对象掩码）建立精确空间锚点，减少语言歧义。

Result: 在LIBERO、RoboTwin和真实世界任务上的评估表明，MaskWAM在语言清晰和语言歧义任务上均显著优于基线方法。

Conclusion: MaskWAM验证了对象中心化的掩码预测范式能够有效克服WAMs的空间瓶颈，由于WAMs本质上是视觉驱动的架构，直接的掩码条件化比纯文本提供更强有力的引导，为操控未知物体建立了精确且鲁棒的范式。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) present a promising paradigm for robotic control via video prediction. However, current WAMs suffer from fundamental spatial bottlenecks: standard text inputs introduce referential ambiguity in cluttered scenes, while unstructured RGB predictions lack semantic grounding and remain biased by task-irrelevant backgrounds. To overcome these limitations, we introduce MaskWAM, an object-centric world-action model. By jointly integrating masks as both explicit inputs and predictions via a unified Mixture of Transformers (MoT), MaskWAM unlocks robust policy generalization. This design provides two key benefits: (1) predicting future masks yields object-centric semantic supervision that suppresses visual noise, significantly enhancing even standard text-conditioned WAMs; and (2) coupling this predictive supervision with first-frame visual prompts, such as target object masks, establishes a precise spatial anchor that substantially reduces language ambiguity. Crucially, as WAMs are inherently vision-driven architectures, direct mask conditioning yields substantially stronger guidance than text alone, establishing a precise and robust paradigm for manipulating unseen objects. Evaluations on LIBERO, RoboTwin, and real-world tasks demonstrate that MaskWAM significantly outperforms baselines in both language-clear and language-ambiguous tasks.

</details>


### [126] [What's Old is New Again: Classical Dimensionality Reduction for Efficient Saliency-Guided Biometric Attack Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13528)
*Samuel Webster,Walter Scheirer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于PCA和LDA的降维显著性图生成方法，无需人工标注或领域知识，在五种生物特征呈现攻击检测（PAD）任务中超越了基线及部分SOTA方法，解决了显著性引导训练因成本高、可扩展性差而难以推广的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有显著性引导训练方法依赖人工标注或领域特定工具，成本高、可扩展性差，限制了其在生物特征PAD领域的广泛应用。

Method: 利用经典降维技术PCA和LDA，直接从原始训练数据生成显著性图，无需人工标注或领域知识。在五个领域（虹膜PAD、合成人脸检测、指纹PAD、指纹静脉PAD、身份证PAD）验证其有效性。

Result: 基于降维显著性图训练的模型在所有测试领域均超过基线方法，部分领域达到或超越SOTA显著性方法，且无需任何资源投入或领域特定工具。

Conclusion: 该方法克服了显著性引导训练在生物特征攻击检测中推广的关键障碍，提供了一种低成本、高可扩展性的显著性获取方案。

Abstract: Saliency-guided training is a paradigm in visual recognition that encourages models to focus on the most relevant image regions during learning. While its application in biometric presentation attack detection (PAD) has shown strong benefits in robustness and generalization, adoption is often limited by the high cost, domain specificity, and limited scalability of existing saliency acquisition methods, such as human annotations over a limited dataset. We present a novel, cost-efficient, and highly-scalable approach to saliency acquisition using maps inspired by classical dimensionality reduction techniques: PCA and LDA. Our proposed methods generate saliency maps directly from raw training data, requiring no human annotation nor domain knowledge. We contextualize the effectiveness of these saliency sources in three saliency-explored domains (iris PAD, synthetic face detection, fingerprint PAD) and demonstrate its scalability in two saliency-novel domains (fingerprint vein PAD and ID card PAD).
