Across all domains tested, models trained using dimensionality reduction-sourced saliency maps exceed baseline and sometimes SOTA saliency methods without any resource investment or domain-specific tooling. Our findings overcome an important yet unaddressed barrier to saliency-guided training for biometric attack detection and beyond.

</details>


### [127] [Edit the Bits, Diff the Codes: Bitwise Residual Editing for Visual Autoregressive Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13558)
*Shengqiang Zhang,Ruotong Liao,Volker Tresp,Barbara Plank,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出BitResEdit，一种针对比特级残差VAR生成器（如Infinity）的无训练编辑方法。通过BitEdit（基于伯努利预测头的源-目标对比引导）和ResEdit（多尺度残差代码门控注入）协同工作，在PIE-Bench上以Infinity-2B实现了SOTA文本对齐效果，区域CLIP比最强基线提升+1.07，同时保持背景保真度竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VAR编辑器主要操作在token流、特征或扁平化的next-token logits上，未能充分利用比特级残差VAR模型的两个原生结构：逐比特伯努利预测头和加性多尺度残差代码场，导致编辑控制能力受限。

Method: 提出BitResEdit，包含两个组件：(1) BitEdit：通过沿共享编辑前缀计算的源-目标对比来倾斜后CFG的逐比特对数几率，实现源-负引导，并将每次更新投影到闭合形式的伯努利KL信任域内；(2) ResEdit：将采样的比特转换为逐尺度连续代码残差，用定位掩码进行门控，并通过生成器原生的尺度求和机制重新注入。

Result: 在PIE-Bench上使用Infinity-2B，BitResEdit达到同骨干网络VAR编辑器中最强的文本对齐性能，编辑区域CLIP比最强基线提升+1.07，同时背景保真度保持竞争力。

Conclusion: BitEdit和ResEdit在目标对齐和背景保真度方面发挥互补作用，通过决策时比特引导与组合时代码构成的耦合，实现了掩码区域潜变量由代码运算精确保留，同时目标区域应用局部化、尺度感知的编辑。

Abstract: Text-guided image editing with visual autoregressive (VAR) generators requires controlling both what the model samples and where the sampled change is written back into the image code. Existing VAR editors mainly operate on token streams, features, or flat next-token logits, leaving two native structures of bitwise-residual VAR models underused: the per-bit Bernoulli prediction head and the additive multi-scale residual code field from which the image is assembled. We propose BitResEdit, a training-free editor for bitwise-residual VAR generators such as Infinity. BitEdit performs source-negative guidance by tilting the post-CFG per-bit log-odds along a source--target contrast computed on a shared edited prefix, then projects each update into a closed-form Bernoulli-KL trust region around the clean CFG sampler. ResEdit converts the sampled bits into per-scale continuous-code residuals, gates them with a localization mask, and re-injects them through the generator's native sum-of-scales. Together they couple decision-time bit guidance with combination-time code composition, so masked-out latent features are preserved exactly by code arithmetic while localized, scale-aware edits are applied inside the target region. On PIE-Bench with Infinity-2B, BitResEdit attains the strongest text alignment among same-backbone VAR editors, improving CLIP on the edited region by +1.07 over the strongest prior editor while keeping background preservation competitive with it. Ablations show BitEdit and ResEdit play complementary roles in target alignment and background preservation.

</details>


### [128] [Contrast-Informed Augmentation and Domain-Adversarial Training for Adult-to-Neonatal MR Reconstruction Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13562)
*Stephen Moore,Lara Leijser,Richard Frayne,Roberto Souza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了对比度感知数据增强和领域对抗训练能否提升E2E-VarNet模型从成人到新生儿的泛化能力。实验表明，混合训练和混合领域对抗训练在新生儿数据上均优于仅用成人数据训练，其中Mixed-DAT在R=4时表现最佳（SSIM=0.924），t-SNE分析显示领域对抗训练增强了不同数据域潜在表征的重叠。结论：对比度感知增强和领域对抗训练可改善深度学习MR重建的跨域泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决深度学习MR重建模型在成人数据上训练后泛化到新生儿数据时性能下降的域偏移问题。

Method: 对比三种训练策略：(1)仅用未增强成人数据训练；(2)混合未增强成人数据和新生儿感知增强成人数据训练；(3)加入领域对抗目标进行混合训练。在回顾性欠采样的多线圈成人T2加权脑MR数据上训练，在新生儿和成人测试数据上评估R=4和R=8加速因子下的性能。

Result: Mixed和Mixed-DAT在新生儿数据上均优于Unaug-Only。R=4时Mixed-DAT最佳（SSIM=0.924±0.027, PSNR=33.98±1.15 dB）；R=8时Mixed-DAT的SSIM最优（0.848±0.031），Mixed的PSNR最优（29.56±0.83 dB）。t-SNE分析显示Mixed-DAT增加了三个域潜在表征的重叠。

Conclusion: 对比度感知数据增强结合领域对抗训练可提升深度学习MR重建模型从成人到新生儿的泛化能力，增强对域偏移的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Purpose: To investigate whether contrast-informed data augmentation and domain-adversarial training improve the adult-to-neonatal generalization of the E2E-VarNet. Methods: Three training regimes were investigated: (1) adult-only training with unaugmented adult data, (2) mixed training with paired unaugmented and neonatal-informed augmented adult data, and (3) mixed training with a domain-adversarial objective. Models were trained on retrospectively undersampled multi-coil adult T2-weighted brain MR data and evaluated on neonatal and adult test data at acceleration factors $R=4$ and $R=8$ using quantitative metrics and qualitative evaluation. Feature analyses assessed whether domain-adversarial training altered the latent representations of unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test samples. Results: Mixed training (Mixed) and mixed domain-adversarial training (Mixed-DAT) outperformed unaugmented adult-only training (Unaug-Only) when evaluated on neonatal data. At R=4, Mixed-DAT achieved the best performance (SSIM = 0.924 +/- 0.027, PSNR = 33.98 +/- 1.15 dB). At R=8, Mixed-DAT performed best when measured using SSIM (0.848 +/- 0.031 vs. 0.766 +/- 0.037 for Unaug-Only and 0.814 +/- 0.035 for Mixed) and Mixed performed best when measured using PSNR (29.56 +/- 0.83 dB vs. 26.26 +/- 0.78 dB for Unaug-Only and 29.43 +/- 0.83 dB for Mixed-DAT). Qualitative assessment of t-SNE plots suggested that Mixed-DAT increased the overlap among the latent representations of the unaugmented adult, augmented adult, and neonatal test data. Conclusion: Contrast-informed augmentation and domain-adversarial training improved adult-to-neonatal generalization of deep learning-based MR reconstruction. These findings suggest that contrast-informed data augmentation combined with adversarial training may improve robustness to domain shift in undersampled neonatal MR reconstruction.

</details>


### [129] [EvTexture++: Event-Driven Texture Enhancement for Video Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13580)
*Dachun Kai,Jiayao Lu,Yueyi Zhang,Xiaoyan Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EvTexture++，首个专为视频超分中纹理增强设计的事件驱动框架，通过迭代纹理增强模块和时序纹理对齐模块，利用事件的高频时空细节提升纹理恢复质量，并在五个数据集上达到SOTA性能，可即插即用地提升现有VSR模型，最高提升1.55dB PSNR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有事件驱动VSR工作将事件信号用于运动细化，本文则聚焦于利用事件信号增强纹理，解决VSR中纹理恢复不足以及大运动导致的纹理闪烁问题。

Method: 提出EvTexture++框架，包含：(1) 定制化纹理增强分支与迭代纹理增强模块，逐步利用高时间分辨率事件信息恢复纹理；(2) 时序纹理对齐模块，利用事件连续运动线索估计纹理感知光流，实现帧间纹理精确对齐；(3) 即插即用设计，可灵活集成到现有VSR模型。

Result: 在五个数据集上取得SOTA性能，集成到现有VSR模型后可获得显著提升，在纹理丰富的Vid4数据集上PSNR提升最高达1.55dB。

Conclusion: EvTexture++证明了事件信号在VSR纹理增强中的有效性，为事件驱动VSR提供了新的研究方向，且其即插即用特性使其具有广泛的实用价值。

Abstract: Event-based vision has drawn increasing attention owing to its distinctive properties, including ultra-high temporal resolution and extreme dynamic range. Recent works have introduced it to video super-resolution (VSR) to enhance flow estimation and temporal alignment. In contrast, this paper shifts the focus of event signals from motion refinement to texture enhancement in VSR. We propose EvTexture++, the first event-driven framework dedicated to texture enhancement in VSR. It leverages high-frequency spatiotemporal details from events to improve texture recovery. EvTexture++ incorporates a customized texture enhancement branch, along with an iterative texture enhancement module that progressively exploits high-temporal-resolution event information for texture restoration. This enables gradual refinement of texture regions across iterations, yielding more accurate and detailed high-resolution outputs. Besides intra-frame texture recovery, large motions could degrade inter-frame temporal consistency, particularly in texture regions, leading to texture flickering. To mitigate this, we further exploit the continuous-time motion cues of events to enhance temporal consistency, introducing a temporal texture alignment module that estimates event-guided texture-aware flow for precise inter-frame texture alignment. Moreover, EvTexture++ is designed as a plug-and-play tool to flexibly boost the performance of existing VSR models. Experiments on five datasets demonstrate that EvTexture++ achieves state-of-the-art performance. When integrated into recent VSR models, it yields significant improvements, with gains of up to 1.55 dB in PSNR on the texture-rich Vid4 dataset. Code: https://github.com/DachunKai/EvTexture.

</details>


### [130] [Towards Effective Waste Segmentation for Automated Waste Recycling in Cluttered Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13587)
*Mamoona Javaid,Mubashir Noman,Abdul Hannan,Shah Nawaz,Mustansar Fiaz,Sajid Ghuffar*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种级联的空域-频域双域特征提取网络，结合辅助特征增强模块，用于自动废品回收场景下的高效语义分割，在ZeroWaste和SpectralWaste数据集上取得了优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在自动废品回收中存在两个问题：一是依赖大型骨干网络导致计算效率低，不适合实际废品回收系统；二是在杂乱场景下分割性能下降。需要一种轻量且鲁棒的废品分割方法。

Method: 提出级联设计的废品分割网络，同时利用空域（捕获局部结构依赖）和频域（提取全局上下文关系）进行互补表示学习；并引入辅助特征增强模块（AFEM），增强目标物体的边界和斑点区域，提升杂乱场景下的分割效果。

Result: 在ZeroWaste-aug、ZeroWaste-f和SpectralWaste三个数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过空域-频域级联设计和AFEM模块，实现了在杂乱废品场景下高效且鲁棒的分割，为自动废品回收提供了可行的深度学习解决方案。

Abstract: Rapid expansion of urban areas and population growth is causing an immense increase in waste production, which demands the need for efficient and automated waste management. In this scenario, automated waste recycling (AWR) using deep learning methods can assist humans in optimal waste management. Recent deep learning approaches for AWR provide promising waste segmentation performance, however, these methods rely on large backbone networks that are inefficient for AWR systems and suffer from performance deterioration in cluttered scenes. To this end, an optimal waste segmentation network is introduced which effectively utilizes the spatial domain to capture localized structural dependencies and the spectral domain to efficiently extract global contextual relationships. This cascaded design allows the network to progressively leverage both local and global representations across complementary domains to highlight the semantic information necessary for effective segmentation of various waste objects. Furthermore, auxiliary feature enhancement module (AFEM) is introduced to enhance the target objects' boundaries and blob amplification for better segmentation in cluttered scenarios. Extensive experimentation on ZeroWaste-aug, ZeroWaste-f and SpectralWaste datasets reveals the merits of the proposed method.

</details>


### [131] [Revisiting Vehicle Color Recognition in Long-Tailed Surveillance Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13625)
*Vinícius Orrú,Bruno H. Foggiatto,Gabriel E. Lima,David Menotti,Rayson Laroca*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对监控场景下车辆颜色识别中严重的类别不平衡问题，提出使用两种生成式策略（文本条件图像生成和图像条件颜色编辑）进行合成少数类数据增强，结合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合等方法，在UFPR-VeSV数据集上实现了94.6%的微平均准确率和79.7%的宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率较近期文献提升8.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监控系统中车辆颜色是车辆识别的重要线索，但真实场景下车辆颜色分布高度不平衡，整体准确率无法反映稀有类别的性能，亟需针对严重类别不平衡的车辆颜色识别方法。

Method: 采用两种现成的生成式策略进行合成少数类数据增强：RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL的文本条件图像生成和Gemini 2.0 Flash的图像条件颜色编辑。结合现代视觉表征、损失重加权、学习率调度、颜色安全增强、前景感知预处理和集成融合等多种技术。

Result: 最佳方法在UFPR-VeSV数据集上达到94.6%微平均准确率和79.7%宏平均准确率，宏平均准确率较近期文献提升8.2个百分点。人工错误分析显示许多剩余失败案例对人类标注者也存在视觉模糊性。

Conclusion: 合成数据增强能有效缓解车辆颜色识别中的类别不平衡问题，但无约束监控图像中基于颜色的车辆识别存在实际性能上限，部分颜色模糊性即使是人类也难以解决。

Abstract: Vehicle color recognition is an important cue for vehicle identification in surveillance systems, especially when license plates are illegible due to low resolution, occlusion, motion blur, or poor illumination. However, real-world vehicle color distributions are highly imbalanced, making overall accuracy insufficient to assess performance on rare but operationally relevant colors. This paper presents a comprehensive study of vehicle color recognition under severe class imbalance using UFPR-VeSV, a challenging real-world surveillance dataset. We investigate synthetic minority-class augmentation through two off-the-shelf generative strategies: text-conditioned image generation with RunDiffusion/JuggernautXL and image-conditioned color editing with Gemini 2.0 Flash. The curated synthetic data are combined with modern visual representations, loss reweighting, learning-rate scheduling, color-safe augmentation, foreground-aware preprocessing, and ensemble fusion. The bestperforming approach achieves 94.6% micro accuracy and 79.7% macro accuracy, improving macro accuracy by 8.2 percentage points over recent literature. A manual error analysis further shows that many remaining failures are visually ambiguous even for human annotators, highlighting the practical limits of color-based vehicle identification in unconstrained surveillance imagery. The generated images and source code are publicly available at https://github.com/viniciusorru/vcr-synthetic

</details>


### [132] [Surflo: Consistent 3D Surface Flow Model with Global State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13644)
*Antoine Guédon,Shu Nakamura,Nicolas Dufour,Jiahui Lei,Ko Nishino,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Surflo通过将任意数量的未定位RGB视图压缩为K个潜在token（单个全局状态），并利用流匹配将噪声点独立传输到表面来解码定向3D表面点，支持从数千到数百万点的单次前向解码，通过推理时光度梯度引导抑制局部不一致性，在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线，速度比优化方法快一个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈重建模型存在两个局限：逐视图方法输出重叠、未对齐的点云且随输入线性增长；全局潜在方法只能输出固定低分辨率结果。需要一种既能利用几何视点不变性，又能支持任意分辨率解码的全局表示方法。

Method: 1) 将可变数量未定位RGB视图压缩为K个潜在token的全局状态；2) 通过流匹配将噪声点独立传输到3D表面，实现从数千到百万量级点的任意分辨率解码；3) 引入推理时光度梯度引导项，在ODE积分过程中注入光度梯度以关联相邻点，抑制独立逐点解码的局部不一致性。

Result: 在表面指标上匹配或超越前馈基线方法，运行速度比需要数百视图的优化方法快一个数量级，是唯一结合全局潜在与任意分辨率解码的前馈方法。

Conclusion: Surflo成功实现了全局潜在表示与任意分辨率解码的统一，克服了前馈重建方法在可扩展性和一致性之间的权衡，为高效3D重建提供了新范式。

Abstract: Geometry is invariant to viewpoint, which makes any collection of images a redundant encoding of a single 3D state. Existing feed-forward reconstruction models fail to exploit this: per-view methods emit overlapping, unaligned pointmaps that grow linearly with input count, while global-latent methods commit to a fixed, low-resolution output. We introduce Surflo, which compresses a variable number of unposed RGB views into K latent tokens-one global state-and decodes oriented 3D surface points by independently transporting them from noise onto the surface via flow matching. This frees the output from any fixed grid or token budget: the same latent yields from a few thousand to a million points in a single forward pass. To suppress the local inconsistencies inherent to independent per-point decoding, an inference-time guidance term correlates nearby points by injecting a photometric gradient during ODE integration. Surflo matches or surpasses feed-forward baselines on surface metrics, runs an order of magnitude faster than optimization-based methods that require hundreds of views, and is the only feed-forward approach to combine a global latent with arbitrary-resolution decoding.

</details>


### [133] [World Tracing: Generative Pixel-Aligned Geometry Beyond the Visible](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13652)
*Hao Zhang,Mohamed El Banani,Jen-Hao Cheng,Paul Zhang,Yi Hua,Ben Mildenhall,Christoph Lassner,Narendra Ahuja,Gengshan Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出World Tracing，一种生成式像素对齐几何表示方法，通过为每个输入像素预测有序的3D点堆栈（可见表面层+遮挡表面层），并基于世界追踪扩散变压器（WT-DiT）实现，在可见表面重建和完整几何生成上均优于现有深度估计器和图像转3D模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像转3D方法在保真度与完整性之间存在权衡：深度估计器忠实于输入像素但仅能重建可见表面，而图像转3D模型能生成完整形状却常与输入图像错位。本文旨在同时实现像素对齐的可见表面重建与合理的遮挡几何补全。

Method: 提出World Tracing表征，为每个输入像素预测一个有序的相机空间3D点堆栈，第一层为可见表面，后续层为前到后的遮挡表面交点。实例化为WT-DiT（世界追踪扩散变压器），将多层几何作为独立去噪token，通过分解注意力和全局注意力耦合。采用像素空间流匹配和混合噪声调度训练，平衡可见表面重建与遮挡几何生成。

Result: 在物体、场景和动态基准上，World Tracing在可见表面重建和完整几何生成上均优于深度预测器和图像转3D生成器，并保持2D到3D对应关系，支持文本驱动的3D场景编辑、几何条件的新颖视角视频合成以及无训练集成到纹理网格生成器。

Conclusion: World Tracing通过像素级多层几何预测统一了可见表面重建与遮挡几何补全，在多个任务上取得最优性能，且具有良好的可扩展性和下游应用兼容性。

Abstract: Image-to-3D methods often trade off faithfulness and completeness: depth estimators are anchored to input pixels but stop at the visible surface, while image-to-3D models generate complete shapes that are often misaligned with the input. We introduce World Tracing, a generative pixel-aligned geometry representation that predicts 3D points aligned with observed pixels while completing geometry beyond the visible surface. For each input pixel, World Tracing predicts an ordered stack of camera-space 3D points, where the first layer represents the visible surface and subsequent layers represent front-to-back intersections with occluded surfaces. We instantiate this representation with a world-tracing diffusion transformer, WT-DiT, which treats multiple geometry layers as separate denoising tokens coupled through factorized and global attention. WT-DiT is trained with pixel-space flow matching and a mixed noise schedule that balances visible-surface reconstruction with occluded-geometry generation. World Tracing achieves strong performance on visible-surface reconstruction and complete geometry generation across object, scene, and dynamic benchmarks, outperforming both depth predictors and image-to-3D generators. It also preserves 2D-to-3D correspondence, enabling text-driven 3D scene editing, geometry-conditioned novel-view video synthesis, and training-free integration with textured-mesh generators.

</details>


### [134] [Flex4DHuman: Flexible Multi-view Video Diffusion for 4D Human Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13655)
*Jen-Hao Cheng,Yipeng Wang,Hao Zhang,Gengshan Yang,Jenq-Neng Hwang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Flex4DHuman是一个多视图视频扩散模型，能将单目或稀疏多视图视频转换为同步密集多视图视频，仅需相对相机姿态条件，无需显式几何先验，并可直接用于下游4D高斯泼溅重建。在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ上超越先前SOTA，且可泛化到动物类别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从单目视频创建动态4D内容的方法通常依赖骨架、深度图、法线或渲染目标视图几何等显式几何先验，限制了灵活性和可扩展性。本文旨在提出一种无需显式几何先验、仅需相对相机姿态条件的多视图视频扩散模型，实现从日常单目视频到4D内容的便捷创作。

Method: 基于Wan 2.1 1.3B文本到视频模型，通过五轴位置编码（扩展时空RoPE，加入视图索引和连续SE(3)相对相机几何）编码相机和视图信息。采用三阶段渐进训练：姿态跟随、灵活参考到目标视图生成、时间展开。训练中使用干净的历史目标视图token，并添加多视图字幕以支持测试时文本控制。

Result: 在DNA-Rendering和ActorsHQ数据集上超越先前最先进方法；同一公式在混合人类-动物训练后可泛化到动物类别。

Conclusion: Flex4DHuman为模拟、游戏、AR/VR和视频重拍等应用提供了从日常单目视频到可扩展4D内容创作的实用路径。

Abstract: We present Flex4DHuman, a multi-view video diffusion model that transforms a monocular or sparse multi-view video of a dynamic subject into synchronized dense multi-view videos using only relative camera-pose conditioning. Unlike prior human-centric methods that rely on skeletons, depth maps, normals, or rendered target-view geometry, Flex4DHuman requires no explicit geometry priors and instead conditions generation through relative camera-pose positional encoding. The generated videos can be directly ingested by downstream reconstruction pipelines to create dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Built on the Wan 2.1 1.3B text-to-video model, Flex4DHuman preserves the backbone architecture and encodes camera and view information through a five-axis positional encoding that extends spatio-temporal RoPE with view indices and continuous SE(3) relative camera geometry. A three-stage curriculum progressively trains the model for pose following, flexible reference-to-target view generation, and temporal rollout. To support temporal rollout, we train with clean historical target-view tokens. We also add multi-view captions to enable test-time text control. Combined with an off-the-shelf 4D Gaussian Splatting stage, our framework lifts monocular static-camera videos into dynamic 4D Gaussian splats. Experiments on DNA-Rendering and ActorsHQ show that Flex4DHuman surpasses prior state-of-the-art methods, while the same formulation generalizes to animal categories after mixed human-animal training. These capabilities make Flex4DHuman a practical step toward scalable 4D content creation from casual monocular videos for simulation, gaming, AR/VR, and video re-shooting.

</details>


### [135] [SpatialClaw: Rethinking Action Interface for Agentic Spatial Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13673)
*Seokju Cho,Ryo Hachiuma,Abhishek Badki,Hang Su,Byung-Kwan Lee,Chan Hee Song,Sifei Liu,Subhashree Radhakrishnan,Seungryong Kim,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Min-Hung Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SpatialClaw提出一种无需训练的代码驱动空间推理框架，通过维护带状态的内核和感知几何原语，让VLM逐步执行代码单元、灵活组合感知结果，在20个3D/4D空间推理基准上平均准确率59.9%，比现有空间智能体高出11.2个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有空间智能体要么采用单次代码执行（提交完整分析策略后才看到中间结果），要么依赖结构化工具调用接口（缺乏灵活组合和任务定制能力），两者都难以应对开放、复杂的3D/4D空间推理任务。

Method: SpatialClaw以代码作为行动接口，维护一个有状态的Python内核，预先加载输入帧和一组感知与几何原语。VLM驱动的智能体每一步基于所有先前输出编写一个可执行单元，从而灵活组合和操作感知结果，根据中间文本/视觉观察和问题需求动态调整分析策略。

Result: 在20个空间推理基准上，SpatialClaw平均准确率59.9%，比最新空间智能体高11.2个百分点，在6种VLM骨干（两个模型家族）上均有一致提升，无需任何基准或模型特化适配。

Conclusion: 采用代码作为行动接口、支持逐步执行的框架能有效提升VLM在开放场景下的空间推理能力。

Abstract: Spatial reasoning, the ability to determine where objects are, how they relate, and how they move in 3D, remains a fundamental challenge for vision-language models (VLMs). Tool-augmented agents attempt to address this by augmenting VLMs with specialist perception modules, yet their effectiveness is bounded by the action interface through which those tools are invoked. In this work, we study how the design of this interface shapes the agent's capacity for open-ended spatial reasoning. Existing spatial agents either employ single-pass code execution, which commits to a full analysis strategy before any intermediate result is observed, or rely on a structured tool-call interface that often offers less flexibility for freely composing operations or tailoring the analysis to each task. Both designs offer limited flexibility for open-ended, complex 3D/4D spatial reasoning. We therefore propose SpatialClaw, a training-free framework for spatial reasoning that adopts code as the action interface. SpatialClaw maintains a stateful Python kernel pre-loaded with input frames and a suite of perception and geometry primitives, letting a VLM-backed agent write one executable cell per step conditioned on all prior outputs, enabling the agent to flexibly compose and manipulate perception results and adapt its analysis to both intermediate text and visual observations and the demands of each problem. Evaluated across 20 spatial reasoning benchmarks spanning a broad range of static and dynamic 3D/4D spatial reasoning tasks, SpatialClaw achieves 59.9% average accuracy, outperforming the recent spatial agent by +11.2 points, with consistent gains across six VLM backbones from two model families without any benchmark- or model-specific adaptation.

</details>


### [136] [RepWAM: World Action Modeling with Representation Visual-Action Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13674)
*Junke Wang,Qihang Zhang,Shuai Yang,Yiming Luo,Yujun Shen,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang,Yinghao Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RepWAM提出了一种基于表征视觉-动作分词器的世界动作模型，通过语义化的视觉-动作潜在空间替代传统重建导向的视频分词器，实现更有效的指令跟随动态建模与机器人操控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）通常继承自预训练视频生成模型的重建导向分词器，虽然能保持视觉保真度，但像素重建本身对学习连接未来预测与机器人控制的指令跟随动态提供的引导有限。

Method: 1) 训练表征视觉-动作分词器，将视觉输入映射为对齐的视觉和潜在动作标记；2) 在语言指令条件下联合建模未来视觉状态及其间的潜在动作，完成世界动作模型预训练；3) 适配真实机器人轨迹进行闭环操控。

Result: 在真实世界操控任务和仿真基准上，RepWAM在多种操控场景中展现了强性能；消融实验证明了语义化视觉-动作分词相比重建导向替代方案的优越性。

Conclusion: 表征视觉-动作分词可作为世界动作模型的有前景的基础，是迈向通用机器人策略的重要一步。

Abstract: This work presents RepWAM, a representation-centric world action model (WAM) built on representation visual-action tokenizers. Existing WAMs typically inherit reconstruction-oriented video tokenizers from pretrained video generation models. Although these tokenizers preserve visual fidelity, pixel reconstruction alone provides limited guidance for learning instruction-following dynamics that connect future prediction with robot control. To address this, we explore a semantic visual-action latent space for representation-centric world action modeling. Specifically, we train a representation visual-action tokenizer that maps visual inputs into aligned visual and latent action tokens. We then pretrain our WAM to jointly model future visual states and the latent actions that connect them under language instructions, followed by adaptation to real robot trajectories for closed-loop manipulation. Experiments on real-world manipulation tasks and simulation benchmarks show that RepWAM delivers strong performance across diverse manipulation settings, while ablations highlight the value of semantic visual-action tokenization over reconstruction-oriented alternatives. These results establish representation visual-action tokenization as a promising foundation for world action models and a step toward generalist robot policies. Code and weights will be available at https://github.com/wdrink/RepWAM.

</details>


### [137] [Modality Forcing for Scalable Spatial Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13676)
*Bardienus Pieter Duisterhof,Deva Ramanan,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Justin Johnson,Keunhong Park*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 Modality Forcing 方法，利用预训练的文本到图像（T2I）扩散模型，通过为不同模态分配独立噪声水平，实现图像与深度图的联合生成与条件生成。该方法仅需稀疏深度数据训练，在单目深度估计上达到SOTA，且随模型规模扩大性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽利用T2I模型进行深度预测，但需要密集深度数据和复杂的训练流程。本文旨在提出一种简单、可扩展的后训练方案，利用稀疏深度数据实现高效的图像-深度联合生成。

Method: 提出 Modality Forcing 方法：为图像和深度模态分配独立的噪声水平，使单个 DiT（Diffusion Transformer）能够在任意排列下进行条件生成或联合生成；使用逐模态解码器，支持在稀疏真实世界深度数据上训练。

Result: 训练了从370M到3.3B参数的T2I模型，发现更大模型和更多图像数据产生更精确的深度估计。最强模型在单目深度估计上与SOTA方法竞争力强，相比现有联合图像-深度生成模型，AbsRel降低了57%。

Conclusion: 图像生成可作为空间感知任务的可扩展预训练目标，Modality Forcing 方法简单有效，继承了T2I预训练的可扩展性优势。

Abstract: Text-to-image (T2I) models contain rich spatial priors. Synthesizing photorealistic, cluttered scenes requires an understanding of geometry, including perspective and relative scale. Prior works adapt T2I models to leverage this prior for depth prediction, but they require dense depth data and involve complex recipes. We propose Modality Forcing, a simple, scalable post-training recipe for joint image-depth generation using a single DiT trained on sparse depth data. Modality Forcing enables conditional and joint generation of image and depth in any permutation by assigning separate noise levels per modality. Per-modality decoders let us train on sparse, real-world depth and achieve strong, generalizable depth prediction. We further show that Modality Forcing inherits the scalability of T2I pre-training: by training a set of T2I models from scratch (370M to 3.3B parameters), we find that larger models trained on more image data produce more accurate depth. Our strongest model is competitive with state-of-the-art monocular depth estimators and reduces AbsRel by 57% relative to existing joint image-depth generative models. These results provide strong evidence that image generation is a scalable pre-training objective for spatial perception. https://modality-forcing.github.io/

</details>


### [138] [InterleaveThinker: Reinforcing Agentic Interleaved Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13679)
*Dian Zheng,Harry Lee,Manyuan Zhang,Kaituo Feng,Zoey Guo,Ray Zhang,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: InterleaveThinker是首个多智能体管线，通过规划器与评论家智能体赋予任意图像生成器交错生成能力，采用GRPO强化学习和单步奖励策略优化，在交错生成和推理基准上均取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成器在单图生成和编辑上表现优异，但由于架构限制无法实现文本-图像序列的交错生成，而交错生成在视觉叙事、指导和具身操作等关键应用中至关重要；即使最新的统一多模态模型（UMMs）在此方面表现也有限。

Method: 提出多智能体管线InterleaveThinker：1) 规划器智能体(Planner Agent)组织图像-文本输入序列，指导图像生成器每步执行内容；2) 评论家智能体(Critic Agent)评估生成输出，识别偏离指令的样本并优化指令进行重新生成。构建Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k和Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k进行格式冷启动，再开发Interleave-Critic-RL-13k使用GRPO强化单步指令修正能力。提出精度奖励(accuracy reward)和单步奖励(step-wise reward)实现单步RL有效引导整个生成轨迹。

Result: 在交错生成基准上达到与Nano Banana和GPT-5相当的性能；在推理基准上显著提升基础模型，例如在4-step FLUX.2-klein上，WISE和RISE指标均有大幅提升。

Conclusion: InterleaveThinker有效赋能任意图像生成器实现交错生成能力，且能反向提升基础模型在推理类基准上的表现，验证了多智能体管线+强化学习策略在跨模态序列生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Recent image generators have demonstrated impressive photorealism and instruction-following capabilities in single-image generation and editing. However, constrained by their architectures, they cannot achieve interleaved generation (text-image sequence), which has crucial applications in visual narratives, guidance, and embodied manipulation. Even the latest open-source Unified Multimodal Models (UMMs) exhibit limited performance in this regard. In this paper, we introduce InterleaveThinker, the first multi-agent pipeline designed to endow any existing image generator with interleaved generation capabilities. Specifically, we employ a planner agent to organize the image-text input sequence, instructing the image generator on the required execution at each step. Subsequently, we introduce a critic agent to evaluate the generator's outputs, identify samples that deviate from the planned instructions, and refine the instructions for regeneration. To implement this pipeline, we construct the Interleave-Planner-SFT-80k and Interleave-Critic-SFT-112k to perform a format cold-start. Then we develop Interleave-Critic-RL-13k to reinforce the step-wise instruction correction capability within a generation trajectory using GRPO. Since a single interleaved generation trajectory may involve over 25 generator calls, optimizing the entire trajectory is computationally impractical. Therefore, we propose accuracy reward and step-wise reward, allowing single-step RL to effectively guide the entire generation trajectory. The results show that InterleaveThinker improves performance across various image generators. On interleaved generation benchmarks, it achieves performance comparable to Nano Banana and GPT-5. Surprisingly, it also significantly enhances the base model on reasoning-based benchmarks; for example, on 4-step FLUX.2-klein, we observe substantial gains on WISE and RISE.

</details>


### [139] [Transformer-Guided Graph Attention for Direct Cardiac Mesh Reconstruction: A Structural Digital Twin Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13188)
*Abhishek H S,Akash Ganamukhi,Abhimanyu Suresh,Aditya G Hiremath,Prasad B Honnavalli,Adithya Balasubramanyam*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种端到端网络，直接从3D医学图像生成平滑、可用于仿真的心脏表面网格，无需传统分割、Marching Cubes和手动清理步骤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统心脏建模流程中，网格生成环节耗时、混乱且依赖操作者经验，成为阻碍患者特异性心脏模型进入临床的主要瓶颈。

Method: 采用3D Swin Transformer编码器-解码器提取体素特征，结合图注意力网络（GAT）头部，通过迭代变形模板网格以拟合患者心脏边界，实现从原始CT/MRI图像到仿真就绪网格的单次前向推理。

Result: 在MM-WHS 2017基准上，CT和MRI的Dice分别为0.84和0.83，平均Chamfer距离1.8 mm，95%表面距离低于5 mm。

Conclusion: 该方法通过消除后处理瓶颈，使患者特异性心脏仿真在几何保真度和拓扑正确性上更满足临床需求，显著提升了临床可及性。

Abstract: Building patient-specific cardiac models sits at the heart of precision cardiology, yet getting those models into clinical use keeps running into the same wall: mesh generation is slow, messy, and frustrating. The standard workflow -- segmenting the image, running Marching Cubes, and then manually cleaning up the result -- is time-consuming, inconsistent across operators, and demands specialist knowledge most clinical teams do not have. We take a fundamentally different approach. Instead of treating segmentation and mesh generation as two separate problems, we train a single end-to-end network that goes directly from a raw 3D medical image to a smooth, simulation-ready cardiac surface mesh. The core is a 3D Swin Transformer encoder-decoder that extracts volumetric features from CT or MRI volumes, paired with a Graph Attention Network (GAT) head that iteratively deforms a template mesh to fit the patient's cardiac boundary. We tested on the MM-WHS 2017 benchmark using both CT and MRI. Segmentation scores were competitive (Dice of 0.84 on CT, 0.83 on MRI), but the primary focus is mesh quality: mean Chamfer distance of 1.8 mm, with 95th-percentile surface distance below 5 mm. Every mesh is produced in a single forward pass -- no Marching Cubes, no smoothing filters, no manual cleanup. We argue that for cardiac digital twin pipelines, geometric fidelity and topological correctness matter more than pixel-level Dice scores. By removing the post-processing bottleneck, this approach makes patient-specific cardiac simulation substantially more accessible for clinical use.

</details>


### [140] [Cross-Modal Masked Compositional Concept Modeling for Enhancing Visio-Linguistic Compositionality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13288)
*Wei Li,Zhen Huang,Xinmei Tian*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MACCO通过跨模态掩码重建和辅助对齐目标，有效提升了视觉-语言模型（如CLIP）的组合理解能力，在五个组合基准及下游任务（文本到图像生成、多模态大语言模型）上均取得显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP等对比训练模型存在"词袋"行为，难以捕捉对象关系、属性-对象绑定和词序依赖等组合结构，原因在于全局单向量表示的局限以及未能充分利用图文对中固有的丰富组合信息。

Method: 提出MACCO框架，在一种模态中掩码组合概念，利用另一模态的完整上下文信息进行重建；同时引入两个辅助目标，分别在模态间和模态内对齐和正则化掩码特征。

Result: 在五个组合基准上显著提升组合性，并增强模型对句法结构和语言信息的捕捉能力；改进的组合能力进而惠及文本到图像生成和多模态大语言模型。

Conclusion: 跨模态掩码重建与多层级对齐正则化是提升视觉-语言模型组合理解的有效范式，且提升具有跨任务泛化性。

Abstract: Contrastively trained vision-language models like CLIP, have made remarkable progress in learning joint image-text representations, but still face challenges in compositional understanding. They often exhibit a "bag-of-words" behavior--struggling to capture the object relations, attribute-object bindings, and word order dependencies. This limitation arises not only from the reliance on global, single-vector representations for optimization, but also from the insufficient exploitation and modeling of the rich compositional information inherently present in paired image text data. In this work, we propose MACCO (MAsked Compositional Concept MOdeling), a framework that masks compositional concepts in one modality and reconstructs them conditioned on the full contextual information from the other, enabling the model to capture and align cross-modal compositional structures more effectively. To facilitate this process, we introduce two auxiliary objectives that jointly align and regularize masked features both inter-modally and intra-modally. Extensive experiments on five compositional benchmarks, along with in-depth analyses, demonstrate that our approach not only significantly enhances compositionality in VLMs but also improves their ability to capture syntactic structure and linguistic information. Additionally, the improved compositionality also benefits text-to-image generation and multimodal large language model. Code is available at https://github.com/hiker-lw/MACCO.

</details>


### [141] [HYDRA-X: Native Unified Multimodal Models with Holistic Visual Tokenizers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13289)
*Guozhen Zhang,Xuerui Qiu,Yutao Cui,Tianhui Song,Changlin Li,Junzhe Li,Tao Huang,Xiao Zhang,Yang Li,Jianbing Wu,Miles Yang,Zhao Zhong,Liefeng Bo,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HYDRA-X是首个统一图像与视频分词的统一多模态模型，通过在单个ViT中实现高效时空重建和语义感知，并在7B规模上取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有统一多模态模型缺乏能够同时处理图像和视频的单一视觉分词器，需要解决如何高效注入时空重建能力以及如何将图像/视频级语义信息嵌入潜在空间这两个核心挑战。

Method: (1) 帧级因果时序注意力优于全时空注意力，分层时间压缩优于单步压缩；(2) 轻量化解压缩器在联合图像-视频教师监督下对压缩特征进行上采样；(3) 源-目标交互在分词器潜在层面而非LLM语义层面进行，提升编辑一致性并加速收敛。

Result: 在7B密集模型上，HYDRA-X在图像和视频的理解与生成任务上均取得了强劲性能。

Conclusion: HYDRA-X证明了统一视觉分词器路线的可行性，为未来统一分词器的多模态模型奠定了基础。

Abstract: Holistic visual tokenizers are fundamental to unified multimodal models (UMMs) as they map diverse visual inputs into a unified representation space. In this paper, we present HYDRA-X, the first UMM that unifies image and video tokenization within a single Vision Transformer (ViT). Our design is driven by two core challenges: efficiently injecting spatiotemporal reconstruction capability into a native ViT, and embedding image- and video-level semantic awareness into the latent space. To address the first, comprehensive ablations reveal two key findings: (1) frame-level causal temporal attention suffices for visual reconstruction, whereas full spatiotemporal attention degrades it; and (2) hierarchical temporal compression substantially outperforms single-step alternatives. To tackle the second, we propose a lightweight decompressor that upsamples temporally compressed features under joint image-video teacher supervision, thereby enforcing complementary semantic structures within the compact latent space. Building on this holistic tokenizer, we further propose a principled improvement of the editing pipeline: source-target interaction should occur at the latent level inside the tokenizer rather than at the semantic level inside the LLM, substantially improving editing consistency and accelerating convergence. Instantiated at the 7B dense model, HYDRA-X achieves strong performance across image and video understanding and generation tasks, paving the way for future unified-tokenizer UMMs.

</details>


### [142] [Heterogeneous LiDAR Early Fusion and Learned Re-Ranking Strategy for Robust Long-Term Place Recognition in Unstructured Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13503)
*Judith Vilella-Cantos,Juan José Cabrera,Mónica Ballesta,David Valiente,Luis Payá*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++，一种融合异构LiDAR数据（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16）并采用学习型重排序策略的地点识别方法，在葡萄园等非结构化环境中相比单传感器方法Recall@1提升20%，加入重排序后提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 农业等非结构化环境中的鲁棒定位是自主系统的关键挑战，现有LiDAR地点识别方法在重复性环境（如葡萄园）中难以有效区分真阳性样本。

Method: 提出MinkUNeXt-VINE++方法，包含两个核心组件：(1) 早期融合异构LiDAR数据（Livox Mid-360和Velodyne VLP-16），利用两种传感器的互补优势；(2) 推理阶段的学习型重排序策略，提升在重复环境中识别真阳性的能力。

Result: 在TEMPO-VINE数据集上，MinkUNeXt-VINE++相比单传感器方法Recall@1提升20%，加入重排序后提升30%，并超越了现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: 异构LiDAR数据早期融合结合学习型重排序策略能显著提升非结构化重复环境中的地点识别性能，且代码已开源可复现。

Abstract: Robust localization in unstructured environments, such as agricultural fields, is a critical challenge for autonomous systems. LiDAR sensors provide detailed 3D information about the environment and are invariant to lighting conditions. For this reason, LiDAR-based place recognition methods have gained significant attention. In this paper, we propose MinkUNeXt-VINE++, a novel approach that combines early fusion of heterogeneous LiDAR data from two sensors (Livox Mid-360 and Velodyne VLP-16) and a learned re-ranking strategy in inference time. This fusion leverages the strengths of each sensor to provide a more comprehensive representation of the environment. Additionally, the re-ranking approach is particularly important in repetitive environments, such as vineyards, as finding true positives is a major challenge. We evaluated our approach using the TEMPO-VINE dataset, which provides heterogeneous LiDAR data in vineyard environments across different phenological stages. Our results demonstrate that MinkUNeXt-VINE++ significantly improves place recognition performance compared to single-sensor approaches and state-of-the-art methods. MinkUNeXt-VINE++ achieves a 20% improvement in the Recall@1 metric compared to single-sensor approaches, and +30% including re-ranking. The code of our method is publicly available for reproduction.

</details>


### [143] [Measurement-Calibrated Multi-Camera Fusion for Vision-Based Indoor Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13509)
*Mateo Toro Diz,Jonathan Hoss,Noah Klarmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种测量校准的多摄像头数据融合方法，通过显式量化单摄像头定位误差（包括单应性校准、人体检测和运动跟踪），来优化室内视觉定位系统的融合策略。实验表明，该方法在绝对精度上提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有室内视觉定位系统中的多摄像头数据融合通常被当作黑箱组件进行端到端评估，缺乏对其机制性贡献的深入理解。本文旨在探究显式表征单摄像头定位误差是否可用于校准和优化多摄像头数据融合。

Method: 提出测量校准融合方法（measurement-calibrated fusion），在融合过程中集成组件级误差量化，分别隔离并量化单应性校准误差、人体检测误差和运动跟踪误差，并进行组件级评估以分析各误差源的贡献。

Result: 数据融合相比单摄像头基线提高了定位精度；测量校准融合相比标准融合在绝对精度上提升有限，但显著降低了轨迹方差并改善了运动平滑度。

Conclusion: 显式误差表征在基于视觉的室内定位系统数据融合策略设计中具有重要价值，尤其对于需要稳定连续运动估计的应用场景（如轨迹平滑性要求高的任务），误差量化带来的收益比绝对精度提升更为关键。

Abstract: Indoor vision-based localization systems are affected by detection noise, occlusions, and limited camera coverage, leading to uncertainty at multiple stages of the pipeline. While multi-camera data fusion is widely used to mitigate these issues, it is typically treated as a black-box component and evaluated solely end-to-end, obscuring its mechanistic contributions. To address this gap, this work investigates whether explicitly characterizing single-camera localization errors can be leveraged to calibrate and optimize multi-camera data fusion.
  We introduce a measurement-calibrated fusion approach that integrates component-wise error quantification, specifically isolating homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking. A component-wise evaluation is conducted to quantify error contributions from homography calibration, human detection, and motion tracking.
  Experimental results show that data fusion improves localization accuracy compared to single-camera baselines. While measurement-calibrated fusion provides only limited improvement in absolute accuracy over standard fusion, it substantially reduces trajectory variance and improves motion smoothness, which are critical for applications requiring stable and continuous motion estimates. These results highlight the value of explicit error characterization when designing data fusion strategies for vision-based indoor positioning systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Influence Factors on RAG Poisoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12469)
*Pedro Pereira,Eva Maia,Isabel Praça,Adrien Bécue*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文通过432种配置的全因子实验，系统研究了检索增强生成（RAG）系统对投毒攻击的鲁棒性，发现检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素，而生成器选择和数据库组成对下游攻击成功率影响最大。投毒漏洞源于检索、生成和知识库配置的交互作用，而非单一组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在推理时依赖外部检索文档来增强大语言模型，但这种对外部内容的依赖引入了投毒攻击的漏洞，即对抗性文档可以操纵检索过程和生成输出。目前缺乏对RAG系统投毒鲁棒性的系统性研究。

Method: 采用全因子实验设计，涵盖432种配置，系统分析了数据集、检索器类型、检索深度、数据库组成、分块策略和生成器模型对检索级和生成级指标的影响。

Result: 检索器架构、数据集和检索深度是影响投毒暴露的最强因素；生成器选择和数据库组成对下游攻击成功率影响最大；密集检索器和基于图的检索器通常比BM25更鲁棒；更大的检索深度增加检索到投毒片段的概率；跨多个数据库复制投毒内容会放大对抗性影响，而增加清洁源可以缓解这种影响。

Conclusion: RAG系统的投毒漏洞不能归因于单一组件，而是检索、生成和知识库配置之间交互作用的结果。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems enhance large language models by grounding responses in retrieved documents from external knowledge sources at inference time. However, this reliance on retrieved content introduces vulnerabilities to poisoning attacks, in which adversarial documents can manipulate both the retrieval process and the generated outputs. This paper investigates poisoning robustness in RAG through a full factorial experimental study covering 432 configurations. We analyze the impacts of dataset, retriever type, retrieval depth, database composition, chunking strategy, and generator model on retrieval-level and generation-level metrics. The results show that retriever architecture, dataset, and retrieval depth are the strongest factors affecting poisoning exposure, while generator choice and database composition have a major impact on downstream attack success. Dense and graph-based retrievers generally improve robustness relative to BM25, whereas larger retrieval depth increases the likelihood of retrieving poisoned passages. We further show that replicating poisoned content across multiple databases amplifies adversarial influence, while additional clean sources can mitigate it. These findings highlight that poisoning vulnerability in RAG is not attributable to a single component, but instead arises from the interaction of retrieval, generation, and knowledge-base configuration.

</details>


### [145] [From Parameters to Feature Space: Task Arithmetic for Backdoor Mitigation in Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12498)
*Zhenqian Zhu,Yamin Hu,Yiya Diao,Weixiang Li,Haodong Li,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出线性特征路径最小化（LFPM）框架，通过特征空间视角下的反后门任务向量优化，在保持干净任务性能的同时有效消除模型合并中的后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型合并易受后门攻击，而基于任务算术的防御方法在消除后门时会显著降低干净任务性能，亟需一种既能有效防御后门又不损害性能的方法。

Method: 基于跨任务线性（CTL）框架，从统一特征空间视角引入反后门任务向量到被后门感染的合并模型中，并通过梯度累积和损失路径积分机制实现沿插值路径的鲁棒后门抑制。

Result: 在全微调和参数高效微调（PEFT）设置下，LFPM均展现出对后门攻击的强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LFPM通过特征空间视角和路径积分优化，有效解决了模型合并中后门防御与性能保持之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Model merging (MM) has gained significant attention as a cost-effective approach to integrate multiple task-specific models into a unified model. However, recent work reveals that MM is highly susceptible to backdoor attacks. Existing defenses based on task arithmetic often fail to eliminate backdoors without substantially degrading clean-task performance, owing to their reliance on direct parameter-space editing. To address this gap, we propose Linear Feature Path Minimization (LFPM), a backdoor mitigation framework for model merging, which introduces an anti-backdoor task vector into the backdoored merged model. Unlike prior approaches, LFPM formulates the backdoor robustness of the merged model from a unified feature-space perspective under the Cross-Task Linearity (CTL) framework, which leverages the approximate linearity of features across tasks. This perspective guides the optimization of the anti-backdoor task to suppress backdoors while preserving clean-task performance. Furthermore, we introduce an effective optimization mechanism based on gradient accumulation and loss path-integral, ensuring robust backdoor suppression along the interpolation path. Extensive experiments demonstrate that LFPM consistently exhibits strong robustness against backdoor attacks in both full fine-tuning and Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) settings.

</details>


### [146] [Beyond Attack Success Rate: Examining Trigger Leakage in Vision-Language Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12586)
*Jiamin Chang,Salil Kanhere,Piotr Koniusz,Jason,Xue,Hammond Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文形式化定义了后门攻击中的"触发器泄漏"问题，并提出邻域泄漏率(NLR)指标来量化该问题。实验表明，标准微调会学习宽激活区域而非精确触发条件，导致视觉或语义上接近的输入也会意外激活隐藏行为。通过添加编辑距离为1的难负样本可以显著缩小激活区域并减少泄漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉后门评估仅关注干净准确率和攻击成功率(ASR)，这些指标只能衡量触发器是否有效，但无法评估攻击是否"精确"——即是否仅在意图触发时才会激活隐藏行为。实际中，视觉语言智能体系统(VLAS)中后门触发器可通过决策管道和连接接口传播，构成系统性威胁，因此需要更精确的评估指标。

Method: 1) 形式化定义"触发器泄漏"问题，提出邻域泄漏率(NLR)指标；2) 在3%投毒率下测试图标和文本触发器对常见视觉变换的鲁棒性及其邻域变体的泄漏情况；3) 使用文本触发器作为受控探针，分析标准微调学习到的激活区域特性；4) 提出添加编辑距离为1的难负样本训练策略来缩小激活区域。

Result: 图标和文本触发器的邻域变体泄漏严重，NLR分别达到0.996和0.944。标准微调学习到的是宽激活区域而非精确触发条件。添加编辑距离为1的难负样本能显著缩小激活区域并减少泄漏，在图像编辑和具身操作工作流中，泄漏触发器可能传播到可执行程序和动作序列中。

Conclusion: 现有后门评估指标存在盲区，触发器泄漏是系统性安全威胁。NLR可作为补充指标评估攻击精确性。通过难负样本训练可以有效缓解泄漏问题，提高后门攻击的精确性。

Abstract: Vision-Language Agentic Systems (VLAS) connect visual perception to planning, tool use, and physical actions. This means backdoor-type triggers can propagate through both decision pipelines and their connected interfaces, thus making visual backdoors a system-level threat. Current evaluations on such backdoors focus on clean accuracy and attack success rate (ASR), metrics that capture whether a trigger works, but not whether an attack is actually "precise" -- i.e. whether it triggers hidden behaviors only when intended. In this work, we formalize the failure of trigger precision as "trigger leakage": inputs that are visually or semantically close to the intended trigger and therefore inadvertently activate the attacker-specified behavior. To quantify this leakage, we introduce Neighbor Leakage Rate (NLR). Our experiments show that at a 3% poisoning ratio, icon and text triggers remain robust to common visual transformations, but their neighboring variants leak heavily, with NLR reaching 0.996 (icon) and 0.944 (text). Using textual triggers as a controlled probe, we show that standard fine-tuning learns a broad activation region rather than an exact trigger condition, causing neighboring strings to invoke the malicious behavior even when the exact trigger is absent. Adding edit-distance-one hard-negative samples during training substantially narrows this activation region and reduces leakage, including in image-editing and embodied-manipulation workflows, where leaked triggers can propagate into executable programs and action sequences.

</details>


### [147] [Amnesia: A Stealthy Replay Attack on Continual Learning Dreams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12655)
*Ahmed Sharshar,Naveen Kumar Kummari,Mohsen Guizani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Amnesia攻击，通过仅控制回放索引选择（不修改像素、标签或模型参数），在可审计约束下最大化持续学习系统的灾难性遗忘，揭示了索引级回放控制是一种实际且可审计的威胁面。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有持续学习攻击往往修改输入或训练流程（投毒/后门），缺乏显式的可审计约束，不够真实。本文旨在研究一种更现实的威胁场景：仅有有限权限的内部攻击者，仅控制回放索引选择，同时保持在可审计限制内。

Method: 提出Amnesia攻击，分为两步：(i) 计算轻量级类别效用（如EMA损失或置信度），将名义类别直方图p0向有害类别倾斜；(ii) 通过KL（指数倾斜）或TV（平衡质量重分布）优化器将倾斜投影回delta-ball内。窗口调度器支持滚动审计。

Result: 在多个持续学习基准和强回放基线下，Amnesia持续降低最终准确率并恶化反向迁移。KL变体影响大且难以检测，TV变体破坏力更强但更易被检测。

Conclusion: 仅控制回放索引即可构成实际、可审计的持续学习系统威胁，且存在原则性的影响-可见性权衡。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) models often use experience replay to reduce catastrophic forgetting, but their robustness to replay sampling interference remains underexplored. Existing CL attacks alter inputs or training pipelines (poisoning/backdoors) and rarely include explicit auditable constraints, limiting realism. Here, auditability means a monitor can verify compliance from sampler-visible telemetry - e.g., logged replay index/label statistics - by checking that the realized replay class histogram stays close to a nominal baseline and that replay rate is unchanged per batch and/or over a rolling window. We study a limited-privilege insider who controls only replay index selection, not pixels, labels, or model parameters, while staying within auditable limits such as queue priorities. We introduce Amnesia, a replay composition attack that maximizes degradation under two budgets: a visibility budget delta bounding the TV/KL divergence from a nominal class histogram p0, and a mass budget f fixing the replay rate. Amnesia has two steps: (i) compute lightweight class utilities, such as EMA loss or confidence, to tilt p0 toward harmful classes; and (ii) project the tilt back into the delta-ball using efficient KL (exponential tilt) or TV (balanced mass redistribution) optimizers. A windowed scheduler enforces rolling audits. Across challenging CL benchmarks and strong replay baselines, Amnesia consistently lowers final accuracy (ACC) and worsens backward transfer (-BWT). The KL variant delivers high impact while remaining largely undetected under multiple audit schemes, including per-batch and rolling-window checks. The TV variant is more damaging but easier to detect, especially under tight per-class constraints. These results expose index-only replay control as a practical, auditable threat surface in CL systems and establish a principled impact-visibility trade-off.

</details>


### [148] [CAPED: Context-Aware Privacy Exposure Defense for Mobile GUI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12666)
*Siyu Shen,Fenghao Xu,Wenrui Diao,Kehuan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CAPED是一个面向移动GUI代理的上下文感知预上传曝光控制层，在截图上传至远程多模态代理之前，选择性暴露任务所需内容、屏蔽无关的隐私信息，在保留高任务效用的同时，将加权隐私泄露从0.766降低至0.268。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于截图的移动GUI代理在运行时，截图会暴露联系人、消息、照片等与用户请求无关的敏感上下文信息，即"偶发视觉隐私暴露"问题。现有文本匿名化方法无法处理视觉和推断性线索，而通用隐私遮蔽会移除GUI代理完成任务所需的证据和控制元素。

Method: CAPED作为手机端保护层，在截图释放给远程多模态代理之前：提取任务需求、利用屏幕上下文作为隐私先验、解析可见UI元素，选择性暴露当前任务所需内容，同时屏蔽无关的偶发隐私内容。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上评估：完整版CAPED将加权的种子隐私泄露从原始截图的0.766降至0.268，同时保持了较高的任务效用。更广泛的AndroidWorld运行显示存在原型级别的效用代价。

Conclusion: 截图上传应被视为明确的设备-云边界决策问题，应由任务驱动的选择性曝光而非全有或全无的屏幕共享来支配。

Abstract: Screenshot-based mobile GUI agents can operate ordinary smartphone apps through the same visual interface as a human user, but this capability also turns every screen observation into a privacy boundary. During normal task execution, screenshots may expose contacts, messages, photos, files, recommendations, health cues, and other sensitive context that is unrelated to the user's request. We call this problem incidental visual privacy exposure. It is difficult to address with existing defenses: text anonymization misses many visual and inferential cues, while generic privacy masking can remove the evidence and controls that a GUI agent needs to complete the task.
  This paper presents CAPED, a context-aware pre-upload exposure control layer for mobile GUI agents. CAPED is designed as a phone-side protection layer: before screenshots are released to a remote multimodal agent, it extracts task requirements, uses screen context as a privacy prior, parses visible UI elements, and selectively exposes only content needed for the current task while masking incidental private content. We evaluate CAPED on AndroidWorld for broad task utility and with a controlled 28-task seeded privacy evaluation used as a measurement instrument for trajectory-level incidental leakage. In this seeded evaluation, Full CAPED reduces success-conditioned weighted seeded leakage from 0.766 under raw screenshots to 0.268 while preserving high task utility. A broader AndroidWorld run shows a remaining prototype-level utility cost, but the results support the central claim that screenshot upload should be treated as an explicit device--cloud boundary decision, governed by task-driven selective exposure rather than all-or-nothing screen sharing.

</details>


### [149] [SMSR: Certified Defence Against Runtime Memory Poisoning in Persistent LLM Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12703)
*Tarun Sharma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出多会话记忆投毒（MSMP）攻击，即攻击者通过正常交互向RAG代理的持久记忆中注入恶意内容，影响未来用户。现有防御均无法认证抵御该攻击。作者提出带签名记忆与平滑检索（SMSR）防御框架，包含写时HMAC-SHA256签名认证（Component 1）和查询时随机记忆消融+基于裁决的多数投票（Component 2），首次为该场景提供可认证的鲁棒性保证。实验表明Component 1将无签名攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%；Component 2在单次注入下将成功率控制在8.0%，低于认证最坏情况；端到端攻击下成功率从65.3%降至5.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG代理的持久记忆跨越用户会话累积，形成了一个新的攻击面：攻击者仅通过正常交互渠道即可注入精心构造的记忆，这些记忆在后续检索时会操纵代理对将来用户的响应，而无需修改模型权重或代码。现有防御（如静态语料防御、启发式过滤器）均无法有效应对此威胁，亟需一种可认证的防御方案。

Method: SMSR包含两个组件：Component 1（写时防护）使用HMAC-SHA256进行来源认证，阻止无签名注入；Component 2（查时防护）应用随机记忆消融结合基于裁决的多数投票（verdict-based majority voting），限制认证攻击者（有签名但恶意）的影响。作者证明了无来源认证的查询时过滤器无法认证抵御自适应注入，推导了Component 2的超几何认证证书，并形式化了"一致少数效应"（Consistent Minority Effect），指出传统基于字符串的投票中一致对抗答案即便数量少也能获胜，而基于裁决的投票可消除这一问题。

Result: 在15个企业场景（3,150次重复试验）中：Component 1将无签名攻击成功率从93-100%降至0%；单次注入的认证攻击者下Component 2将成功率控制在8.0%（95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450），低于认证最坏情况；端到端仅查询攻击（代理自行写入毒化记忆而非预植入）中SMSR将成功率从65.3%降至5.3%（n=150, 置信区间无重叠）。干净查询的实用性为90%（Component 1）和85%（组合）。

Conclusion: SMSR是首个针对多会话记忆投毒攻击提供可认证鲁棒性保证的防御方案，在保留实用性的同时有效抵御各类注入攻击，填补了RAG记忆安全领域的空白。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) agents increasingly run with persistent memory that accumulates across user sessions. This creates a new attack surface: an adversary interacting only through normal channels can inject crafted memories that, once retrieved, steer the agent's responses for future users, without touching model weights or code. We call this Multi-Session Memory Poisoning (MSMP) and show that no existing defence certifies against it; static-corpus defences (RobustRAG, ReliabilityRAG) assume a fixed knowledge base, and heuristic filters are bypassed by fluent enterprise-style text. We present Signed Memory with Smoothed Retrieval (SMSR), the first defence with a certified robustness bound for this setting. Component 1 adds HMAC-SHA256 provenance at write time, blocking unsigned injection. Component 2 applies randomised memory ablation with verdict-based majority voting at query time, bounding the influence of authenticated adversaries. We prove that no provenance-free retrieval-time filter can certify against adaptive injection, derive a hypergeometric certificate for Component 2, and formalise the Consistent Minority Effect, whereby a consistent adversarial answer wins string-based voting as a numerical minority while verdict-based voting removes it. Across 15 enterprise scenarios (3,150 repeated trials), Component 1 cuts attack success from 93-100% to 0% for all unsigned variants. For an authenticated adversary with a single injection, Component 2 holds success to 8.0% (95% CI [5.8, 10.9], n=450), below the certified worst case. In an end-to-end query-only attack where the agent itself writes the poison rather than it being pre-seeded, SMSR reduces success from 65.3% to 5.3% (n=150, non-overlapping CIs) on a live agent stack. Clean-query utility is 90% (Component 1) and 85% (combined).

</details>


### [150] [PI-Hunter: Automated Red-Teaming for Exposing and Localizing Prompt Injections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12737)
*Pengfei He,Lesly Miculicich,Vishesh Sharma,Ash Fox,George Lee,Jiliang Tang,Tomas Pfister,Long T. Le*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出PI-Hunter框架，一种用于LLM智能体的自动化审计框架，通过构建真实场景测试用例并迭代演化，主动暴露智能体在外部环境中的潜在提示注入漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御主要关注推理时阻断恶意内容，红队方法主要优化攻击成功率，导致开发者对潜在提示注入如何在智能体中产生和传播的可见性有限。

Method: PI-Hunter构建真实场景的源感知测试用例，并通过反馈驱动的探索迭代演化，诱导智能体从外部环境检索并暴露潜在恶意指令。

Result: 在多个基准、智能体架构、攻击和防御下，PI-Hunter在漏洞暴露和攻击面覆盖率上显著优于强自动化红队基线，且在现有提示注入防御下仍保持有效性。

Conclusion: PI-Hunter为LLM智能体提供了一种主动的漏洞暴露审计方法，弥补了现有红队方法仅关注攻击成功率的不足，提升了安全防御的可见性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are rapidly evolving into agentic systems that interact with external tools and environments, introducing new security risks such as indirect prompt injection attacks through untrusted external sources. Existing defenses mainly focus on blocking malicious content at inference time, and current red-teaming methods primarily optimize attack success. As a result, developers have limited visibility into how latent prompt injections emerge and propagate through agents. We propose PI-Hunter, an automated agentic auditing framework for proactive vulnerability exposure in LLM agents. PI-Hunter constructs realistic source-aware test cases and iteratively evolves them through feedback-driven exploration to induce agents to retrieve and reveal latent malicious instructions embedded within external environments. Extensive experiments across multiple benchmarks, agent architectures, attacks, and defenses demonstrate that PI-Hunter substantially improves vulnerability exposure and attack-surface coverage over strong automated red-teaming baselines, while remaining effective under existing prompt injection defenses.

</details>


### [151] [Semantic Identification of IoT Devices from Behavioral Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12793)
*Samuel Witt,Hassan Habibi Gharakheili*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究基于制造商使用描述（MUD）配置文件的物联网设备识别方法，利用访问控制条目（ACE）的语义表示来克服传统基于流量特征的识别方法在部署环境、软件版本和用户交互变化下的局限性。通过28个公开MUD配置文件（含1,023个ACE实例）的实验，证明了ACE级语义表示比整体配置文件嵌入更有效，且在白化校准后依然保持有效性。在受控运行时变化和真实物联网流量（超过80万条流）评估中，语义ACE匹配在稀疏重叠或早期观测阶段比精确ACE匹配提供更强的识别证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统物联网设备识别方法依赖从数据包或流记录中学习的设备签名，但通信模式会因部署环境、软件版本和用户交互而变化，导致识别效果不稳定。本文旨在探索利用MUD配置文件中ACE的语义信息进行设备识别，以克服上述局限性。

Method: 使用28个公开MUD配置文件（1,023个ACE实例）构建ACE级语义表示（从紧凑行为文本生成），并分析其几何特性；对比精确ACE匹配与语义ACE匹配在受控运行时变化（未见ACE、主机名漂移、部分运行时观测）和真实物联网流量（超过80万条流）下的识别效果。

Result: ACE级语义表示比整体配置文件嵌入更有效地区分设备，白化校准后仍然有效。精确ACE匹配在重叠度高时表现良好，但稀疏或无重叠时性能急剧下降；语义ACE匹配在各种条件下均能保留有效识别证据。在真实流量中，精确匹配在稳定重叠时最强，但语义匹配在早期观测阶段提供更强证据，且常将正确设备排在最高候选位置。

Conclusion: 基于MUD配置文件的ACE语义表示能够有效进行物联网设备识别，尤其在精确匹配失效的稀疏重叠和早期观测场景下具有显著优势，为物联网设备识别提供了新的可行方向。

Abstract: Accurate identification of IoT devices is important for security management and policy enforcement. Existing approaches typically learn device signatures from packets or flow records. These methods operate on low-level communication observations whose traffic patterns may vary across deployments, software versions, and user interactions. This paper studies device identification using Manufacturer Usage Description (MUD) profiles. MUD profiles describe device behavior using Access Control Entries (ACEs), where each ACE represents a behavioral primitive consisting of protocol, endpoint, direction, and port semantics derived from device communication policy. Our contributions are threefold. First, using 28 publicly available MUD profiles containing 1,023 ACE instances, we construct ACE-level semantic representations from compact behavioral text and analyze their geometric properties. ACE-level representations preserve device-level behavioral distinctions more effectively than whole-profile embeddings and remain effective after whitening calibration. Second, we evaluate semantic ACE matching under controlled runtime variations, including unseen ACEs, drifted hostnames, and partial runtime observation. Exact ACE matching performs well when the overlap with the canonical MUD profile remains high, but degrades sharply when the overlap becomes sparse or disappears. In contrast, semantic ACE matching preserves useful identification evidence across these conditions. Third, we evaluate the same approach on real IoT traffic traces comprising more than 800,000 observed flows. Exact overlap remains the strongest signal when stable overlap exists, while semantic ACE matching provides stronger identification evidence during the early stages of observation, frequently retains the correct device among the highest-ranked candidates, and remains effective under sparse-overlap runtime traffic.

</details>


### [152] [A Privacy-Preserving Framework Using Remote Data Science for Inter-Institutional Student Retention Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12845)
*John Fields,K M Sajjadul Islam,Ruchitha Thota,Victor Chen,Praveen Madiraju*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究基于PySyft平台开发了远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用半气隙架构实现跨机构的学生保留率协作预测，在保护隐私（符合FERPA）的前提下取得了稳定的分类性能（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），并提出了数据感知模板（Data-Type-Aware Templates）这一注重隐私的合成数据方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索在跨机构协作预测学生保留率时，如何通过隐私保护机器学习（PPML）技术解决学生敏感数据的共享难题，同时遵守FERPA等隐私法规。

Method: 基于PySyft平台构建远程数据科学（RDS）框架，采用高侧服务器与低侧服务器的半气隙架构；利用一所小型私立大学的历史数据（N=720）评估三种合成数据生成方法；提出Data-Type-Aware Templates合成数据方法。

Result: 跨机构分类性能一致（Macro F1: 0.690-0.695），严格遵守FERPA合规要求。

Conclusion: 基于RDS的PPML在教育场景中技术上可行，为小规模跨机构协作提供了联邦学习的实用替代方案。

Abstract: This study explores privacy-preserving machine learning (PPML) techniques using the PySyft platform to enable collaborative prediction of student retention between institutions. We developed a remote data science (RDS) framework with a semi-air-gapped architecture consisting of high-side and low-side servers, allowing researchers from three universities to build predictive models on sensitive student data without direct data access. Using historical data from a small private university (N=720), we evaluated three synthetic data generation approaches and validated the framework through inter-institutional collaboration. The results demonstrate consistent classification performance across institutions (Macro F1: 0.690--0.695) while maintaining strict Family Educational Rights and Privacy Act (FERPA) compliance. We also propose Data-Type-Aware Templates, a novel synthetic data method that prioritizes privacy over distributional fidelity. Our findings confirm that RDS-based PPML is technically feasible for educational settings and offers a practical alternative to federated learning for small-scale inter-institutional collaborations. The code is available at https://github.com/jtfields/NAIRR240195-Privacy-Preserving-Machine-Learning.

</details>


### [153] [LNTest: A Testbed for Evaluating Bitcoin Lightning Network-Based Botnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12887)
*Thomas Bakaysa,Ahmet Kurt,Abdul-Salem Beibitkhan,Jesus Maria Romo Diaz de Leon,Tag Kalat,Joshua Kramer,Estela Rodriguez,Abraham Watkins,Abdullah Aydeger*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出LNTest，首个针对基于闪电网络的僵尸网络的可重用测试平台，通过实验发现D-LNBot的自组织协议实际生成聚类链而非均匀链，命令传播复杂度为Θ(n)而非O(m log n)，且不同覆盖拓扑对拆除策略的鲁棒性各异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于闪电网络的僵尸网络方案（LNBot、D-LNBot）仅停留在概念验证和仿真评估阶段，未解决真实拓扑形成、传播复杂度和抗拆除能力等关键问题。

Method: 基于Core Lightning节点和Docker容器化技术，在共享Bitcoin Core regtest链上构建LNTest测试平台，支持确定性链、自主节点发现和用户指定图三种覆盖拓扑模式，用于对比实验。

Result: (1) D-LNBot自组织协议生成的是聚类链（团块由桥接节点连接），而非理论设计的均匀链；(2) 命令传播复杂度为线性Θ(n)，而非之前声称的O(m log n)；(3) 均匀度链抗定向删除但脆弱于随机故障，无标度拓扑相反，自组织聚类链在两种策略下均脆弱。

Conclusion: LNTest作为首个可重用测试平台，揭示了LN僵尸网络的实际特性与理论假设的偏差，为后续防御研究提供了可复现的实验基础设施。

Abstract: Bitcoin's Lightning Network (LN) can be exploited as a covert, low-cost command-and-control (C&C) channel for botnets, as demonstrated by the LNBot and D-LNBot designs. However, both remain proof-of-concept prototypes evaluated only through simulation, leaving key questions about real-world topology formation, propagation complexity, and resilience to takedowns unanswered. We present LNTest, the first reusable testbed for LN-based botnets, built from Core Lightning nodes containerized with Docker over a shared Bitcoin Core regtest chain. LNTest supports three overlay topology modes (a deterministic chain, autonomous peer discovery, and user-supplied graphs), enabling controlled experiments across different botnet structures. Using LNTest, we report three main findings. First, D-LNBot's autonomous formation protocol does not produce the uniform chain from its design; instead, it creates a clustered chain in which cliques are linked by bridge nodes whose removal fragments the network. Second, command propagation scales linearly with botnet size ($Θ(n)$), not the $O(m \log n)$ previously claimed, and gains nothing from higher neighbor connectivity. Third, the overlay topology determines the effectiveness of takedown strategies: uniform-degree chains resist targeted removal but fragment under random failure, scale-free topologies show the opposite pattern, and the autonomous clustered chain is fragile under both, making it the most vulnerable of the three. LNTest is released as open source, with a script that reproduces all our experiments, to support reproducible research on LN-based botnet defenses.

</details>


### [154] [MAStrike: Shapley-Guided Collusive Red-Teaming on Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12918)
*Chejian Xu,Zhaorun Chen,Jingyang Zhang,Freddy Lecue,Avni Kothari,Sarah Tan,Wenbo Guo,Bo Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MAStrike是一个针对层级式多智能体系统的闭环红队测试框架，通过引入智能体级别的Shapley值分析来量化每个智能体对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献，识别脆弱联盟并生成协同的、角色感知的对抗性攻击，在金融、软件工程和CRM等多个领域的实验中显著优于启发式基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层级式多智能体系统在金融和软件工程等高安全领域快速部署，但其安全风险因智能体角色专化而高度分布化，现有红队测试方法仅依赖于对目标智能体的启发式选择和孤立消息流的扰动，无法回答哪些智能体对系统安全最负责以及被攻破的智能体如何协同绕过防御等关键问题。

Method: 提出MAStrike闭环框架：首先引入智能体级别的Shapley值分析量化每个智能体在任务特定分布下对系统鲁棒性的边际贡献；基于此归因识别脆弱智能体联盟并生成协同的、角色感知的对抗性操纵；通过结构化因果诊断迭代优化攻击，将失败案例归因于阻碍对抗尝试的未攻破智能体；构建了涵盖多种层级拓扑和领域的综合红队测试基准与可控环境。

Result: 在基于多个前沿模型构建的MAS上的广泛实验表明，MAStrike显著优于启发式基线方法；分析揭示了非平凡的Shapley值分布和智能体间高阶交互结构，发现了被先前的单智能体或模板化方法忽视的关键漏洞和协同模式。

Conclusion: MAStrike框架有效解决了层级式MAS中协同红队测试的难题，通过Shapley值归因和结构化因果诊断实现了对系统安全薄弱环节的准确识别与针对性攻击生成，为MAS安全性评估提供了更全面的方法论。

Abstract: Hierarchical multi-agent systems (MAS) are rapidly being deployed in high-stakes workflows across domains such as finance and software engineering. In these systems, safety and security are inherently distributed across role-specialized agents, significantly expanding the attack surface, particularly under coordinated adversarial behaviors such as privilege escalation and cross-agent collusion. Existing red-teaming approaches for MAS remain limited: they rely on heuristic selection of target agents and perturb isolated message streams, leaving critical questions unanswered as which agents are most responsible for system safety, and how compromised agents can coordinate to bypass defenses. We propose MAStrike, a closed-loop framework for collusive red-teaming in hierarchical MAS. We propose the first agent-level Shapley value analysis for MAS, quantifying each agent's marginal contribution to system robustness under task-specific distributions. GGuided by this attribution, MAStrike identifies vulnerable agent coalitions and generates coordinated, role-aware adversarial manipulations. These attacks are iteratively refined through structured causal diagnosis, attributing failure cases to uncompromised agents that block adversarial attempts. We further build a comprehensive MAS red-teaming benchmark and controllable environments spanning diverse hierarchical topologies and domains, including finance, software engineering, and CRM. Extensive experiments across MAS built on multiple frontier models show that MAStrike substantially outperforms heuristic baselines. Our analysis further uncovers non-trivial Shapley value distributions and higher-order interaction structures among agents, revealing critical vulnerabilities and coordination patterns that are overlooked by prior single-agent or template-based methods.

</details>


### [155] [ViPER: Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust Malware Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12949)
*Fatima Qaiser,Bisma Tahir,Muhammad Abid Mughal,Nauman Shamim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ViPER提出了一种基于视觉的打包感知编码器，通过LoRA适配的ViT-B/14骨干网络和双头架构（联合学习恶意软件分类与打包检测），结合打包感知门控机制和频率加权损失，在200,000个Windows PE字节图图像上实现了优于现有方法的恶意软件检测性能（平衡准确率0.8521，ROC-AUC 0.9260，AUPR 0.9279）和极高的打包检测AUC（0.9949）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于可视化的恶意软件检测方法虽能抵御逃避攻击且无需反汇编，但可执行文件打包（packing）导致的高熵图像会掩盖结构模式，造成模型失效。由于良性软件中也普遍存在打包（如压缩或版权保护），仅靠打包状态无法判断恶意性，现有方法未能在统一监督框架下解决此问题。

Method: ViPER采用LoRA微调的ViT-B/14骨干网络，设计双头架构同时学习恶意软件分类和打包检测；提出打包感知门控机制，根据推断的打包状态条件化恶意软件预测，为打包和未打包输入建立不同决策边界；针对训练中打包标签分布倾斜，采用频率加权损失和联合类别-打包层的分层采样策略。

Result: 在200,000个Windows PE字节图图像上，ViPER的平衡准确率为0.8521，ROC-AUC为0.9260，AUPR为0.9279，在所有主要指标上均优于代表性SOTA基线；打包检测AUC达到0.9949。

Conclusion: ViPER通过显式建模打包状态并使其影响恶意性判定，有效解决了打包对可视化恶意软件检测的干扰问题，验证了打包感知机制在统一监督框架下的有效性。

Abstract: Visualization-based malware detection maps raw binary bytes to grayscale images and applies learned visual classifiers, providing an evasion-resistant and disassembly-free alternative to conventional analysis pipelines. However, executable packing remains a critical failure mode: packed binaries produce high-entropy images that obscure the structural patterns these models rely on. Because packing is also prevalent in benign software (e.g., for compression or copy protection), packing state alone is not a reliable indicator of maliciousness, and existing approaches do not address this challenge within a unified supervised framework. We present ViPER, a Vision-based Packing-Aware Encoder for Robust malware detection. ViPER builds on a LoRA-adapted ViT-B/14 backbone with a dual-head architecture that jointly learns malware classification and packing detection. A packing-aware gating mechanism conditions malware predictions on the inferred packing state, enabling distinct decision boundaries for packed and unpacked inputs. To address packing label skew during training, we employ frequency-weighted losses with stratified sampling over joint class-packing strata. Evaluated on 200,000 Windows PE byteplot images, ViPER achieves a balanced accuracy of 0.8521, ROC-AUC of 0.9260, and AUPR of 0.9279, outperforming representative state-of-the-art baselines across all primary metrics, while attaining a packing detection AUC of 0.9949.

</details>


### [156] [SoK: The Constant Time Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13000)
*Billy Bob Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统化了常量时间编程模型及其演化，发现模型保护范围与规范假设之间的差距，并提炼出一种发现密码学原语边界外时序漏洞的攻击方法论。利用该方法发现了私钥加载相关的规范级漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了泄漏。令人意外的是，尽管BoringSSL威胁模型更严格，但其每次观测的信号强度比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术界与工业界对"常量时间"的定义存在差异，现有模型保护范围与密码学规范的实际假设之间存在未被系统化研究的间隙，导致时序漏洞可能源自密码学原语边界之外。

Method: 系统化梳理常量时间模型及其演化，识别模型与规范假设之间的差距，提炼出一套针对密码学原语边界外时序漏洞的攻击方法论。

Result: 发现了与私钥加载相关的规范级时序漏洞，并在OpenSSL和BoringSSL中确认了信息泄漏。BoringSSL虽然威胁模型更严格，但每次观测的信号强度比OpenSSL高出数个数量级。

Conclusion: 常量时间模型的保护范围与密码学规范假设之间存在系统性差距，攻击者可利用该差距发现边界外的时序漏洞；威胁模型更严格的实现未必实际更安全，需从规范层面弥合这一鸿沟。

Abstract: Constant time programming patterns is the primary defense against timing attacks on cryptographic implementations, yet what "constant time" means varies across academia and industry. This work systematizes constant time models and their evolution, identifies a recurring gap between what models protect and what specifications assume, and distills an offensive methodology for discovering timing vulnerabilities that originate outside the cryptographic primitive boundary. Applying this methodology, we locate a specification-level vulnerability related to private key loading, and confirm the leak in both OpenSSL and BoringSSL. Counterintuitively, BoringSSL's per-observation signal is several orders of magnitude stronger than OpenSSL's, despite an explicitly stricter threat model.

</details>


### [157] [DIG: Oracle-Guided Directed Input Generation for One-Day Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13037)
*Andrew Bao,Haochen Zeng,Peng Chen,Stephen McCamant,Pen-Chung Yew*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DIG提出了一种利用补丁信息引导的PoC生成方法，通过LLM分析补丁合成oracle，结合高层代理推理约束求解和低层模糊测试分支距离反馈，在138个真实CVE上触发80个漏洞，比最优基线提升40%，并发现6个零日漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对单日漏洞因补丁采用延迟或不完整带来的风险，现有定向模糊测试无法有效识别和求解必要约束，而智能体方法在长程推理中容易目标漂移，亟需一种能显式利用补丁信息进行高效PoC生成的方法。

Method: DIG利用单日漏洞的一个关键特性——补丁常揭示触发漏洞的必要前置条件。首先使用LLM分析补丁并合成显式的oracle（条件断言）；高层通过代理推理并求解约束以满足oracle（oracle引导的生成器进化）；低层将oracle植入目标程序，利用分支距离反馈指导定向模糊测试中的随机变异。

Result: 在138个真实CVE上，DIG触发80个漏洞，超越最佳基线40%（57 vs. 80），且独家触发9个其他技术无法触发的漏洞；在92.9%的案例中比同类工具更快，48.8%的案例加速超100倍，最大加速比达3664倍；此外还发现6个已知库中的未知零日漏洞。

Conclusion: DIG通过显式利用补丁信息构建oracle并实现高层约束求解与低层模糊测试的协同，显著提升了单日漏洞PoC生成的效率和覆盖率，并展现出零日漏洞发现能力。

Abstract: One-day vulnerabilities pose significant risks due to delayed or incomplete patch adoption. Generating proof-of-concept (PoC) inputs is therefore essential for assessing real-world impact. The key challenge is identifying necessary constraints for triggering the vulnerability and solving them effectively. Existing directed fuzzing approaches prioritize inputs toward target locations, but neither explicitly identify necessary constraints nor solve them effectively, relying instead on target-distance feedback and random mutation. Agentic approaches show strong potential through code reasoning and structured input generation, but goal drift in long-horizon reasoning limits their effectiveness.
  DIG addresses this challenge by exploiting a key property of one-day vulnerabilities: patches often reveal necessary preconditions for triggering. DIG uses an LLM to analyze the patch and synthesize an oracle making these conditions explicit. The oracle supports effective PoC generation at two levels. At the high level, DIG performs oracle-guided generator evolution, where an agent infers and solves constraints to satisfy the oracle. At the low level, DIG instruments the oracle into the target program and uses branch-distance feedback to guide random mutation in directed fuzzing. Evaluation shows DIG outperforms 2 state-of-the-art agents and 10 fuzzers across 138 real-world CVEs. DIG triggers 80 vulnerabilities, surpassing prior results and outperforming the best baseline by 40% (57 vs. 80 CVEs). Notably, DIG exclusively triggers 9 vulnerabilities no existing technique can trigger. Compared to the average of other tools, DIG triggers vulnerabilities faster in 92.9% of cases, achieving over 100x speedup in 48.8% of cases, with a maximum speedup of 3,664x. Beyond one-day PoC generation, DIG uncovers 6 previously unknown vulnerabilities in widely deployed libraries, enabling zero-day discovery.

</details>


### [158] [The Emergence of Autonomous Penetration Capabilities in Large Language Model-Powered AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13079)
*Jiaqi Luo,Jiarun Dai,Zhile Chen,Jia Xu,Weibing Wang,Yawen Duan,Brian Tse,Geng Hong,Xudong Pan,Yuan Zhang,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM驱动的AI系统自主渗透能力评估中存在的评估方法不透明、场景过于简化、先验知识过多等问题，构建了一个包含两级目标服务器（Tier-1和Tier-2，共300个）和通用智能体架构的新评估框架。对19个开源和闭源LLM的评估显示，当前模型的自主渗透成功率在10.7%至69.3%之间，且该能力随模型整体能力提升而持续进步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主渗透能力评估方法存在方法论不透明、测试场景过于简化或不现实、为LLM提供过多先验知识和任务特定指导等问题，无法准确衡量现代AI系统在真实高影响力网络攻击场景中自主执行渗透这一核心能力。

Method: 构建包含两级目标环境（Tier-1：1个安全服务+1个脆弱服务；Tier-2：3个安全服务+1个脆弱服务）共300个目标服务器的评估框架；采用通用智能体架构，配备通用网络安全工具集，不含任何目标服务器的先验知识；对19个开源和闭源LLM进行系统评估。

Result: 当前模型的自主渗透成功率范围为10.7%至69.3%；自主渗透能力随模型整体能力提升而持续改善。

Conclusion: 构建了一个更贴近实际的自主渗透评估框架，发现当前AI系统的自主渗透能力仍有限（最高69.3%），但该能力与模型整体能力呈正相关趋势，表明需持续关注此安全红线能力的发展。

Abstract: Nowadays, the autonomous execution of cyberattacks capable of causing substantial real-world harm is widely regarded as one of the critical red lines that frontier AI systems must not cross. Within this broader red-line scenario, autonomous penetration represents a core enabling capability and subtask: the ability of LLM-powered AI systems to independently conduct adversarial operations against a target server without human intervention, identify and exploit vulnerabilities, and obtain unauthorized access or control. A growing body of work has sought to assess the autonomous penetration capabilities of AI systems. However, existing evaluations often employ opaque methodologies, rely on unrealistic or overly simplified penetration-testing scenarios, or provide LLMs with excessive prior knowledge and task-specific guidance, and cannot accurately capture the extent to which modern AI systems can autonomously perform this core capability within broader high-impact cyberattack scenarios.
  To address these limitations, we construct a new autonomous penetration evaluation framework consisting of two components: target servers and agent scaffolding. Specifically, on the target-server side, we design two levels of target environments based on the number of secure services without known vulnerabilities deployed alongside a vulnerable service: Tier~1 (one secure service) and Tier~2 (three secure services), resulting in a total of 300 target servers. Meanwhile, the agent scaffolding adopts a general-purpose agent architecture equipped with a set of general-purpose cybersecurity tools, without any target-specific prior knowledge. We evaluate 19 open-weight and proprietary LLMs, and find that current models achieve penetration success rates ranging from 10.7% to 69.3%. Moreover, we observe that autonomous penetration capability continues to improve alongside advances in overall model capability.

</details>


### [159] [The Invisible Ink of the Android Malware World: A Longitudinal Study on the Usage of Covert Communication Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13107)
*Zeya Umayya,Manan Aggarwal,Manan Chugh,Mann Nariya,Yogesh Kaushik,Sambuddho Chakravarty*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对Android恶意软件生态系统中隐蔽通道（CC）的使用进行了纵向研究。通过对2009年至2025年间350万款Android恶意软件的分析，发现使用CC的恶意软件从2012年的0.30%激增至2025年的50%，涉及511个恶意家族，并识别出19,308个独特IP地址，其中59个IP明确验证存在CC。研究还发现CC使用模式随时间演变，如恶意软件采用多种CC或在不同CC间周期性切换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管代理、VPN和Tor等工具既能帮助隐私保护和绕过审查，也可能被恶意软件和僵尸网络利用来隐藏与C2服务器的通信，但目前缺乏对恶意软件如何利用隐蔽通道逃避检测的纵向研究。

Method: 开发了一个结合静态与动态分析的多阶段分析管道，在350万款Android恶意软件（2009-2025年）上对系统和网络层面特征进行检测。静态分析使用精心构建的验证规则识别CC使用，动态分析进一步验证IP地址。

Result: 发现288,834个APK使用CC，涵盖511个恶意家族；CC使用率从2012年的0.30%指数级增长至2025年的50%；动态分析识别出19,308个独特IP（85个国家），其中59个IP（17个国家）明确验证存在CC；发现CC使用模式随时间演变，包括多CC使用和周期性切换。

Conclusion: 隐蔽通道在Android恶意软件中的使用呈现指数级增长趋势，且使用模式不断演化，表明恶意软件正越来越多地利用这些技术来逃避检测，亟需针对性的检测和防御措施。

Abstract: Proxies, VPNs and Tor have long helped the privacy community and users in censored regions to fight censorship. However, the same tools can be maliciously exploited by malware and botnets to conceal their communication to external command and control servers. Despite being a critical concern fueled by the proliferation of malware based attacks, no longitudinal studies have analyzed how malware applications use covert channels (CC) to evade detection.
  We fill this gap by performing the first study of the usage of covert channels in the Android malware ecosystem. To that end, we develop a multistage pipeline that combines static and dynamic analysis to investigate both system and network-level features. We applied this pipeline on a corpus of 3.5M Android malware spanning 2009 to July 2025. Our carefully crafted static validation rules uncovered 288K APKs that used CCs spanning 511 malware families and CC usage growing exponentially from 0.30\% (2012) to 50\% (2025). Overall, in dynamic analysis, we identified 19,308 unique IP addresses being contacted in 85 countries, out of which we were able to explicitly validate the presence of CCs for 59 IP addresses across 17 countries. Further, we performed a longitudinal dataset study spanning over 16 years for CC based malware and found that CC usage has evolved, \textit{e.g.,} some malware adopted by using more than one CCs; others switched between them periodically (one family switched CC usage 40 times from 2019 to 2025).

</details>


### [160] [Who Pays the Price? Stakeholder-Centric Prompt Injection Benchmarking for Real-world Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13385)
