*Zihao Wang,Yiming Li,Yutong Wu,Zheyu Liu,Kangjie Chen,Fok Kar Wai,Pin-Yu Chen,Vrizlynn L. L. Thing,Bo Li,Dacheng Tao,Tianwei Zhang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出 StakeBench，一个以利益相关者为中心的基准测试，用于系统评估 LLM 驱动的 Web 代理在面对提示注入攻击时对不同实体（用户、卖家、平台）造成的危害分布，揭示了现有代理无法可靠抵御任何攻击目标，且失败模式呈现多种异质性（如隐蔽寄生、失调干扰、复合失败），呼吁采用利益相关者感知的评估方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准采用攻击中心视角，仅关注注入的技术可行性，忽略了提示注入攻击对不同利益相关者造成的危害分布差异——同一攻击对不同受害者的后果不对称，且攻击模式的有效性因目标实体而异。

Method: 提出 StakeBench 基准，采用利益相关者中心视角：(1) 区分受影响实体（用户、卖家、平台）；(2) 将攻击分解为具体目标；(3) 使用互补的结果级和过程级指标进行评估。

Result: 没有一种攻击目标能被当前代理可靠抵御；失败模式呈现异质性，包括隐蔽寄生（攻击成功但未干扰用户任务）、失调干扰（任务中断但攻击未成功）和复合失败（对抗目标与任务完整性同时被破坏）。

Conclusion: 现有评估方式忽略了这些模式差异，凸显了在真实部署环境中需要采用利益相关者感知的评估方法来全面衡量 LLM Web 代理的安全性。

Abstract: Web agents driven by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in real-world environments, where they operate over untrusted web content and execute actions with direct consequences. This makes them vulnerable to prompt-injection attacks, in which seemingly benign content embeds adversarial instructions that manipulate agent behaviour. Existing security benchmarks adopt an \textit{attack-centric} perspective, focusing on the technical feasibility of injections while overlooking the nuanced distribution of resulting harms. In practice, however, prompt-injection risk is victim-dependent: a single exploit can produce asymmetric consequences for different stakeholders, and the same attack pattern may exhibit substantially different effectiveness depending on whom it targets. To capture these properties, we introduce \textbf{\sysname}, a \textit{stakeholder-centric} benchmark to systematically categorize and attribute harm in real-world web agent systems. It distinguishes between affected entities (e.g., user, seller, platform), decomposes the attacks into concrete objectives, and evaluates each case with complementary outcome- and process-level metrics. Our results reveal substantial and heterogeneous vulnerabilities: not a single attack objective is reliably resisted by current agents, and failures distribute across qualitatively distinct modes ranging from \emph{stealthy parasitism} (attack succeeds without disrupting the user's delegated task) to \emph{misaligned disruption} (task disrupted without attack success) and \emph{compounded failure} (both adversarial objective and task integrity simultaneously violated). These patterns are missed by conventional evaluation, highlighting the need for stakeholder-aware assessment of LLM-based agents in real-world deployments. Benchmark is available at https://github.com/StakeBench/SBC.

</details>


### [161] [An Assessment Framework for Application-Level Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13425)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个七维组件化评估框架，用于系统评估应用层密码敏捷性。对六种代表性API的分析发现三个普遍缺失的能力：意图驱动的密钥创建、策略驱动的算法选择、以及现有密钥的算法转换操作。这些缺口独立地阻碍了后量子迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码迁移需要全面替换算法，但现有密码API在设计时未考虑敏捷性，缺乏系统评估密码敏捷性的方法，且"密码敏捷性"一词本身指代多种独立能力（算法替换、策略选择、实现替换），缺乏结构化分解限制了系统评估和API开发。

Method: 提出一个基于组件的评估框架，沿七个正交维度刻画应用层密码敏捷性：三个耦合维度（应用代码对算法和提供者的认知程度）、一个横切解耦机制、一个治理权威维度、以及两个敏捷性使能维度（衡量实际迁移能力）。框架是非线性的，支持非层次化配置描述。使用该框架评估六种代表性API。

Result: 六种API（PKCS#11、OpenSSL 3.0、JCA、Google Tink、AWS KMS、HashiCorp Vault Transit）均存在三个普适性缺口：不支持意图驱动的密钥创建、不提供策略驱动的算法选择（区别于访问控制）、不支持现有密钥的算法转换操作。每个缺口单独足以阻碍敏捷迁移。

Conclusion: 尽管API发展数十年，后量子迁移仍然是一个软件工程问题。现有的密码敏捷性缺口解释了为何过渡难以实现，并指明了未来API设计需要填补的关键空白。

Abstract: The impending post-quantum transition to new cryptography will require complete replacement of algorithms within all software. The cryptographic APIs used today make this transition challenging because they were not designed with agility as a concern. There is no method for systematically assessing cryptographic agility as an overall ability. In addition to this, the term itself refers to multiple independent capabilities. Specifically, it includes replacing algorithms, selecting by policy, and substituting implementations. This lack of structured decomposition limits both the evaluation of systems and the development of cryptographically agile APIs.
  We introduce a component-based assessment framework that characterizes application-level cryptographic agility along seven orthogonal dimensions: three coupling dimensions that measure what the application code knows about algorithms and providers, a cross-cutting decoupling mechanism, a governance authority dimension, and two agility enablers that measure actual migration capability. The framework is non-linear and captures non-hierarchical profiles: a system may achieve high operation decoupling yet low creation decoupling, or strong versioning without externalized configuration.
  We evaluate six representative APIs (PKCS#11, OpenSSL~3.0, JCA, Google Tink, AWS KMS, and HashiCorp Vault Transit) against the framework, revealing three pervasive and independent gaps: no system supports intent-based key creation, none provides policy-driven algorithm selection (as distinct from access control), and none offers dedicated/first-class operations for algorithm transformation of existing keys. These gaps are individually sufficient to prevent agile migration, explaining why the post-quantum transition remains a software engineering problem despite decades of API progress.

</details>


### [162] [Intent-Based Cryptographic API Design for Cryptographic Agility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13445)
*Navaneeth Rameshan,Gregoire Messmer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套密码敏捷性API设计原则，通过意图词汇、抽象策略API和密钥演化机制，将密码学升级从代码重写转变为操作流程，解决后量子密码迁移中的软件工程挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前密码学API围绕特定算法设计，不支持策略化算法选择，无法实现已有密钥向新算法的迁移，使得向后量子密码过渡成为一项严峻的软件工程挑战。

Method: 从五大架构特征（抽象性、稳定性、时间灵活性、分离性、可扩展性）推导出API设计原则；通过Protocol Buffers模式实现意图词汇（基于作用域解耦密钥创建与算法身份）、抽象策略API（不规定策略格式）、稳定密钥标识符及密钥演化操作（轮换、转换、迁移）。

Result: 实现了算法在作用域内的透明替换，支持密钥跨算法和跨提供商的迁移，同时追踪原始密钥身份及其演化历史，使密码学更新成为无需重写应用代码的运营流程。

Conclusion: 密码敏捷性API通过架构层面的解耦设计，将算法迁移从代码变更转变为运维操作，为后量子密码迁移提供了可行的软件工程路径。

Abstract: As organizations move toward post-quantum cryptography, they face the major challenge of updating cryptographic algorithms across large, complex software portfolios. However, most cryptographic APIs in use today were designed around specific algorithms. These APIs expect explicit use of specific algorithms, provide little or no support for policy-based algorithm selection, and offer no straightforward way to migrate existing keys to newer algorithms. This makes the transition to post-quantum cryptography challenging. The companion assessment framework identifies the barriers to cryptographic agility and explains why algorithm transition is largely a software engineering problem.
  To address the limitations of current cryptographic APIs, we identify the principles necessary to design a cryptographically agile API. The design principles are derived from five fundamental architectural characteristics (Abstraction, Stability, Temporal Flexibility, Separation, and Extensibility). We also show how the design principles can be implemented using several examples of Protocol Buffers API design patterns. In particular, we present an intent vocabulary that is based on scopes which allows for decoupling key creation from algorithm identities. It also supports transparent substitutions of algorithms in the applicable scope. Cryptographic governance is enabled by an abstract policy API that does not prescribe the policy format. Keys are represented by stable identifiers and support key evolution operations (rotation, transformation, migration), facilitating migration between algorithms and providers while tracking both the original key identity and its evolution history. With this approach, updating cryptography becomes an operational process without the need to rewrite application code.

</details>


### [163] [Differentially Private Hierarchical Heavy Hitters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13563)
*Ari Biswas,Graham Cormode,Yaron Kanza,Divesh Srivastava,Zhengyi Zhou*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次研究了差分隐私下的层次化重击者（HHH）问题，涵盖非流式和流式两种场景。在非流式设置中，发现前缀残差计数的相对误差与层次高度和重击者数量无关；在流式设置中，尽管近似函数具有高全局敏感度，但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HHH问题自Cormode等人提出以来已被广泛研究，但在隐私保护场景下（尤其是差分隐私）的HHH释放问题尚未被探索。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 针对非流式设置和流式设置分别设计差分隐私机制；分析两种场景下误差的敏感度与关键参数（层次高度、重击者数量、可用空间）的理论关系。

Result: 非流式设置中，前缀残差计数的相对误差与层次高度和重击者数量无关；流式设置中，尽管流式近似函数具有高全局敏感度（线性依赖于可用空间），但频率估计的绝对误差与可用空间无关。

Conclusion: 在非流式设置中实现了层次无关的相对误差保证；在流式设置中克服了高敏感度的障碍，实现了空间无关的绝对误差保证，为隐私保护的HHH释放提供了理论基础。

Abstract: The task of finding _Hierarchical_ Heavy Hitters (HHH) was introduced by Cormode et al. [VLDB 2003] as a generalisation of the heavy hitter problem. While finding HHH in data streams has been studied extensively, the question of releasing HHH when the underlying data is private remains unexplored. In this paper, we study differentially private HHH release in both the streaming and non-streaming setting. In the non-streaming setting, we show the surprising result that the relative error in estimating the residual count for any prefix is independent of the height of the hierarchy and the number of heavy hitters in the stream. Meanwhile, in the streaming setting, although the exact version of HHH has low global sensitivity (as counting queries are 1-sensitive), the approximation functions due to streaming have high global sensitivity, linear in the available space. Despite this obstacle, we show that the absolute error for estimating frequencies in the steaming setting is independent of the available space.

</details>


### [164] [Beyond the IT Checklist: Engineering a Reasonable Standard of Care for Cyber Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13612)
*Matthew E. Jablonski,Linton Wells,Kathryn B. Laskey,F. Brett Berlin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文批判当前美国网络政策以合规代安全，指出其在网宇物理系统中的不足。通过对292份关键基础设施政策文档（2000-2025）的分析，发现义务集中于NIST SP 800-160 Vol.2弹性生命周期的"预期"阶段，强调行政合规，而"承受"和"恢复"阶段严重依赖与物理危害脱节的IT控制目录。作者提出以危害特定可追溯性、结构化保证案例和网络弹性工程为核心的现代化注意标准，并建议将工程义务与定向激励相结合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前美国网络政策以文档合规替代实际安全，无法应对网宇物理系统中数字故障导致动能危害的风险，需重新审视"合理注意"的实现方式。

Method: 构建并编码包含292份关键基础设施政策文档（2000-2025年）的语料库，分析"合理注意"在NIST SP 800-160 Vol.2弹性生命周期各阶段的操作化分布。

Result: 义务集中在"预期"阶段且强调行政合规；"承受"和"恢复"阶段依赖与物理危害脱节的IT控制目录引用；发现三大脱节：委托标准校准不当、恢复被定义为通知而非工程化导航、跨部门适应要求不均衡。

Conclusion: 应建立以危害可追溯性、结构化保证案例和网络弹性工程为基础的现代化注意标准，并将工程义务与定向激励相结合，使弹性架构成为可行的商业决策而非无资金支持的期望。

Abstract: Current U.S. cyber policy, centered on security, often treats documentation of controls and incident reports as a proxy for safety in the built environment. This paper argues that such an approach is inadequate for cyber-physical systems, where digital failures can produce kinetic harm. We construct and code a corpus of critical infrastructure policy documents (N=292, 2000-2025) to examine how "reasonable care" is operationalized across the NIST SP 800-160 Vol.~2 resilience lifecycle. The resulting maps show that obligations are concentrated in the Anticipate phase and emphasize administrative compliance, while Withstand and Recover phases rely heavily on delegated references to IT-focused control catalogs that are poorly aligned with physics-based hazards. We identify three major disconnects: miscalibrated delegated standards, recovery defined as notification rather than engineered navigation, and uneven adaptation requirements across sectors. We then propose a modernized standard of care anchored in hazard-specific traceability, structured assurance cases, and cyber resiliency engineering. Finally, we recommend that federal policy pair these engineering obligations with targeted incentives so that resilient architectures for critical infrastructure become a viable business decision rather than an unfunded expectation.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [Identifiability Without Gaussianity: Symbolic World Models and Near-Infinite Temporal Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12471)
*Seth Dobrin,Łukasz Chmiel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明，Klindt等人提出的JEPA在非高斯物理系统中存在时间一致性极限，而作者提出的物理接地符号架构（PGSA）能突破这一极限，实现近乎无限的时间一致性。核心结论：在非高斯系统中，符号化因果接地是实现长期时间一致性的充分必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Klindt等人证明了JEPA仅在潜变量服从高斯平稳过程时才能实现线性可识别性，这意味着对任何非高斯物理系统，统计世界模型的重构误差会随时间单调增长。作者试图挑战这一结论，证明该限制并非世界模型的固有性质，而是统计对齐机制的artifact。

Method: 提出物理接地符号架构（PGSA），从理论上证明：（1）PGSA对所有物理系统（无论潜分布如何）都能实现精确线性可识别性；（2）PGSA的每步误差仅受数值精度限制；（3）PGSA能维持近乎无限的时间一致性。同时证明统计世界模型在任何非高斯系统中都无法实现该性质。使用Lean 4 + Mathlib4对四个定理进行形式化验证。

Result: PGSA在理论上突破了Klindt等人的高斯边界，实现了非高斯物理系统中的近乎无限时间一致性。四个核心定理已通过Lean 4形式化验证（零漏洞），仅将Klindt等人的逆命题作为外部前提引用。

Conclusion: 符号化接地于世界动力学的因果生成器，是实现近乎无限时间一致性的充分条件；在非高斯系统中，这是唯一条件。该结果明确了统计世界模型与物理接地符号架构之间的根本性差异。

Abstract: Klindt, LeCun, and Balestriero (arXiv:2605.26379) proved that Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) achieve linear identifiability, the linear recovery of the world's true latent variables, if and only if the world's latent dynamics follow a Gaussian, stationary process. This Gaussian boundary implies a fundamental limit on temporal consistency: for any non-Gaussian physical system, the representation error of a statistical World Model grows monotonically with time. We prove that this limit is an artifact of the statistical alignment mechanism, not a property of World Models in general. We introduce the Physics-Grounded Symbolic Architecture (PGSA) and prove three results: (1) a PGSA achieves exact linear identifiability for all physical regimes, regardless of the latent distribution; (2) the per-step error of a PGSA is bounded by numerical precision alone; and (3) as a direct consequence, a PGSA maintains temporal consistency for an unbounded number of transitions, a property we term near-infinite temporal consistency. We further prove that statistical World Models cannot achieve this property for any non-Gaussian system, regardless of model capacity or the volume of training data. The algebraic cores of four of the theorems are formalized in Lean 4 with Mathlib4 v4.31.0 (zero sorry placeholders); the Klindt et al. converse is taken as an external premise. The contrast establishes that symbolic grounding in the causal generator of the world's dynamics is the sufficient condition and, in non-Gaussian regimes, the only condition for near-infinite temporal consistency.

</details>


### [166] [Epistemic Uncertainty Is Not the Reducible Kind](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12646)
*Robin Young*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明标准预测不确定性分类中，将认知不确定性定义为可通过收集更多数据消除的部分，与其用互信息项度量的标准方法在扩展上不一致。通过构造实例，该度量将所有不确定性归为认知类，但增加训练数据量无法减少。可约简性实际上是(不确定性，获取类)配对的性质，传统二分法应细分为三类：偶然不确定性、样本可约简认知不确定性、机制可约简认知不确定性。观测价值的精确等式表明，分布内数据从不减少机制不可约简不确定性，且通常会增加它。集成分歧（广泛使用的认知估计）追踪的实际上是训练过程而非认知项，在一致训练下会坍缩至零（低于正真值），在插值下等于超参数缩放后的初始化噪声。有限样本的证伪检验和种子扫描实验证实了该理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准不确定性分类中，定义（认知不确定性=可通过收集更多数据消除的部分）与标准度量（用互信息项识别）在扩展上不一致。现有方法存在根本性缺陷，需要澄清这一矛盾并建立更精确的分类框架。

Method: 通过构造反例证明定义与度量的不一致性；提出三分法（偶然、样本可约简、机制可约简）；推导观测价值的精确恒等式；理论分析集成分歧行为；设计有限样本证伪检验和种子扫描实验进行验证。

Result: 1) 标准定义与度量在扩展上不一致，存在构造实例使度量将所有不确定性归为认知类但数据无法减少；2) 不确定性可约简性是(不确定性，获取类)配对的性质；3) 分布内数据从不减少机制不可约简不确定性且通常增加之；4) 集成分歧追踪训练过程而非认知项，在一致训练下坍缩至零，在插值下等于超参数缩放初始化噪声；5) 实验验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 标准预测不确定性分类存在根本性缺陷，需要重新定义。推动不确定性分类从二分法向三分法演进，并对集成分歧等流行方法的实际行为提出警示。

Abstract: The standard taxonomy of predictive uncertainty defines epistemic uncertainty as the part removable by collecting more data, while the standard measure identifies it with a mutual-information term. We prove the definition and the measure are extensionally inconsistent. On an explicit construction, the measure assigns all uncertainty to the epistemic class, yet no quantity of training data reduces it. Reducibility is instead a property of the pair (uncertainty, acquisition class), and the dichotomy resolves into three parts: aleatoric, sample-reducible epistemic, and mechanism-reducible epistemic uncertainty. An exact identity for the value of an observation shows that in-distribution data never reduces mechanism-irreducible uncertainty and generically increases it. Ensemble disagreement, the deployed epistemic estimate, tracks the training procedure rather than the epistemic term. It collapses to zero beneath a positive truth under consistent training, and equals hyperparameter-scaled initialization noise under interpolation. A finite-sample falsification test and seed-swept experiments confirm the theory.

</details>


### [167] [Prediction-Powered Causal Inference by Automatic Debiased Machine Learning and Semi-Supervised Riesz Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12892)
*Masahiro Kato*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出预测驱动因果推断（PPCI）框架，在半监督设置下利用未标记辅助变量提升因果/结构参数估计效率，推导了有效影响函数与效率界，并基于去偏机器学习（DML）构建了两种渐近有效估计方法（EE-DML-PPCI和TMLE-DML-PPCI），同时开发了半监督广义Riesz回归用于Riesz表示子的估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在因果推断中，标记数据通常稀少且获取成本高，而大量未标记辅助变量（如代理变量、预测结果）可能包含有价值信息。现有半监督学习方法多聚焦于预测任务，缺乏对因果参数半监督有效估计的系统研究。本文旨在填补这一空白，利用未标记数据提升因果推断的效率。

Method: 1）推导半监督设置下因果参数的有效影响函数与半参数效率界；2）将有效影响函数与去偏机器学习（DML）框架结合，提出两种估计量：基于估计方程的EE-DML-PPCI和基于 Targeted Learning 的TMLE-DML-PPCI；3）开发半监督广义Riesz回归（Semi-supervised Generalized Riesz Regression）用于估计有效影响函数中的Riesz表示子，并提供收敛速率保证。

Result: 两种估计量（EE-DML-PPCI和TMLE-DML-PPCI）的渐近方差均达到推导的效率界，证明了半监督设置下利用未标记辅助变量可以严格降低渐近方差。半监督广义Riesz回归估计器具有理论保证的收敛速率。

Conclusion: PPCI框架成功将半监督学习与因果推断结合，证明了未标记辅助数据在因果参数估计中的价值。有效影响函数同时具有Neyman正交性，为双重稳健估计提供了基础。该方法在标记数据有限但未标记数据丰富的实际场景中具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: This study investigates semiparametric efficient estimation of causal and structural parameters in a semi-supervised setting. In our setting, unlabeled auxiliary regressors are available in addition to labeled observations consisting of outcomes and regressors. Our goal is to construct estimators of causal and structural parameters whose asymptotic variances are smaller than those of estimators constructed using only labeled data. We refer to this framework as prediction-powered causal inference (PPCI). We first derive the efficient influence function and the efficiency bound, which imply that the use of auxiliary regressors can attain a smaller asymptotic variance than the efficiency bound attainable from labeled observations alone. Then, by combining the efficient influence function with the debiased machine learning (DML) framework, we propose methods that we call DML-PPCI. If we construct an estimating-equation estimator, we refer to the method as EE-DML-PPCI; if we construct a targeted-learning estimator, we refer to the method as TMLE-DML-PPCI. The asymptotic variances of both estimators match our derived efficiency bound. In the construction of the estimators, estimation of the efficient influence function plays an important role. In our study, the efficient influence function is also a Neyman orthogonal score, which depends on the Riesz representer and the regression function. For Riesz representer estimation, we develop semi-supervised generalized Riesz regression with convergence rate guarantees.

</details>


### [168] [Robust State-Conditional Feature-Weighted Jump Models for Temporal Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13146)
*Federico P. Cortese,Alessio Farcomeni*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种鲁棒的特征加权跳跃模型用于时变聚类，通过Tukey双权损失函数增强鲁棒性，并引入惩罚项平滑状态间过渡，同时允许特征权重随状态变化。模拟和实证（科索沃冲突死亡人数、12个欧洲国家宏观经济）表明该方法能准确恢复聚类序列并识别关键特征，尤其在存在异常值时优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间依赖聚类方法在存在异常值时表现不佳，且难以同时实现状态间特征权重的自适应分配与过渡平滑性的控制。

Method: 提出鲁棒特征加权跳跃模型：使用Tukey双权损失函数抵御异常值影响；引入惩罚项促进时间上状态过渡的平滑性；通过额外参数控制特征权重在不同状态间的变异性，使每个状态能够赋予特征不同的重要性。

Result: 模拟实验表明该方法能准确恢复真实聚类序列并可靠识别相关特征，在存在异常值时性能优于对比方法。两个实证应用（科索沃冲突死亡人数、12个欧洲国家宏观经济）进一步验证了模型的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒特征加权跳跃模型在时变聚类任务中，通过结合鲁棒损失、平滑惩罚和状态自适应特征加权，有效处理了异常值干扰和特征重要性动态变化问题。

Abstract: We propose a robust feature-weighted jump model for time-dependent clustering. A penalty is used to encourage smoothness of transitions over time, while robustness is achieved through the use of a Tukey's biweight loss function. An additional parameter controls the variability of feature weights across states, allowing the model to assign state-specific relevance to each feature. We illustrate in simulation how the method accurately recovers the true cluster sequence and reliably identifies relevant features, outperforming competing approaches, particularly in the presence of outliers. We conclude with two empirical applications, one on the number of conflict-related homicides in Kosovo in the period 1998-2000, and another on macroeconomic performance of twelve European countries in the period 1949-2024.

</details>


### [169] [ProtoX-AD: Self-Explainable Time Series Anomaly Detection and Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13277)
*Aitor Sánchez-Ferrera,Elisabeth Wetzer,Kristoffer Wickstrøm,Michael Kampffmeyer,Robert Jenssen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: ProtoX-AD是一个基于原型自解释框架，用于自监督时间序列异常检测，能在保持检测性能的同时提供可解释的异常特征分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自监督时间序列异常检测方法虽然性能优秀，但缺乏可解释性，无法说明被标记异常的具体特征。

Method: ProtoX-AD学习变换感知的潜在表示和可解释的原型，通过基于原型的解释来识别不同的异常轮廓，并系统分析变换设计对检测性能和可解释性的影响。

Result: 在合成和真实数据集上，ProtoX-AD实现了与黑盒方法相当的检测性能，同时比现有的可解释基线提供更一致且语义上有意义的解释。

Conclusion: ProtoX-AD有效解决了自监督TSAD中的可解释性问题，在不牺牲检测性能的前提下提供了可理解的异常解释。

Abstract: Recent advances in time series anomaly detection (TSAD) have highlighted the effectiveness of self-supervised classification-based approaches. These methods apply transformations to normal training samples, training a classifier to recognize transformation-specific patterns that help identify anomalies through increased classification errors. Despite their strong performance, a significant challenge is their lack of explainability, as they provide limited insight into the characteristics of flagged anomalies. To address this limitation, we propose ProtoX-AD, a prototype-based self-explainable framework for self-supervised TSAD. ProtoX-AD learns transformation-aware latent representations alongside interpretable prototypes, enabling both accurate anomaly detection and the identification of distinct anomalous profiles through prototype-based explanations. Additionally, it allows for systematic analysis of how transformation design impacts detection performance and explainability. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that ProtoX-AD achieves detection performance comparable to its black-box counterparts while offering more consistent and semantically meaningful explanations than existing explainable baselines. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/Aitorzan3/ProtoX-AD.

</details>


### [170] [Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13295)
*Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees for Stratified Classification Cristian Buoncompagni,Stefano Pellegrino,Giulia Vannucci,Roberta Siciliano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees (SLBT) 的概率机器学习框架，用于在存在分层因素（如时间、空间或人口统计变量）的情况下构建可解释的分类树，该框架使用基于模型的拆分规则（同时潜预算模型），并通过最小二乘法估计参数，支持交互式可视化与剪枝，最后应用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症中性别差异的研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可解释人工智能时代，单一决策树因其易于解释而受到关注。然而，标准树生长过程无法优化条件拆分规则，尤其是在存在分层因素（如时间、空间或人口统计变量）作为控制变量或潜在混杂因素时，需要一种能处理此类结构的模型化拆分方法。

Method: 提出 Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees (SLBT) 框架，采用基于模型的拆分规则：子节点被解释为同时混合模型（如 Simultaneous Latent Budget Model 及其约束版本）的潜在成分，拟合于父节点。混合参数驱动不同组别的观测分配到子节点，潜预算参数更新控制变量各水平的响应类别分布。参数通过最小二乘法估计，并采用神经网络视角建模。提供交互式可视化工具（节点与路径解释辅助、视觉剪枝、决策树选择），并设计了应对不平衡响应类分布的措施。

Result: 该方法被应用于肌萎缩侧索硬化症（ALS）中性别相关的疾病进展差异研究，验证了其有效性。SLBT 库及相关树算法已在 GitHub 上公开。

Conclusion: SLBT 为存在分层因素（作为控制变量或潜在混杂因素）的分类问题提供了一种可解释的树模型方法，能够生成信息丰富的树结构，并支持交互式可视化和剪枝选择，具有良好的实用性和可解释性。

Abstract: In the era of Explainable Artificial Intelligence, there is a renewed focus on single trees for their ease of interpretation. This paper introduces Simultaneous Latent Budget Trees, a probabilistic machine learning framework for classification trees in the presence of a stratification factor such as a temporal, spatial, or demographic variable, acting as a control variable or potential confounder. Standard tree growth procedures are not designed to optimize a conditional split rule. A model-based split rule is proposed in which child nodes are interpreted as latent components of a simultaneous mixture model, such as the Simultaneous Latent Budget Model and its constrained versions, fitted to the parent node. Mixing parameters drive the observations, differently for each group, to the child nodes whereas latent budgets parameters update the response classes profile of each level of the control variable. Parameters are estimated by least squares considering a neural network perspective of the model. An informative tree structure can be interactively visualized with interpretation aids on the node and the paths, including visual pruning and decision tree selection procedure. Suitable measures are proposed to handle an unbalanced response class distribution. The proposed methodology is applied to investigate gender-related differences in disease progression of Amyotrophic Lateral Sclerosis. The SLBT library with the various tree-based algorithms is available in the linked GitHub repository.

</details>


### [171] [Majority-of-Three is Optimal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13614)
*Divit Rawal,Nikita Zhivotovskiy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票在可实现PAC学习中是最优的，简化了之前的投票学习算法和概率分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前人提出的投票学习算法（如Hanneke的算法和Green Larsen的装袋分析）结构复杂、概率分析繁琐，本文希望找到一种更简单的投票方案，同时仍能保证最优学习性能。

Method: 本文通过简短证明，展示了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票机制在可实现PAC设定下的最优性。该方法仅需三个独立训练的、在训练集上一致的分类器进行投票，即可达到最优学习率。

Result: 证明了三个独立一致分类器的多数投票是最优学习器，简化了算法结构（从复杂投票方案简化为最简单的三分类器投票）和概率分析。

Conclusion: 最简单的投票方案（三个独立一致分类器的多数投票）即可在可实现PAC学习中达到最优，无需更复杂的投票机制。

Abstract: We give a short proof that the majority vote of three independent consistent classifiers is an optimal learner in the realizable PAC setting. This proves optimality for the simplest voting scheme, while simplifying both the algorithmic structure and the probabilistic analysis of previous voting learners, including the algorithm of S. Hanneke and the analysis of bagging by K. Green Larsen.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Phase transition of Schott's statistic for high-dimensional heavy-tailed data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12943)
*Hantao Chen,Guangming Pan,Cheng Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Schott统计量在α正则变化总体下的渐近分布，发现了相变现象：当α>3（轻尾）时，放松了对p/n比率的约束；当α<3（重尾）时，导出了新的渐近正态分布，其方差显式依赖于α。同时提出了渐近方差的一致估计量，使标准化检验统计量适用于未知位置参数和所有α>0的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对Schott统计量在α正则变化总体下的渐近分布理解有限，特别是在不同尾部行为（轻尾与重尾）下的相变现象以及p/n比率约束方面存在空白，需要更一般的理论框架。

Method: 采用理论渐近分析的方法，针对数据维度p、样本量n和正则变化系数α构建一般框架，分别研究轻尾（α>3）和重尾（α<3）情形下的渐近分布，并构造渐近方差的一致估计量。

Result: 识别出相变现象：α>3时获得α自由渐近分布且放松了p/n比率约束；α<3时获得新的渐近正态分布，方差显式依赖α。提出了适用于所有α>0和未知位置参数的渐近方差一致估计量。

Conclusion: 该研究完善了Schott统计量在正则变化总体下的渐近理论，为实际应用中不同尾部特征数据的相关性检验提供了理论支持。

Abstract: Consider Schott's statistic (Schott, 2005) defined as the squared Frobenius norm of the sample correlation matrix for data from $α$-regularly varying populations. We investigate its asymptotic distribution in a general framework characterized by data dimension p, sample size n, and regularly varying coefficients $α$. In particular, we identify a phase transition phenomenon in the asymptotic behavior. For light-tailed populations ($α> 3$), we revisit the $α$-free asymptotic distribution but relax the constraint on the ratio of $p/n$. For heavy-tailed populations ($α< 3$), we derive a new asymptotic normal distribution whose variance explicitly depends on $α$. We also propose a consistent estimator for the asymptotic variance such that the standardized Schott's test statistic remains applicable for unknown location parameters and all $α> 0$.

</details>


### [173] [Characterizing metric-space-valued processes: separating classes and weak invariance principles for measure-theoretic inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13084)
*Anne van Delft*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究取值于缺乏拓扑向量空间结构的度量空间中的随机过程，利用球性质（Borel概率测度由球上取值唯一确定）建立测度论推断框架，推导时间依赖随机测度的一致收敛及非平稳随机场的弱不变原理，并针对小球概率快速衰减导致的极限分布不存在问题，提出L^p替代方案；对于可等距Hilbert嵌入的空间，则建立退化与非退化多参数U-过程的极限理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有核方法在无法等距嵌入Hilbert空间的度量空间中存在几何失真问题，且此类空间中随机过程的极限理论尚不完善，缺乏统一的推断框架。本文旨在利用更广泛度量空间类（满足球性质）的结构特性，绕过等距嵌入的限制，建立系统的测度论推断方法。

Method: (1) 证明满足等距Hilbert嵌入的度量空间是满足球性质空间类的严格子类；(2) 利用球作为分离类（Borel概率测度由球上取值唯一确定）构建测度论推断框架；(3) 推导时间依赖随机测度的一致收敛与非平稳随机场的弱不变原理；(4) 针对小概率事件难以建立基于上确界的极限分布，发展L^p替代方法；(5) 对可等距Hilbert嵌入的空间，建立退化与非退化多参数U-过程的极限理论。

Result: (1) 严格划定了满足等距Hilbert嵌入的空间与满足球性质的空间之间的包含关系；(2) 建立了基于球分离类的统一收敛理论；(3) 揭示了依赖性与几何复杂性对样本路径正则性的影响机制；(4) 提出了规避U-过程框架的L^p基替代检验方法；(5) 在多参数U-过程情形下，证明了局部差异检验在动态参数机制下的渐近稳定性。

Conclusion: 本文所提出的框架有效拓展了非欧几何度量空间上随机过程的推断能力，为处理复杂几何结构下的统计推断问题提供了理论支撑。球性质为超越Hilbert嵌入限制提供了关键结构基础，而L^p替代方案则解决了实际应用中极限分布不可行的问题。

Abstract: This article investigates stochastic processes taking values in metric spaces that lack a topological vector space structure, a regime characterized by intricate interplay between topological, geometric, and temporal dependence structures. It is formally established that spaces admitting an isometric Hilbertian embedding constitute a strict subclass within the much broader class of metric spaces possessing the ball property. While traditional kernel methods are susceptible to geometric distortion when the underlying space cannot be isometrically embedded into a Hilbert space, we bypass such limitations by exploiting a fundamental structural property inherent to this broader class; namely, that Borel probability measures are uniquely determined by their values on balls. These separating classes provide the foundation for the subsequently introduced measure-theoretic inference methodology. We derive uniform convergence of a family of time-dependent random measures, alongside weak invariance principles for the corresponding nonstationary random fields. This framework explicitly exposes how dependence and geometric complexity influence sample path regularity. Furthermore, because the rapid decay of small-ball probabilities can prohibit the existence of limiting distributions for supremum-based discrepancy measures, we develop $L^p$-based alternatives. By directly leveraging the introduced convergence results, this approach circumvents the need for higher-order $U$-process formulations. Finally, for spaces that do admit an isometric Hilbertian embedding, and where $U$-processes naturally arise, we establish limit theory for both degenerate and nondegenerate multi-parameter $U$-processes, and demonstrate that local discrepancy tests maintain asymptotic stability under dynamic parameter regimes.

</details>


### [174] [Consistency of variational approximations under bounded Kullback--Leibler divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13230)
*Hien Duy Nguyen,Jacob Westerhout,Thomas Guilmeau,Julyan Arbel*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究变分近似是否继承贝叶斯后验一致性。主要结论是：在度量空间上，如果变分近似分布与目标后验分布之间的KL散度一致有界，且目标后验弱收敛到真实参数处的Dirac测度，则变分序列也是一致的。研究还给出了验证该有界性的简单对数矩条件，并针对光滑广义后验分布进行了说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分方法在贝叶斯推断中被广泛用于近似难以精确计算的后验分布，但变分近似是否能够保持后验一致性这一重要统计性质尚不明确。本文旨在从理论上回答这一问题。

Method: 首先在一般度量空间上证明：若变分近似分布与目标后验序列之间的KL散度一致有界，且目标后验序列是紧的，则变分近似序列也是紧的。进而推出，若目标后验弱收敛到真实参数处的Dirac测度，则KL散度有界的变分序列也是一致的。进一步给出验证KL散度有界性的对数矩条件。

Result: 建立了变分近似继承后验一致性的充分条件：变分近似与目标后验之间的KL散度一致有界。同时给出了便于验证的对数矩条件，并在光滑广义后验分布上进行了应用展示。

Conclusion: 变分近似在KL散度有界的条件下能够继承目标后验的一致性，这为变分方法在贝叶斯推断中的理论可靠性提供了支持。对数矩条件为实际应用中验证该条件提供了便捷工具。

Abstract: Variational methods are widely used to approximate posterior distributions in Bayesian inference when exact computation is infeasible. We study when such approximations inherit posterior consistency. Our first result shows that, on a general metric space, a uniform bound on the Kullback--Leibler divergence from the approximating measures to a tight sequence of target measures forces the approximating sequence to be tight. It follows that if the target posteriors converge weakly to a Dirac mass at the true parameter, then any variational sequence with bounded Kullback--Leibler divergence to the targets is also consistent. We also give simple logarithmic-moment conditions that verify this boundedness condition, and illustrate them for smooth generalised posterior distributions.

</details>


### [175] [Generalization Bounds for Transformer-Based Next-Token Prediction in a Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13280)
*Insung Kong,Niklas Dexheimer,Johannes Schmidt-Hieber*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于扩展的对数双线性语言模型（log-bilinear language model）的文本数据分布，用于分析Transformer架构在预训练中的泛化性能，并推导了深层Transformer网络在该分布下的泛化界，揭示了网络架构、词汇量、文档数量和文档长度等因素的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM预训练理论分析缺乏能够封装文本数据关键特征的合适数据分布，因此需要提出一种更贴近真实文本数据特性的数据生成过程，以严格分析Transformer架构的泛化性能。

Method: 基于自然语言处理中的对数双线性语言模型进行扩展，构建文本数据分布；在此数据生成过程下，对深层Transformer架构推导泛化界（generalization bounds）。

Result: 推导出了深层Transformer架构在该数据分布下的泛化界，明确了泛化性能与网络架构、词汇量、文档数量和文档长度之间的依赖关系。

Conclusion: 通过提出更贴合文本数据特性的分布并进行理论分析，为理解LLM预训练中Transformer的统计泛化行为提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: A refined statistical understanding of LLM pre-training requires the analysis of the transformer architecture for data distributions that encapsulate key characteristics of text data. To address this, we propose a text data distribution based on an extension of the log-bilinear language model from the natural language processing literature. For this data generating process, we derive generalization bounds for deep transformer architectures, highlighting the dependence on the network architecture, the vocabulary size, the number of documents and the document length.

</details>


### [176] [Asymptotic regimes for maximum likelihood estimation in the Ewens--Pitman model: When the strength parameter matters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13554)
*Filippo Ascolani,Mario Beraha,Stefano Favaro*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了Ewens-Pitman随机分区模型中折扣参数α和强度参数θ的最大似然估计的大样本渐近行为，发现存在四种不同渐近机制，并指出θ在渐近分析中可能起关键作用。通过引入尺度化Ewens-Pitman模型（允许θ随样本量n增长），克服了无限可交换性带来的刚性结构约束，实证表明该扩展模型能更好地拟合真实数据中的频率谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献对Ewens-Pitman模型中(α,θ)的MLE渐近行为研究存在局限，隐含地只关注了两种渐近机制，且忽视了θ在渐近分析中的潜在关键作用。作者希望揭示完整的渐近图景，并解决无限可交换性对模型灵活性的限制问题。

Method: 在数据生成机制温和假设下，对Ewens-Pitman模型MLE进行大样本渐近分析，推导出四种不同渐近机制；提出尺度化Ewens-Pitman模型，允许θ随样本量n增长以打破无限可交换性带来的结构约束；通过真实数据验证扩展模型的必要性。

Result: 发现四种不同的渐近机制，θ在渐近分析中起关键作用；指出现有文献仅关注其中两种机制，并将此限制归因于无限可交换性施加的刚性约束；尺度化Ewens-Pitman模型能有效克服这一限制，更好地拟合真实数据中的频率谱。

Conclusion: Ewens-Pitman模型的MLE渐近行为远比先前认识到的丰富，θ的作用不可忽视；无限可交换性限制了模型的灵活性，通过尺度化扩展可显著提升模型对真实数据频率谱的拟合能力。

Abstract: We study the large sample asymptotic behaviour of the Maximum Likelihood Estimator of the discount and strength parameters $(α,θ)$ in the Ewens--Pitman model for random partitions, under mild assumptions on the data-generating mechanism. We show that four distinct regimes arise, depending on the limiting behaviour of the frequency spectrum. In particular, in contrast with previous work, we find that $θ$ may play a crucial role asymptotically. We further show that the existing literature implicitly focuses on only two of these regimes, and we relate this restriction to the constraints imposed by infinite exchangeability. Under the latter, indeed, the number of distinct blocks and the frequency spectrum are necessarily tied by a rigid structural relation. We prove that this lack of flexibility can be overcome through what we call the scaled Ewens--Pitman model, in which $θ$ is allowed to grow with the sample size $n$. Finally, we provide empirical evidence from real-world data showing that such extensions are needed to capture frequency spectra that fall outside the classical Ewens--Pitman framework.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [A Two-Parameter Weibull Framework for Diagnosing Transformer Weight Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.18898)
*Tiexin Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将威布尔分布作为诊断Transformer中权重矩阵元素级幅度分布的理论框架。通过对12个模型、7个架构家族的分析，发现FFN和注意力输出投影（传输类）的威布尔形状参数k稳定在[1.186,1.204]区间，而注意力输入投影（选择类）的k值分布则受到存储结构影响。威布尔两个参数分别编码功能类别与训练进度，提供了超越传统聚合统计的细粒度诊断能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多采用聚合统计量（如范数、均值、方差）分析权重演化，但这些统计量无法区分不同模块在训练中的差异化行为，缺乏一个架构无关的理论框架来统一描述权重幅度分布的动态变化。

Method: 将威布尔分布（极值理论的两参数族）作为诊断框架，通过中80%概率图拟合提取形状参数k和尺度参数λ；对每个层、每个组件在每个检查点独立拟合，实现细粒度诊断。分析涵盖Pythia、OLMo-1/2、LLaMA-3、Mistral、Qwen2.5/3等7个架构家族的12个模型条目。

Result: 1) 传输类（FFN+Wo）的k值跨12个模型高度稳定，中位终值在[1.186,1.204]，跨族变异系数仅0.51%；2) 选择类（Wq,Wk）偏离威布尔分布，且偏离程度受Q/K存储方式影响；3) λ参数随训练增长，且在Pythia族内与sqrt(η/λ_wd)强相关（Pearson r=0.94）。

Conclusion: 威布尔分布的形状参数k能标记权重模块的功能类别（传输类vs选择类），尺度参数λ能标记训练进度，两者携带独立信息。该框架为理解Transformer训练动力学提供了架构无关的定量诊断工具。

Abstract: We apply the Weibull distribution -- a two-parameter family from extreme-value theory -- as a diagnostic framework for element-wise weight magnitude distributions in transformers. At initialization, i.i.d. Gaussian weights give |w| ~ HalfNormal, yielding k ~ 1.20 via middle-80% probability-plot fit (the protocol used throughout this work). This anchor makes k a principled, architecture-independent measuring stick for training dynamics; fitting each weight matrix independently at every layer at every checkpoint enables per-component, per-layer, and per-step diagnostics that aggregate statistics cannot resolve.
  Applying this framework to 12 model entries spanning 7 architectural families (Pythia, OLMo-1/2, LLaMA-3, Mistral, Qwen2.5/3) reveals three findings. First, FFN modules and the attention output projection W_o -- the Transmission Class -- fall in a narrow k band: median terminal k in [1.186, 1.204] across 12 entries (cross-family CV = 0.51%), shared across SwiGLU/GeLU activations, Pre-LN/QK-Norm placements, and 70M-14B sizes. Second, the attention input projections W_q, W_k -- the Selection Class -- depart from the Weibull family, with severity shaped by storage: separately-stored Q/K (OLMo-1, OLMo-2) yields k in [0.76, 0.99] (deep); GQA models yield k in [1.10, 1.16] (mild); Pythia's merged W_qkv occupies a transitional zone tracking training budget T/tau monotonically. Third, lambda grows substantially during training and scales with sqrt(eta/lambda_wd) within the Pythia family (Pearson r = 0.94, three Transmission kinds), directionally consistent with Fan et al. (2025). The two parameters carry independent information: k labels the functional class, lambda labels training progress.
  We release npm-weibull-py v0.4 (Python library) and DATABASE_v9_1 at https://github.com/tiexinding/NPM-Weibull-public .

</details>


### [178] [Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Chemotherapy Pharmacokinetics: Benchmarking the Clinical Estimator and Exposing Parameter Identifiability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12658)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对比了物理信息神经网络（PINN）与标准临床基线（非线性最小二乘NLS）及纯数据MLP在化疗药代动力学问题上的表现。在线性二室模型中，PINN与近最优的NLS性能相当且能同时预测组织浓度；在Michaelis-Menten非线性扩展中，NLS因模型误设而失效，PINN则揭示了模型不可辨识性问题，且能整合稀疏组织观测数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物学中存在大量部分可观测问题（如化疗药代动力学中血浆浓度可测但组织浓度不可测），传统方法（如NLS）依赖解析解且无法处理模型误设或不可辨识性问题，而纯数据驱动方法在组织预测上表现差。需要一种既能利用已知物理规律又能处理部分观测的通用方法。

Method: 将PINN应用于线性二室PK模型和Michaelis-Menten非线性二室模型，以血浆数据训练，同时预测组织浓度。对比方法包括NLS（标准临床基线，基于双指数血浆解析解）和纯数据MLP（无物理约束）。在线性问题上评估预测误差；在非线性问题上通过多随机种子实验分析参数可辨识性。

Result: 线性二室：PINN与NLS性能相当（误差仅差小常数倍），同时输出组织曲线；MLP组织预测误差约10倍。Michaelis-Menten：NLS因模型误设返回无意义速率常数；PINN自动收敛到k12→0的盆地，表明模型不可辨识。加入两个稀疏组织观测后，PINN恢复k21误差<1%，Vmax、Km在1个标准差内，k12从0.02恢复至0.82（仍偏低2σ）。

Conclusion: PINN并非要击败NLS，而是提供一种统一框架：在标准问题上匹配教科书级估计器，在模型误设时暴露结构可辨识性问题，并能无缝整合异质观测数据（血浆+组织），而传统闭式方法无法实现。

Abstract: Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) are an attractive tool for partial-observation problems in biology, where the governing dynamics are known but some compartments cannot be measured. Chemotherapy pharmacokinetics (PK) is a clean instance: drug concentration in plasma is routinely measured, but concentration in tissue -- which determines tumour kill and off-target toxicity -- is not. We benchmark a PINN against the standard clinical baseline (nonlinear least-squares on the analytical biexponential plasma solution, hereafter NLS) and a physics-agnostic neural baseline (a data-only MLP) on two PK problems. On the linear two-compartment problem, NLS is near-optimal; the PINN matches it to within a small constant factor while also producing the tissue curve in a single training pass, whereas the data-only MLP fails on tissue by roughly 10x. On a Michaelis-Menten extension (saturable elimination), the biexponential closed form no longer exists, so NLS is mis-specified and silently returns meaningless rate constants. The PINN instead exposes a deeper fact: the Michaelis-Menten two-compartment model is non-identifiable from plasma alone, and the PINN reports this honestly by converging to a basin with k12 -> 0. Adding two sparse tissue observations largely resolves identifiability: across five seeds the PINN recovers k21 to within 1% of truth and Vmax, Km to within one standard-deviation bar, while k12 moves in the correct direction (0.02 -> 0.82) but remains ~2 sigma below truth -- a recovery the closed-form NLS estimator cannot attempt at all, because its biexponential ansatz describes only plasma. Our claim is not that PINNs beat NLS. It is that PINNs offer a uniform recipe that ties the textbook estimator on the textbook problem, exposes structural identifiability that
  the textbook estimator hides, and absorbs heterogeneous measurements within a single loss.

</details>


### [179] [How Useful is Causal Invariance for Domain Adaptation in Finite-Sample Settings?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12680)
*Julia Kostin,Kasra Jalaldoust,Elias Bareinboim,Samory Kpotufe,Fanny Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨了因果结构知识在何种条件下能够可证明地提升监督域适应（sDA）的性能。在线性回归框架下，分析了基于不变特征子集的候选预测器集合，推导了匹配的上下界，表明有限样本增益受目标风险边际和源估计误差共同控制。当边际足够大时，自适应聚合方法可匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移；反之则无法获得更快的有限样本速率。进一步将边际与线性结构因果模型中的偏移幅度联系起来，并在真实因果基准上验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管因果域泛化在群体层面展示了不变预测器的优势，但这类因果不变性在有限样本场景下（尤其是有少量标记目标样本的监督域适应）能否带来实际增益尚未被充分探索。本文旨在弥补这一理论空白，回答因果知识何时能可证明地改善监督域适应。

Method: 在线性回归设定下，将完全或部分因果知识转化为一组不变或可能不变的特征子集，每个子集对应一个源训练候选预测器。推导了匹配的上界和下界，分析目标风险边际和源估计误差对有限样本性能的影响；提出自适应聚合过程在边际足够大时匹配最佳候选预测器。进一步将边际与线性SCM中结构偏移幅度建立联系。

Result: 推导了匹配的上下界，揭示有限样本增益由目标风险边际和源估计误差共同决定；当边际相对于目标样本量n_Q足够大时，自适应聚合可匹配最佳候选预测器并避免负迁移；当边际过小时，任何算法都无法可靠利用候选集合获得更快的有限样本速率；将边际与线性SCM的结构偏移幅度相连接。

Conclusion: 因果知识在监督域适应中的有限样本增益并非无条件成立，而是取决于目标风险边际与源估计误差之间的相对大小。这一理论为实践应用中因果先验知识的有效利用提供了明确的边界条件。

Abstract: Machine learning models often degrade when they are deployed on a target distribution that differs from the source distributions they were trained on. Recent work in causality-based domain generalization has shown how shared causal structure between domains can induce invariant predictors, e.g., models on a subset of features which have stable risk across structured domain shifts. However, the extent to which such population-level causal invariances can lead to gains in finite-sample settings remains underexplored. In particular, in practice we often have access to a few labeled target samples, a setting called supervised domain adaptation (sDA). In this paper, we explore when (full or partial) causal knowledge can provably improve supervised domain adaptation.
  As a first step, we study linear regression, where full or partial causal knowledge specifies a collection of invariant or possibly invariant feature subsets, each yielding a source-trained candidate predictor. We derive matching upper and lower bounds showing that finite-sample gains are governed by the target-risk margins separating the candidates, together with the finite-source estimation error. When these margins are sufficiently large relative to $n_Q$, an adaptive aggregation procedure can match the best candidate predictor while avoiding negative transfer relative to target-only learning. On the other hand, when the margins are too small, no algorithm can reliably exploit the candidate collection to obtain faster finite-sample rates. We further connect these margins to structural shift magnitude in linear SCMs and validate the theory on real-world causal benchmarks.

</details>


### [180] [Fed-FBD: Federated Functional Block Diversification for Isolation, Privacy, and Surgical Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12679)
*Weijie Chen,Alan B. McMillan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Fed-FBD是一种模块化联邦学习架构，通过将ResNet分解为6个功能块并维护N个颜色变体仓库，实现了块级隔离、隐私保护和机器遗忘三大能力，在保持与FedAvg相近性能的同时（IID准确率差距0.3%-3.1%），有效防御六种对抗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准联邦学习（如FedAvg）将客户端视为黑箱，缺乏隔离对抗性贡献者、审计每客户端影响、以及实现已退出参与者遗忘权的机制。

Method: 将ResNet骨干分解为6个功能块（stem、四个残差组、分类头），维护N个颜色变体仓库，每个变体由独立追踪和贡献者标记的块组装而成，实现块级隔离。

Result: 在六个MedMNIST-2D数据集、PathMNIST和CIFAR-10上，Fed-FBD在IID场景下准确率差距仅0.3%-3.1%，在Dirichlet alpha=1.0非IID场景下差距0.8%-4.0%，可抵御六种对抗攻击且使受污染块的干净颜色AUC漂移不超过±0.01。

Conclusion: Fed-FBD以较小的性能代价换取了块级隔离、隐私保护和高效机器遗忘三大能力，为联邦学习提供了安全性和隐私性的有效保障。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without sharing raw patient data, but standard approaches such as FedAvg treat each client as a black box and provide no mechanism for isolating an adversarial contributor, auditing per-client influence, or honoring a departed participant's right to be forgotten. We present Fed-FBD (Federated Functional Block Diversification), a modular federated architecture that decomposes a ResNet backbone into six functional blocks (the stem, four residual groups, and the classification head) and maintains a warehouse of N color variants, each assembled from independently tracked and contributor-stamped blocks. Fed-FBD provides three capabilities absent in FedAvg: (i) architecturally guaranteed block-level isolation, so that an adversarial or mislabelled client cannot contaminate the clean colous; (ii) privacy-by-design, where membership inference advantage is already indistinguishable from chance before any privacy mechanism is applied; and (iii) surgical machine unlearning of a departed participant's contribution at sub-second cost and without retraining. Experiments on six MedMNIST-2D datasets, PathMNIST at 224x224, and CIFAR-10 show that Fed-FBD trades a modest 0.3%-3.1% IID accuracy gap on the adequately sized datasets for these guarantees, remains within 0.8%-4.0% of FedAvg at Dirichlet alpha=1.0 on three of four datasets, and confines all six adversarial attacks we study to the poisoned client's own blocks with at most +/-0.01 AUC drift on the clean colors.

</details>


### [181] [Two-Layer Linear Auto-Regressive Models Estimate Latent States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12691)
*Yahya Sattar,Sunmook Choi,Leo Maynard-Zhang,Yassir Jedra,Maryam Fazel,Sarah Dean*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明，在部分观测线性 dynamical systems 数据上通过经验风险最小化训练的双层线性自回归模型，能够自然地学习近似卡尔曼滤波，其学到的隐表示与最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计等价（相差一个相似变换）。主要贡献包括：证明卡尔曼滤波可被自回归模型以有界截断误差近似；证明双层优化景观是良性的（所有驻点要么是严格鞍点要么是全局最小值）；提供预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复的有限样本保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解自回归模型为何以及如何学习到潜在的隐表示，目前仍是一个开放的理论问题。本文旨在从理论上解释这一现象，特别是针对部分观测线性动态系统场景下双层线性自回归模型的学习行为。

Method: 理论分析结合数值仿真。具体思路：(1) 建立卡尔曼滤波与有界截断误差的自回归近似之间的关系；(2) 分析双层线性自回归模型的非凸优化景观，证明其驻点性质；(3) 推导预测误差、参数估计误差和隐状态恢复的有限样本保证。

Result: (1) 卡尔曼滤波可以被自回归模型以有界截断误差近似；(2) 双层模型的优化景观良性，所有驻点要么是严格鞍点要么是全局最小值；(3) 提供了预测误差、参数估计误差以及隐状态恢复的有限样本理论保证；(4) 数值仿真验证了理论结果，表明自回归模型的隐表示能够恢复状态估计。

Conclusion: 自回归模型在部分观测线性动态系统数据上训练时，其隐表示自然地逼近最优卡尔曼滤波的状态估计。这一发现为理解自回归模型学习隐含表征的内在机制提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Auto-regressive models have emerged as powerful tools for sequential data, from language to video. Understanding how and why these models learn latent representations remains an open theoretical question. In this work, we demonstrate that when trained by empirical risk minimization on data from partially observed linear dynamical systems, two-layer linear auto-regressive models naturally learn to approximate Kalman filtering. In particular, we show that the learned hidden representation coincides, up to a similarity transformation, with the state estimates produced by the optimal (Kalman) filter, even though the model has no explicit knowledge of the underlying dynamics or state. The result follows from three main insights. First, we establish that the Kalman filter is well approximated by an auto-regressive model with bounded truncation error. Second, we show that despite non-convexity, the two-layer optimization landscape is benign, i.e., all stationary points are either strict saddles or global minima. Finally, as our main contributions, we provide finite-sample guarantees on prediction error, parameter estimation error, and latent state recovery. Numerical simulations support the theoretical results and demonstrate that the latent representations of auto-regressive models recover state estimates.

</details>


### [182] [Detecting Functional Memorization in Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12764)
*Matthieu Meeus,Anil Ramakrishna,Matthew Grange,Zheng Xu,Luca Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 大语言模型在代码生成中存在"功能记忆"现象：即使生成的代码文本不同，其功能逻辑可能仍源于训练数据。实验通过构建反事实场景（对比暴露/未暴露目标代码的模型），使用Python函数签名进行提示，结合文本相似度和功能相似度（LLM评判+执行验证）测量，发现功能记忆显著存在，表明现有文本重叠审计指标不足以检测此类记忆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要从文本重叠角度审计训练数据是否可从模型输出中恢复，但代码具有"功能等价但文本不相似"的特性，因此需要探究模型是否记忆了代码的功能逻辑而不仅仅是文本。

Method: 构建反事实场景：对比中期训练模型（已暴露目标代码）与预训练参考模型（未暴露）；使用Python函数签名进行提示；同时测量文本相似度和功能相似度（采用LLM-as-a-judge和基于执行的评估）。

Result: 实验清晰展示了功能记忆的存在，即模型能够复现训练数据中的功能逻辑，即使文本上不相似。

Conclusion: 需要超越文本重叠的审计指标来检测代码生成中的功能记忆现象。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate code at scale. Meanwhile, prior work has investigated whether training data may be recoverable from model outputs, by auditing the textual overlap between training examples and model generations. Code, however, can be functionally equivalent while textually dissimilar. In this work, we study functional memorization: extraction of functional logic beyond what verbatim metrics detect. We construct a counterfactual setup for Olmo-3-32B, comparing a midtrained model (exposed to target code) against a pretrained reference (not exposed). We prompt both models with Python function signatures and measure both textual and functional similarity (i.e., LLM-as-a-judge, execution-based). Our results show clear evidence of functional memorization, highlighting the need for auditing metrics that go beyond textual overlap.

</details>


### [183] [Reliability of Probabilistic Emulation of Physical Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12997)
*Sam F. Greenbury,Radka Jersakova,Paolo Conti,Marjan Famili,Christopher Iliffe Sprague,Edwin Brown,Jason D. McEwen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统性比较了两种主流概率预测方法——生成模型（扩散/流匹配）与CRPS训练的确定性集成模型——在二维时空物理系统中的应用，发现CRPS集成在不确定性校准、推理速度和自回归滚动预测方面均优于或媲美生成模型，并发布了AutoCast和AutoSim工具包。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型与CRPS集成方法在概率物理预测中均有良好表现，但两者在不确定性可靠性的系统评估方面存在空白，本文旨在填补这一评估缺口。

Method: 在匹配模型规模和计算预算的条件下，构建统一评估框架，对两类方法在多种二维时空物理系统上进行单步预测和自回归滚动预测，通过预测区间经验覆盖评估不确定性可靠性，同时考虑准确性和计算效率指标。

Result: CRPS集成在不确定性校准上更可靠，推理速度显著更快；生成模型在原始空间训练时覆盖性能与CRPS集成相当但推理延迟大；潜空间CRPS集成性能退化不明显；两者预测准确性均良好。

Conclusion: CRPS集成是物理系统概率预测的更优选择，尤其在需要可靠不确定性和高效推理的场景；同时发布了模块化框架AutoCast和数据集生成包AutoSim以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Two dominant approaches have emerged for generating probabilistic forecasts of physical systems: generative models, such as diffusion or flow matching; and ensembles of deterministic models with stochasticity injected, trained using the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) loss. While both approaches have demonstrated strong predictive accuracy, the reliability of their uncertainties has not been systematically assessed. We address this gap by developing a framework to evaluate both approaches across diverse 2D spatiotemporal physical systems, under matched model size and computational budget. We assess the reliability of probabilistic emulation by inspecting the empirical coverage of predictive intervals, while also considering accuracy and computational efficiency metrics. CRPS-trained ensembles typically achieve more reliable uncertainties on both single-step prediction and autoregressive rollouts, demonstrating better coverage than the standard alternative of training generative models in a latent space. Moreover, the CRPS approach offers significantly faster inference. When generative models are trained in ambient rather than a compressed latent space, which is often infeasible for high-dimensional problems, they exhibit comparable coverage to CRPS-trained ensembles, though with substantially larger inference latency. In contrast, when CRPS-trained ensembles are trained in latent space they do not show a marked degradation in coverage with respect to ambient space. Both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles demonstrate good predictive accuracy. To facilitate future research and application, we release AutoCast, a modular framework implementing both generative models and CRPS-trained ensembles, alongside AutoSim, a flexible dataset generation package for rapid prototyping.

</details>


### [184] [PolicyGuard: Towards Test-time and Step-level Adversary Defense for Reinforcement Learning Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12896)
*Junfeng Guo Heng Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PolicyGuard，一种基于高斯过程后验方差的测试时步级后门防御方法，通过在单个时间步上计算不确定性来检测RL智能体中的后门攻击，在7个RL游戏中取得了SOTA检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL后门防御方法要么需要访问智能体内部参数，要么仅在模型或轨迹级别操作，或仅适用于特定攻击类型，缺乏通用的细粒度步级防御方案。

Method: PolicyGuard利用高斯过程(GP)后验方差，通过自适应生成伪轨迹使得能够在单个时间步上计算不确定性，从而在测试时检测恶意行为。

Result: 在7个RL游戏上，PolicyGuard在大多数情况下达到SOTA检测性能，对基于扰动的攻击平均AUROC为0.856，对对抗智能体攻击平均AUROC为0.859。

Conclusion: PolicyGuard作为一种测试时步级后门防御方法，能够在不依赖内部参数的前提下有效检测多种类型后门攻击，并提供了理论依据解释其有效性。

Abstract: While real-world applications of reinforcement learning (RL) are becoming increasingly popular, the security of RL systems deserve more attention and exploration. In particular, recent work has revealed that RL agents are vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where a victim agent behaves normally under standard conditions but executes malicious actions when a specific trigger is activated. Existing backdoor defenses for RL either require access to the agent's internal parameters, operate only at the model or trajectory level, or are limited to specific attack types. To ensure the security of RL agents, we propose \texttt{PolicyGuard}, a \textit{test-time step-level} backdoor defense which leverages Gaussian Process (GP) posterior variance and adapts pseudo trajectories to enable uncertainty computation for individual time step. Besides, we also provide theoretical foundations to explain the efficacy of GP posterior variance. Extensive experiments across seven RL games demonstrate that PolicyGuard achieves state-of-the-art detection performance in most cases, with average AUROC of 0.856 for perturbation-based attacks and 0.859 for adversary-agent attacks.

</details>


### [185] [Towards More General Control of Diffusion Models Using Jeffrey Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13240)
*Raphaël Razafindralambo,Rémy Sun,Frédéric Precioso,Jes Frellsen,Pierre-Alexandre Mattei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Jeffrey guidance框架，通过Jeffrey条件规则扩展扩散模型的控制能力，实现比标准指导更灵活的目标分布操控，在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上降低FID，并在CelebA-HQ上实现属性间的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型在采样时可通过指导控制输出，但超出简单条件采样场景时，目标分布通常仅通过采样规则或启发式能量函数隐式定义，缺乏一个统一且原则性的框架来处理这些复杂情况。

Method: 提出Jeffrey guidance框架，利用Jeffrey条件规则更新边际分布使其朝向预设目标，同时保持条件结构并最小化对联合分布的扰动。具体应用于目标嵌入分布（如Inception嵌入）和属性间独立性约束。

Result: 在CIFAR-10和FFHQ上以Inception嵌入为目标，显著降低FID；在CelebA-HQ上成功实现无条件扩散模型在属性间的公平性控制。

Conclusion: Jeffrey guidance为扩散模型提供了一种原则性的扩展框架，能够处理标准指导无法表达的应用场景，有效提升生成质量和可控性。

Abstract: A key strength of diffusion models lies in their flexibility, since their outputs can be controlled at sampling time through guidance. However, beyond simple cases such as conditional sampling, the target distribution is often left implicit, defined only through a sampling rule or a heuristic energy function. To address this, we propose Jeffrey guidance, a principled framework that extends diffusion-model control to applications beyond what standard guidance can express. It leverages Jeffrey's rule of conditioning to update marginal distributions towards a prescribed target, preserving the conditional structure and minimally perturbing the joint distribution. We first demonstrate Jeffrey guidance by targeting a prescribed embedding distribution. With Inception embeddings as the target, this leads to substantial reductions in FID on both CIFAR-10 and FFHQ. We further apply Jeffrey guidance to fairness on CelebA-HQ, updating an unconditional diffusion model to enforce independence between attributes.

</details>


### [186] [Accelerating Speculative Diffusions via Block Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13426)
*Alexander Soen,Hisham Husain,Valentin De Bortoli,Arnaud Doucet*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的推测性采样机制，将LLM中的块验证技术适配到扩散模型，实现了无需训练的自我推测解码器（Free Drafter），在连续空间中高效采样残差分布，相比现有方法可获得最高6.3%的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的推测性解码方法在将LLM中的推测采样机制适配到连续扩散模型时存在困难，因为连续空间中残差分布的采样效率低下，现有方法要么计算效率低，要么依赖替代方案。

Method: 提出了一种适用于扩散模型的原始推测采样机制的高效实现方案，将LLM中的块验证技术适配到扩散模型；并形式化了一种无需训练的自我推测解码器（Free Drafter）。

Result: 块验证技术可证明地提高草稿的接受率；Free Drafter相比现有推测性方法可带来最高6.3%的加速，且无需额外训练，仅带来可忽略的额外开销。

Conclusion: 该工作成功将推测采样机制无缝适配到扩散模型，并通过块验证提升了采样效率，Free Drafter作为一种无需训练的自推测方法，在性能和实用性上具有优势。

Abstract: Speculative decoding speeds up LLM inference by using a draft model to generate tokens, with an acceptance-rejection scheme that ensures that the output matches the target distribution. Adapting this to continuous diffusions is difficult because speculative sampling requires drawing from a residual distribution. While straightforward in discrete spaces, efficiently sampling this residual in continuous space is non-trivial. Consequently, existing diffusion adaptations either use computationally inefficient sampling techniques or rely on an alternative scheme. In this work, we introduce a novel scheme that efficiently implements the original speculative sampling mechanism for diffusion models. Our approach offers a critical advantage over current methods: it enables us to adapt block verification from LLMs to diffusions -- which provably improves the acceptance rate of drafts. Furthermore, we formalize and analyze the Free Drafter, a heuristic self-speculative drafter for diffusions that requires no training. By enabling block verification, our Free Drafter yields up to a 6.3% speedup over existing speculative methods with no additional training and negligible overhead beyond the existing parallel verification pass.

</details>


### [187] [Learning with Simulators: No Regret in a Computationally Bounded World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.13576)
*Sasha Voitovych,Abhishek Shetty,Noah Golowich,Alexander Rakhlin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可模拟过程"框架，证明当学习者能访问数据生成过程的模拟器时，即使数据高度依赖，也能恢复与独立数据相同的VC维泛化保证，并展示了条件采样的统计与计算优势，最终给出一个能同时学习所有有界多项式时间可采样过程的单一算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习理论中大多数泛化结果依赖于数据独立性假设，而强依赖数据下的结果非常有限。为弥合这一差距，作者希望探索在弱假设下（允许任意复杂和依赖的过程）如何恢复经典学习保证。

Method: 引入"可模拟过程"框架：学习者可访问一个逼近真实数据分布（可能任意复杂且依赖）的模拟器。利用该模拟器，推导出与经典独立数据设定相同的VC维依赖误差界；进一步分析条件采样的能力，展示统计与计算优势；最后提出单一算法，利用有界多项式时间Kolmogorov复杂度控制遗憾，同时学习所有此类过程下的任意VC类。

Result: 1) 在可模拟过程框架下，即使数据高度依赖，仍能恢复与独立数据相同的VC维泛化保证；2) 条件采样在此设定中具有严格的统计和计算优势；3) 存在单一算法可同时学习任意VC类在所有有界多项式时间可采样过程下的概念，遗憾由时间有界Kolmogorov复杂度控制。

Conclusion: 该工作显著拓宽了经典PAC模型，证明在依赖数据下通过模拟器访问也能获得强泛化保证，为学习理论中最小假设问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: Understanding the minimal assumptions necessary for generalization is the fundamental question in learning theory. Unfortunately, most results rely heavily on independence (or some proxy thereof) of the data-generating process, while results for strongly dependent data are far more limited. Towards addressing this gap, we introduce the framework of simulatable processes, where the learner has access to a simulator that approximates the distribution generating the data (which may be an arbitrarily complex and dependent process). Surprisingly, given access to such a simulator, we show that we can recover the same learning guarantees as in the classical setting with independent data, namely, error bounds that depend on the VC dimension. Further, we use this framework to study the power of conditional sampling and show strict statistical and computational advantages in this setting. As a highlight of our framework, we exhibit a single algorithm that simultaneously learns any given VC class under all processes samplable in bounded polynomial time, with regret controlled by the time-bounded Kolmogorov complexity of the process. This provides a significant conceptual broadening of the classical PAC model.

</details>


### [188] [Quickest Detection of Hallucination Onset: Delay Bounds and Learned CUSUM Statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12476)
*Igor Itkin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将幻觉检测重新定义为快速变化检测问题，利用马尔可夫模型和CUSUM方法，发现基于token的分类指标掩盖了检测延迟这一关键性能维度，而序贯分析可以揭示该结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的token级幻觉检测器使用AUC等分类指标进行评估，但这无法捕捉流式监控场景中最重要的性能指标——检测延迟（从幻觉发生到发出警报之间的token数）。作者希望将幻觉检测问题形式化并建立理论下界。

Method: 将幻觉检测建模为快速变化检测问题，构建忠实/幻觉状态的马尔可夫模型（在RAGTruth数据集上验证），利用Lorden下界理论，使用因果循环标注器实现CUSUM方法，并与线性逐token基线、分类指标进行对比分析。

Result: Lorden下界显示在虚警率0.01时理论最小检测延迟约1.3个token；因果循环标注器（CUSUM）在匹配虚警率下检测延迟为11-13个token，优于线性基线的31个token；优势主要来自更好的逐token分数而非时间累积。学习到的分数仅实现了特征所携带散度的1/4.5，残差为有限时域效应。

Conclusion: 分类指标（如AUC）掩盖了检测延迟这一关键维度，序贯分析可以使之可测量。Donsker-Varadhan型信息率最优性定理解释了数量级差距的原因。

Abstract: Token-level hallucination detectors are evaluated as classifiers, by AUC over all tokens, yet a streaming monitor is judged by its reaction time: the number of tokens that pass between the onset of a hallucination and the alarm. We formulate hallucination onset detection as a quickest change detection problem. A first-order Markov model of the latent faithful/hallucinated state, validated on RAGTruth, places the task inside classical change-point theory and yields Lorden's lower bound on detection delay: about 1.3 tokens at a false-alarm rate of 0.01. We then show that a causal recurrent labeler acts as a CUSUM with a learned increment; at a matched false-alarm rate it detects in 11-13 tokens, against 31 for a linear per-token baseline, and a controlled decomposition attributes most of this advantage to a better per-token score rather than to temporal accumulation. An information-rate optimality theorem of Donsker-Varadhan type explains the remaining order-of-magnitude gap: the learned score realizes only 1/4.5 of the divergence the features carry, a deficit that recalibration cannot remove, with the remainder a finite-horizon effect. Classification metrics conceal this delay structure; sequential analysis makes it measurable

</details>


### [189] [Boltzmann Attention: Learnable Ising Couplings for Cooperative Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12478)
*Gilhan Kim,Daniel K. Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出玻尔兹曼注意力（Boltzmann attention），通过将注意力模式建模为伊辛模型，引入可学习的成对耦合项来显式建模注意力决策间的交互作用，在字符级语言建模和括号匹配任务上优于标准softmax注意力，并展示了量子退火在训练中的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制仅通过查询-键相似度独立计算各位置的相关性，缺乏对注意力决策之间协作或竞争关系的显式建模，限制了其捕捉序列中位置间关联结构的能力。

Method: 将注意力模式推广为基于能量的伊辛模型，在标准数据相关的局域场基础上，添加可学习的成对耦合参数，使模型能表示位置间的相关性。训练可采用精确玻尔兹曼计算或非绝热量子退火采样。

Result: 在字符级语言建模和合成括号匹配任务上，玻尔兹曼注意力始终优于标准softmax注意力，且优势随序列长度增加而扩大。四路消融实验证实改进来源于可学习的成对耦合项。

Conclusion: 显式的位置间交互为基于注意力的序列建模提供了原则性增强，且伊辛公式为基于量子计算的采样策略开辟了自然路径。

Abstract: Attention mechanisms are central to modern sequence models, yet standard attention computes relevance primarily through individual query--key similarities. Although softmax normalization introduces competition among positions, a standard attention layer does not explicitly parameterize learnable interactions between attention decisions. This limits its ability to directly model cooperative or antagonistic co-attention structure within the attention mechanism itself. We propose Boltzmann attention, an energy-based generalization in which attention patterns are governed by an interacting Ising model. The method augments the usual data-dependent local fields with learnable pairwise couplings, allowing the model to represent inter-position correlations beyond those captured by softmax or sigmoid attention. Experiments on character-level language modeling and synthetic bracket matching show that Boltzmann attention consistently improves over standard softmax attention within a standard Transformer architecture, with the advantage becoming more pronounced as sequence length increases. A four-way ablation confirms that the improvement arises from the learnable pairwise couplings. These results suggest that explicit inter-position interactions provide a principled enhancement for attention-based sequence modeling. Moreover, the Ising formulation opens a natural path toward quantum-computing-based sampling strategies: we demonstrate that diabatic quantum annealing provides a practical training method while maintaining competitive performance with exact Boltzmann computation.

</details>


### [190] [ReCal: Reward Calibration for RL-based LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12479)
*Qihang Yu,Hanwen Tong,Zhengqi Zhang,Bo Zheng,Feng Wei,Shengyu Zhang,Zemin Liu,Fei Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCal框架，通过层次化奖励分解和分布感知优化策略，解决RL-based LLM路由中异构任务下奖励信号模糊、优化有偏的问题，在7个数据集上提升路由性能和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RL-based LLM路由方法在异构任务（难度不同、多目标）中，将多个目标聚合为单一标量奖励，导致信用分配模糊和优化冲突；同时奖励信号在不同实例间波动大，引入优化偏差，使模型偏向简单样本而非信息量大的样本。

Method: 1) 层次化奖励分解机制：将奖励分解为多个组件，并对每个组件进行优势估计（advantage estimation）；2) 分布感知优化策略：通过方差感知重加权（variance-aware reweighting）和每数据集归一化（per-dataset normalization）来校准优化变异性。

Result: 在7个数据集上的实验表明，ReCal相比基线方法一致提升了路由性能和训练稳定性。

Conclusion: ReCal通过奖励校准有效解决了多目标冲突和样本间奖励差异问题，为LLM路由提供更稳定的优化信号。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) routing has emerged as an effective paradigm for leveraging the complementary strengths of multiple LLMs through dynamic model and reasoning-strategy selection. Recent reinforcement learning (RL)-based routing methods further improve routing quality by optimizing routing policies from interaction feedback. However, they still struggle to provide informative and comparable learning signals under heterogeneous tasks with varying difficulty. In practice, multiple objectives (e.g., correctness, format behavior) are aggregated into a single scalar reward, leading to ambiguous credit assignment and conflicting optimization signals. Moreover, reward signals exhibit significant variability across instances, where some instances produce higher or more variable rewards, introducing optimization bias that favors trivial samples over informative ones. To address these issues, we propose \textbf{ReCal}, a \textbf{\underline{Re}}ward \textbf{\underline{Cal}}ibration framework for RL-based LLM routing. We first introduce a hierarchical reward decomposition mechanism with component-wise advantage estimation. We further propose a distribution-aware optimization strategy that calibrates optimization variability through variance-aware reweighting and per-dataset normalization. Experiments on seven datasets demonstrate that ReCal consistently improves routing performance, and training stability over baselines. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/ReCal.

</details>


### [191] [Representing Time Series as Structured Programs for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12481)
*Jaeho Kim,Changhun Oh,Seokhyun Lee,Irina Rish,Changhee Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出T2SP方法，将时间序列转化为结构化符号程序表示，使预训练LLM无需微调即可有效推理时间序列，在编辑、描述和问答三个任务上优于原始序列表示，减少了推理时间和失败率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法直接序列化数值或微调LLM来处理时间序列，存在模态不匹配、长序列性能下降和计算开销大等问题。核心动机是设计一种表示方法，让LLM能像处理文本和代码一样高效推理时间序列。

Method: T2SP将时间序列分解为趋势、周期和显著事件，并将其表达为结构化的符号程序格式，无需训练即可将时序结构提取从模型转移到表示本身，使LLM直接利用其已有的推理能力。

Result: 在编辑、描述和问答三项推理任务上，T2SP相比原始字符串表示持续提升了性能，减少了推理时间，并降低了失败率。

Conclusion: T2SP为时间序列与LLM之间提供了一种有效的接口，通过将时序结构外化到表示层，解决了模态不匹配问题，使LLM能更高效地理解时间序列。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated strong reasoning and instruction-following capabilities, making them potentially powerful tools for time-series analysis. However, time series lie outside their native textual modality, raising a fundamental question: how should time series be represented so that LLMs can reason about them effectively? Existing work typically serializes raw numerical sequences or fine-tunes pre-trained LLMs on time-series data. These approaches place the burden of extracting temporal structure directly on the LLM, creating a modality mismatch that often degrades performance on long sequences and introduces substantial computational overhead. In this work, we introduce Time-Series-to-Structured-Program representation (T2SP), a deterministic, training-free method that represents a time series as a structured symbolic program. T2SP decomposes time series into trends, periods, and salient events, expressing them in a program-friendly format aligned with the textual and code-like modalities on which LLMs are natively trained. By shifting temporal-structure extraction from the model to the representation itself, T2SP enables off-the-shelf LLMs to leverage their existing reasoning capabilities for time-series understanding. We evaluate T2SP on three reasoning tasks -- editing, captioning, and question answering -- where it consistently improves performance, reduces reasoning time, and lowers failure rates compared with raw-string representations. Our results demonstrate that T2SP provides an effective interface between time series and LLMs.

</details>


### [192] [Scalable anomaly detection via a univariate Christoffel function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12483)
*Florian Grivet,Didier Henrion,Jean-Bernard Lasserre,Louise Travé-Massuyès*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），通过基于查询点与支持点之间平方距离的方法，解决了Christoffel函数在高维数据中矩阵规模指数增长的可扩展性瓶颈，在ADBench基准上平均精度优于14种SOTA方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Christoffel函数方法虽有坚实数学基础和计算节俭性，但因需逆矩阵规模随数据维度指数增长，导致中高维数据集上无法实用。本文旨在突破该维度限制，同时保留其关键理论性质（开-关支撑二分性与精确支撑形状捕获）。

Method: 提出UCF（单变量Christoffel函数），基于查询点与支持点之间的平方距离构建，避免了原始方法中高维矩阵求逆的计算瓶颈。

Result: 在ADBench基准上的大量实验中，UCF在平均精度（Average Precision）上持续优于14种最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过解决Christoffel函数的可扩展性瓶颈，该工作为异常检测工具箱增添了一种鲁棒、有理论根基且普遍适用的方法。

Abstract: Anomaly detection plays a critical role in identifying unusual patterns across domains such as fraud detection, network intrusion, and system fault diagnosis. Recently, Christoffel function-based methods, rooted in polynomial optimization, have emerged as promising alternatives to deep learning due to their strong mathematical foundations and computational frugality. However, their practical applicability is hindered by the need to invert a matrix whose size grows exponentially with the data dimension, rendering the method intractable even for moderate-dimensional datasets. This paper addresses the dimensionality limitations of Christoffel function-based anomaly detection while preserving its key theoretical properties, i.e., the on-off support dichotomy behavior and the accurate support shape capture. We introduce UCF, a univariate Christoffel function which is based on the squared distance between the query point and the support points. Extensive experiments on the ADBench benchmark demonstrate that UCF consistently outperforms 14 state-of-the-art baselines in terms of Average Precision. By resolving the scalability bottleneck of the Christoffel Function, this work expands the toolkit of anomaly detection methods with a robust, theoretically grounded, and universally applicable approach.

</details>


### [193] [Speculative Rollback Correction for Quality-Diverse Web Agent Imitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12485)
*Longkun Hao,Hongyu Lin,Hao Li,Zhichao Yang,Haojie Hao,Dongshuo Huang,Haitao Yang,Hongyu Ge,Ming jie Xie,Yanjun Wu,Zi Hao Yin,Yan Bai,Yihang Lou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC) 的分支级模仿学习框架，用于可重置的网页代理环境。SRC 通过固定视野分支审查替代逐状态或逐轨迹的专家干预，在局部进度中断时定位首次有害偏移并回滚，保留有用的前缀轨迹，并将成功轨迹通过硬验证器过滤后存入轻量级质量-多样性存档。在 WebArena-Infinity 上，SRC 收集了977条验证通过轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例，在恢复与查询之间的权衡上优于逐步审查方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的交互式网页代理模仿学习面临专家干预时机选择的困境：延迟干预导致早期错误累积使页面状态不可恢复，过早或过度干预则使代理过度依赖专家策略，陷入单一刚性轨迹的局部最优。

Method: SRC 采用固定视野分支审查机制：学生代理先执行一段短推测性轨迹，仅在局部进度中断时，教师才定位第一个有害偏差并回滚；回滚保留有用前缀，成功轨迹经硬验证器过滤后存入质量-多样性存档，用于下一动作的监督微调。

Result: 在 WebArena-Infinity 上，SRC 收集了977条验证通过轨迹和9,183个下一步动作示例；固定视野审查在恢复与查询权衡上优于逐步审查，同时保留了验证通过的解变体。

Conclusion: SRC 通过分支级审查和回滚机制，在专家干预频率与代理自主探索能力之间实现了更好的平衡，有效提升了交互式网页代理的学习效率和轨迹多样性。

Abstract: Training interactive web agents through imitation learning from expert trajectories has emerged as a highly effective approach. However, determining the optimal timing for expert intervention presents a critical challenge in this context. Delayed intervention often leads to the accumulation of early-stage errors, pushing the page state into an irrecoverable regime. Conversely, premature or excessive intervention causes the agent to become overly reliant on expert policies, trapping the model in local optima characterized by a single, rigid trajectory. We propose Speculative Rollback Correction (SRC), a branch-level imitation framework for resettable agent environments. Instead of requesting teacher labels at every visited state or correcting only after a completed trajectory, SRC uses fixed-horizon branch review: the student executes a short speculative segment before teacher review, and the teacher localizes the first harmful deviation only when local progress breaks. Rollback preserves useful prefixes, while successful rollouts are filtered by a hard verifier and retained in a lightweight quality-diversity archive. The resulting data supports next-action supervised fine-tuning on both localized corrections and verifier-passing trajectories. On WebArena-Infinity, SRC collects 977 verifier-passing trajectories and 9,183 next-action examples; fixed-horizon review improves the recovery-versus-query tradeoff over step-level review while retaining verifier-passing solution variants. Code is available at https://github.com/LongkunHao/SRC_gui_agent.

</details>


### [194] [An Empirical Study on Predictive Maintenance for Component X in Heavy-Duty Scania Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12486)
*Valeriu Dimidov,Sasan Jafarnejad,Raphaël Frank*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种基于条件的预测性维护（PdM）方法，假设监控组件的磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列。该方法通过选取时间序列中最新观测值并转化为表格格式，使用针对表格数据设计的机器学习模型进行分类。在Scania Component X数据集上的结果表明，该方法相比现有SOTA方法降低了成本，并通过AutoML简化了建模过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卡车车队的预测性维护实施面临挑战：数据量大、通过传感器检测故障的固有复杂性、以及解决方案实施中难以找到成本效益权衡。

Method: 假设组件磨损状态可表示为单调非递减时间序列，仅选取时间序列中最新观测值，将其转换为表格格式，使用针对表格数据设计的机器学习模型（通过AutoML）进行分类。

Result: 在Scania Component X数据集上，相比当前SOTA方法降低了成本。

Conclusion: 提出的方法在降低成本的同时，通过AutoML简化了建模过程，为条件基预测性维护提供了更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Condition-based Predictive Maintenance (PdM) for truck fleets has gained momentum in recent years. This maintenance strategy aims to minimize unplanned downtimes and reduce costs by monitoring the health status of vehicles and taking proactive action based on their condition. However, the implementation of condition-based PdM systems is challenging due to the large volume of data generated by the trucks, the inherent complexity of detecting failures through sensor data and the difficulties in finding cost-effective trade-offs in the solution's implementation. In this paper, we define and validate a condition-based PdM methodology built on the assumption that the wear-and-tear state of the monitored component can be represented as a monotonically non-decreasing time series. It involves selecting only the most recent observations from the time series and transforming them into a tabular format for classification using machine learning (ML) models designed for tabular data. Our results indicate that the proposed methodology reduces costs on the Scania Component X dataset compared to current state-of-the-art (SOTA) approaches, while also simplifying the modeling process through AutoML.

</details>


### [195] [DynamicPTQ: Mitigating Activation Quantization Collapse via Residual-Stream Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12487)
*Zimo Zhao,Maolin Wang,Bowen Yu,Bowen Liu,Xiao Han,Xiangyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DynamicPTQ，一种动态后训练量化策略，通过识别残差流中量化敏感层并仅对这些层使用8位激活精度（其余保持4位），解决了W4A4KV4量化中因大规模激活跨层动态变化导致的精度下降问题，在LLaMA-2/3上提升困惑度和零样本性能，并实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法（如QuaRot、SpinQuant）通过正交旋转和仿射缩放等变换平滑来缓解大规模激活问题，但忽略了残差流的跨层动态特性。本文发现大规模激活以阶段式模式在网络深度上出现和消失，引发大的残差变化，使静态变换平滑无法完全解决由跨层残差变化引起的动态量化不稳定性。

Method: 提出Jump Ratio和Historical Feature SNR两个指标刻画跨层残差动态行为；基于此设计DynamicPTQ策略，从残差流动态中识别量化敏感层，仅对这些层分配8位激活精度，而权重、KV缓存和其他激活保持4位精度；可直接集成到QuaRot、SpinQuant、FlatQuant等PTQ基线中。

Result: 在LLaMA-2和LLaMA-3上的实验表明，DynamicPTQ在W4A4KV4量化下持续提升困惑度和零样本QA性能，同时实现1.05-1.07倍吞吐量提升，且内存开销适中。

Conclusion: 动态PTQ策略通过阶段感知的混合精度激活量化，有效解决了静态变换平滑无法处理的跨层残差动态变化问题，为鲁棒的低比特LLM推理提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is essential for efficient large language model inference, but reliably quantizing activations remains challenging when weights, activations, and KV caches are all quantized to 4-bit precision. A key difficulty lies in massive activations, whose extreme values dominate the activation range and amplify quantization errors. State-of-the-art methods mainly mitigate massive activations through transformation-based smoothing, such as orthogonal rotations and affine scaling, but overlook the cross-layer dynamics of the residual stream. In this paper, we show that massive activations emerge and disappear in a phase-wise pattern across network depth, triggering large residual changes. These changes cause newly injected layer-wise updates to dominate the 4-bit quantization scale and weaken historical residual information. To characterize this behavior, we introduce Jump Ratio and Historical Feature SNR. This suggests that static transformation-based smoothing cannot fully resolve dynamic quantization instability caused by cross-layer residual changes. Based on this analysis, we propose DynamicPTQ, a Dynamic Post-Training Quantization policy for phase-aware mixed-precision activation quantization. DynamicPTQ identifies quantization-sensitive layers from residual-stream dynamics and assigns 8-bit activation precision only to these layers, while keeping weights, KV caches, and other activations in 4-bit precision. It can be directly integrated with strong PTQ baselines such as QuaRot, SpinQuant, and FlatQuant. Experiments on LLaMA-2 and LLaMA-3 show that DynamicPTQ consistently improves perplexity and zero-shot QA performance under W4A4KV4 quantization, while achieving 1.05 to 1.07 times throughput improvement with modest memory overhead. These results demonstrate a practical path toward robust low-bit LLM inference.

</details>


### [196] [A Stationary (and Therefore Compatible) Representation is All You Need](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12488)
*Niccolò Biondi,Federico Pernici,Simone Ricci,Alberto Del Bimbo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示天然具备兼容性，并提出结合交叉熵与对比损失的凸组合方法以捕获高阶依赖，在模型连续微调和替换场景下实现无需重处理图库的持续检索服务，达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在模型更新时，希望新旧模型提取的特征可互换使用（即兼容性），但现有方法未能充分捕捉表示间的高阶依赖关系，限制了兼容性的实际应用效果。

Method: 利用d-Simplex固定分类器学习平稳表示以实现兼容性，并通过交叉熵损失与对比损失的凸组合训练模型，在保持兼容性约束的同时捕获更高阶的表示依赖关系。

Result: 实验证明该方法在模型连续微调和替换场景下，无需重新处理图库图像即可实现不间断检索服务，性能达到SOTA。

Conclusion: d-Simplex固定分类器学习的平稳表示在理论上满足兼容性定义，结合对比损失的训练策略能有效捕获高阶依赖，为兼容表示学习奠定了理论基础并提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Learning compatible representations aims to learn feature representations that can be used interchangeably over time whenever a model undergoes updates. In this paper, we demonstrate that stationary representations learned by d-Simplex fixed classifiers imply compatibility as in its formal definition. This result establishes a foundation for future works and can be directly exploited in practical learning scenarios.
