We address the challenge of learning compatibility using $d$-Simplex fixed classifiers when the model is sequentially fine-tuned. Learning according to a d-Simplex fixed classifier with the cross-entropy loss aligns feature distributions at the first-order statistics. Consequently, it may not fully capture higher-order dependencies in the representation between model updates. To address this issue, we demonstrate that training the model using a $d$-Simplex fixed classifier through a convex combination of the cross-entropy loss and a contrastive loss not only captures higher-order dependencies, but is also equivalent to learning with the cross-entropy under the compatibility constraints. We confirm our findings with extensive experiments also considering a new scenario where a pre-trained model is sequentially fine-tuned and occasionally replaced with an improved model. We show that stationary representations enable uninterrupted retrieval services (without reprocessing gallery images) while improving performance during model updates and replacements, achieving state-of-the-art. Code at https://github.com/miccunifi/iamcl2r.

</details>


### [197] [Robustness Verification of Recurrent Neural Networks with Abstraction Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12490)
*Li-Jen Lin,Chih-Duo Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对RNN局部鲁棒性验证中因非线性松弛导致的过度保守问题，提出一种抽象精化框架，通过划分预激活区间消除ReLU误差并收紧tanh/sigmoid边界，结合SHAP引导的时间步选择策略控制组合爆炸，实验验证了该方法在验证成功率和边界紧致性上的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RNN的局部鲁棒性验证面临挑战：线性边界传播方法因非线性松弛引入的近似误差在循环连接中累积，尤其是当预激活区间跨越零点时，导致验证结果过于保守，无法证明实际鲁棒的输入。

Method: 提出抽象精化框架：(1) 划分跨越零点的预激活区间，使ReLU精确化，并为tanh/sigmoid构造更紧的线性包络；(2) 引入SHAP引导的时间步选择策略，按对验证目标的贡献度对隐藏状态排序，仅在时序上精化最关键的时间步，以控制长序列的分支开销。

Result: 在CIFAR10和MNIST stroke基准上，相比仅使用抽象的方法，验证成功率和鲁棒性边界的紧致性均得到一致提升，同时揭示了ReLU与tanh模型之间明确的运行时权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的抽象精化框架有效缓解了RNN验证中的保守性问题，通过有选择地精化关键时间步，在提升验证精度的同时控制了计算成本，适用于不同激活函数的RNN模型。

Abstract: Certified local robustness verification for recurrent neural networks (RNNs) is challenging because approximation errors introduced by nonlinear relaxations can propagate through recurrent connections and accumulate over time. As a result, scalable linear bound propagation methods often become overly conservative and fail to certify inputs that are in fact robust, especially when many pre-activation intervals cross zero. We propose an abstraction-refinement framework for RNN verification that partitions such intervals to remove the dominant relaxation error: on each refined branch, ReLU becomes exact, and smooth activations such as tanh and sigmoid admit substantially tighter linear envelopes. To control the combinatorial cost of splitting in long sequences, we introduce a SHAP-guided timestep selection strategy that ranks hidden states by their contribution to the verification objective and refines only the most critical timesteps in temporal order. Experiments on CIFAR10 and MNIST stroke benchmarks demonstrate consistent improvements in verification success and robustness-margin tightness over abstraction-only baselines, while exposing clear runtime trade-offs between ReLU and tanh models.

</details>


### [198] [Net-Ev$^2$: A Generative Simulator for Network Event Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12494)
*Guangyu Wang,Zhaonan Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Net-Ev²，一个结合事件语义与网络拓扑结构的生成式模拟器，用于模拟扰动事件在网络中的演化传播，在多个大规模道路网络数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法无法同时建模事件的结构化属性和非结构化语义，也无法在模拟网络事件演化时捕捉拓扑结构，因此需要一种能够联合利用事件线索并保持网络拓扑的生成式模拟器。

Method: 两阶段框架：结构引导的掩码预训练（structure-guided masked pre-training）和拓扑感知的扩散过程（topology-aware diffusion process），其中扩散去噪使用U-Net风格的图下采样与上采样；推理时仅需自然语言事件输入。

Result: 构建了Net-Ev²-6.5M多模态基准数据集（包含四个大规模道路网络的 aligned event 和网络流量数据），提出新拓扑保真度指标JL-MMD，实验达到SOTA性能并具备强泛化能力。

Conclusion: Net-Ev²有效解决了事件驱动网络演化模拟中语义与拓扑联合建模的难题，为实际决策中的试错成本降低提供了新工具。

Abstract: Reducing real-world trial and error has long been a central goal of decision making, and generative simulators advance this goal by modeling the evolution of future states. An even more challenging yet meaningful task is simulating how disturbance events (e.g., accidents) propagate their impacts across real-world networks. The existing approaches fall short of modeling both structured attributes and unstructured semantics of events, and capturing topological structures in simulating network event evolution. Therefore, we are motivated to propose Net-Ev$^2$ ($\underline{\textbf{Net}}$work $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$ent $\underline{\textbf{Ev}}$olution), a novel generative simulator that jointly leverages event cues while preserving network topology in simulations. Specifically, the framework consists of two stages, namely structure-guided masked pre-training and topology-aware diffusion process, which is achieved by U-Net-like graph downsampling and upsampling during denoising. At inference time, Net-Ev$^2$ can generate simulations using natural-language event input only, with greater flexibility for practical usage. Furthermore, we introduce Net-Ev$^2$-6.5M, a multimodal benchmark of aligned event and network traffic data across four large-scale road networks, as well as a new topology-aware metric, namely JL-MMD, to evaluate topological fidelity in generated network dynamics. Extensive experiments demonstrate the state-of-the-art performance and strong generalization ability of Net-Ev$^2$. Code is made available at https://github.com/Guangyu4/Net-Ev-2.

</details>


### [199] [$μ$VLA: On Recurrent Memory for Partially Observable Manipulation in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12497)
*Egor Cherepanov,Nikita Kachaev,Daniil Zelezetsky,Aydar Bulatov,Artem Pshenitsyn,Yuri Kuratov,Alexey Skrynnik,Aleksandr I. Panov,Alexey K. Kovalev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，在强预训练VLA骨干网络中仅添加跨时间步的记忆token和截断BPTT训练，发现最小化循环机制在部分可观测任务上显著提升性能（MIKASA-Robo成功率0.42→0.84），而在完全可观测任务上无退化（LIBERO达96.2%），标定了纯循环机制的能力边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA模型同时引入循环、检索、压缩、辅助目标、层级记忆或任务特定架构修改，导致循环机制本身的贡献与附加组件纠缠不清，缺乏对循环在VLA中独立作用的隔离研究。

Method: 在强预训练VLA骨干（OpenVLA-OFT）的Transformer中仅添加少量可学习记忆token，这些token跨时间步携带并通过自注意力更新，使用截断随时间反向传播（TBPTT）端到端训练，无辅助损失和架构改变。参数化为记忆宽度m、TBPTT长度K和记忆更新规则（跨步梯度或解耦EMA）。

Result: 在MIKASA-Robo上，最强设置将平均成功率从0.42提升至0.84，在保留任务上达0.23（基线0.07）；在需要不同记忆结构的任务上性能接近基线。LIBERO上最强变体达96.2%平均成功率，完全可观测下无退化。

Conclusion: 标定了最小化骨干内循环机制的能力边界：在部分可观测任务中循环足够有效，但在需要不同记忆结构的任务中需额外架构支持，为未来记忆增强VLA设计提供了基准参考。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models predict chunks of future actions from the current observation, an assumption that fails under partial observability, where decisions depend on information no longer visible. Existing memory-augmented VLAs simultaneously introduce recurrence, retrieval, compression modules, auxiliary objectives, hierarchical memory, or task-specific architectural changes, so the contribution of recurrence itself remains entangled with surrounding machinery. We present a controlled isolation study of recurrence in a strong pretrained VLA backbone. Our formulation augments the transformer with a small set of learnable memory tokens carried across timesteps and updated through self-attention, trained end to end with truncated backpropagation through time, with no auxiliary losses and no architectural changes. We instantiate this as $μ$VLA, a family of OpenVLA-OFT variants parameterized by memory width m, TBPTT length K, and the memory update rule (cross-step gradients or a detached EMA), so that recurrence is the only varying factor. On MIKASA-Robo, $μ$VLA improves average success rate on five training tasks from 0.42 to 0.84 at the strongest setting and reaches 0.23 on held-out tasks with the same memory structure versus 0.07 for the memoryless baseline. On tasks requiring different memory structure, performance remains near baseline. On LIBERO, the strongest recurrent variant achieves 96.2% average success, indicating no regression under full observability. We interpret these results as a calibration of the capability envelope of minimal in-backbone recurrence, identifying the regime in which it is sufficient and the regime where additional memory structure is required. Demos and videos can be found in https://avanturist322.github.io/mu-vla/.

</details>


### [200] [Improving Crash Frequency Prediction from Simulated Traffic Conflicts Using Machine Learning Based Microsimulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12500)
*Xian Liu,Carlo G. Prato,Gustav Markkula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过对比基于规则的传统模型和基于机器学习的驾驶行为模型在微观交通仿真中的表现，发现ML模型生成的冲突数据结合极值理论可以更准确地预测真实事故频率，且无需针对特定地点进行模型标定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于微观仿真的交通安全研究采用简化的规则型行为模型，虽能较好复现交通流，但难以生成真实的冲突动态，限制了事故预测精度。机器学习行为模型从大规模轨迹数据中学习人类驾驶行为，有望提升仿真真实性和事故预测能力，但尚未被系统验证。

Method: 选取英国利兹五个真实信号交叉口，分别使用标准规则型模型和先进ML模型进行微观交通仿真；提取车辆轨迹后采用二维碰撞时间指标识别冲突，并利用极值理论建模预测事故频率。

Result: ML模型生成的冲突所预测的事故频率与真实事故数据吻合，而规则型模型因缺乏针对特定交叉口的标定无法产生有意义的预测。直接使用ML模型生成的仿真事故来预测真实事故频率效果较差。

Conclusion: 基于ML的驾驶行为模型能真实再现冲突动态，在无需地点特定标定的情况下改善事故预测，具有显著优越性；但当前ML模型尚无法生成真实的仿真事故，这为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: Traffic microsimulation combined with surrogate safety measures has increasingly been used as a proactive alternative to historical crash data for predicting crash frequency for current or planned road infrastructure designs. However, existing microsimulation-based safety studies have adopted simplified rule-based behaviour models, which reproduce traffic flow reasonably well but often fail to generate realistic conflict dynamics, limiting crash prediction accuracy. Recent advances in machine learning (ML)-based behaviour models offer a promising opportunity to potentially improve microsimulation realism and crash frequency predictions by learning human driving behaviour directly from large-scale trajectory datasets. To investigate this possibility, traffic microsimulation was conducted for five real-world signalised intersections in Leeds, UK, using both a standard rule-based model and a state-of-the-art ML model. Simulated vehicle trajectories were analysed using a two-dimensional Time-to-Collision metric to identify simulated conflicts, which were then modelled using Extreme Value Theory to predict crash frequency. Results show that conflicts from the ML model yielded crash predictions in line with the real-world crash data, whereas the rule-based model did not permit meaningful predictions, presumably due to a lack of model calibration to the specific simulated intersections. Directly using ML-generated simulated crashes to predict real-world crash frequency also yielded poor results, suggesting that while current ML models can realistically reproduce conflicts, they are not yet able to generate realistic crashes. Overall, the findings demonstrate that ML-based behaviour models are promising for improving crash prediction from simulated conflicts, without a need for location-specific model calibration, and suggest clear future directions for ML-based traffic microsimulation.

</details>


### [201] [Policy-driven Conformal Prediction for Trustworthy QoT Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12501)
*Kiarash Rezaei,Omran Ayoub,Paolo Monti,Carlos Natalino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Conformal QoT框架，将统计保证的QoT估计与操作决策策略相结合，在领域偏移下实现可靠的光路可行性预测，并将准确率从92%提升至99.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有光路可行性预测方法在领域偏移（domain shift）情况下可靠性不足，需要一种能提供统计保证且适应环境变化的QoT估计方法。

Method: 提出Conformal QoT框架，核心思想是将保形预测（conformal prediction）与操作决策策略相结合，通过策略驱动的方式在统计保证下进行QoT估计。

Result: 在开放数据集上，准确率从92%提升至99.6%，显著提高了领域偏移下的预测可靠性。

Conclusion: Conformal QoT框架能够有效解决光路可行性预测中的领域偏移问题，通过统计保证的预测显著提升准确率和可靠性。

Abstract: We propose Conformal QoT, a policy-driven framework that combines statistically guaranteed QoT estimation with operational decision policies, enabling reliable lightpath-feasibility predictions under domain shift and improving accuracy from 92\% to 99.6\% on open datasets.

</details>


### [202] [Dolph2Vec: Self-Supervised Representations of Dolphin Vocalizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12503)
*Chiara Semenzin,Faadil Mustun,Roberto Dessi,Pierre Orhan,Alexis Emanuelli,Yair Lakretz,Gonzalo de Polavieja,German Sumbre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dolph2Vec，首个针对特定物种（海豚）的大规模自监督学习模型，用于分析海豚交流信号中的细粒度结构。该模型在签名口哨分类和检测任务上显著优于通用基线，且其嵌入和码本结构能捕捉可解释的声学单元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生物声学自监督学习模型侧重于物种间的广泛泛化，未针对个体通信系统中的细粒度结构进行优化，缺乏对特定物种（如海豚）交流模式深入分析的能力。

Method: 收集了超过五年、来自五只已知海豚在半自然海洋环境中的纵向录音数据集；基于Wav2Vec2.0架构进行适配，构建了Dolph2Vec模型，仅在该数据集上进行大规模自监督预训练。

Result: 在签名口哨分类和检测两项生物学相关任务中，Dolph2Vec显著优于通用基线模型；学习到的嵌入和码本结构能够捕捉与海豚口哨类别对应的可解释声学单元，甚至可能揭示亚口哨级别的结构。

Conclusion: 自监督学习可以作为探索动物交流研究假设的模型和科学工具，为细粒度分析动物通信模式提供了新途径。

Abstract: Self-supervised learning (SSL) has opened new opportunities in bioacoustics by enabling scalable modeling of animal vocalizations without the need for expensive manual annotation. However, current SSL models in this domain prioritize broad generalization across species and are not optimized for uncovering the fine-grained structure of individual communication systems. In this work, we collect and release a novel dataset of over five years of longitudinal recordings, from five known dolphins in a semi-naturalistic marine environment, an unprecedented resource for studying dolphin communication. We adapt the Wav2Vec2.0 Baevski et al. (2020) architecture to this domain and introduce Dolph2Vec, the first large-scale, species-specific SSL model trained exclusively on this data. We benchmark our model on two biologically relevant tasks: signature whistle classification and whistle detection. Dolph2Vec significantly outperforms general-purpose baselines in both tasks. Beyond performance, we show that learned embeddings and codebook structure capture interpretable acoustic units aligned with dolphin whistle categories and possibly sub-whistle structure, enabling fine-grained analysis of communication patterns. Our findings demonstrate how SSL can serve as both a model and a scientific tool to explore hypotheses in animal communication research.

</details>


### [203] [Boosting Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12505)
*Pengwei Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DPOP方法，在DPO基础上增加参考模型贪婪响应的门控惩罚，当当前策略对偏好响应分配的概率仍低于拒绝响应时激活该惩罚，在AlpacaEval 2.0上超越DPO、SimPO、AlphaDPO等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO等离线偏好优化方法仅利用数据集中已存储的选中和拒绝响应，忽略了参考模型自身对同一提示生成的有用信号。

Method: 在DPO基础上引入一个门控惩罚项，当当前策略对偏好响应的似然仍低于拒绝响应时，对参考模型贪婪响应施加惩罚，防止模型过度偏离参考分布。

Result: 在AlpacaEval 2.0上，DPOP在Llama-3-8b-it和Gemma-2-9b-it上分别比基线提升5.3%和4.4%的长度控制胜率；消融实验表明SimNPO风格的长度归一化惩罚优于NPO和词级unlikelihood。

Conclusion: 利用参考模型生成响应作为额外信号能有效提升离线偏好优化性能，门控机制和适当的惩罚形式是关键设计选择。

Abstract: Offline preference optimization has become a practical substitute for reinforcement learning from human feedback, but pairwise objectives such as Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and its variants use only the chosen and rejected responses stored in a static dataset. This leaves a useful signal unused: the response that the reference model itself would generate for the same prompt. We propose Direct Preference Optimization with Penalization (DPOP), a simple extension of DPO that augments the base preference loss with a gated penalty on reference-greedy responses. DPOP activates this penalty only when the current policy still assigns a lower likelihood to the preferred response than to the rejected response. On AlpacaEval 2.0, DPOP improves length-controlled win rate over DPO, SimPO, and AlphaDPO on both Llama-3-8b-it and Gemma-2-9b-it, achieving relative gains of 5.3\% and 4.4\% over baselines on the two models, respectively. Ablations further show that a SimNPO-style length-normalized penalty is stronger than NPO and token-level unlikelihood in this setting.

</details>


### [204] [Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation: Post-Training Without Rubric Verifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12507)
*MohammadHossein Rezaei,Anas Mahmoud,Zihao Wang,Utkarsh Tyagi,Advait Gosai,Razvan-Gabriel Dumitru,Aakash Sabharwal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RGSD是一种无验证器的策略蒸馏方法，通过将基于rubric的条件策略分布逐token蒸馏到无条件学生模型中，替代了传统RLVR中的LLM评判器，在医学和科学领域多个模型上取得了与基于评判器的GRPO相当的rubric满意度，同时大幅降低了训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于rubric的训练方法依赖LLM验证器对每个rollout进行评分，这带来了大量训练时间开销、验证器特有偏见的暴露，以及将rubric反馈简化为稀疏的轨迹末端信号等问题。

Method: RGSD通过将基策略在rubric条件下的分布作为教师，蒸馏到无条件的student模型中，实现逐token的密集学习信号替代稀疏的轨迹级奖励，完全移除训练循环中的LLM评判器。

Result: 在Qwen-2.5(3B,7B)和Qwen3-Thinking(4B,8B)模型上，RGSD在医学和科学领域取得了与基于评判器的GRPO相当的rubric满意度，且每个prompt仅需一次on-policy rollout，无需训练时验证器调用。

Conclusion: 原始rubric比自生成的参考回答提供了更强的教师增强信号；更强GRPO评判器在某些设置下可超越RGSD；RGSD作为无验证器的互补替代方案，在验证器成本或可靠性成为瓶颈时具有优势。

Abstract: Rubrics have emerged as an alternative to RLVR in open-ended domains where a single ground-truth final answer is not available. Existing rubric-based training methods rely on an LLM verifier that scores each rollout against rubrics. This introduces substantial training-time overhead, exposes optimization to verifier-specific biases, and reduces rubric feedback to a sparse end-of-trajectory signal. We propose Rubric-Guided Self-Distillation (RGSD), a verifier-free training method in which the base policy, conditioned on the rubric, serves as the teacher for the unconditioned student. RGSD distills the rubric-conditioned teacher distribution into the student token-by-token, replacing sparse trajectory-level rewards with dense per-token learning signals and removing the LLM judge from the training loop entirely. Across Qwen-2.5 (3B, 7B) and Qwen3-Thinking (4B, 8B) models on medical and science domains, RGSD achieves rubric satisfaction comparable to judge-based GRPO while using one on-policy rollout per prompt and no training-time verifier calls. Ablations show that raw rubrics provide a stronger teacher enrichment signal than self-generated reference responses, while a stronger GRPO judge can outperform RGSD in some settings, positioning RGSD as a complementary verifier-free alternative when verifier cost or reliability is the bottleneck.

</details>


### [205] [Crossing the Validation Crisis: Cross-Validation Reduces Benchmarking Variance Surprisingly Well](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12552)
*Célestin Eve,Gaël Varoquaux,Thomas Moreau*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出机器学习性能评估因统计变异性和有限测试样本而不可靠，提出使用交叉验证可显著提升评估置信度。引入“样本增益”概念量化多重交叉验证分割带来的虚拟数据增强效果。实验表明多分割能大幅提升性能估计的可靠性与稳定性，并提出了基于前几折动态提前停止交叉验证的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习通过实证评估来比较新方法的性能，但由于算法随机性导致的统计变异性和有限的测试样本，性能估计往往不可靠，导致难以区分真正的进步，即存在“验证危机”。

Method: 引入“样本增益”（sample gain）概念，量化通过使用多个交叉验证分割来减少基准测试方差所实现的虚拟数据增强效果；提出一种动态提前停止交叉验证的程序，通过前几折估计后续折是否带来大幅样本增益。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据集（组织病理学扫描和NLP微调）上的实验表明，多重分割能显著提升性能估计的可靠性和稳定性，收益递减点往往比预期出现得更晚；提出的动态提前停止方法可有效节省计算资源。

Conclusion: 强调在可用样本上充分利用交叉验证对于实现稳健可靠的基准测试具有重要价值。

Abstract: Modern machine learning progresses through empirical work, benchmarking new methods to evaluate relative performance. However, the statistical variability inherent to evaluation - exacerbated by the stochastic nature of many algorithms - often makes performance estimation unreliable due to the limited test samples available, leading to a validation crisis in which genuine advances are difficult to discern. In this work, we show that cross-validation improves markedly confidence when evaluating and comparing learning algorithm performances. We introduce the concept of sample gain, which quantifies the virtual data augmentation achieved by using multiple cross-validation splits to reduce benchmarking variance. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets (histopathologic scans and NLP fine-tuning) demonstrate that multiple splits can substantially improve the reliability and stability of performance estimates, with diminishing returns often setting in later than expected. We also introduce a procedure to dynamically early-stop cross-validation by estimating from the first few folds if subsequent folds will bring large sample gains. Our findings highlight the value of pushing cross-validation on available samples to achieve robust and reliable benchmarking.

</details>


### [206] [Emerging Flexible Designs for Geospatial Multimodal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12595)
*Philipe Dias,Waqwoya Abebe,Abhishek Potnis,Aristeidis Tsaris,Dan Lu,Xiao Wang,Dalton Lunga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对地球观测领域多种基础模型架构（编码器-only、编码器-解码器、掩码自编码）进行了公平比较，在统一的自监督学习目标、训练数据集和参数设置下，评估它们在GEOBench基准上的分类与分割任务表现，揭示了模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游性能之间的设计权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地球观测基础模型架构多样（编码器-only、编码器-解码器、掩码自编码等），缺乏一致的评估框架来公平比较其性能权衡，尤其是在多模态地理空间推理和不同光谱波段配置下的灵活性。

Method: 采用相同的自监督学习目标和训练数据集进行标准化预训练，在一致的参数化条件下，使用GEOBench基准对多种主流基础模型架构进行分类和分割任务的苹果对苹果比较。

Result: 提供了关于模型灵活性、模态对齐与下游任务性能之间设计权衡的新见解，揭示了不同架构在受控条件下的优势和局限性。

Conclusion: 本研究为构建具备鲁棒多模态推理能力的下一代地理空间基础模型提供了实用指导。

Abstract: Foundation models are rapidly transforming Earth observation by enabling scalable pretraining across diverse unlabeled geospatial modalities. However, their architectural diversity ranging from encoder-only to encoder-decoder and masked autoencoding paradigms makes it challenging to assess performance trade offs in a consistent manner. In this work, we present an apples-to-apples comparison of leading FM architectures designed for geospatial multimodal reasoning, with a particular focus on flexibility across varied spectral band configurations. We standardize pretraining using identical self supervised learning objectives and training datasets, and evaluate all models under consistent parameterization on the GEOBench benchmark across classification and segmentation tasks. Our results offer new insights into the design trade-offs between model flexibility, modality alignment, and downstream task performance. By highlighting architectural strengths and limitations under controlled conditions, this study provides practical guidance for building next generation geospatial foundation models capable of robust multimodal reasoning.

</details>


### [207] [Viral Proteins Reveal Geometry of Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12609)
*Arthur Bigot,Harmon Bhasin,Core Francisco Park,Eugene Shakhnovich,Dianzhuo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡的数据上训练，本文以病毒蛋白为例，发现嵌入空间中存在一个主导的"自然性"轴，该轴与掩码重建困惑度对齐，将序列从良好建模的细胞蛋白到病毒蛋白再到随机序列有序排列。模型缩放对不同病毒家族的影响不均，但嵌入仍保留病毒特异性信号，病毒蛋白在零样本困惑度和浅层序列特征之外仍可线性分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究蛋白质语言模型在高度不平衡的训练数据下，如何表征欠代表性的生物序列（以病毒蛋白为例），以及模型嵌入空间中是否存在某种组织原则。

Method: 以病毒蛋白为案例，使用ESM模型家族，分析嵌入空间中的"自然性"轴及其与掩码重建困惑度的关系，考察模型缩放对不同病毒家族的影响，并评估嵌入中病毒特异性信号的保留程度。

Result: 发现了主导的"自然性"轴，缩放对此轴的压缩不均匀；病毒蛋白在零样本任务和浅层特征之外仍可线性分离，表明嵌入保留了病毒特异性信息。

Conclusion: 蛋白质语言模型的表征结构由一种广义的"自然性"概念组织，同时保留不同生物组别的特异性信息。

Abstract: Protein language models are trained on highly imbalanced datasets, raising the question of how they represent underrepresented biological sequences. Using viral proteins as a case study across ESM model families, we identify a dominant nativeness axis in embedding space, aligned with masked reconstruction perplexity, that orders sequences from well-modeled cellular proteins through viral proteins to shuffled and random sequences. Scaling contracts this axis unevenly across viral families. Despite this, protein language model embeddings retain viral-specific signal: viral proteins remain linearly separable beyond zero-shot perplexity and shallow sequence features. Together, these results suggest that pLM representations are structured by a general notion of nativeness while preserving information specific to distinct biological groups.

</details>


### [208] [The Metric Picks the Winner: Evaluation Choice Flips Model Rankings for Drug-Response Prediction in Unseen Chemistry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12639)
*Dhruv Agarwal,Riya Bisht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在VCPI药物响应预测竞赛中证明：指标选择决定了模型排名；在使用真实活性加权的wMSE指标下，深度融合模型显著优于线性指纹基线和检索方法，而使用代理指标时简单基线反而胜出。这是首次在真实药物化学数据上展示指标校准效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未见药物的转录组响应是计算细胞生物学的核心难题，但近期基准测试表明复杂模型在按化学结构划分的测试化合物上往往无法击败简单基线。作者旨在解决这一困境，并探究指标选择对模型排名的根本性影响。

Method: 采用分阶段方法：(1)简单基线（未处理对照、平均训练化合物响应）；(2)非参数检索（基于Tanimoto加权的最近邻平均）；(3)融合阶段——将冻结的化学嵌入与检索支持特征结合，预测相对于均值的残差，并包含不确定性头和基因程序。在THP-1 DRUG-seq数据（14,026训练化合物）上，使用Bemis-Murcko骨架划分，对比了线性回归、深度模型、检索和ChemBERTa等方法。

Result: 在逆方差每基因代理指标下，基于Morgan指纹的线性回归胜出；但在竞赛真实活性集指标（Mejia权重wMSE）下，排名完全逆转：深度融合解码器显著优于线性指纹基线（wMSE降低0.012，配对bootstrap p<10^-4），代理指标的胜者反而成为最差的化学感知模型。

Conclusion: 指标选择决定了模型排名——这是首次在真实药物化学数据上证明指标校准效应（此前仅在遗传扰动中建立）。作者发布了可复现的pipeline，连接官方评分器，可在真实的1064×12,995网格上生成有效提交。

Abstract: Predicting how a cell's transcriptome responds to a drug it has never seen is a core, hard problem in computational cell biology: recent benchmarks show complex models often fail to beat trivial baselines once test compounds are held out by chemistry. We study one cell line and assay, THP-1 cells profiled by DRUG-seq, scored by the active-compound weighted MSE(wMSE) of the VCPI prediction contest. We propose a staged approach: dumb baselines (untreated control and mean training-compound response) that the field keeps failing to beat; non-parametric retrieval (a Tanimoto-weighted average of a held-out compound's nearest training compounds); and a fusion stage combining a frozen chemistry embedding with retrieval-support features to predict the residual over the mean, with an uncertainty head and gene programs. On the released VCPI THP-1 drug-seq data (14,026 training compounds), under a Bemis-Murcko scaffold split, the model ranking inverts depending on the metric. Under an inverse-variance per-gene proxy, a regularized linear regression on Morgan fingerprints appears to win over the deep models, retrieval, and ChemBERTa -- the textbook "simple baselines win" result. But under the contest's true active-set metric (per-(gene, compound) Mejia weights, validated against the official scorer; mean baseline 0.535 vs the organizers' 0.507 reference), that reverses: the deep models win, our fusion decoder significantly beats the linear fingerprint baseline (-0.012 wMSE, paired bootstrap p < 10^-4), and the proxy's winner becomes the worst chemistry-aware predictor. Picking the metric picks the winner -- to our knowledge the first demonstration on real held-out drug chemistry of the metric-calibration effect established largely on genetic perturbation. We release a reproducible pipeline
  wired to the official scorer that emits a valid submission over the real 1064 x 12,995 grid.

</details>


### [209] [Individual Control Barrier Functions-Guided Diffusion Model for Safe Offline Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12640)
*Qingyun Guo,Junyi Shi,Jianuo Huang,Tianyu Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种安全离线多智能体强化学习算法，通过将神经个体控制屏障函数嵌入扩散模型以增强轨迹生成的安全性，并使用逆动力学恢复控制策略，在多个基准测试中显著提升了安全性并保持了有竞争力的奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离线强化学习中的扩散模型主要聚焦于单智能体场景，多智能体环境中的安全问题尚未被充分探索。

Method: 将神经个体控制屏障函数嵌入扩散模型以增强轨迹生成的安全性，并通过逆动力学恢复控制策略。

Result: 在多个基准测试中实现了显著的安全性提升，同时保持了有竞争力的奖励表现。

Conclusion: 所提出的安全离线多智能体强化学习算法能够有效提升安全性且不显著牺牲任务奖励。

Abstract: Offline reinforcement learning allows control policies to be learned directly from data without online interaction, making it suitable for safety-critical tasks. Recent studies have applied diffusion models to offline reinforcement learning to leverage their strong capacity for modeling complex data distributions. However, existing approaches primarily focus on single-agent settings, leaving the safety challenges in multi-agent environments largely unexplored. In this work, we propose a safe offline multi-agent reinforcement learning algorithm that embeds neural individual control barrier functions into the diffusion model to enhance safety during trajectory generation, with control policies recovered through inverse dynamics. We evaluate our algorithm across diverse benchmarks, demonstrating substantial safety improvements while maintaining competitive rewards.

</details>


### [210] [TEDD: Robust Detection of Unstable Temporal Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12643)
*Ricardo Ribeiro Pereira,Bruno Casal Laraña,Nádia Soares,Miguel Araújo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TEDD方法，通过回归模型预测实例时间戳来检测随时间变化的特征，以解决机器学习模型在时变数据上性能退化的问题。该方法能检测数值和类别特征的各种基本变化模式、多变量漂移，无需参数调优，且具有可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界的时间序列数据中，特征分布随时间变化，导致机器学习模型性能迅速下降，因此需要自动识别随时间变化的特征以构建更鲁棒的模型。

Method: 提出TEDD技术，利用回归模型识别哪些特征有助于预测实例的时间戳，从而检测导致模型不稳定的特征。包含两步：(a)识别数据集何时可能导致不稳定的模型；(b)自动检测哪些特征导致缺乏鲁棒性。

Result: 在真实和合成数据上的实验表明，TEDD能检测所有类型的基本变化（数值和类别特征）、检测多变量漂移、返回可比较的变化度量值，且无需参数调优，在特征数和实例数上均具有可扩展性。

Conclusion: TEDD是一种有效的无参数特征漂移检测方法，能全面识别各种类型的分布变化，帮助数据科学家及时采取数据变换等缓解措施，从而构建更稳定的机器学习模型。

Abstract: When working with real-world temporal data, it is common to encounter features whose distribution is changing over time. The naive employment of Machine Learning models on this unstable data might lead to rapidly degrading performance, especially if the new distribution is much different from what was previously seen during training. In order to cope with this problem, it is critical to automatically identify features that are changing over time. With these features detected, data scientists and other practitioners will be able to mitigate the issue (for instance, by applying data transformations), deploying more robust models that retain high performance for longer periods of time. In this paper, we describe which temporal changes a feature should not suffer from, and propose TEDD, a technique to a) identify when a dataset might lead to an unstable Machine Learning model and b) automatically detect which features cause such lack of robustness. In order to achieve it, we leverage a regression model to highlight which features contribute to a good prediction of an instance's timestamp. We compare our approach to other methods in real and synthetic data, testing their detection capability on all simple change patterns. We show that our method: detects all types of basic changes, both for numerical and categorical features; can detect multivariate drifts; returns a comparable value measuring the amount of change of each feature; requires no parameter tuning; and is scalable both on number of features and instances of the dataset.

</details>


### [211] [Physics-Aware Auxiliary Losses Improve Out-of-Distribution Generalization of a GNN Synthesizability Filter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12651)
*Riya Bisht,Dhruv Agarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过在GNN中引入Bertz指数和MMFF94力场能量作为辅助损失，在分子生成模型的分布外（OOD）泛化上取得了微小但统计显著的改进（AUC提升约0.003-0.007），并发现单次种子实验的结论不可靠，需多种子重复验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子生成管道中的合成性过滤器（如SAScore等纯统计模型）在分布外（OOD）场景下性能严重退化，作者希望探索廉价的闭式物理先验作为辅助监督信号是否能提升图神经网络在OOD条件下的泛化能力。

Method: 在GINE主干网络上添加两项辅助损失：基于Bertz指数的拓扑复杂度回归损失，以及基于MMFF94力场能量的应变能软惩罚损失。在65,177分子数据集（HIV+Tox21训练，COCONUT测试）上进行4路消融实验（基线/+复杂度/+应变/+两者），采用5种子+配对bootstrap置信区间进行统计评估。

Result: 所有物理感知变体在OOD上均取得统计显著的微小提升：+复杂度Δ=+0.0060 (95%CI [+0.0023, +0.0102])，+应变Δ=+0.0032 (95%CI [+0.0008, +0.0052])，两者结合Δ=+0.0066 (95%CI [+0.0038, +0.0093])，且仅在OOD评估下可见。同时发现单次种子实验会产生定性不同的误导性结论。

Conclusion: 物理先验作为辅助损失可小幅但可靠地改善OOD泛化，效果适中；方法学上强调多种子重复评估的必要性，单次实验结论不可靠。

Abstract: Machine-learning drug-discovery pipelines increasingly rely on generative models that propose molecules far from the data used to train downstream synthesizability filters. Existing filters (SAScore, SCScore, RAscore, DeepSA) are purely statistical and degrade in exactly this out-of-distribution (OOD) regime. We ask whether cheap, closed-form physical priors, used as auxiliary supervision on a graph neural network (GNN), improve OOD generalization. We add two auxiliary losses to a GINE backbone: a topological complexity regression supervised by the Bertz index, and a strain-energy soft penalty supervised by MMFF94 force-field energy. On a 65,177-molecule corpus (HIV, Tox21, COCONUT) labeled by SAScore thresholds we reproduce a strong in-distribution baseline, then evaluate a 4-way ablation (baseline / +complexity / +strain / +both) on a single-source OOD split (train on drug-like HIV+Tox21, test on COCONUT natural products), repeated over 5 seeds with paired bootstrap confidence intervals. All three physics-aware variants give a small but statistically significant OOD improvement over the baseline (mean OOD AUC 0.9774): +complexity Delta = +0.0060 (95% CI [+0.0023, +0.0102]), +strain Delta = +0.0032 ([+0.0008, +0.0052]), +both Delta = +0.0066 ([+0.0038, +0.0093]); every interval excludes zero, and the combination is best. The variants are indistinguishable in-distribution, so the effect is visible only under OOD evaluation. We are explicit that the effects are modest, and we report a cautionary methodological finding: a single-seed version of this experiment produced a qualitatively different (non-monotone) story that did not survive multi-seed evaluation.

</details>


### [212] [A Zero-shot Generalized Graph Anomaly Detection Framework via Node Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12673)
*Phan Nguyen,Dat Cao,Hien Chu,Khue Hoang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AlignGAD是一个零样本跨域图异常检测框架，通过全局统一模块对齐异构特征、聚类模块捕获组级异常模式、节点差异评分模块聚合异常证据，在多个数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域图异常检测方法依赖数据集特定的特征语义和结构模式，泛化能力受限，无法有效处理异构数据的不同域之间的迁移问题。

Method: AlignGAD包含三个核心模块：(1) 全局统一模块：对齐异构节点特征并在谱域归一化图信号；(2) 聚类模块：构建聚类感知的图视图以捕获组级异常模式；(3) 节点差异评分模块：测量重构差异并从不同图视图聚合异常证据。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验表明，AlignGAD在零样本图异常检测设置下表现有效。

Conclusion: AlignGAD通过特征对齐和多视图异常聚合实现了跨域零样本图异常检测，解决了现有方法泛化能力不足的问题。

Abstract: Cross-domain graph anomaly detection (GAD) aims to identify abnormal nodes in unseen target graphs, showing strong potential in real-world applications with heterogeneous graph data. However, existing methods often depend on dataset-specific feature semantics and structural patterns, which limits their ability to generalize across different domains. To address this challenge, we propose AlignGAD, a zero-shot generalized graph anomaly detection framework. Our framework is built upon three key components: a Global Unification Module that aligns heterogeneous node features and normalizes graph signals in the spectral domain; a Clustering Module that constructs cluster-aware graph views to capture group-level abnormal patterns; and a Node Discrepancy Scoring Module that measures reconstruction discrepancy and aggregates anomaly evidence from different graph views. Experiments on multiple real-world datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of AlignGAD under the zero-shot GAD setting.

</details>


### [213] [Forecasting Is Not Attribution: Localizing Decoder Bypass in Graph-Based Neural Marketing Mix Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12687)
*Yunbo Wang,Bolbi Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DICE-MMM框架，用于诊断图基神经网络营销组合模型中的“归因旁路”问题——即高容量解码器在获得低预测误差的同时，未能将反事实敏感性路由至作为归因对象的图结构。通过三阶段分离（图恢复、预测准确性和图对齐），实验表明低MSE不能作为归因证书，核心瓶颈在于图支撑选择而非预测能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销组合模型常同时追求预测业务结果和归因到营销渠道，但这两个目标并不等价。作者发现图基神经MMM中存在“归因旁路”失效模式：高容量解码器可通过目标自回归、密集通信、共同运动、上下文或潜在记忆获得低预测误差，却未能使反事实敏感性通过作为归因对象的图结构。现有方法混淆了图恢复、预测准确性和归因对齐这三个问题。

Method: 提出DICE-MMM有界诊断与训练框架：Stage 1训练受限图介导解码器的图编码器；Stage 2冻结所选编码器，训练图安全潜在解码器，其跨节点通信必须通过所提供图结构。通过CIG、AR-CIG和图交换测试评估解码器使用情况。在受控R/d/T交换和外部多图原始日志压力测试上验证。

Result: 与CausalMMM相比，DICE提高了稳定图恢复能力。在稀疏目标基准测试中，无图/全图解碼器MSE@7约0.004，AR-CIG nAUPRC接近或低于零，而oracle图达到0.807±0.129且MSE相当。冻结图交换定位瓶颈：相同DICE-hard训练的解码器从学习图输入下的nAUPRC -0.044±0.006到oracle图的0.894±0.027。

Conclusion: 低MSE可能隐藏归因旁路，预测准确性不能作为归因证书。未解决的瓶颈是图支撑选择，而非预测或解码器容量。DICE提供了压力测试和故障定位框架，分离了图基MMM中常被混淆的三个问题。

Abstract: Marketing mix models are used to forecast business outcomes and to attribute those outcomes to marketing channels, but these goals are not equivalent. We study a failure mode in graph-based neural MMM called attribution bypass: a high-capacity decoder can obtain low forecasting error through target autoregression, dense communication, co-movement, context, or latent memory while failing to route counterfactual sensitivity through the graph used as the attribution object. We introduce DICE-MMM as a bounded diagnostic and training framework. We do not claim that observational neural MMM identifies causal effects. Instead, DICE separates three questions often conflated in graph-based MMM: graph recovery, forecasting accuracy, and whether the trained decoder's perturbation-induced influence is graph aligned. Stage 1 trains a graph encoder with a restricted graph-mediated decoder. Stage 2 freezes the selected encoder and trains a graph-safe latent decoder whose cross-node communication must pass through the supplied graph. Decoder use is evaluated with CIG, AR-CIG, and graph-swap tests. Across controlled R/d/T swaps and an external multi-graph rawlog stress test, DICE improves stable graph recovery over CausalMMM. The experiments show that forecasting accuracy is not an attribution certificate: in a sparse-target benchmark, no-graph and full-graph decoders achieve MSE@7 around 0.004 while AR-CIG nAUPRC remains near or below zero, whereas an oracle graph reaches 0.807 +/- 0.129 at comparable MSE. Frozen graph-swap localizes the bottleneck: the same DICE-hard-trained decoder moves from nAUPRC -0.044 +/- 0.006 under learned graph inputs to 0.894 +/- 0.027 with the oracle graph. The contribution is a stress test and failure-localization framework showing that low MSE can hide attribution bypass and that the unresolved bottleneck is graph-support selection, not forecasting or decoder capacity.

</details>


### [214] [M*: A Modular, Extensible, Serving System for Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12688)
*Atindra Jha,Naomi Sagan,Keisuke Kamahori,Irmak Sivgin,Rohan Sanda,Steven Gao,Mark Horowitz,Luke Zettlemoyer,Olivia Hsu,Jure Leskovec,Baris Kasikci,Stephanie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出M*，一个面向复合AI模型的高效通用服务系统。M*将模型表示为数据流图，通过Walk Graph抽象支持任意组件组合、灵活集群放置和模型无关优化，在多个代表性模型上实现了显著优于现有系统的性能（端到端延迟降低20%，实时因子降低2.9倍，吞吐量提升2.7倍，机器人规划速度提升12.5倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型服务框架基于对模型结构的狭隘假设构建，无法适应新出现的复合模型架构（如视觉编码器、语言主干、扩散头、音频编解码器、动作生成器、世界模型预测器等异构组件的集成），亟需一个能够高效服务这类多样化复合模型的通用系统。

Method: M*将复合模型表示为数据流图，处理跨模态和任务的请求作为对这些图的遍历。核心创新是Walk Graph模块化抽象，支持：1) 模型组件的任意组合；2) 物理集群上的灵活放置；3) 分布式运行时中的模型无关优化。

Result: 1) 在BAGEL模型上，文本到图像工作负载的端到端延迟比vLLM-Omni低20%；2) 在Qwen3-Omni模型上，文本到语音工作负载的实时因子降低2.9倍，吞吐量提升2.7倍；3) 在机器人规划任务上，V-JEPA 2-AC rollout基准的速度提升达12.5倍。

Conclusion: M*为高效服务复杂复合模型铺平了道路，且开发工作量极小，证明了Walk Graph抽象在应对复合AI模型服务挑战方面的有效性。

Abstract: We are entering a new era of composite model architectures that integrate diverse components such as vision encoders, language backbones, diffusion and flow heads, audio codecs, action generators, and world-model predictors. Such architectures underpin a broad class of multimodal models, including unified multimodal models, omni models, speech-language models, vision-language-action policies, and world models. However, existing model serving frameworks were built on narrow assumptions about model structure, making them ill-suited to accommodate this new architectural diversity. Here we present M*, a universal serving system for efficient serving of composite AI models. M* represents models as dataflow graphs, processing requests spanning diverse modalities and tasks as traversals over these graphs. The core insight is a modular abstraction that supports arbitrary composition of model components, flexible placement onto a physical cluster, and model-agnostic optimizations within a distributed runtime. We call this abstraction the Walk Graph and show how it can concisely capture composite models from a broad range of families. We instantiate M* on representative models and find that it achieves, on average, 20% lower end-to-end latency than vLLM-Omni for text-to-image workloads on BAGEL, while delivering up to 2.9x lower real-time factor and 2.7x higher throughput for text-to-speech workloads on Qwen3-Omni. M* also outperforms the V-JEPA 2-AC rollout baseline for robotic planning by up to 12.5x. Thus, our work paves the road towards more efficient serving of complex models with minimal developer effort.

</details>


### [215] [LLM-Powered Personalized Glycemic Assessment in Type 2 Diabetes with Wearable Sensor Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12699)
*Yifan Gao,Yanmin Gong,Yun Shi,Yuanxiong Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GlyLLM框架，利用大语言模型整合CGM可穿戴传感器数据与结构化元数据，在血糖预测（RMSE提升13.66%）和糖尿病分类（AUROC提升13.08%）任务上优于传统机器学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2型糖尿病（T2D）血糖管理需要个性化评估，但现有方法多依赖传统机器学习且仅关注历史血糖数据，忽视了个体化信息，导致在不同人群中的性能受限。大语言模型在整合多模态数据和建模序列依赖方面展现出潜力，促使作者探索其在个性化血糖评估中的应用。

Method: 提出GlyLLM框架，利用预训练LLM的先验知识，将CGM传感器数据与结构化元数据（如人口统计、糖尿病调查、生物特征测试等）进行传感器-文本语义抽象融合，实现时序血糖动力学建模。

Result: 在AI-READI数据集上，GlyLLM在血糖预测任务中RMSE平均降低13.66%，在糖尿病分类任务中AUROC平均提升13.08%。消融实验表明，糖尿病调查和生物特征测试比其他健康信息对血糖评估更为关键。

Conclusion: GlyLLM展示了利用大语言模型推进个性化血糖评估的可行性和潜力，为2型糖尿病护理提供了新的有效方法。

Abstract: Type 2 Diabetes (T2D) poses an increasing global health threat, demanding effective glycemic assessment to support personalized and improved diabetes care. Wearable sensors such as continuous glucose monitors (CGM) and fitness trackers offer many valuable insights for glycemic assessment. However, effectively analyzing these data requires integration with essential individual-level context. Existing methods are often based on traditional machine learning (ML) and rely primarily on historical blood glucose measurements and overlook personalized information, which limits their performance across diverse diabetes populations. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated their ability to integrate diverse data modalities while modeling sequential dependencies, motivating the exploration of their potential for personalized glycemic assessment.
  In this paper, we propose GlyLLM, an LLM-powered framework for modeling CGM-based glycemic dynamics through the integration of wearable sensor data and structured metadata. GlyLLM can leverage the extensive prior knowledge of pre-trained LLMs and achieve sensor-text semantic abstraction at decision time. Experiments on two related tasks on the AI-READI dataset demonstrate that our model outperforms traditional ML methods by an average of 13.66\% in Root Mean Squared Error (RMSE) for glucose forecasting and 13.08\% in Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) for diabetes categorization. Additionally, our ablation study shows that diabetes surveys and biometric tests are more critical than other health information for glycemic assessment. Our work presents a promising step toward harnessing the power of LLMs to advance personalized glycemic assessment in T2D care.

</details>


### [216] [A Stabilized Path-Space Approach to Diffusion-Based Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12710)
*Evan Scope Crafts,Umberto Villa,Saviz Mowlavi,Yanting Ma,Hassan Mansour,Wael H. Ali*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于稳定路径空间框架的扩散模型后验采样方法，通过随机最优控制与信任域优化，在非线性算子和多峰后验场景下实现更准确、鲁棒的贝叶斯逆问题求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散后验采样器依赖启发式引导近似，在非线性算子和多峰后验分布中容易失效，缺乏理论保证和不确定性量化能力。

Method: 从基础扩散过程出发，定义轨迹上的似然加权目标测度；引入时间重参数化消除未知初值函数偏差；采用信任域路径空间优化与对数方差目标学习控制律；支持重要性采样校正。

Result: 在具有解析或高质量参考后验的基准逆问题套件上，所提方法在采样精度和不确定性量化上优于主流引导式采样器。

Conclusion: 路径空间视角统一了学习控制与引导式采样，量化了近似控制引入的误差，并为渐进精确后验期望提供了重要性采样校正，显著提升了扩散后验采样的鲁棒性和准确性。

Abstract: Diffusion models provide expressive data-driven priors for Bayesian inverse problems, but many diffusion posterior samplers rely on heuristic guidance approximations that can fail for nonlinear operators and multimodal posteriors. In this work, we develop a stabilized path-space framework for diffusion-based posterior sampling. Starting from a base diffusion process whose terminal marginal represents the prior, we define a likelihood-weighted target measure on trajectories and cast posterior sampling as learning a controlled stochastic process whose path measure matches this target. This formulation connects diffusion posterior sampling to stochastic optimal control while preserving the Bayesian structure needed for uncertainty quantification. We introduce a time reparameterization that makes the path-space control problem well posed by removing the bias induced by the unknown initial value function, without auxiliary training. We then learn the control via a trust-region path-space optimization method with log-variance objectives. The path-space perspective also unifies our learned control approach with existing guidance-based samplers, quantifies the sampling error induced by approximate controls, and yields importance sampling corrections for asymptotically exact posterior expectations. We evaluate the proposed framework on a suite of benchmark inverse problems with analytically characterized or high-quality reference posteriors, enabling principled assessment of sampling accuracy and uncertainty quantification. These experiments provide insight into the behavior of diffusion-based posterior samplers and demonstrate improved accuracy and robustness over leading approaches.

</details>


### [217] [Out-of-Distribution (OOD) Detectors for Open-Set RF Fingerprinting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12718)
*Sudeepta Mondal,Ganesh Sundaramoorthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于信息论的OOD检测方法应用于开放集射频指纹识别（RFF），并引入无需OOD数据即可调参的技术，在POWDER数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 射频指纹识别系统需要在开放世界环境中运行，面临未知发射机信号和时变漂移导致的分布偏移问题。现有OOD检测方法需要辅助OOD数据进行参数调优，但在射频环境中收集代表性OOD数据不切实际，亟需无需OOD数据的调参方案。

Method: 从机器学习文献中引入一组适用于开放集RFF的OOD检测方法，基于信息论建立统一数学框架以系统分析方法并开发新方法；引入无需给定OOD数据即可调优OOD检测器的最新工作，应用于开放集RFF场景。

Result: 在POWDER射频指纹数据集上，无需任何OOD数据调优的检测器性能与使用真实OOD调优数据的基线相当，且显著优于无OOD调优数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: 证明了无需OOD调优数据的OOD检测方法在射频指纹识别问题中具有实际可行性，为开放集RFF提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Radio-frequency (RF) fingerprinting systems must operate in open-world environments where signals from unknown transmitters and temporal drift introduce distribution shift at test time. Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection provides a natural framework for this problem, yet its application to RF fingerprinting (RFF) remains limited. A key barrier to their adoption is that most OOD detectors require auxiliary OOD data for parameter tuning, an assumption that is difficult to satisfy in RF environments where representative OOD data is impractical to collect. In this work, we introduce a promising set of OOD detection methods from the machine learning literature to open-set RFF domain. We present these methods within a unified mathematical framework based on information theory, which is a natural framework for communication systems. Our framework allows for the systematic analysis of methods and development of new methods. We further demonstrate the applicability of recent work on tuning OOD detectors without given OOD tuning data for open-set RFF. We evaluate on the POWDER RF fingerprinting dataset, showing that detectors tuned without any given OOD data achieve performance comparable to baselines with access to true OOD tuning data and greatly out-perform baseline approaches without access to true OOD tuning data, showcasing the practical viability for the RFF problem.

</details>


### [218] [ProPlay: Procedural World Models for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12780)
*Yijun Ma,Zehong Wang,Yiyang Li,Ziming Li,Xiaoguang Guo,Weixiang Sun,Chuxu Zhang,Yanfang Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ProPlay 提出了一种程序性世界模型，通过抽象成功轨迹为程序并构建程序图，在部分可观测环境中实现自我进化。Agent 通过执行前模拟（preplay）规划未来路径，执行后利用环境反馈更新图结构，显著提升了环境理解与自我进化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM-agent 方法虽拥有记忆或规划模块，但很少能闭环地将两者结合以持续精炼对环境动态的内在理解，尤其是在部分可观测环境中，agent 需要主动探索、从有限反馈中学习并判断何时信任先验经验，这仍然是困难的。

Method: ProPlay 将成功轨迹抽象为"程序"，并组织成程序图（procedure graph），图中每条边（状态转移）关联一个可靠性记录嵌入（reliability record embedding），用于估计该转移对任务的贡献。每个 episode 前，agent 在已知图结构上模拟未来程序轨迹作为软引导；执行后，利用环境反馈精炼图结构。

Result: 在公开基准上，ProPlay 在环境理解能力和自我进化能力上一致优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: ProPlay 通过程序级别的预演（procedure-level preplay）闭环了记忆与规划，使得 agent 能够在与环境交互中持续改进对动态环境的理解，实现无需外部监督的自我进化。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents are expected to improve through interaction without external supervision, but this remains difficult in partially observable environments where agents must explore actively, learn from limited feedback, and decide when to trust prior experience. Existing LLM-agent methods often rely on memory or planning modules, yet they rarely close the loop between them to continually refine an internal understanding of environment dynamics. We introduce ProPlay, a procedural world model that supports procedure-level preplay, where agents can rehearse future procedural paths using the learned world knowledge. Rather than representing experience as isolated rules or low-level action constraints, ProPlay abstracts successful trajectories into procedures and organizes them in a procedure graph that captures causal transitions among task stages. Each transition is associated with a reliability record embedding to estimate its task-specific contribution from past outcomes. Before each episode, ProPlay simulates future procedural trajectories over known graph structures as structured soft guidance; after execution, it refines the graph using environment feedback. Experiments on public benchmarks show that ProPlay consistently improves environment understanding and self-evolution capability over strong baselines. Our code has been released in https://github.com/antman9914/proplay.

</details>


### [219] [SymQNet: Amortized Acquisition for Low-Latency Adaptive Hamiltonian Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12808)
*Yash Vardhan Tomar,Dheeraj Peddireddy,Vaneet Aggarwal*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SymQNet，一种基于摊销强化学习的低延迟自适应哈密顿学习方法，通过离线学习后验条件采集策略并在在线阶段快速执行，显著降低采集延迟（在5量子位上比在线基线降低47.1倍和72.6倍），使自适应哈密顿学习适用于重复低延迟工作负载。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应哈密顿学习中，每次实验选择都需要重新计算贝叶斯设计规则，这一步骤耗时数秒，在数百次实验的累积下导致显著的壁钟时间开销，限制了自适应方法的实用性。

Method: SymQNet采用摊销强化学习方法，离线学习一个后验条件化的采集策略，在线阶段仅需一次快速的前向传播即可做出决策，同时保留贝叶斯后验反馈机制。

Result: 在横向场伊辛模型基准测试中，SymQNet显著降低了采集延迟：5量子位时，相比有界Fisher信息搜索和有界两步BALD，采集决策延迟分别降低47.1倍和72.6倍；12量子位时，完整模拟步骤耗时SymQNet为1.02秒，而有界两步BALD为13.27秒。

Conclusion: 学习式采集策略能够使自适应哈密顿学习在重复低延迟工作负载场景下变得实用。

Abstract: Adaptive Hamiltonian learning is central to calibrating and characterizing quantum devices. In an adaptive controller, choosing the next experiment is itself a computation. Bayesian design rules are recomputed after every posterior update, and that step can take seconds. Across hundreds of shots, those seconds become a significant wall-clock cost for adaptivity. We introduce SymQNet, an amortized reinforcement-learning approach for low-latency adaptive Hamiltonian learning. SymQNet learns a posterior-conditioned acquisition policy offline, then uses a fast policy forward pass online while retaining Bayesian posterior feedback. On transverse-field Ising benchmarks, SymQNet substantially reduces acquisition latency relative to bounded Fisher-information search and bounded two-step Bayesian active learning by disagreement (BALD). At five qubits, it reduces acquisition-only decision latency by $47.1\times$ and $72.6\times$ relative to these online baselines; at twelve qubits, full simulated steps take $1.02$ s for SymQNet versus $13.27$ s for bounded two-step BALD. Overall, we show that learned acquisition can make adaptive Hamiltonian learning practical for repeated low-latency workloads.

</details>


### [220] [CLARITree: Cholesky and Lookahead Accelerations for Regression with Interpretable Piecewise Linear Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12840)
*Yixiao Wang,Hayden McTavish,Varun Babbar,Margo Seltzer,Cynthia Rudin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合前瞻搜索策略和高效秩一Cholesky更新的近最优稀疏分段线性回归树算法，在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间实现了有利权衡，并显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回归树虽具有可解释性，但贪心算法性能有限，而最优方法（如动态规划、分支定界）计算代价过高，尤其对一般线性回归树而言。现有前瞻策略主要在分类任务中有效，缺乏针对回归树的高效近最优方法。

Method: 将前瞻式搜索策略与高效的秩一Cholesky更新（Gram矩阵）相结合，构建近最优的稀疏分段线性回归树。通过Cholesky更新避免重复计算，提升搜索效率。

Result: 理论和实验均表明，该方法在计算效率、预测精度和稀疏性之间实现了更优的权衡，且扩展性显著优于现有最优方法。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法有效解决了线性回归树在近最优构建时面临的计算瓶颈，验证了前瞻搜索结合高效矩阵更新策略在回归场景中的有效性，并为可解释且高性能的模型构建提供了新途径。

Abstract: Regression trees are among the most interpretable yet expressive model classes in machine learning. Historically, greedy induction has been the dominant approach for constructing well-performing regression trees. While optimal methods based on dynamic programming and branch-and-bound exist, they are computationally prohibitive for general linear regression trees, despite often achieving substantially better performance than greedy approaches. Recent work has shown that specialized lookahead strategies can dramatically improve runtime while maintaining near-optimal performance, primarily in classification settings. In this work, we develop a novel algorithm for near-optimal, sparse, piecewise linear regression trees that combines a lookahead-style search strategy with efficient rank-one Cholesky updates of the Gram matrix. We demonstrate, both theoretically and empirically, that our method achieves a favorable trade-off between computational efficiency, predictive accuracy, and sparsity, and scales significantly better than the current state of the art.

</details>


### [221] [TimeROME-DLM: Temporal Causal Tracing and Low-Rank Inference-Time Knowledge Editing for Masked Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12841)
*Zhengtao Yao,Liuyang Song,Hongbo Zhang,Chenhao Wei,Haoyan Xu,Guang Yang,Siheng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TimeROME-DLM，首个无需训练、无需梯度、推理时执行的MDLM知识编辑框架。它通过时序因果追踪（TIE）定位关键编辑坐标，并利用低秩残差编辑记忆在扩散前向过程中施加岭回归更新，无需修改模型权重。在TOFU forget01任务上，对LLaDA-8B的遗忘集log-probability降低约83 nats，保持集几乎不变，速度提升4-14倍，零额外VRAM开销，且可跨模型迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的知识编辑与遗忘方法（如ROME、MEMIT）均针对自回归Transformer设计，在掩码扩散语言模型（MDLM）中因迭代去噪的假设失效或梯度更新导致显存开销大（数十GB）且学习率敏感导致模型崩溃，亟需一种适用于MDLM的轻量级编辑方案。

Method: TimeROME-DLM由两部分组成：（1）时序间接效应（TIE）因果追踪协议，识别每个事实中在后续去噪步骤中最强驱动对象预测的坐标；（2）封闭形式低秩残差编辑记忆，跨所有遗忘事实聚合主体键和目标增量，在该坐标处施加单次岭回归正则化更新，并通过稀疏化限制效用溢出。模型权重完全冻结，仅需调优三个超参数（alpha, lambda, q）。

Result: 在TOFU forget01任务上，针对TOFU微调的LLaDA-8B-Base，遗忘集log-probability降低约83 nats；保持集log-probability在安全操作点保持近乎平坦（约1 nat内）；可跨LLaDA-8B-Instruct、Dream-7B、MMaDA-8B、DiffuLLaMA-7B、LLaDA-MoE-1.4B迁移；支持50个连续插入事实；相比最强训练时基线实现4-14倍加速且零额外VRAM；可亚线性扩展至400个事实。

Conclusion: TimeROME-DLM填补了自回归LLM与MDLM之间在"定位-编辑"范式上的差距，以极低计算代价实现了MDLM的高效知识编辑与遗忘，证明了无需梯度更新即可在扩散模型上进行精准知识操作的可能性。

Abstract: Masked diffusion language models (MDLMs) such as LLaDA now rival autoregressive (AR) LLMs, but every existing knowledge-editing and unlearning method (ROME, MEMIT, etc.) targets AR transformers and either makes assumptions that fail under iterative denoising, or requires gradient updates whose backward-pass activations cost tens of GB of extra VRAM and which collapse MDLMs at standard learning rates. We introduce TimeROME-DLM, the first training-free, gradient-free, inference-time knowledge-editing framework for MDLMs. It couples two components: a Temporal Indirect Effect (TIE) causal-tracing protocol that identifies, for each fact, the coordinate whose intervention most strongly drives the object prediction at later denoising steps; and a closed-form, low-rank residual edit memory that aggregates subject keys and target deltas across all forget facts and applies a single ridge-regularised update at that coordinate at every diffusion forward, with sparsification to limit utility spillover. Backbone weights stay frozen; only three hyperparameters (alpha, lambda, q) are tuned on a small validation split. On TOFU forget01 with TOFU-finetuned LLaDA-8B-Base, TimeROME-DLM cuts forget-set log-probability by roughly 83 nats. The same configuration transfers to LLaDA-8B-Instruct, Dream-7B, MMaDA-8B, DiffuLLaMA-7B, and LLaDA-MoE-1.4B. It keeps retain-set log-probability nearly flat (within ~1 nat at the utility-safe operating point) across 50 sequentially inserted facts, delivers a four- to fourteen-fold wall-clock speedup with zero additional VRAM over the strongest converged training-time baseline, and scales sub-linearly to 400 facts. TimeROME-DLM closes the locate-then-edit gap between AR LLMs and MDLMs at a fraction of the computational cost.

</details>


### [222] [Interpretable Factor Decomposition for Decision Intelligence in Large-Scale Financial Markets: Evidence from China's A-Share Market](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12843)
*Xiao Han,Yao Xiao,Zhen Zhang,Moxuan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可解释的机器学习管道，使用XGBoost+TreeSHAP对2009-2019年中国A股3632只股票进行截面收益可预测性分解。模型在55个月滚动窗口外样本中实现AUC 0.547，多空组合月收益+2.38%（年化夏普2.23），经Carhart四因子调整后alpha仍显著（+2.31%/月，t=7.48）。SHAP归因显示行为信号（换手率和动量）贡献58.2%的预测力，估值比率仅占10.7%。消融分析与SHAP的差异揭示了特征可替代性结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器学习选股模型的"黑箱"特性缺乏可解释性，投资者无法理解模型预测的来源，难以满足合规与风控要求。本文旨在通过可解释的归因分解，量化各因子对收益预测的贡献，并揭示特征之间的交互与替代结构。

Method: 采用XGBoost模型进行收益预测，结合TreeSHAP进行特征归因分解。使用60个月滚动窗口，55个月外样本测试，涵盖2009-2019年3632只中国A股。通过消融分析交叉验证SHAP归因排序，并对比两者差异以揭示特征可替代性结构。

Result: XGBoost模型AUC为0.547，多空组合月收益2.38%（t=5.94，年化夏普2.23），Carhart四因子调整后alpha为2.31%/月（t=7.48）。SHAP分解表明，行为信号（换手率和动量）平均贡献58.2%的预测归因，估值比率仅贡献10.7%。消融分析与SHAP排序的差异揭示了特征间的可替代性结构。

Conclusion: 可解释机器学习方法能够有效分解股票收益可预测性的因子来源，行为信号在中国A股市场中主导预测力。SHAP与消融分析的结合应用比单一方法更能揭示特征间的替代关系，为投资组合构建和风险监控提供了可审计的透明框架。

Abstract: We present an interpretable machine learning pipeline to decompose Cross-Sectional Equity Return Predictability into auditable factor contribution. We apply an XGBoost model with TreeSHAP attribution and conduct stress testing on 3632 Chinese A-share stocks from 2009 until 2019. Using 60-month, rolling windows over 55 months of out-of-sample data, XGBoost obtains a mean AUC of 0.547 and +2.38%/month (Newey-West t = 5.94; Annualized Sharpe 2.23) long-short spread for the top vs bottom quintiles. This alpha is persistent after adjusting for the Carhart four-factor model (+2.31%/month; t = 7.48). SHAP Decomposition indicates that behavioral signals (turnover and momentum) account for 58.2% of predictive attribution compared to 10.7% for valuation ratios, on average, across 55 industry groups. Ablation analysis serves to cross-validate this ranking and provides evidence that SHAP and ablation diverge in a manner that highlights feature substitutability structure that is largely invisible to either method used in isolation.

</details>


### [223] [Multimodal Graph Negative Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12863)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphMNL提出了一种图感知的多模态负学习方法，通过负学习指导分支间训练，解决多模态属性图中节点级分支语义不平衡问题，在Grocery和Reddit M数据集上取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态属性图中不同模态分支（结构拓扑、文本、视觉）在节点级别的语义信息量和可靠性存在差异，主导分支可能存在偏差，现有方法通过跨分支对齐强迫次优分支模仿主导预测，可能传播偏差并抑制有用语义。

Method: 提出GraphMNL框架：构建分支库，通过图感知可靠性仲裁识别主导分支和次优分支，门控不稳定迁移，对非目标类别应用目标保留的负学习，实现监督损失与分支指导的解耦。

Result: 在Grocery数据集上准确率达72.47%，在Reddit M数据集上F1分数达76.60，综合实验取得最佳性能。

Conclusion: 负学习作为跨分支指导能有效缓解分支语义不平衡问题，避免主导分支偏差传播，保留各分支有用语义信息。

Abstract: Multimodal attributed graphs (MAGs) integrate graph topology with heterogeneous modality attributes, such as text and images, thereby enabling richer modeling of complex relational systems. However, such expressiveness also makes learning on MAGs depend on multiple semantic sources, including structural topology, textual and visual attributes, each of which can be regarded as a branch for node representation. Node-level branch semantic imbalance arises when these branches differ across nodes in semantic informativeness and reliability: a branch that provides discriminative semantics for one node may mislead another due to bias in modality quality or structural context. Existing methods often mitigate such heterogeneity through cross-branch agreement or alignment, implicitly treating the dominant prediction as reliable supervision. When the dominant branch is biased, forced imitation may propagate its bias to other branches and suppress original semantics that are useful for classification. We propose GraphMNL, a graph-aware multimodal negative learning framework that addresses this issue by using Negative Learning as cross-branch guidance. Instead of forcing inferior branches to imitate a teacher prediction, the model teaches them which classes a node is unlikely to belong to. GraphMNL builds a branch library, identifies dominant and inferior branches via graph-aware reliability arbitration, gates unstable transfer, and applies target-preserving negative learning over non-target classes. This design decouples target supervision from branch guidance so that supervised losses learn the correct class, while Negative Learning suppresses unlikely alternatives when branch agreement is unreliable. Through the comprehensive experimental evaluation, GraphMNL achieves the best performance on Grocery datasets with 72.47% accuracy and 76.60 F1 score on Reddit M datasets.

</details>


### [224] [SMGFM: Spectral Multimodal Graph Pretraining for Multimodal-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12867)
*Zhengyu Wu,Xu Wang,Hongchao Qin,Xunkai Li,Guang Zeng,Rong-Hua Li,Guoren Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SMGFM是一个频谱多模态图预训练框架，通过图频分解将节点信号拆分为低频（拓扑一致语义）和高频（模态特有语义），实现频带级语义角色分配和跨模态交互，在MAG数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态属性图（MAGs）中，结构诱导语义和模态固有语义对下游任务贡献不同，传统方法将两者耦合设计导致问题：前者强调拓扑平滑一致性，后者需保留局部细粒度差异，不应统一平滑或对齐。关键在于跨模态融合前识别语义角色。

Method: 利用图频变化作为先验，提出SMGFM框架：(1) 通过可扩展Chebyshev滤波器构建频率分辨模态令牌；(2) 通过拓扑条件路由估计频带耦合可靠性；(3) 在融合前进行频带-模态交互；(4) 频率路由目标函数对齐平滑共识路径同时保留模态特定路径。

Result: 在MAG数据集上的图级和模态级任务中均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: SMGFM通过图频分解有效地解耦了多模态图中结构语义和模态语义，缓解了空间域纠缠和统一跨模态对齐问题。

Abstract: Multimodal-attributed graphs (MAGs) couple graph topology with node semantics from text, images, and other modalities. Traditional graph learning contextualizes node semantics by coupling topology with node features. However, this coupling design becomes troublesome in MAGs, where structure-induced and modality-intrinsic semantics may contribute differently to downstream tasks. Structure-induced semantics promote relational consistency through smooth topological variation, whereas modality-intrinsic semantics often encode local, fine-grained distinctions that should not be uniformly smoothed or aligned. Therefore, the key challenge is to identify semantic roles before cross-modal fusion. To this end, we leverage graph-frequency variation as a prior, where low-frequency components capture topology-consistent semantics and high-frequency components preserve modality-specific semantics. Based on this intuition, we propose SMGFM, a spectral multimodal graph pretraining framework that decomposes each modality-specific node signal into graph-frequency bands and assigns band-level semantic roles before cross-modal interaction. Concretely, SMGFM constructs frequency-resolved modality tokens with scalable Chebyshev filters, estimates their coupling reliability through topology-conditioned routing, and performs band-modality interaction before fusion. Its frequency-routed objectives align smooth consensus routes while preserving modality-specific routes, mitigating spatial-domain entanglement and uniform cross-modal alignment. Extensive experiments conducted on the MAG datasets demonstrate that SMGFM achieves state-of-the-art performance across graph-level and modality-level tasks.

</details>


### [225] [Multi-Bitwidth Quantization for LLMs Using Additive Codebooks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12876)
*Liza Babaoglu,Shuangyi Chen,Ashish Khisti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Drop-by-Drop提出了一种多比特宽度的后训练量化框架，允许从单个训练好的模型中实现推理时自适应精度控制，在无需重训练的情况下管理性能与效率的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型需要在异构硬件和资源约束下部署，但现有方法无法在不重训练的情况下自适应地管理性能与效率的权衡。

Method: 基于信息论和逐次细化理论，利用LLM权重服从高斯分布的特性，在加权均方误差失真下优化逐比特重建；引入Matryoshka式监督到损失函数中，利用可加性码本结构，使有序子集能在每个精度级别上生成准确的局部重建。

Result: 单个检查点即可支持多种比特宽度，显著减少存储和内存开销，同时在Qwen、LLaMA、Gemma、Mistral等主流架构上保持具有竞争力的困惑度和准确率。

Conclusion: Drop-by-Drop通过理论驱动的后训练量化框架，实现了从单模型到多比特宽度的灵活部署，有效解决了LLM在不同硬件环境下的自适应推理问题。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed across heterogeneous hardware with varying resource constraints, the ability to adaptively manage the trade-off between performance and efficiency without retraining is critical. We propose Drop-by-Drop, a novel multi-bitwidth post-training quantization framework that enables inference-time precision control over LLM weights from a single trained model. Our method is theoretically grounded in information theory and successive refinement. We establish that LLM weights, which commonly follow a Gaussian distribution, can be optimally reconstructed with increasing fidelity as additional bits are incorporated, under a weighted mean squared error distortion motivated by LLM loss functions. To realize this in practice, Drop-by-Drop incorporates Matryoshka-style supervision into the loss function, exploiting the structure of additive codebooks. Drop-by-Drop produces a single model where ordered subsets of codebooks yield accurate partial reconstructions at each precision level. This approach significantly reduces storage and memory overhead by allowing a single checkpoint to serve multiple bitwidths, while maintaining competitive perplexity and accuracy across major architectures, such as Qwen, LLaMA, Gemma, and Mistral.

</details>


### [226] [LongSpike: Fractional Order Spiking State Space Models for Efficient Long Sequence Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12895)
*Xinrui He,Qiyu Kang,Xuhao Li,Zheng-Jun Zha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LongSpike 将分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）引入脉冲神经网络（SNN），通过分数阶微积分实现长程记忆依赖，在长序列任务中显著优于现有SNN方法，同时保持稀疏突触计算的高能效特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN架构普遍基于一阶常微分方程（ODE）建模神经元状态演化，这种一阶假设导致"无记忆"瓶颈，限制了模型对长序列任务中复杂长程依赖关系的捕获能力。

Method: 将控制理论中的分数阶状态空间模型（f-SSM）扩展至脉冲神经域，突破传统整数阶SSM限制，通过分数阶微积分实现具有长记忆核的神经元动力学层次化整合；同时利用状态空间公式支持高效的并行训练，缓解分数阶算子的计算开销和并行化挑战。

Result: 在Long Range Arena（LRA）、大规模WikiText-103、Speech Commands等基准测试中，LongSpike在准确率上超越当前最优的SNN方法，同时保持了稀疏突触计算的优势。

Conclusion: LongSpike通过分数阶状态空间建模有效解决了SNN在长序列任务中的记忆瓶颈问题，为SNN处理长程依赖提供了一种高效且精确的新范式。

Abstract: Spiking Neural Networks (SNNs) are well-regarded for their biological plausibility and energy efficiency in processing sequential data. However, dominant SNN architectures typically rely on first-order Ordinary Differential Equations (ODEs) to govern neuronal state transitions. This first-order assumption imposes a "memoryless" bottleneck, limiting the model's capacity to capture the complex, long-range dependencies inherent in long-sequence tasks. In this work, we propose LongSpike, a novel SNN framework that integrates fractional-order State-Space Modeling, or f-SSM, from control theory into the spiking domain. By extending traditional integer-order SSMs to the fractional-calculus regime, LongSpike enables the hierarchical integration of neuronal dynamics with long-memory kernels. To mitigate the computational overhead and parallelization challenges typically associated with fractional operators, we leverage a state-space formulation that supports efficient, parallel training. Empirical evaluations on challenging benchmarks, including Long Range Arena (LRA), large-scale WikiText-103, and Speech Commands, demonstrate that LongSpike outperforms state-of-the-art SNNs in accuracy while preserving sparse synaptic computation. The code is available at https://github.com/xinruihe389-commits/LongSpike.

</details>


### [227] [Selecting Samples on Graphs: A Unified Dataset Pruning Framework for Lossless Training Acceleration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12913)
*Dongyue Wu,Zilin Guo,Xiaoyu Li,Jiajia Liu,Jingdong Chen,Nong Sang,Changxin Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于图的统一数据集剪枝框架，通过最大权重团问题（MWCP）联合建模样本的内在价值和外在多样性，在理论上提供近似保证，并在ImageNet-1k上实现40%以上的训练时间缩减且不损失精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集剪枝方法要么依赖内在信号（独立评估样本），要么依赖外在信号（通过成对关系促进多样性），各自只捕捉了样本效用的一方面，在不同剪枝比率或数据分布下缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 将数据集建模为加权图：节点权重编码内在价值，边权重编码外在价值；将剪枝问题转化为最大权重团问题（MWCP）；利用MWCP的贪心解（基于样本边际增益）进行求解，并在温和条件下给出理论近似保证。

Result: 在ImageNet-1k上使用ResNet-50，在保持精度的前提下将训练时间减少40%以上，且优于现有剪枝方法。

Conclusion: 统一了内在信号和外在信号的数据集剪枝框架，具备理论保证和实际有效性，适用于广泛的样本重要性度量指标。

Abstract: The rapid growth of modern training datasets has significantly increased computational cost, motivating dataset pruning~(DP) methods which retain only a subset of informative samples to reduce training cost.
  Existing pruning criteria typically rely on either intrinsic signals that assess samples independently or extrinsic signals that promote diversity via pairwise relations.
  While effective in their own specific regimes, each captures only one aspect of sample utility and lacks robustness across different pruning ratios or data distribution.
  In this work, we present a unified graph-based DP framework.
  By modeling the dataset as a weighted graph, where node weights encode intrinsic value and edge weights encode extrinsic value, DP can be cast as a Maximum Weight Clique Problem (MWCP).
  Although MWCP is NP-hard, its structure admits a principled greedy solution based on sample-wise marginal gains.
  Under a few mild conditions, we further prove that this unified objective enjoys a formal approximation guarantee, which applies to a broad family of importance metrics and provides practical design guidelines.
  Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing DP methods while substantially reducing training cost, reducing training time by over 40\% without sacrificing accuracy on ImageNet-1k with ResNet-50.

</details>


### [228] [Where Computation Lives Inside TabPFN: Causal Localisation of Attention Head Function](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12917)
*Atharva Gupta,Dhruv Kumar,Murari Mandal,Saurabh Deshpande*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5进行因果机制分析，发现其逐特征的注意力头在层间存在明确的时间特化：主导注意力头在峰值层的因果必要性比其他头高出2-5倍，且其主导层随任务复杂度变化。对比激活引导在跨样本迁移中失败，归因于TabPFN的上下文学习机制依赖上下文注意力而非稳定的参数方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解表格基础模型TabPFN 2.5的内部计算机制，特别是逐特征注意力头如何在层间分配计算，以及模型的推理可操控性。

Method: 使用激活修补（activation patching）、消融实验（ablation）和注意力熵（attention entropy）分析两个合成回归数据集。

Result: 主导注意力头的因果必要性在峰值层比其他头高2-5倍，其主导层随任务复杂度变化；其余注意力头呈现对称的后期层分布。注意力熵和修补实验提供了主导头计算活跃层的一致性证据。对比激活引导在跨样本迁移中失败。

Conclusion: TabPFN的计算机制存在明确的时间特化结构，但其上下文学习机制依赖上下文注意力而非稳定参数方向，导致对比激活引导无法跨样本迁移。

Abstract: We present the first causal mechanistic analysis of a tabular foundation model, investigating how TabPFN 2.5's feature wise attention heads distribute computation across layers. Using activation patching, ablation, and attention entropy across two synthetic regression datasets, we find clear temporal specialisation: one head's causal necessity dominates that of the others by 2 to 5 times at peak layer, with its dominant layer shifting across tasks of different complexity, while the remaining heads exhibit symmetric late layer profiles. Attention entropy and patching provide convergent evidence for the computationally active layers of the dominant head. We additionally investigate inference time steerability via contrastive activation steering, which fails to transfer across samples. We attribute this result to TabPFN's in context learning mechanism, which encodes task structure through context dependent attention rather than the stable parametric directions that make steering tractable in language models.

</details>


### [229] [LoRA-Muon: Spectral Steepest Descent on the Low-Rank Manifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12921)
*Franz Louis Cesista,Katherine Crowson,Cédric Simal,Stella Biderman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoRA-Muon优化器，将Muon优化器的谱最速下降规则应用于低秩设置，解决了LoRA在初始化敏感、学习率跨秩迁移性差及难以超越密集基线等问题。实验表明，其最优学习率可在秩、宽度、深度间迁移，且低秩代理能恢复密集训练的最佳学习率，在计算匹配的实验中达到更低验证损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA虽能显著降低微调的计算和内存成本，但使用因子级优化器（如AdamW）时存在初始化敏感、最优学习率难以跨秩迁移、常无法击败密集基线等问题，亟需一种更适合低秩设置的优化方法。

Method: 将Muon优化器的谱最速下降规则推广至低秩场景，提出LoRA-Muon，并引入分离权重衰减规则。该优化器无需QR分解、不存储二阶矩，比LoRA-RITE更高效且对加速器友好。

Result: 在TinyShakespeare实验中，秩-2代理恢复了密集训练的最佳学习率；秩-32 LoRA-Muon在种子平均扫掠中达到比密集基线更低的平均验证损失；且最优学习率可跨秩、宽度、深度和因子重缩放迁移。

Conclusion: LoRA-Muon是低秩场景下全秩Muon及Shampoo系列优化器的良好代理，兼具计算效率与性能优势，解决了LoRA优化中的关键痛点。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) significantly reduces compute and memory costs for finetuning Deep Learning models but is often harder to tune than dense training: when using factor-wise optimizers such as AdamW, it is sensitive to initialization choices, its optimal learning rates transfer poorly across ranks, and it often fails to beat dense baselines. We derive LoRA-Muon by applying the Muon optimizer's spectral steepest-descent rule to the low-rank setting. Along with our split weight-decay rule, our main claim is that LoRA-Muon is a good low-rank proxy for full-rank Muon and Shampoo-family optimizers. Its optimal learning rates transfer across rank, width, depth, and factor-rescaling. In our compute-matched TinyShakespeare study, a rank-$2$ proxy recovers the dense best tested learning rate, and a rank-$32$ LoRA-Muon run attains lower mean validation loss than the dense baseline in the seed-averaged sweep. We further show that the Spectron optimizer depends on arbitrary factor scaling, so it would likely be a poor fit when finetuning starts from badly imbalanced factors, and that LoRA-RITE's simplified QR-coordinate core implements the same spectral update. LoRA-Muon computes that update without QR-decomposition and avoids storing second moments, making it more accelerator-friendly and memory-efficient.

</details>


### [230] [Order Is Not Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12923)
*Gareth Seneque,Lap-Hang Ho,Nafise Erfanian Saeedi,Jeffrey Molendijk,Tim Elson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"序≠控"的核心论点，定义了一种接收器门控响应律（分母索引算子），并在生物系统（小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼）、大语言模型、适配器和随机算子面板中验证。控制被定义为在有限努力下实现目标移动，同时损伤、逃避、无效格式、过驱和不必要努力保持有界。LLM面板显示响应向量成分符号预测准确率达72.8-73.7%，非零成分达84.3-84.8%；观察者预测系统效应93.6%、目标/预言家家族91.7%。研究建立了介观控制层级的驱动-耗散响应系统框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI对齐、可解释性、引导和神经扰动研究识别了产生"序"的对象，但"序"并不等同于"控制"。作者旨在严格区分二者，并建立一种普适的、接收器门控的控制响应律框架，跨越生物系统和人工系统。

Method: 定义分母索引算子作为响应律的形式化表达，在多个系统面板（生物：小鼠ALM、线虫、斑马鱼；LLM；适配器；随机算子）中实证检验该算子的存在性和性质。LLM实验中采用四种物质条件测量响应向量，使用成分符号准确率和观察者预测准确率作为评估指标。

Result: 1）生物系统提供物理响应算子证据，同时排除坐标同一性和控制器结论；2）LLM响应向量成分符号预测准确率72.8-73.7%，非零成分84.3-84.8%；3）观察者预测系统效应准确率93.6%，目标/预言家家族91.7%；4）适配器作为制备介质重塑易感性；5）随机算子面板区分测量机会与可部署行动策略。

Conclusion: 证据支持局域可接受控制和可测量随机响应算子，但将可部署预生成控制、隐藏/logit因果充分性、生物到LLM的坐标同一性以及字面热力学量排除在研究范围之外。研究建立了介观控制层级的驱动-耗散响应系统理论。

Abstract: AI alignment, interpretability, steering, and neural perturbation studies identify order-inducing objects. We argue that order is not control. Control requires a receiver-gated response law: a denominator-indexed operator mapping material state, action/drive, bath, and receiver state to response displacement, sinks, effort, and basin projection. We identify it across biological, LLM, adapter, and stochastic-operator panels. The laws are local: an intervention can be admitted, saturated, sign-changing, leaky, or overdriven depending on medium, bath, receiver state, action port, and comparator. Control is assigned when finite effort moves a target or outcome-readout class under the same denominator while damage, null/evasive, invalid format, overdrive, and unnecessary effort stay bounded. Mouse ALM, C. elegans, and zebrafish panels provide physical response-operator evidence while excluding coordinate identity and controller conclusions. LLM panels show generated-output response laws: across four material conditions, response vectors are predictable at 72.8-73.7% component-sign accuracy, rising to 84.3-84.8% on nonzero components; held-out observers predict system-effect and target/oracle families at 93.6% and 91.7% accuracy. Constitution-conditioned adapters reshape susceptibility as prepared media, and stochastic-operator panels separate measured opportunity from deployable action policies. This gives a driven-dissipative response-system account at the mesoscopic control level: drives act through prepared media, baths, and receivers, producing admitted movement, impedance, sinks, or overdrive. The evidence supports local admitted control and measurable stochastic response operators, while leaving deployable pre-generation control, hidden/logit causal sufficiency, biological-to-LLM coordinate identity, and literal thermodynamic quantities outside scope.

</details>


### [231] [Is Spurious Correlation Removal Always Learnable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12930)
*Yibo Zhou,Bo Li,Hai-Miao Hu,Hanzi Wang,Xiaokang Zhang,Ruifan Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了不变性学习存在计算障碍：即使不变结构在统计上可识别，在一种黑盒可采样监督稀疏恢复原语假设下，存在可采样的多环境实例（一维不变子空间），穷举搜索可用多项式样本学习，但任何多项式时间算法都无法达到恒定精度恢复。在充分环境多样性和局部高斯正则性下，最小最大风险为Θ(k(d-k)/(n|ℰ|))；标签诱导偏移下相变阈值n*∝k(d-k)/(|ℰ|γ²)，估计误差随1/γ²缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示不变性学习在统计可识别情况下仍可能面临计算上的根本性障碍，并量化环境多样性对学习难度的影响。

Method: 基于黑盒可采样监督稀疏恢复原语的理论假设，构造可采样的多环境实例，结合穷举搜索与多项式时间算法的对比分析；引入分离参数γ刻画环境多样性，推导最小最大风险界和相变条件。

Result: 存在计算障碍：一维不变子空间问题在多项式时间内无法达到恒定精度恢复；最小最大风险与样本数、环境数、维度成Θ关系；标签诱导偏移下出现相变，估计误差与1/γ²成比例。

Conclusion: 不变性学习不仅面临统计可识别性问题，还存在计算复杂性障碍；环境多样性是关键因素，γ参数控制识别能力和目标函数曲率；合成与真实实验验证了理论预测，并启发简单的多样性诊断方法。

Abstract: Invariant learning can fail even when the invariant structure is statistically identifiable. We show a conditional computational barrier: under a black-box samplable supervised sparse recovery primitive motivated by average-case sparse-recovery reductions, there exist \emph{samplable} multi-environment instances with a one-dimensional predictive invariant subspace ($k=1$) that are learnable with polynomial samples by exhaustive search, while any polynomial-time constant-accuracy recovery algorithm would contradict the primitive. We further quantify environment diversity by a separation parameter $γ$, which controls identifiability and the curvature of invariance objectives. Under sufficient diversity and local Gaussian regularity, the minimax risk is $\mathbb{E}[\dist(\hat{V},V_{\mathrm{inv}})^2]=Θ(k(d-k)/(n|\mathcal{E}|))$, and under label-induced shifts a phase transition occurs at $n^*\propto k(d-k)/(|\mathcal{E}|γ^2)$ with refined estimation error scaling proportional to $1/γ^2$. Synthetic and real datasets illustrate the predicted gaps and transitions and motivate simple diversity diagnostics.

</details>


### [232] [Circuit Synchronization Precedes Generalization: Causal Evidence from Fourier Structure in Grokking Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.12966)
*Achyuthan Sivasankar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出频率同步度（FSD）指标，能在Grokking现象发生前500-3000步（平均提前1722步）检测到傅里叶电路的同步化，并揭示了Grokking时间与权重衰减的严格反比关系（Δ_t ∝ 1/λ），且该前兆信号是跨模块电路特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象中Transformer在模算术任务上从接近随机精度突然跃迁到近乎完美验证精度的机制、时序因果结构及可控性尚不清晰，现有方法缺乏早期可预测指标。

Method: 1. 提出频率同步度（FSD）：基于置换检验的归一化傅里叶电路同步指标，无需先验电路知识；2. 在9种模加法配置（素数p∈{53,71,97,113,131}，3种随机种子）上验证；3. 通过分叉训练实验控制权重衰减λ，建立Grokking时间提前量Δ_t与1/λ的线性关系；4. 架构消融实验（仅注意力、仅MLP、单层模型）。

Result: 1. FSD比Grokking平均提前1722步（全部9个案例均为正值，符号检验p≈0.004）；2. FSD在所有9个案例中均早于受限logit损失基线（Nanda等人的排除损失）；3. 权重衰减控制实验显示Δ_t ∝ 1/λ，在p=53、97、131上R²=1.00和0.99；4. 仅注意力模型有强FSD前兆并发生Grokking，仅MLP模型永不Grokking，单层模型的FSD滞后。

Conclusion: FSD是目前最早可用的Grokking预测指标；Grokking的相位间差距本质上是正则化现象，其时间由权重衰减控制（Δ_t ∝ 1/λ）；傅里叶电路同步前兆是多模块（跨层）电路特性，非单层或MLP独有。

Abstract: Grokking -- where a transformer on modular arithmetic suddenly transitions from near-chance to near-perfect validation accuracy -- is attributed to a Fourier circuit, but its timing, causal structure, and controllability remain poorly understood. We introduce the Frequency Synchronization Degree (FSD), a normalised, permutation-tested metric for Fourier circuit synchronisation requiring no prior circuit knowledge. Across nine modular addition configurations (primes p in {53, 71, 97, 113, 131}, three seeds), FSD synchronises 500-3,000 steps before grokking (mean lead +1,722 steps; all nine positive, sign-test p~0.004), and precedes a restricted-logit loss baseline (Nanda et al.'s excluded loss) in all nine cases, making it the earliest available predictor.
